import sys
import os
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path

APP_NAME = "CutterLog"
APP_VERSION = "1.0.1"

# ----------------------------------------------------
# 1. مدیریت مسیرهای استاندارد ذخیره‌سازی داده (Cross-Platform)
# ----------------------------------------------------
if sys.platform == "win32":
    APP_DATA_DIR = Path.home() / "AppData" / "Roaming" / APP_NAME
elif sys.platform == "darwin":
    APP_DATA_DIR = Path.home() / "Library" / "Application Support" / APP_NAME
else:
    APP_DATA_DIR = Path.home() / ".local" / "share" / APP_NAME

APP_DATA_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
DB_PATH = APP_DATA_DIR / "cutterlog.db"


# ----------------------------------------------------
# 2. هلپر داینامیک پیدا کردن Assetها
# ----------------------------------------------------
def get_bundle_dir() -> Path:
    if getattr(sys, "frozen", False):
        if hasattr(sys, "_MEIPASS"):
            return Path(sys._MEIPASS)
        return Path(sys.executable).parent
    return Path(__file__).resolve().parent


def get_asset_path(relative_path: str) -> Path:
    base = get_bundle_dir()
    return base / relative_path


# ----------------------------------------------------
# 3. هلپر استاندارد باز کردن پوشه بدون باز شدن پنجره کنسول سیاه
# ----------------------------------------------------
def open_path_in_os(path_str: str) -> bool:
    if not path_str or not os.path.exists(path_str):
        return False
    try:
        resolved = str(Path(path_str).resolve())
        if sys.platform == "win32":
            os.startfile(resolved)
        elif sys.platform == "darwin":
            subprocess.Popen(["open", resolved], stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL)
        else:
            subprocess.Popen(["xdg-open", resolved], stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL)
        return True
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Error opening path in OS: {e}")
        return False


# ----------------------------------------------------
# 4. پالت رنگی و استیج‌های پایپ‌لاین
# ----------------------------------------------------

# ==================================================
# FILE: main.py
# ==================================================
# ==================================================
# FILE: main.py
# ==================================================
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from PySide6.QtWidgets import QApplication
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt
from PySide6.QtGui import QFont, QIcon

from config import get_asset_path
from core.font_manager import FontManager
from core.app_setup import initialize_application
from views.main_window import MainWindow


def bootstrap_app():
    if sys.platform == "win32":
        QApplication.setHighDpiScaleFactorRoundingPolicy(
            Qt.HighDpiScaleFactorRoundingPolicy.PassThrough
        )
        
    app = QApplication(sys.argv)
    app.setLayoutDirection(Qt.LayoutDirection.RightToLeft)
    
    # ----------------------------------------------------
    # تنظیم آیکون سراسری برنامه برای نوار عنوان و تسک‌بار
    # ----------------------------------------------------
    icon_path = get_asset_path("assets/icons/icon.ico")
    if icon_path.exists():
        app.setWindowIcon(QIcon(str(icon_path.resolve())))

    # راه‌اندازی زیرساخت‌های پایه (دیتابیس، پوشه‌ها، تنظیمات)
    initialize_application()

    # اعمال فونت و تم
    app_font = FontManager.get_font(size=10, weight=QFont.Weight.Normal)
    app.setFont(app_font)

    qss_paths = [
        get_asset_path("styles/dark_theme.qss"),
        Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "styles" / "dark_theme.qss",
        Path("styles/dark_theme.qss"),
    ]
    
    for qss_p in qss_paths:
        try:
            if qss_p.exists():
                with open(str(qss_p.resolve()), "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    app.setStyleSheet(f.read())
                break
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"QSS load notice for {qss_p}: {e}")

    window = MainWindow()
    window.show()
    sys.exit(app.exec())


if __name__ == "__main__":
    bootstrap_app()

import sys
from pathlib import Path
from config import APP_DATA_DIR, DB_PATH
from database.db_manager import db
from core.font_manager import FontManager
from core.settings_manager import SettingsManager


def initialize_application() -> bool:
    """
    راه‌اندازی زیرساخت‌های اولیه نرم‌افزار قبل از لود UI:
    - ایجاد دایرکتوری داده‌های برنامه
    - راه‌اندازی دیتابیس و اجرای مایگریشن‌ها
    - بارگذاری فونت‌های سیستم
    - بارگذاری تنظیمات اولیه
    """
    try:
        # ۱. اطمینان از وجود پوشه داده‌ها
        APP_DATA_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

        # ۲. مقداردهی اولیه پایگاه داده
        db.init_db()

        # ۳. بارگذاری فونت‌ها
        FontManager.load_fonts()

        # ۴. اطمینان از مقداردهی تنظیمات
        SettingsManager.load_settings()

        return True
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Error during application bootstrap: {e}")
        return False

# ==================================================
# FILE: core/audio_vault_manager.py
# ==================================================
import os
import json
import stat
import shutil
from pathlib import Path
from typing import List, Dict, Any, Optional

from config import APP_DATA_DIR
from core.settings_manager import SettingsManager

SUPPORTED_AUDIO_EXTS = {".mp3", ".wav", ".flac", ".aac", ".m4a", ".ogg", ".wma", ".aiff"}
VIRTUAL_INDEX_FILE = APP_DATA_DIR / "music_vault_refs.json"


def get_vault_root_dir() -> Path:
    settings = SettingsManager.load_settings()
    custom_path = settings.get("music_vault_path")
    
    if custom_path and os.path.exists(custom_path):
        root = Path(custom_path)
    else:
        docs = Path.home() / "Documents" / "CutterLog_Music_Vault"
        docs.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        root = docs
        
    return root


def is_copy_mode_enabled() -> bool:
    settings = SettingsManager.load_settings()
    return settings.get("copy_audio_to_vault", True)


def _load_virtual_index() -> Dict[str, List[str]]:
    """خواندن لیست دسته‌بندی‌های مجازی و آدرس فایل‌های متصل به آن"""
    if not VIRTUAL_INDEX_FILE.exists():
        return {}
    try:
        with open(VIRTUAL_INDEX_FILE, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            return json.load(f)
    except Exception:
        return {}


def _save_virtual_index(data: Dict[str, List[str]]) -> bool:
    try:
        with open(VIRTUAL_INDEX_FILE, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            json.dump(data, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=4)
        return True
    except Exception:
        return False


def _remove_readonly(func, path, excinfo):
    try:
        os.chmod(path, stat.S_IWRITE)
        func(path)
    except Exception:
        pass


def extract_audio_cover(file_path: str) -> Optional[bytes]:
    """استخراج تصویر کاور تعبیه‌شده در فایل صوتی بدون نیاز به پکیج‌های سنگین جانبی"""
    try:
        ext = Path(file_path).suffix.lower()
        if ext == ".mp3" and os.path.exists(file_path):
            with open(file_path, "rb") as f:
                data = f.read(500000)
                apic_idx = data.find(b"APIC")
                if apic_idx != -1:
                    jpg_start = data.find(b"\xff\xd8\xff", apic_idx)
                    if jpg_start != -1:
                        jpg_end = data.find(b"\xff\xd9", jpg_start)
                        if jpg_end != -1:
                            return data[jpg_start:jpg_end + 2]
                    
                    png_start = data.find(b"\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n", apic_idx)
                    if png_start != -1:
                        png_end = data.find(b"IEND\xaeB`\x82", png_start)
                        if png_end != -1:
                            return data[png_start:png_end + 8]
    except Exception:
        pass
    return None


class AudioVaultManager:
    @staticmethod
    def get_categories() -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        categories = []
        copy_mode = is_copy_mode_enabled()

        if copy_mode:
            root = get_vault_root_dir()
            try:
                if root.exists():
                    for entry in sorted(root.iterdir()):
                        if entry.is_dir() and not entry.name.startswith("."):
                            count = sum(1 for f in entry.iterdir() if f.is_file() and f.suffix.lower() in SUPPORTED_AUDIO_EXTS)
                            categories.append({
                                "name": entry.name,
                                "path": str(entry.resolve()),
                                "count": count,
                                "is_virtual": False
                            })
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"Error scanning physical categories: {e}")
        else:
            v_index = _load_virtual_index()
            for cat_name, file_list in sorted(v_index.items()):
                valid_files = [fp for fp in file_list if os.path.exists(fp) and Path(fp).suffix.lower() in SUPPORTED_AUDIO_EXTS]
                categories.append({
                    "name": cat_name,
                    "path": f"virtual://{cat_name}",
                    "count": len(valid_files),
                    "is_virtual": True
                })
        return categories

    @staticmethod
    def create_category(name: str) -> bool:
        clean_name = "".join(c for c in name if c not in r'\/:*?"<>|').strip()
        if not clean_name:
            return False

        if is_copy_mode_enabled():
            root = get_vault_root_dir()
            target = root / clean_name
            if target.exists():
                return False
            try:
                target.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
                return True
            except Exception:
                return False
        else:
            v_index = _load_virtual_index()
            if clean_name in v_index:
                return False
            v_index[clean_name] = []
            return _save_virtual_index(v_index)

    @staticmethod
    def rename_category(old_name: str, new_name: str) -> bool:
        clean_new = "".join(c for c in new_name if c not in r'\/:*?"<>|').strip()
        if not clean_new or old_name == clean_new:
            return False

        if is_copy_mode_enabled():
            root = get_vault_root_dir()
            src = root / old_name
            dest = root / clean_new
            if not src.exists() or dest.exists():
                return False
            try:
                src.rename(dest)
                return True
            except Exception:
                return False
        else:
            v_index = _load_virtual_index()
            if old_name not in v_index or clean_new in v_index:
                return False
            v_index[clean_new] = v_index.pop(old_name)
            return _save_virtual_index(v_index)

    @staticmethod
    def delete_category(folder_name: str) -> bool:
        if is_copy_mode_enabled():
            root = get_vault_root_dir()
            target = root / folder_name
            if target.exists() and target.is_dir():
                try:
                    shutil.rmtree(str(target), onerror=_remove_readonly)
                    return not target.exists()
                except Exception:
                    return False
            return False
        else:
            v_index = _load_virtual_index()
            if folder_name in v_index:
                del v_index[folder_name]
                return _save_virtual_index(v_index)
            return False

    @staticmethod
    def get_tracks_in_category(folder_identifier: str, search_query: str = "") -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        tracks = []
        query = search_query.strip().lower()

        if folder_identifier.startswith("virtual://"):
            cat_name = folder_identifier.replace("virtual://", "")
            v_index = _load_virtual_index()
            file_paths = v_index.get(cat_name, [])

            for f_path_str in file_paths:
                file = Path(f_path_str)
                if file.is_file() and file.suffix.lower() in SUPPORTED_AUDIO_EXTS:
                    if query and query not in file.name.lower():
                        continue
                    try:
                        size_bytes = file.stat().st_size
                        size_str = f"{size_bytes / 1024:.1f} KB" if size_bytes < 1024 * 1024 else f"{size_bytes / (1024 * 1024):.1f} MB"
                        cover_bytes = extract_audio_cover(str(file.resolve()))
                        tracks.append({
                            "name": file.stem,
                            "full_name": file.name,
                            "file_path": str(file.resolve()),
                            "ext": file.suffix.replace(".", "").upper(),
                            "size_str": size_str,
                            "cover_bytes": cover_bytes
                        })
                    except Exception:
                        continue
        else:
            path = Path(folder_identifier)
            if not path.exists() or not path.is_dir():
                return tracks

            try:
                for file in sorted(path.iterdir()):
                    if file.is_file() and file.suffix.lower() in SUPPORTED_AUDIO_EXTS:
                        if query and query not in file.name.lower():
                            continue
                        size_bytes = file.stat().st_size
                        size_str = f"{size_bytes / 1024:.1f} KB" if size_bytes < 1024 * 1024 else f"{size_bytes / (1024 * 1024):.1f} MB"
                        cover_bytes = extract_audio_cover(str(file.resolve()))

                        tracks.append({
                            "name": file.stem,
                            "full_name": file.name,
                            "file_path": str(file.resolve()),
                            "ext": file.suffix.replace(".", "").upper(),
                            "size_str": size_str,
                            "cover_bytes": cover_bytes
                        })
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"Error scanning tracks: {e}")

        return tracks

    @staticmethod
    def ingest_files_to_category(target_folder_identifier: str, source_file_paths: List[str]) -> int:
        success_count = 0
        copy_mode = is_copy_mode_enabled()

        if not copy_mode or target_folder_identifier.startswith("virtual://"):
            cat_name = target_folder_identifier.replace("virtual://", "") if target_folder_identifier.startswith("virtual://") else Path(target_folder_identifier).name
            v_index = _load_virtual_index()
            if cat_name not in v_index:
                v_index[cat_name] = []

            existing_set = set(v_index[cat_name])

            for src in source_file_paths:
                src_path = Path(src)
                if src_path.is_file() and src_path.suffix.lower() in SUPPORTED_AUDIO_EXTS:
                    full_p = str(src_path.resolve())
                    if full_p not in existing_set:
                        v_index[cat_name].append(full_p)
                        existing_set.add(full_p)
                        success_count += 1
                elif src_path.is_dir():
                    for sub_file in src_path.rglob("*"):
                        if sub_file.is_file() and sub_file.suffix.lower() in SUPPORTED_AUDIO_EXTS:
                            full_p = str(sub_file.resolve())
                            if full_p not in existing_set:
                                v_index[cat_name].append(full_p)
                                existing_set.add(full_p)
                                success_count += 1

            _save_virtual_index(v_index)
            return success_count

        target_dir = Path(target_folder_identifier)
        if not target_dir.exists() or not target_dir.is_dir():
            return 0

        for src in source_file_paths:
            src_path = Path(src)
            if src_path.is_file() and src_path.suffix.lower() in SUPPORTED_AUDIO_EXTS:
                dest_file = target_dir / src_path.name
                if dest_file.exists():
                    stem, ext = src_path.stem, src_path.suffix
                    counter = 1
                    while (target_dir / f"{stem}_{counter}{ext}").exists():
                        counter += 1
                    dest_file = target_dir / f"{stem}_{counter}{ext}"
                try:
                    shutil.copy2(src_path, dest_file)
                    success_count += 1
                except Exception:
                    pass
            elif src_path.is_dir():
                for sub_file in src_path.rglob("*"):
                    if sub_file.is_file() and sub_file.suffix.lower() in SUPPORTED_AUDIO_EXTS:
                        dest_file = target_dir / sub_file.name
                        if dest_file.exists():
                            stem, ext = sub_file.stem, sub_file.suffix
                            counter = 1
                            while (target_dir / f"{stem}_{counter}{ext}").exists():
                                counter += 1
                            dest_file = target_dir / f"{stem}_{counter}{ext}"
                        try:
                            shutil.copy2(sub_file, dest_file)
                            success_count += 1
                        except Exception:
                            pass

        return success_count

    @staticmethod
    def set_custom_vault_path(new_path: str) -> bool:
        if not new_path or not os.path.exists(new_path):
            return False
        settings = SettingsManager.load_settings()
        settings["music_vault_path"] = str(Path(new_path).resolve())
        SettingsManager.save_settings(settings)
        return True

# FILE: core\copilot_engine.py
# ==================================================
# ==================================================
# FILE: core/copilot_engine.py
# ==================================================
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from typing import Dict, List, Any, Optional
from sqlalchemy import select, func
from sqlalchemy.orm import joinedload, selectinload

from database.db_manager import db
from database.models import (
    Project, Client, Transaction, ProjectRevision,
    StageEnum, WorkflowStatusEnum
)
from time_tracking.models import WorkSession
from core.finance_controller import FinanceController
from core.project_controller import ProjectController
from core.settings_manager import SettingsManager
from core.persian_utils import format_toman, to_persian_numbers, gregorian_to_jalali


class CopilotEngine:
    """
    موتور محاسباتی و هوش تحلیلی CutterLog Copilot
    تحلیل راندمان، سنجش اعتبار استودیوها، رادار ریسک و پیشنهاد اولویت‌بندی
    """

    # =========================================================================
    # ۱. تحلیل راندمان، تمرکز و ساعات طلایی کار (Productivity & Focus Analytics)
    # =========================================================================
    @staticmethod
    def get_productivity_insights(days_back: int = 30) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        محاسبه ساعات طلایی تمرکز، شاخص تمرکز مفید و میانگین سرعت کار
        """
        since_date = datetime.utcnow() - timedelta(days=days_back)

        with db.get_session() as session:
            stmt = select(WorkSession).where(WorkSession.start_time >= since_date)
            sessions = session.scalars(stmt).all()

        if not sessions:
            return {
                "has_data": False,
                "golden_hours_label": "داده کافی نیست",
                "focus_ratio_pct": 0,
                "total_hours_analyzed": 0,
                "efficiency_advice": "با ثبت جلسات تدوین، تحلیل عادات کاری و ساعات طلایی تمرکز شما فعال خواهد شد.",
                "hourly_breakdown": {}
            }

        # تحلیل بازه‌های ساعتی (بخش‌بندی ۲ ساعته در شبانه‌روز)
        hourly_active_sec = [0] * 24
        total_active_sec = 0
        total_idle_sec = 0
        total_paused_sec = 0

        for s in sessions:
            act = s.active_duration or 0
            idl = s.idle_duration or 0
            pau = s.paused_duration or 0

            total_active_sec += act
            total_idle_sec += idl
            total_paused_sec += pau

            if s.start_time:
                h = s.start_time.hour
                hourly_active_sec[h] += act

        # یافتن ساعات اوج فعالیت و راندمان (بازه ۳ ساعته با بیشترین کارکرد)
        max_3h_work = 0
        peak_start_h = 16
        for h in range(22):
            w_sum = hourly_active_sec[h] + hourly_active_sec[h + 1] + hourly_active_sec[h + 2]
            if w_sum > max_3h_work:
                max_3h_work = w_sum
                peak_start_h = h

        peak_end_h = (peak_start_h + 3) % 24
        golden_hours_label = f"ساعت {peak_start_h:02d}:00 الی {peak_end_h:02d}:00"

        # ضریب تمرکز (Focus Ratio): نسبت زمان خالص کار به کل زمان‌های غیرفعال/استراحت
        total_time = total_active_sec + total_idle_sec + total_paused_sec
        focus_pct = int((total_active_sec / total_time) * 100) if total_time > 0 else 85

        if focus_pct >= 80:
            advice = f"بیشترین راندمان کاری شما در بازه {golden_hours_label} ثبت شده است. تمرکز شما روی تایم‌لاین عالی و بالای ۸۰٪ است."
        elif focus_pct >= 60:
            advice = f"ساعات طلایی شما {golden_hours_label} است؛ پیشنهاد می‌شود کات‌های سنگین و اصلاح رنگ را در این ساعات متمرکز کنید."
        else:
            advice = "مدت زمان توقف و حواس‌پرتی در حین جلسات بالاست. پیشنهاد می‌شود از تایمرهای تمرکز پومودورو استفاده کنید."

        return {
            "has_data": True,
            "golden_hours_label": golden_hours_label,
            "focus_ratio_pct": focus_pct,
            "total_active_hours": round(total_active_sec / 3600, 1),
            "efficiency_advice": advice,
            "sessions_count": len(sessions)
        }

    # =========================================================================
    # ۲. ارزیابی خوش‌حسابی و اعتبار استودیوها (Studio Health & Credit Score)
    # =========================================================================
    @staticmethod
    def get_studio_credit_scores() -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """
        محاسبه نمره خوش‌حسابی (از ۱۰۰) برای هر استودیو بر اساس:
        - سرعت تسویه بعد از تحویل
        - نسبت پروژه‌های تسویه‌شده به بدهکار
        - حجم کل معاملات
        """
        studio_insights = []

        with db.get_session() as session:
            clients = session.scalars(
                select(Client).options(
                    selectinload(Client.projects).selectinload(Project.transactions)
                )
            ).all()

        for c in clients:
            summary = FinanceController.get_studio_debt_summary(c.id)
            total_projects = summary.get("all_projects_count", 0)
            if total_projects == 0:
                continue

            total_fee = summary.get("total_fee", 0)
            total_paid = summary.get("total_paid", 0)
            remaining = summary.get("total_remaining", 0)
            unsettled_count = len(summary.get("unsettled_projects", []))

            # پایه امتیاز: ۱۰۰
            score = 100

            # ۱. کسر امتیاز به ازای پروژه‌های تسویه‌نشده
            if total_projects > 0:
                unsettled_ratio = unsettled_count / total_projects
                score -= int(unsettled_ratio * 40)

            # ۲. کسر امتیاز بر اساس درصد مانده حساب به کل قراردادها
            if total_fee > 0:
                debt_ratio = remaining / total_fee
                score -= int(debt_ratio * 35)

            score = max(10, min(100, score))

            # تعیین وضعیت و توصیه تدوین‌گر
            if score >= 85:
                tag = "خوش‌حساب و مطمئن"
                tag_color = "#10B981"
                action_tip = "در اولویت تحویل قرار دارد؛ تسویه حساب‌ها منظم و مطمئن است."
                grade = "A"
            elif score >= 60:
                tag = "متوسط (نیازمند پیگیری)"
                tag_color = "#F59E0B"
                action_tip = f"دارای {to_persian_numbers(unsettled_count)} پروژه معوق؛ قبل از تحویل کار جدید، مانده‌های قبلی را پیگیری کنید."
                grade = "B"
            else:
                tag = "بدحساب / پرخطر"
                tag_color = "#EF4444"
                action_tip = f"هشدار: مانده حساب بالا ({format_toman(remaining)})؛ از تحویل خروجی نهایی بدون تسویه کامل خودداری شود."
                grade = "C"

            studio_insights.append({
                "client_id": c.id,
                "studio_name": c.company or c.full_name,
                "credit_score": score,
                "grade": grade,
                "tag": tag,
                "tag_color": tag_color,
                "total_fee": total_fee,
                "total_paid": total_paid,
                "remaining": remaining,
                "unsettled_projects_count": unsettled_count,
                "action_tip": action_tip
            })

        studio_insights.sort(key=lambda x: x["credit_score"])
        return studio_insights

    # =========================================================================
    # ۳. رادار ریسک و تحلیل گلوگاه‌های پایپ‌لاین (Pipeline Radar & Bottlenecks)
    # =========================================================================
    @staticmethod
    def get_pipeline_radar() -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        اسکن هوشمند پایپ‌لاین برای کشف خطرات احتمالی، ددلاین‌های بحرانی و آرشیو خودکار
        """
        urgent_deadlines = []
        pending_review_alerts = []
        heavy_revisions = []

        now = datetime.utcnow()
        settings = SettingsManager.load_settings()
        limit_days = settings.get("auto_archive_delivered_days", 7)

        active_projects = ProjectController.get_active_projects()

        for p in active_projects:
            # ۱. بررسی ددلاین‌های نزدیک (زیر ۳ روز با پیشرفت ناقص)
            if p.deadline:
                days_left = (p.deadline.date() - now.date()).days
                c_cnt, t_cnt, pct = ProjectController.calculate_project_progress(p)
                if days_left <= 2 and pct < 100:
                    urgent_deadlines.append({
                        "id": p.id,
                        "title": p.couple_names or p.title,
                        "days_left": days_left,
                        "progress_pct": pct,
                        "remaining_clips": t_cnt - c_cnt,
                        "warning": f"تنها {to_persian_numbers(max(0, days_left))} روز تا موعد تحویل باقی است و {to_persian_numbers(t_cnt - c_cnt)} کلیپ هنوز تدوین نشده است!"
                    })

            # ۲. بررسی پروژه‌های تحویل‌شده و در آستانه آرشیو خودکار
            if p.workflow_status == WorkflowStatusEnum.UNDER_REVIEW and p.delivered_at and limit_days > 0:
                elapsed = (now - p.delivered_at).days
                days_to_archive = limit_days - elapsed
                if 0 <= days_to_archive <= 3:
                    pending_review_alerts.append({
                        "id": p.id,
                        "title": p.couple_names or p.title,
                        "days_to_archive": days_to_archive,
                        "delivered_date": gregorian_to_jalali(p.delivered_at),
                        "notice": f"تحویل‌شده به آتلیه؛ در صورت عدم ارسال اصلاحیه، تا {to_persian_numbers(days_to_archive)} روز دیگر به صورت خودکار آرشیو می‌شود."
                    })

            # ۳. شناسایی گلوگاه‌های اصلاحیه (پروژه‌های دارای بیش از ۲ دور اصلاحیه)
            if p.revisions and len(p.revisions) >= 2:
                active_rev = ProjectController.get_active_revision(p)
                if active_rev:
                    heavy_revisions.append({
                        "id": p.id,
                        "title": p.couple_names or p.title,
                        "revision_round": len(p.revisions),
                        "extra_fee": sum(r.extra_fee for r in p.revisions),
                        "notice": f"این پروژه در دور {to_persian_numbers(len(p.revisions))} اصلاحیه قرار دارد؛ هزینه مازاد این دوره‌ها را بررسی کنید."
                    })

        return {
            "urgent_deadlines": urgent_deadlines,
            "pending_review_alerts": pending_review_alerts,
            "heavy_revisions": heavy_revisions,
            "total_risks_count": len(urgent_deadlines) + len(pending_review_alerts) + len(heavy_revisions)
        }

    # =========================================================================
    # ۴. پیشنهاد اولویت‌بندی هوشمند میز کار (Smart Project Next Action)
    # =========================================================================
    @staticmethod
    def recommend_next_project() -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """
        پیشنهاد هوشمند اینکه الان بهتر است تدوین کدام پروژه شروع شود
        """
        active_projects = ProjectController.get_active_projects()
        if not active_projects:
            return None

        candidates = []
        now = datetime.utcnow()

        for p in active_projects:
            active_rev = ProjectController.get_active_revision(p)
            c_cnt, t_cnt, pct = ProjectController.calculate_project_progress(p)

            # پروژه‌ای که ۱۰۰٪ شده و تحویل داده شده اولویت تدوین ندارد
            if pct == 100 and not active_rev:
                continue

            priority_score = 0
            reasons = []

            # اولویت فوق‌العاده بالا: اصلاحیه فعال
            if active_rev:
                priority_score += 50
                reasons.append("اصلاحیه فعال در انتظار تدوین")

            # اولویت بر اساس ددلاین
            if p.deadline:
                days_left = (p.deadline.date() - now.date()).days
                if days_left < 0:
                    priority_score += 60
                    reasons.append("ددلاین گذشته (تأخیر بحرانی)")
                elif days_left <= 2:
                    priority_score += 40
                    reasons.append("ددلاین بسیار نزدیک (کمتر از ۴۸ ساعت)")
                elif days_left <= 5:
                    priority_score += 20
                    reasons.append("سررسید در هفته جاری")

            # اولویت بر اساس پیشرفت (پروژه‌های نزدیک به اتمام سریع‌تر تحویل شوند)
            if 60 <= pct < 100:
                priority_score += 15
                reasons.append("پیشرفت بالای ۶۰٪ (آماده بستن پرونده)")

            candidates.append({
                "project": p,
                "score": priority_score,
                "reason": " • ".join(reasons) or "پروژه در جریان عادی پایپ‌لاین",
                "progress_pct": pct,
                "clips_left": t_cnt - c_cnt if not active_rev else 0
            })

        if not candidates:
            return None

        candidates.sort(key=lambda x: x["score"], reverse=True)
        top = candidates[0]
        p = top["project"]

        return {
            "project_id": p.id,
            "title": p.couple_names or p.title,
            "studio_name": p.client.company if (p.client and p.client.company) else (p.client.full_name if p.client else "بدون استودیو"),
            "progress_pct": top["progress_pct"],
            "recommendation_reason": top["reason"],
            "deadline_str": gregorian_to_jalali(p.deadline) if p.deadline else "تعیین نشده"
        }

    # =========================================================================
    # ۵. بریفینگ مدیریتی روزانه (Daily Copilot Executive Summary)
    # =========================================================================
    @staticmethod
    def generate_daily_briefing() -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        تولید گزارش متنی/آماری ساختاریافته برای شروع روز کاری تدوین‌گر
        """
        prod = CopilotEngine.get_productivity_insights(days_back=14)
        radar = CopilotEngine.get_pipeline_radar()
        rec_proj = CopilotEngine.recommend_next_project()
        fin_data = FinanceController.get_financial_dashboard_data(date_range_key="all")

        cards = fin_data.get("summary_cards", {})
        total_remaining = cards.get("total_remaining", 0)

        # ساخت بولت‌پوینت‌های تحلیلی
        bullet_points = []

        # ۱. نکته تمرکز
        bullet_points.append({
            "type": "focus",
            "icon": "⚡",
            "text": prod.get("efficiency_advice")
        })

        # ۲. اولویت فوری میز کار
        if rec_proj:
            bullet_points.append({
                "type": "priority",
                "icon": "🎯",
                "text": f"پیشنهاد اولویت اول: تدوین «{rec_proj['title']}» ({rec_proj['recommendation_reason']})."
            })

        # ۳. هشدار ددلاین یا ریسک
        if radar["urgent_deadlines"]:
            first_risk = radar["urgent_deadlines"][0]
            bullet_points.append({
                "type": "warning",
                "icon": "⚠️",
                "text": f"ددلاین فوری: {first_risk['title']} ({first_risk['warning']})."
            })
        elif radar["pending_review_alerts"]:
            first_alert = radar["pending_review_alerts"][0]
            bullet_points.append({
                "type": "review",
                "icon": "⏳",
                "text": f"یادآوری بازبینی: {first_alert['title']} ({first_alert['notice']})."
            })

        # ۴. نکته مالی
        if total_remaining > 0:
            bullet_points.append({
                "type": "finance",
                "icon": "💰",
                "text": f"کل مطالبات وصول‌نشده از استودیوها: {format_toman(total_remaining)}."
            })
        else:
            bullet_points.append({
                "type": "finance",
                "icon": "✓",
                "text": "حساب تمامی پروژه‌های تحویل‌شده کاملاً تسویه است."
            })

        return {
            "generated_at": datetime.now(),
            "bullet_points": bullet_points,
            "recommended_project": rec_proj,
            "focus_pct": prod.get("focus_ratio_pct", 85),
            "total_receivables": total_remaining,
            "active_risks_count": radar.get("total_risks_count", 0)
        }

# ==================================================
# FILE: core\excel_exporter.py
# ==================================================
# core/excel_exporter.py
from typing import List, Dict, Any
from core.persian_utils import format_toman, to_persian_numbers


def export_transactions_to_excel(transactions_data: List[Dict[str, Any]], output_path: str) -> bool:
    """تولید فایل خروجی اکسل از تراکنش‌ها با پشتیبانی از باز شدن در Excel"""
    try:
        # ۱. تلاش برای خروجی با openpyxl در صورت نصب بودن
        try:
            import openpyxl
            from openpyxl.styles import Font, Alignment, PatternFill, Border, Side

            wb = openpyxl.Workbook()
            ws = wb.active
            ws.title = "دفتر مالی و تراکنش‌ها"
            ws.sheet_view.rightToLeft = True

            headers = ["کد تراکنش", "پروژه / عروس و داماد", "استودیو / آتلیه", "مبلغ واریزی (تومان)", "تاریخ واریز"]
            ws.append(headers)

            # استایل هدر
            header_fill = PatternFill(start_color="1E1E24", end_color="1E1E24", fill_type="solid")
            header_font = Font(name="Tahoma", size=11, bold=True, color="FFFFFF")
            border_thin = Border(
                left=Side(style="thin", color="CCCCCC"),
                right=Side(style="thin", color="CCCCCC"),
                top=Side(style="thin", color="CCCCCC"),
                bottom=Side(style="thin", color="CCCCCC")
            )

            for col_idx in range(1, len(headers) + 1):
                cell = ws.cell(row=1, column=col_idx)
                cell.fill = header_fill
                cell.font = header_font
                cell.alignment = Alignment(horizontal="center", vertical="center")
                cell.border = border_thin

            for row_idx, item in enumerate(transactions_data, start=2):
                ws.append([
                    f"TX-{item.get('id', 0):04d}",
                    item.get("project", "-"),
                    item.get("client", "-"),
                    item.get("amount", 0),
                    item.get("date", "-")
                ])
                for col_idx in range(1, len(headers) + 1):
                    c = ws.cell(row=row_idx, column=col_idx)
                    c.font = Font(name="Tahoma", size=10)
                    c.alignment = Alignment(horizontal="center", vertical="center")
                    c.border = border_thin

            # تنظیم عرض ستون‌ها
            for col in ws.columns:
                max_len = max(len(str(cell.value or "")) for cell in col)
                col_letter = openpyxl.utils.get_column_letter(col[0].column)
                ws.column_dimensions[col_letter].width = max(max_len + 5, 18)

            wb.save(output_path)
            return True

        except ImportError:
            # ۲. خروجی جایگزین CSV استاندارد سازگار با اکسل در صورت نبود openpyxl
            import csv
            with open(output_path.replace(".xlsx", ".csv"), "w", encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as f:
                writer = csv.writer(f)
                writer.writerow(["کد تراکنش", "پروژه / عروس و داماد", "استودیو / آتلیه", "مبلغ واریزی (تومان)", "تاریخ"])
                for item in transactions_data:
                    writer.writerow([
                        f"TX-{item.get('id', 0):04d}",
                        item.get("project", "-"),
                        item.get("client", "-"),
                        item.get("amount", 0),
                        item.get("date", "-")
                    ])
            return True

    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Excel Export Error: {e}")
        return False

# ==================================================
# FILE: core\finance_controller.py
# ==================================================
# ==================================================
# FILE: core/finance_controller.py
# ==================================================
from typing import Dict, Any, List, Optional, Tuple
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
import jdatetime
from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import joinedload, selectinload
from database.db_manager import db
from database.models import Project, Transaction, Client, StageEnum, ProjectRevision, PaymentTypeEnum, WorkflowStatusEnum
from core.project_controller import ProjectController


class FinanceController:
    @staticmethod
    def is_project_eligible_for_finance(project: Project) -> bool:
        if project.is_archived:
            return True
        if project.delivered_at is not None:
            return True
        if project.current_stage == StageEnum.DELIVERED:
            return True
        if project.workflow_status in [
            WorkflowStatusEnum.UNDER_REVIEW, 
            WorkflowStatusEnum.REVISION_IN_PROGRESS, 
            WorkflowStatusEnum.REVISION_DONE, 
            WorkflowStatusEnum.ARCHIVED
        ]:
            return True
        return False

    @staticmethod
    def get_date_range_bounds(range_key: str, custom_start: Optional[datetime] = None, custom_end: Optional[datetime] = None) -> Tuple[Optional[datetime], Optional[datetime]]:
        now_j = jdatetime.datetime.now()
        
        if range_key == "today":
            start_j = now_j.replace(hour=0, minute=0, second=0, microsecond=0)
            end_j = now_j.replace(hour=23, minute=59, second=59, microsecond=999999)
            return start_j.togregorian(), end_j.togregorian()
            
        elif range_key == "this_week":
            start_j = (now_j - timedelta(days=now_j.weekday())).replace(hour=0, minute=0, second=0, microsecond=0)
            end_j = now_j.replace(hour=23, minute=59, second=59, microsecond=999999)
            return start_j.togregorian(), end_j.togregorian()
            
        elif range_key == "this_month":
            start_j = jdatetime.datetime(now_j.year, now_j.month, 1, 0, 0, 0)
            end_j = now_j.replace(hour=23, minute=59, second=59, microsecond=999999)
            return start_j.togregorian(), end_j.togregorian()
            
        elif range_key == "last_month":
            if now_j.month == 1:
                prev_year = now_j.year - 1
                prev_month = 12
            else:
                prev_year = now_j.year
                prev_month = now_j.month - 1
            
            start_j = jdatetime.datetime(prev_year, prev_month, 1, 0, 0, 0)
            days_in_month = 29 if (prev_month == 12 and not jdatetime.date(prev_year, 1, 1).isleap()) else (30 if prev_month > 6 else 31)
            end_j = jdatetime.datetime(prev_year, prev_month, days_in_month, 23, 59, 59)
            return start_j.togregorian(), end_j.togregorian()
            
        elif range_key == "this_year":
            start_j = jdatetime.datetime(now_j.year, 1, 1, 0, 0, 0)
            end_j = now_j.replace(hour=23, minute=59, second=59, microsecond=999999)
            return start_j.togregorian(), end_j.togregorian()
            
        elif range_key == "last_year":
            prev_year = now_j.year - 1
            start_j = jdatetime.datetime(prev_year, 1, 1, 0, 0, 0)
            days_in_12 = 30 if jdatetime.date(prev_year, 1, 1).isleap() else 29
            end_j = jdatetime.datetime(prev_year, 12, days_in_12, 23, 59, 59)
            return start_j.togregorian(), end_j.togregorian()
            
        elif range_key == "custom":
            return custom_start, custom_end
            
        return None, None

    @staticmethod
    def get_studio_debt_summary(client_id: int) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        with db.get_session() as session:
            stmt = (
                select(Project)
                .options(
                    joinedload(Project.client),
                    selectinload(Project.revisions),
                    joinedload(Project.transactions)
                )
                .where(Project.client_id == client_id)
                .order_by(Project.created_at.asc())
            )
            all_projects = session.scalars(stmt).unique().all()

        eligible_projects = [p for p in all_projects if FinanceController.is_project_eligible_for_finance(p)]

        total_fee = 0
        total_paid = 0
        unsettled_projects = []
        settled_projects = []

        for p in eligible_projects:
            fin = ProjectController.get_project_financial_summary(p)
            total_fee += fin["total_fee"]
            total_paid += fin["total_paid"]

            p_summary = {
                "id": p.id,
                "project_number": p.project_number or p.id,
                "title": p.couple_names or p.title,
                "total_fee": fin["total_fee"],
                "total_paid": fin["total_paid"],
                "remaining": fin["remaining"],
                "is_settled": fin["is_settled"],
                "created_at": p.created_at
            }

            if fin["remaining"] > 0:
                unsettled_projects.append(p_summary)
            else:
                settled_projects.append(p_summary)

        return {
            "total_fee": total_fee,
            "total_paid": total_paid,
            "total_remaining": max(0, total_fee - total_paid),
            "unsettled_projects": unsettled_projects,
            "settled_projects": settled_projects,
            "all_projects_count": len(eligible_projects)
        }

    @staticmethod
    def allocate_client_payment(
        client_id: int,
        total_amount: int,
        payment_type: PaymentTypeEnum = PaymentTypeEnum.STAGE_PAYMENT,
        notes: str = "",
        tracking_code: str = "",
        paid_at: Optional[datetime] = None
    ) -> List[int]:
        if total_amount <= 0:
            return []

        paid_at = paid_at or datetime.utcnow()
        created_transaction_ids = []

        with db.get_session() as session:
            stmt = (
                select(Project)
                .options(
                    selectinload(Project.revisions),
                    joinedload(Project.transactions)
                )
                .where(Project.client_id == client_id)
                .order_by(Project.created_at.asc())
            )
            all_projects = session.scalars(stmt).unique().all()
            eligible_projects = [p for p in all_projects if FinanceController.is_project_eligible_for_finance(p)]

            remaining_to_allocate = total_amount

            for p in eligible_projects:
                if remaining_to_allocate <= 0:
                    break

                fin = ProjectController.get_project_financial_summary(p)
                proj_remaining = fin["remaining"]

                if proj_remaining > 0:
                    allocated_for_this = min(proj_remaining, remaining_to_allocate)
                    combined_note = f"{notes.strip()} [تخصیص از واریزی استودیو: {allocated_for_this:,} تومان]".strip()

                    trx = Transaction(
                        project_id=p.id,
                        client_id=client_id,
                        amount=allocated_for_this,
                        payment_type=payment_type,
                        notes=combined_note,
                        tracking_code=tracking_code.strip() or None,
                        paid_at=paid_at
                    )
                    session.add(trx)
                    session.flush()
                    created_transaction_ids.append(trx.id)

                    ProjectController.log_event(
                        session, p.id, "TRANSACTION_ADDED",
                        f"دریافت مبلغ {allocated_for_this:,} تومان (تسویه خودکار)",
                        combined_note
                    )

                    remaining_to_allocate -= allocated_for_this

            if remaining_to_allocate > 0:
                last_project = eligible_projects[-1] if eligible_projects else None
                trx = Transaction(
                    project_id=last_project.id if last_project else None,
                    client_id=client_id,
                    amount=remaining_to_allocate,
                    payment_type=payment_type,
                    notes=f"{notes.strip()} [مانده بستانکار استودیو]".strip(),
                    tracking_code=tracking_code.strip() or None,
                    paid_at=paid_at
                )
                session.add(trx)
                session.flush()
                created_transaction_ids.append(trx.id)

            session.commit()

        return created_transaction_ids

    @staticmethod
    def delete_transaction(transaction_id: int) -> bool:
        """حذف ایمن تراکنش مالی و بازگردانی مانده حساب"""
        with db.get_session() as session:
            trx = session.get(Transaction, transaction_id)
            if not trx:
                return False
            
            project_id = trx.project_id
            client_id = trx.client_id
            amount_val = trx.amount

            if project_id:
                ProjectController.log_event(
                    session, project_id, "TRANSACTION_DELETED",
                    f"حذف تراکنش دریافت مبلغ {amount_val:,} تومان",
                    f"تراکنش با کد پیگیری {trx.tracking_code or '-'} حذف شد."
                )

            session.delete(trx)
            session.commit()

        return True

    @staticmethod
    def get_financial_dashboard_data(
        client_id: Optional[int] = None,
        date_range_key: str = "all",
        status_filter: str = "all",
        custom_start: Optional[datetime] = None,
        custom_end: Optional[datetime] = None
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        start_dt, end_dt = FinanceController.get_date_range_bounds(date_range_key, custom_start, custom_end)

        with db.get_session() as session:
            stmt = (
                select(Project)
                .options(
                    joinedload(Project.client),
                    selectinload(Project.clips),
                    selectinload(Project.revisions).selectinload(ProjectRevision.tasks),
                    joinedload(Project.transactions).joinedload(Transaction.revision)
                )
                .order_by(Project.created_at.desc())
            )
            if client_id is not None and client_id > 0:
                stmt = stmt.where(Project.client_id == client_id)

            all_projects = session.scalars(stmt).unique().all()

        filtered_projects = []
        for p in all_projects:
            if not FinanceController.is_project_eligible_for_finance(p):
                continue

            p_date = p.delivered_at or p.created_at
            if start_dt and p_date and p_date < start_dt:
                continue
            if end_dt and p_date and p_date > end_dt:
                continue

            fin = ProjectController.get_project_financial_summary(p)
            
            if status_filter == "unsettled" and fin["remaining"] <= 0:
                continue
            elif status_filter == "settled" and not fin["is_settled"]:
                continue
            elif status_filter == "overpaid" and fin["raw_balance"] >= 0:
                continue

            filtered_projects.append((p, fin))

        total_contracts_value = sum(fin["total_fee"] for _, fin in filtered_projects)
        total_received_amount = sum(fin["total_paid"] for _, fin in filtered_projects)
        total_remaining_amount = sum(fin["remaining"] for _, fin in filtered_projects)
        unsettled_projects_count = sum(1 for _, fin in filtered_projects if fin["remaining"] > 0)
        total_projects_count = len(filtered_projects)

        all_transactions = []
        for p, fin in filtered_projects:
            for t in p.transactions:
                t_date = t.paid_at or p.created_at
                if start_dt and t_date and t_date < start_dt:
                    continue
                if end_dt and t_date and t_date > end_dt:
                    continue

                all_transactions.append({
                    "id": t.id,
                    "project_id": p.id,
                    "project_title": p.couple_names or p.title,
                    "client_name": p.client.company if (p.client and p.client.company) else (p.client.full_name if p.client else "بدون استودیو"),
                    "amount": int(t.amount or 0),
                    "tracking_code": t.tracking_code or "",
                    "payment_type": t.payment_type.value if hasattr(t.payment_type, "value") else str(t.payment_type),
                    "notes": t.notes or "-",
                    "paid_at": t.paid_at,
                    "target_name": f"({t.revision.title})" if t.revision else "(پروژه اصلی)"
                })

        all_transactions.sort(key=lambda x: x["paid_at"] or datetime.min, reverse=True)

        receivables_list = []
        for p, fin in filtered_projects:
            if fin["remaining"] > 0:
                last_trx = p.transactions[0] if p.transactions else None
                receivables_list.append({
                    "project_id": p.id,
                    "project_number": p.project_number or p.id,
                    "couple_names": p.couple_names or p.title,
                    "client_name": p.client.company if (p.client and p.client.company) else (p.client.full_name if p.client else "بدون استودیو"),
                    "total_fee": fin["total_fee"],
                    "total_paid": fin["total_paid"],
                    "remaining": fin["remaining"],
                    "last_paid_at": last_trx.paid_at if last_trx else None,
                    "workflow_status_info": ProjectController.get_status_info(p),
                    "is_archived": p.is_archived
                })

        receivables_list.sort(key=lambda x: x["remaining"], reverse=True)

        studio_stats = {}
        for p, fin in filtered_projects:
            c_name = p.client.company if (p.client and p.client.company) else (p.client.full_name if p.client else "سایر / بدون استودیو")
            c_id = p.client_id or 0
            if c_id not in studio_stats:
                studio_stats[c_id] = {
                    "client_id": c_id,
                    "studio_name": c_name,
                    "projects_count": 0,
                    "total_fee": 0,
                    "total_paid": 0,
                    "remaining": 0
                }
            studio_stats[c_id]["projects_count"] += 1
            studio_stats[c_id]["total_fee"] += fin["total_fee"]
            studio_stats[c_id]["total_paid"] += fin["total_paid"]
            studio_stats[c_id]["remaining"] += fin["remaining"]

        studio_matrix = list(studio_stats.values())
        studio_matrix.sort(key=lambda x: x["remaining"], reverse=True)

        current_j_year = jdatetime.datetime.now().year
        month_names = [
            "فروردین", "اردیبهشت", "خرداد", "تیر", "مرداد", "شهریور",
            "مهر", "آبان", "آذر", "دی", "بهمن", "اسفند"
        ]
        monthly_income = [{"month": name, "month_idx": i+1, "amount": 0, "count": 0} for i, name in enumerate(month_names)]
        
        for t in all_transactions:
            t_dt = t.get("paid_at")
            if t_dt:
                try:
                    j_dt = jdatetime.datetime.fromgregorian(datetime=t_dt)
                    if j_dt.year == current_j_year:
                        m_idx = j_dt.month - 1
                        if 0 <= m_idx < 12:
                            monthly_income[m_idx]["amount"] += t["amount"]
                            monthly_income[m_idx]["count"] += 1
                except Exception:
                    pass

        return {
            "summary_cards": {
                "total_contracts": total_contracts_value,
                "total_received": total_received_amount,
                "total_remaining": total_remaining_amount,
                "unsettled_count": unsettled_projects_count,
                "total_projects": total_projects_count
            },
            "transactions": all_transactions,
            "receivables": receivables_list,
            "studio_matrix": studio_matrix,
            "monthly_income": monthly_income
        }

    @staticmethod
    def get_dashboard_metrics() -> Dict[str, Any]:
        data = FinanceController.get_financial_dashboard_data()
        with db.get_session() as session:
            stmt = (
                select(Project)
                .options(
                    selectinload(Project.clips),
                    selectinload(Project.revisions).selectinload(ProjectRevision.tasks)
                )
                .where(Project.is_archived == False)
            )
            active_projects = session.scalars(stmt).unique().all()

            in_editing_projects = [
                p for p in active_projects 
                if not FinanceController.is_project_eligible_for_finance(p)
            ]

            review_count = len([
                p for p in active_projects
                if ProjectController.get_status_info(p).get("key") in ["under_review", "revision_done"]
            ])

        return {
            "total_revenue": data["summary_cards"]["total_received"],
            "active_pipeline_value": data["summary_cards"]["total_contracts"],
            "active_projects_count": len(in_editing_projects),
            "review_count": review_count,
        }

# FILE: core\font_manager.py

# ==================================================
# FILE: core/font_manager.py
# ==================================================
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from PySide6.QtGui import QFontDatabase, QFont
from config import get_asset_path, get_bundle_dir


class FontManager:
    FONT_FAMILY = "Vazirmatn"

    @classmethod
    def load_fonts(cls) -> bool:
        """
        اسکن و بارگذاری خودکار تمام وزن‌های فونت وزیرمتن با پشتیبانی کامل
        از حالت Development و پکیج‌های کامپایل‌شده (.app در macOS و .exe در Windows)
        """
        candidate_dirs = [
            get_asset_path("fonts"),
            get_asset_path("assets/fonts"),
            get_asset_path("styles/fonts"),
            get_bundle_dir() / "fonts",
            get_bundle_dir() / "assets" / "fonts",
            Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "fonts",
            Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "assets" / "fonts",
            Path("fonts"),
            Path("assets/fonts"),
        ]

        loaded_any = False
        seen_dirs = set()

        for font_dir in candidate_dirs:
            try:
                resolved_dir = font_dir.resolve()
                if resolved_dir in seen_dirs or not resolved_dir.exists() or not resolved_dir.is_dir():
                    continue
                seen_dirs.add(resolved_dir)

                for font_file in resolved_dir.glob("*.[tT][tT][fF]"):
                    font_id = QFontDatabase.addApplicationFont(str(font_file.resolve()))
                    if font_id != -1:
                        loaded_any = True

                for font_file in resolved_dir.glob("*.[oO][tT][fF]"):
                    font_id = QFontDatabase.addApplicationFont(str(font_file.resolve()))
                    if font_id != -1:
                        loaded_any = True
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"Font scan notice for {font_dir}: {e}")

        return loaded_any

    @classmethod
    def get_font(cls, size: int = 10, weight: QFont.Weight = QFont.Weight.Normal) -> QFont:
        """
        دریافت شیء QFont استاندارد با سیستم Fallback مناسب برای هر دو سیستم‌عامل
        """
        font = QFont(cls.FONT_FAMILY, size)
        font.setWeight(weight)
        font.setStyleStrategy(QFont.StyleStrategy.PreferAntialias)
        
        # Fallback برای مک در صورتی که فونت وزیرمتن در دسترس نباشد
        if sys.platform == "darwin":
            font.setStyleHint(QFont.StyleHint.SansSerif)
            
        return font

        # ==========================================
        # پکیج‌ها، نشان‌ها و المان‌های تخصصی تدوین

        "clp_brand_mark": """<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="{color}" stroke-width="{stroke_width}" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><circle cx="6" cy="6" r="3"></circle><circle cx="6" cy="18" r="3"></circle><line x1="20" y1="4" x2="8.12" y2="15.88"></line><line x1="14.47" y1="14.48" x2="20" y2="20"></line><line x1="8.12" y1="8.12" x2="12" y2="12"></line></svg>""",
    }

    @classmethod
    def get_pixmap(cls, key: str, color: str = "#818CF8", size: int = 18, stroke_width: float = 2.0, **kwargs) -> QPixmap:
        """رندر و برگرداندن QPixmap با کیفیت وکتور"""
        svg_xml = cls.SVG_TEMPLATES.get(key)
        if not svg_xml:
            # فال‌بک تمیز به شکل لوزی نقطه‌ای به جای دایره خام
            svg_xml = """<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="{color}" stroke-width="{stroke_width}"><polygon points="12 2 22 12 12 22 2 12"></polygon></svg>"""

        formatted_svg = svg_xml.replace("{color}", color).replace("{stroke_width}", str(stroke_width))
        byte_array = QByteArray(formatted_svg.encode("utf-8"))
        renderer = QSvgRenderer(byte_array)

        pixmap = QPixmap(size, size)
        pixmap.fill(Qt.GlobalColor.transparent)

        painter = QPainter(pixmap)
        painter.setRenderHint(QPainter.RenderHint.Antialiasing)
        renderer.render(painter)
        painter.end()

        return pixmap

    @classmethod
    def get_icon(cls, key: str, color: str = "#818CF8", size: int = 18, stroke_width: float = 2.0, **kwargs) -> QIcon:
        """برگرداندن شیء استاندارد QIcon"""
        return QIcon(cls.get_pixmap(key, color, size, stroke_width, **kwargs))

# FILE: core\image_exporter.py
# ==================================================
# ==================================================
# FILE: core/image_exporter.py
# ==================================================
import os
from pathlib import Path
from PySide6.QtWidgets import QWidget
from PySide6.QtGui import QImage, QPainter, QColor, QRegion
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt, QPoint


class ProjectPassportImageExporter:
    @staticmethod
    def export_widget_to_image(target_widget: QWidget, output_path: str, scale_factor: float = 3.0) -> bool:
        """
        رندر مستقیم و شفاف با کیفیت بسیار بالا (Ultra High-DPI / 300 DPI Crisp)
        بدون تاری یا افت کیفیت فونت‌های فارسی و آیکون‌ها.
        """
        try:
            if not target_widget:
                return False

            # ذخیره اندازه اصلی ویجت
            orig_size = target_widget.size()

            # محاسبه دقیق ابعاد بر اساس محتوا
            target_widget.adjustSize()
            target_width = max(target_widget.width(), target_widget.sizeHint().width(), 820)
            target_height = max(target_widget.height(), target_widget.sizeHint().height(), 900)

            # تنظیم موقت اندازه واقعی روی ویجت
            target_widget.resize(target_width, target_height)

            # ساخت بوم با ابعاد بزرگ واقعی (کیفیت اصلی 3X بدون Stretch بعدی)
            img_w = int(target_width * scale_factor)
            img_h = int(target_height * scale_factor)

            image = QImage(img_w, img_h, QImage.Format.Format_ARGB32_Premultiplied)
            image.setDevicePixelRatio(scale_factor)
            image.fill(QColor("#0B0F17"))

            # نقاشی مستقیم المان‌ها روی رزولوشن بالا
            painter = QPainter(image)
            painter.setRenderHint(QPainter.RenderHint.Antialiasing, True)
            painter.setRenderHint(QPainter.RenderHint.TextAntialiasing, True)
            painter.setRenderHint(QPainter.RenderHint.SmoothPixmapTransform, True)

            target_widget.render(
                painter, 
                QPoint(0, 0), 
                QRegion(), 
                QWidget.RenderFlag.DrawWindowBackground | QWidget.RenderFlag.DrawChildren
            )
            painter.end()

            # بازگردانی ویجت به اندازه پنجره اصلی
            target_widget.resize(orig_size)

            # اطمینان از وجود پوشه خروجی و ذخیره نهایی
            out_dir = os.path.dirname(output_path)
            if out_dir and not os.path.exists(out_dir):
                os.makedirs(out_dir, exist_ok=True)

            return image.save(output_path, "PNG")

        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Export High-DPI Widget Error: {e}")
            return False

# FILE: core\media_scanner.py

import os
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Dict, List, Any, Optional

from PySide6.QtCore import Qt, QObject, Signal, QRunnable, QThreadPool
from PySide6.QtGui import QImage, QPixmap

try:
    import cv2
    HAS_OPENCV = True
except ImportError:
    HAS_OPENCV = False

MEDIA_EXTENSIONS = {
    "project": {".prproj", ".drp", ".aep", ".fcpxml", ".edl", ".xml"},
    "video": {".mp4", ".mov", ".mkv", ".avi", ".mxf", ".braw", ".r3d", ".crm", ".prores"},
    "audio": {".wav", ".mp3", ".aac", ".m4a", ".flac", ".ogg"},
    "image": {".jpg", ".jpeg", ".png", ".webp", ".tiff", ".tga", ".dng", ".arw", ".cr2"},
    "document": {".pdf", ".docx", ".xlsx", ".txt"}
}

THUMBNAIL_CACHE: Dict[str, QPixmap] = {}
METADATA_CACHE: Dict[str, dict] = {}


def format_file_size(size_bytes: int) -> str:
    if size_bytes < 1024:
        return f"{size_bytes} B"
    elif size_bytes < 1024 * 1024:
        return f"{size_bytes / 1024:.1f} KB"
    elif size_bytes < 1024 * 1024 * 1024:
        return f"{size_bytes / (1024 * 1024):.1f} MB"
    else:
        return f"{size_bytes / (1024 * 1024 * 1024):.2f} GB"


def format_duration(seconds: float) -> str:
    if not seconds or seconds <= 0:
        return "--:--"
    total_sec = int(seconds)
    hours = total_sec // 3600
    mins = (total_sec % 3600) // 60
    secs = total_sec % 60
    if hours > 0:
        return f"{hours:02d}:{mins:02d}:{secs:02d}"
    return f"{mins:02d}:{secs:02d}"


def scan_directory_fast(dir_path: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    path = Path(dir_path)
    if not path.exists() or not path.is_dir():
        return {
            "exists": False,
            "path": dir_path,
            "folders": [],
            "files": [],
            "total_items": 0,
            "total_size_str": "۰ B"
        }

    folders = []
    files = []
    total_size = 0

    try:
        with os.scandir(path) as entries:
            for entry in entries:
                try:
                    if entry.is_dir(follow_symlinks=False):
                        try:
                            sub_count = len(os.listdir(entry.path))
                        except Exception:
                            sub_count = 0

                        folders.append({
                            "name": entry.name,
                            "path": str(Path(entry.path).resolve()),
                            "sub_items_count": sub_count,
                            "is_dir": True
                        })
                    elif entry.is_file(follow_symlinks=False):
                        size = entry.stat().st_size
                        total_size += size
                        ext = Path(entry.name).suffix.lower()

                        cat = "other"
                        for c_name, ext_set in MEDIA_EXTENSIONS.items():
                            if ext in ext_set:
                                cat = c_name
                                break

                        f_path = str(Path(entry.path).resolve())
                        cached_meta = METADATA_CACHE.get(f_path, {})

                        files.append({
                            "name": entry.name,
                            "path": f_path,
                            "ext": ext.replace(".", "").upper(),
                            "size": size,
                            "size_str": format_file_size(size),
                            "category": cat,
                            "resolution": cached_meta.get("resolution", "—"),
                            "duration_str": cached_meta.get("duration_str", "--:--"),
                            "fps": cached_meta.get("fps", "—"),
                            "thumbnail_pixmap": THUMBNAIL_CACHE.get(f_path),
                            "is_dir": False
                        })
                except Exception:
                    continue
    except Exception:
        pass

    folders.sort(key=lambda x: x["name"].lower())
    sort_order = {"video": 0, "project": 1, "audio": 2, "image": 3, "document": 4, "other": 5}
    files.sort(key=lambda x: (sort_order.get(x["category"], 6), x["name"].lower()))

    return {
        "exists": True,
        "path": str(path.resolve()),
        "parent_path": str(path.parent.resolve()) if path.parent != path else None,
        "folders": folders,
        "files": files,
        "total_items": len(folders) + len(files),
        "total_size_str": format_file_size(total_size)
    }


class ThumbnailWorkerSignals(QObject):
    loaded = Signal(str, dict, object)


class ThumbnailWorker(QRunnable):
    def __init__(self, file_path: str, category: str, generation_id: int):
        super().__init__()
        self.file_path = file_path
        self.category = category
        self.generation_id = generation_id
        self.is_cancelled = False
        self.signals = ThumbnailWorkerSignals()
        self.setAutoDelete(True)

    def cancel(self):
        self.is_cancelled = True

    def run(self):
        if self.is_cancelled:
            return

        meta = {
            "resolution": "—",
            "duration_str": "--:--",
            "fps": "—"
        }
        pixmap = None
        path_obj = Path(self.file_path)

        if not path_obj.exists():
            return

        if self.file_path in THUMBNAIL_CACHE:
            cached_pix = THUMBNAIL_CACHE[self.file_path]
            cached_meta = METADATA_CACHE.get(self.file_path, meta)
            self.signals.loaded.emit(self.file_path, cached_meta, cached_pix)
            return

        if self.category == "image":
            try:
                img = QImage(str(path_obj.resolve()))
                if not img.isNull() and not self.is_cancelled:
                    meta["resolution"] = f"{img.width()} × {img.height()}"
                    pixmap = QPixmap.fromImage(img).scaled(
                        180, 95, 
                        Qt.AspectRatioMode.KeepAspectRatioByExpanding, 
                        Qt.TransformationMode.FastTransformation
                    )
            except Exception:
                pass

        elif self.category == "video" and HAS_OPENCV:
            cap = None
            try:
                cap = cv2.VideoCapture(str(path_obj.resolve()))
                if cap.isOpened() and not self.is_cancelled:
                    w = int(cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_WIDTH))
                    h = int(cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_HEIGHT))
                    fps = cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FPS)
                    frame_count = cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_COUNT)

                    if w >= 3840:
                        meta["resolution"] = f"4K ({w}×{h})"
                    elif w >= 1920:
                        meta["resolution"] = f"FHD ({w}×{h})"
                    elif w > 0:
                        meta["resolution"] = f"{w}×{h}"

                    if fps and fps > 0:
                        meta["fps"] = f"{fps:.2f} fps".rstrip('0').rstrip('.')
                        if frame_count and frame_count > 0:
                            meta["duration_str"] = format_duration(frame_count / fps)

                    ret, frame = cap.read()
                    if ret and frame is not None and not self.is_cancelled:
                        small_frame = cv2.resize(frame, (160, 90), interpolation=cv2.INTER_NEAREST)
                        frame_rgb = cv2.cvtColor(small_frame, cv2.COLOR_BGR2RGB)
                        fh, fw, ch = frame_rgb.shape
                        qimg = QImage(frame_rgb.data, fw, fh, ch * fw, QImage.Format.Format_RGB888).copy()
                        pixmap = QPixmap.fromImage(qimg)
            except Exception:
                pass
            finally:
                if cap is not None:
                    try:
                        cap.release()
                    except Exception:
                        pass

        if not self.is_cancelled:
            if pixmap:
                THUMBNAIL_CACHE[self.file_path] = pixmap
            METADATA_CACHE[self.file_path] = meta
            self.signals.loaded.emit(self.file_path, meta, pixmap)

import os
import sys
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Tuple, Dict, Any

from PySide6.QtGui import QGuiApplication, QDesktopServices
from PySide6.QtCore import QUrl

EXTENSION_COLOR_PALETTE = {
    "PRPROJ": {"label": "PR", "color": "#9999FF", "name": "Adobe Premiere Pro"},
    "DRP": {"label": "DRP", "color": "#FF4A4A", "name": "DaVinci Resolve"},
    "AEP": {"label": "AE", "color": "#D291FF", "name": "Adobe After Effects"},
    "FCPXML": {"label": "FCP", "color": "#38BDF8", "name": "Final Cut Pro XML"},
    "XML": {"label": "XML", "color": "#38BDF8", "name": "Timeline XML"},

    "PSD": {"label": "PSD", "color": "#31A8FF", "name": "Adobe Photoshop"},
    "AI": {"label": "AI", "color": "#FF9A00", "name": "Adobe Illustrator"},
    "C4D": {"label": "C4D", "color": "#0070FF", "name": "Cinema 4D"},
    "SESX": {"label": "AU", "color": "#00E5FF", "name": "Adobe Audition"},
    "CPR": {"label": "CB", "color": "#EF4444", "name": "Cubase Project"},
    "FLM": {"label": "FL", "color": "#FB923C", "name": "FL Studio"},

    "MP4": {"label": "MP4", "color": "#38BDF8", "name": "MPEG-4 Video"},
    "MXF": {"label": "MXF", "color": "#818CF8", "name": "Material eXchange Format"}
}


class NleLauncherHelper:
    @staticmethod
    def get_file_badge_info(file_path: str) -> Dict[str, str]:
        if not file_path:
            return {
                "ext": "FILE",
                "label": "FILE",
                "color": "#64748B",
                "name": "فایل تدوین متصل نشده",
                "has_file": False
            }
        
        p = Path(file_path)
        ext_clean = p.suffix.replace(".", "").upper() if p.suffix else "FILE"
        has_file = p.exists() and p.is_file()

        if ext_clean in EXTENSION_COLOR_PALETTE:
            info = EXTENSION_COLOR_PALETTE[ext_clean].copy()
            info["ext"] = ext_clean
            info["has_file"] = has_file
            if not has_file:
                info["color"] = "#64748B"
            return info

        short_label = ext_clean[:4] if len(ext_clean) > 4 else ext_clean
        return {
            "ext": ext_clean,
            "label": short_label,
            "color": "#818CF8" if has_file else "#64748B",
            "name": f"فایل {ext_clean}",
            "has_file": has_file
        }

    @staticmethod
    def launch_project(file_path: str) -> Tuple[bool, str]:
        if not file_path:
            return False, "مسیر فایل پروژه مشخص نشده است."
        
        p = Path(file_path)
        if not p.exists() or not p.is_file():
            return False, f"فایل پروژه در مسیر مشخص‌شده یافت نشد:\n{file_path}"
        
        # استفاده امن از متد سیستم‌عامل
        if open_path_in_os(str(p.resolve())):
            badge_info = NleLauncherHelper.get_file_badge_info(file_path)
            return True, f"فایل پروژه ({badge_info['label']}) با موفقیت در نرم‌افزار مربوطه باز شد."
        else:
            return False, "خطا در اجرای فایل پروژه."


class ClientMessengerHelper:
    @staticmethod
    def generate_client_message(project_dict: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
        settings = SettingsManager.load_settings()
        template = settings.get("client_message_template", "")
        
        couple = project_dict.get("couple_names") or project_dict.get("title", "عروس و داماد")
        studio = project_dict.get("client_name", "استودیو")
        editor = settings.get("editor_name", "تدوین‌گر")
        
        event_dt = project_dict.get("event_date")
        event_str = gregorian_to_jalali(event_dt) if event_dt else "-"
        
        status_info = project_dict.get("status_info", {})
        status_label = status_info.get("label", "در حال انجام")
        progress = project_dict.get("progress_pct", 0)

        message = template.replace("{couple_names}", str(couple))\
                          .replace("{studio_name}", str(studio))\
                          .replace("{editor_name}", str(editor))\
                          .replace("{event_date}", to_persian_numbers(str(event_str)))\
                          .replace("{workflow_status}", str(status_label))\
                          .replace("{progress_pct}", to_persian_numbers(str(progress)))
        return message.strip()

    @staticmethod
    def copy_message_to_clipboard(text_content: str) -> bool:
        clipboard = QGuiApplication.clipboard()
        if clipboard:
            clipboard.setText(text_content)
            return True
        return False

    @staticmethod
    def launch_messenger_app(app_type: str) -> Tuple[bool, str]:
        try:
            uri = "tg://" if app_type == "telegram" else "whatsapp://" if app_type == "whatsapp" else None
            if not uri:
                return False, "نوع پیام‌رسان نامعتبر است."

            url = QUrl(uri)
            # اولویت اول با هندلر دسکتاپ کیوت
            if QDesktopServices.openUrl(url):
                return True, "متن پیام در کلیپ‌بورد کپی شد و پیام‌رسان باز شد."

            if sys.platform == "win32":
                os.startfile(uri)
                return True, "متن پیام در کلیپ‌بورد کپی شد و پیام‌رسان باز شد."
            elif sys.platform == "darwin":
                subprocess.Popen(["open", uri], stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL)
                return True, "متن پیام در کلیپ‌بورد کپی شد و پیام‌رسان در macOS باز شد."
            else:
                subprocess.Popen(["xdg-open", uri], stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL)
                return True, "متن پیام در کلیپ‌بورد کپی شد و پیام‌رسان باز شد."

        except Exception as e:
            return False, f"خطا در باز کردن پیام‌رسان: {e}"
