# FILE: core\pdf_invoice_generator.py
# ==================================================
# core/pdf_invoice_generator.py
from typing import Dict, Any
from core.persian_utils import format_toman, to_persian_numbers
from core.settings_manager import SettingsManager


def generate_persian_pdf_invoice(invoice_data: Dict[str, Any], output_path: str) -> bool:
    """تولید فاکتور رسمی یا ذخیره فایل مشخصات قرارداد"""
    try:
        settings = SettingsManager.load_settings()
        studio_name = settings.get("studio_name", "استودیوی تدوین دیاکو")
        phone = settings.get("phone", "09120000000")
        card_number = settings.get("card_number", "6037-9918-0000-0000")
        footer_note = settings.get("invoice_footer_note", "کلیه حقوق مادی و معنوی راش‌ها تا زمان تسویه کامل متعلق به استودیو می‌باشد.")

        # ساخت فایل متنی/HTML ساختاریافته قابل پرینت مستقیم
        html_content = f"""
        <!DOCTYPE html>
        <html dir="rtl" lang="fa">
        <head>
            <meta charset="utf-8">
            <title>فاکتور رسمی خدمات تدوین</title>
            <style>
                body {{ font-family: Tahoma, sans-serif; margin: 30px; color: #222; background: #FFF; }}
                .invoice-box {{ border: 1px solid #CCC; padding: 24px; border-radius: 8px; max-width: 800px; margin: auto; }}
                .header {{ border-bottom: 2px solid #6366F1; padding-bottom: 12px; margin-bottom: 20px; }}
                .title {{ font-size: 20px; font-weight: bold; color: #312E81; }}
                .meta {{ font-size: 12px; color: #666; margin-top: 5px; }}
                table {{ width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-top: 20px; }}
                th, td {{ border: 1px solid #DDD; padding: 10px; text-align: center; font-size: 13px; }}
                th {{ background-color: #F3F4F6; font-weight: bold; }}
                .total {{ font-size: 15px; font-weight: bold; color: #10B981; margin-top: 20px; text-align: left; }}
                .footer {{ margin-top: 30px; border-top: 1px solid #EEE; padding-top: 10px; font-size: 11px; color: #777; }}
            </style>
        </head>
        <body>
            <div class="invoice-box">
                <div class="header">
                    <div class="title">{studio_name}</div>
                    <div class="meta">شماره فاکتور: {to_persian_numbers(invoice_data.get('invoice_number', 'INV-001'))} | تاریخ صدور: {to_persian_numbers(invoice_data.get('date', ''))}</div>
                </div>

                <p><b>طرف قرارداد / استودیو:</b> {invoice_data.get('client_name', '-')}</p>
                <p><b>عنوان پروژه:</b> {invoice_data.get('project_title', '-')}</p>

                <table>
                    <thead>
                        <tr>
                            <th>شرح خدمات</th>
                            <th>مبلغ قابل پرداخت (تومان)</th>
                        </tr>
                    </thead>
                    <tbody>
                        <tr>
                            <td>تدوین و پس‌تولید کامل پکیج مراسم (شامل راف‌کات، تصحیح رنگ، صداگذاری و رندر 4K)</td>
                            <td>{format_toman(invoice_data.get('amount', 0))}</td>
                        </tr>
                    </tbody>
                </table>

                <div class="total">
                    مبلغ کل: {format_toman(invoice_data.get('amount', 0))}
                </div>

                <div class="footer">
                    <p><b>اطلاعات پرداخت:</b> شماره کارت: {to_persian_numbers(card_number)} | شماره تماس: {to_persian_numbers(phone)}</p>
                    <p>{footer_note}</p>
                </div>
            </div>
        </body>
        </html>
        """

        # اگر مسیر خروجی .pdf بود و reportlab یا weasyprint نبود، به صورت .html ذخیره و پرینت‌پذیر می‌شود
        export_file = output_path if output_path.endswith(".html") else output_path.replace(".pdf", ".html")
        with open(export_file, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write(html_content)

        return True

    except Exception as e:
        print(f"PDF Invoice Error: {e}")
        return False

from datetime import datetime, date
from typing import Optional
import jdatetime


def to_persian_numbers(input_val) -> str:
    """تبدیل تمام ارقام انگلیسی به فارسی"""
    if input_val is None:
        return ""
    text = str(input_val)
    en_to_fa = str.maketrans("0123456789", "۰۱۲۳۴۵۶۷۸۹")
    return text.translate(en_to_fa)


def normalize_to_english_digits(input_val) -> str:
    """تبدیل تمام ارقام فارسی و عربی به انگلیسی جهت اعتبارسنجی و تبدیل عددی"""
    if input_val is None:
        return ""
    text = str(input_val).strip()
    fa_to_en = str.maketrans("۰۱۲۳۴۵۶۷۸۹٠١٢٣٤٥٦٧٨٩", "01234567890123456789")
    return text.translate(fa_to_en)


def format_toman(amount: int) -> str:
    """فرمت‌دهی ۳ رقم ۳ رقم مبالغ با پسوند تومان و ارقام فارسی"""
    if not amount:
        return "۰ تومان"
    formatted = f"{int(amount):,}"
    return f"{to_persian_numbers(formatted)} تومان"


def gregorian_to_jalali(dt: Optional[datetime]) -> str:
    """تبدیل تاریخ میلادی به تاریخ شمسی خوانا"""
    if not dt:
        return "-"
    try:
        j_dt = jdatetime.datetime.fromgregorian(datetime=dt)
        return to_persian_numbers(j_dt.strftime("%Y/%m/%d"))
    except Exception:
        return "-"


def number_to_persian_words(number: int) -> str:
    """تبدیل مبالغ عددی به حروف فارسی با پسوند تومان"""
    if not number or number <= 0:
        return ""

    ones = ["", "یک", "دو", "سه", "چهار", "پنج", "شش", "هفت", "هشت", "نه"]
    teens = ["ده", "یازده", "دوازده", "سیزده", "چهارده", "پانزده", "شانزده", "هفده", "هجده", "نوزده"]
    tens = ["", "", "بیست", "سی", "چهل", "پنجاه", "شصت", "هفتاد", "هشتاد", "نود"]
    hundreds = ["", "صد", "دویست", "سیصد", "چهارصد", "پانصد", "ششصد", "هفتصد", "هشتصد", "نهصد"]
    thousands = ["", "هزار", "میلیون", "میلیارد", "تریلیون"]

    def _convert_group(n: int) -> str:
        parts = []
        c = n // 100
        r = n % 100
        if c > 0:
            parts.append(hundreds[c])
        if 10 <= r <= 19:
            parts.append(teens[r - 10])
        else:
            t = r // 10
            o = r % 10
            if t > 0:
                parts.append(tens[t])
            if o > 0:
                parts.append(ones[o])
        return " و ".join(parts)

    num_str = str(int(number))
    groups = []
    while len(num_str) > 0:
        groups.append(int(num_str[-3:]))
        num_str = num_str[:-3]

    words = []
    for i, g in enumerate(groups):
        if g > 0:
            converted = _convert_group(g)
            scale = thousands[i]
            if scale:
                words.append(f"{converted} {scale}")
            else:
                words.append(converted)

    words.reverse()
    result = " و ".join(words)
    return f"{result} تومان" if result else ""


def parse_jalali_text(text_val: str) -> Optional[jdatetime.date]:
    """تبدیل رشته تاریخ شمسی (مانند ۱۴۰۵/۰۵/۲۵) به شیء jdatetime.date"""
    if not text_val:
        return None
    clean_text = normalize_to_english_digits(text_val).replace("-", "/").strip()
    try:
        parts = [int(p) for p in clean_text.split("/") if p.strip()]
        if len(parts) == 3:
            return jdatetime.date(parts[0], parts[1], parts[2])
    except Exception:
        return None
    return None


def get_persian_date_readable(j_date: Optional[jdatetime.date]) -> str:
    """تبدیل تاریخ شمسی به متن روز هفته و ماه (مثلاً: شنبه، ۲۵ مرداد ۱۴۰۵)"""
    if not j_date:
        return ""
    weekdays = {
        0: "شنبه", 1: "یکشنبه", 2: "دوشنبه", 3: "سه‌شنبه",
        4: "چهارشنبه", 5: "پنج‌شنبه", 6: "جمعه"
    }
    month_names = {
        1: "فروردین", 2: "اردیبهشت", 3: "خرداد", 4: "تیر", 5: "مرداد", 6: "شهریور",
        7: "مهر", 8: "آبان", 9: "آذر", 10: "دی", 11: "بهمن", 12: "اسفند"
    }
    weekday_name = weekdays.get(j_date.weekday(), "")
    month_name = month_names.get(j_date.month, "")
    day_str = to_persian_numbers(j_date.day)
    year_str = to_persian_numbers(j_date.year)
    return f"{weekday_name}، {day_str} {month_name} {year_str}"


def get_deadline_relative_text(deadline_j_date: Optional[jdatetime.date]) -> str:
    """محاسبه دقیق فاصله زمانی تا ددلاین بر مبنای تاریخ امروز سیستم"""
    if not deadline_j_date:
        return ""
    today_j = jdatetime.date.today()
    delta_days = (deadline_j_date - today_j).days

    if delta_days == 0:
        return "امروز مهلت تحویل است ⏳"
    elif delta_days > 0:
        return f"{to_persian_numbers(delta_days)} روز مانده تا تحویل ⏳"
    else:
        passed = abs(delta_days)
        return f"{to_persian_numbers(passed)} روز از مهلت تحویل گذشته ⚠️"

from typing import List, Optional, Dict, Any, Tuple
from datetime import datetime
from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import joinedload, selectinload
from database.db_manager import db
from database.models import (
    Project, Client, DriveLocation, ProjectClipItem, 
    ProjectRevision, ProjectRevisionTask, Transaction, ProjectEventLog,
    StageEnum, WorkflowStatusEnum, RevisionStatusEnum
)
from core.settings_manager import SettingsManager
from core.persian_utils import to_persian_numbers
from core.signals import global_signals
from widgets.status_badge import StatusType


def get_persian_round_title(number: int) -> str:
    ordinals = {
        1: "اول", 2: "دوم", 3: "سوم", 4: "چهارم", 5: "پنجم",
        6: "ششم", 7: "هفتم", 8: "هشتم", 9: "نهم", 10: "دهم"
    }
    suffix = ordinals.get(number, f"{number}م")
    return f"اصلاحیه دور {suffix}"


class ProjectController:
    @staticmethod
    def log_event(session, project_id: int, event_type: str, title: str, description: str = ""):
        try:
            log = ProjectEventLog(
                project_id=project_id,
                event_type=event_type,
                title=title,
                description=description,
                created_at=datetime.utcnow()
            )
            session.add(log)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Log Event Error: {e}")

    @staticmethod
    def get_project_financial_summary(project: Project) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        base_fee = int(project.base_fee or 0)
        revisions_fee = sum(int(r.extra_fee or 0) for r in (project.revisions or []))
        
        if base_fee == 0 and int(project.total_fee or 0) > 0:
            base_fee = int(project.total_fee) - revisions_fee
            base_fee = max(0, base_fee)

        total_fee = int(base_fee + revisions_fee)
        total_paid = sum(int(t.amount or 0) for t in (project.transactions or []))
        remaining = int(total_fee - total_paid)

        if total_fee > 0:
            if remaining > 0:
                is_settled = False
                status_color = "#F59E0B"
                status_label = f"مانده بدهی: {remaining:,} تومان"
            elif remaining == 0:
                is_settled = True
                status_color = "#10B981"
                status_label = "تسویه کامل (۰ تومان) ✓"
            else:
                is_settled = True
                status_color = "#38BDF8"
                status_label = f"اضافه پرداخت: {abs(remaining):,} تومان"
        else:
            is_settled = False
            status_color = "#94A3B8"
            status_label = "بدون مبلغ قرارداد"

        return {
            "base_fee": base_fee,
            "revisions_fee": revisions_fee,
            "total_fee": total_fee,
            "total_paid": total_paid,
            "remaining": max(0, remaining),
            "raw_balance": remaining,
            "is_settled": is_settled,
            "status_label": status_label,
            "status_color": status_color
        }

    @staticmethod
    def get_status_info(project: Project) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        now = datetime.utcnow()

        if project.is_archived:
            return {
                "key": WorkflowStatusEnum.ARCHIVED.value,
                "label": "آرشیو شده",
                "type": StatusType.NEUTRAL,
                "symbol": "✓"
            }

        active_rev = ProjectController.get_active_revision(project)
        if active_rev:
            c, t, pct = ProjectController.calculate_revision_progress(active_rev)
            if pct == 100 and t > 0:
                return {
                    "key": WorkflowStatusEnum.REVISION_DONE.value,
                    "label": "اصلاحیه تکمیل شد",
                    "type": StatusType.PENDING,
                    "symbol": "✓"
                }
            return {
                "key": WorkflowStatusEnum.REVISION_IN_PROGRESS.value,
                "label": "در حال اصلاح",
                "type": StatusType.WARNING,
                "symbol": "●"
            }

        c_clips, t_clips, pct_clips = ProjectController.calculate_project_progress(project)
        if t_clips > 0 and c_clips == t_clips:
            if project.workflow_status == WorkflowStatusEnum.UNDER_REVIEW or project.current_stage == StageEnum.DELIVERED:
                settings = SettingsManager.load_settings()
                limit_days = settings.get("auto_archive_delivered_days", 7)
                
                label_text = "تحویل شد / منتظر بازبینی"
                status_symbol = "⏳"
                if limit_days > 0 and project.delivered_at:
                    elapsed_days = (now - project.delivered_at).days
                    days_left = max(0, limit_days - elapsed_days)
                    if days_left > 0:
                        label_text = f"تحویل شد (آرشیو تا {to_persian_numbers(days_left)} روز دیگر ⏳)"
                    else:
                        label_text = "مهلت بازبینی پایان یافت (آماده آرشیو)"
                        status_symbol = "📁"

                return {
                    "key": WorkflowStatusEnum.UNDER_REVIEW.value,
                    "label": label_text,
                    "type": StatusType.PENDING,
                    "symbol": status_symbol
                }
            return {
                "key": WorkflowStatusEnum.READY_FOR_DELIVERY.value,
                "label": "آماده تحویل به آتلیه",
                "type": StatusType.ACTIVE,
                "symbol": "●"
            }

        if project.deadline:
            delta_days = (project.deadline.date() - now.date()).days
            if delta_days < 0:
                return {
                    "key": "overdue",
                    "label": "تأخیر در تحویل",
                    "type": StatusType.DANGER,
                    "symbol": "!"
                }
            elif delta_days <= 2:
                return {
                    "key": "near_deadline",
                    "label": "نزدیک به تحویل",
                    "type": StatusType.WARNING,
                    "symbol": "!"
                }

        stage_val = project.current_stage.value if hasattr(project.current_stage, "value") else str(project.current_stage)
        if stage_val == StageEnum.INGEST.value:
            return {"key": "ingest", "label": "آماده‌سازی راش‌ها", "type": StatusType.NEUTRAL, "symbol": "●"}
        elif stage_val == StageEnum.COLOR_AUDIO.value:
            return {"key": "color_audio", "label": "اصلاح رنگ و صدا", "type": StatusType.ACTIVE, "symbol": "●"}
        elif stage_val == StageEnum.FINAL_RENDER.value:
            return {"key": "final_render", "label": "رندر نهایی", "type": StatusType.ACTIVE, "symbol": "●"}

        return {
            "key": WorkflowStatusEnum.EDITING.value,
            "label": "در حال تدوین",
            "type": StatusType.ACTIVE,
            "symbol": "●"
        }

    @staticmethod
    def mark_as_delivered(project_id: int) -> bool:
        with db.get_session() as session:
            project = session.get(Project, project_id)
            if not project:
                return False
            
            project.workflow_status = WorkflowStatusEnum.UNDER_REVIEW
            project.current_stage = StageEnum.DELIVERED
            project.delivered_at = datetime.utcnow()
            
            ProjectController.log_event(
                session, project_id, "DELIVERED_TO_CLIENT", 
                "تحویل فایل به آتلیه", 
                "فایل‌های تدوین‌شده جهت بررسی برای آتلیه ارسال شد. تایمر بازبینی و آرشیو خودکار آغاز گردید."
            )
            session.commit()

        global_signals.stage_changed.emit(project_id, StageEnum.DELIVERED.value)
        global_signals.project_updated.emit(project_id)
        return True

    @staticmethod
    def archive_project(project_id: int) -> bool:
        with db.get_session() as session:
            project = session.get(Project, project_id)
            if not project:
                return False

            project.is_archived = True
            project.archived_at = datetime.utcnow()
            project.current_stage = StageEnum.DELIVERED
            project.workflow_status = WorkflowStatusEnum.ARCHIVED

            for rev in project.revisions:
                rev.status = RevisionStatusEnum.ARCHIVED

            ProjectController.log_event(
                session, project_id, "ARCHIVED", 
                "انتقال رسمی به آرشیو", 
                "پرونده پروژه با تأیید تدوین‌گر به بخش آرشیو منتقل شد."
            )
            session.commit()

        global_signals.project_archived.emit(project_id)
        global_signals.project_updated.emit(project_id)
        return True

    @staticmethod
    def unarchive_project(project_id: int) -> bool:
        with db.get_session() as session:
            project = session.get(Project, project_id)
            if not project:
                return False

            project.is_archived = False
            project.archived_at = None
            project.workflow_status = WorkflowStatusEnum.EDITING

            ProjectController.log_event(
                session, project_id, "UNARCHIVED", 
                "خروج از آرشیو", 
                "پروژه به لیست پروژه‌های فعال بازگردانده شد."
            )
            session.commit()

        global_signals.project_unarchived.emit(project_id)
        global_signals.project_updated.emit(project_id)
        return True

    @staticmethod
    def check_and_process_auto_archive_delivered():
        settings = SettingsManager.load_settings()
        limit_days = settings.get("auto_archive_delivered_days", 7)
        if limit_days <= 0:
            return

        archived_ids = []
        with db.get_session() as session:
            stmt = (
                select(Project)
                .options(selectinload(Project.revisions))
                .where(
                    Project.is_archived == False,
                    Project.workflow_status == WorkflowStatusEnum.UNDER_REVIEW,
                    Project.delivered_at != None
                )
            )
            projects = session.scalars(stmt).unique().all()
            now = datetime.utcnow()

            for p in projects:
                active_rev = ProjectController.get_active_revision(p)
                if not active_rev and p.delivered_at:
                    if (now - p.delivered_at).days >= limit_days:
                        p.is_archived = True
                        p.archived_at = now
                        p.workflow_status = WorkflowStatusEnum.ARCHIVED
                        for rev in p.revisions:
                            rev.status = RevisionStatusEnum.ARCHIVED
                        
                        ProjectController.log_event(
                            session, p.id, "AUTO_ARCHIVED_SILENT", 
                            f"آرشیو خودکار پس از {limit_days} روز تحویل", 
                            "به دلیل عدم ارسال اصلاحیه توسط آتلیه در مهلت مقرر، پروژه به آرشیو منتقل شد."
                        )
                        archived_ids.append(p.id)

            if archived_ids:
                session.commit()

        for pid in archived_ids:
            global_signals.project_archived.emit(pid)
            global_signals.project_updated.emit(pid)

    @staticmethod
    def get_active_projects() -> List[Project]:
        ProjectController.check_and_process_auto_archive_delivered()
        with db.get_session() as session:
            stmt = (
                select(Project)
                .options(
                    joinedload(Project.client),
                    joinedload(Project.drive_locations),
                    selectinload(Project.clips),
                    selectinload(Project.revisions).selectinload(ProjectRevision.tasks),
                    joinedload(Project.transactions).joinedload(Transaction.revision)
                )
                .where(Project.is_archived == False)
                .order_by(Project.created_at.desc())
            )
            return session.scalars(stmt).unique().all()

    @staticmethod
    def get_archived_projects() -> List[Project]:
        with db.get_session() as session:
            stmt = (
                select(Project)
                .options(
                    joinedload(Project.client),
                    joinedload(Project.drive_locations),
                    selectinload(Project.clips),
                    selectinload(Project.revisions).selectinload(ProjectRevision.tasks),
                    joinedload(Project.transactions).joinedload(Transaction.revision)
                )
                .where(Project.is_archived == True)
                .order_by(Project.archived_at.desc())
            )
            return session.scalars(stmt).unique().all()

    @staticmethod
    def get_all_projects() -> List[Project]:
        with db.get_session() as session:
            stmt = (
                select(Project)
                .options(
                    joinedload(Project.client),
                    joinedload(Project.drive_locations),
                    selectinload(Project.clips),
                    selectinload(Project.revisions).selectinload(ProjectRevision.tasks),
                    joinedload(Project.transactions).joinedload(Transaction.revision)
                )
                .order_by(Project.created_at.desc())
            )
            return session.scalars(stmt).unique().all()

    @staticmethod
    def get_project_by_id(project_id: int) -> Optional[Project]:
        with db.get_session() as session:
            stmt = (
                select(Project)
                .options(
                    joinedload(Project.client),
                    joinedload(Project.drive_locations),
                    selectinload(Project.clips),
                    selectinload(Project.revisions).selectinload(ProjectRevision.tasks),
                    joinedload(Project.transactions).joinedload(Transaction.revision),
                    selectinload(Project.event_logs)
                )
                .where(Project.id == project_id)
            )
            return session.scalars(stmt).unique().first()

    @staticmethod
    def calculate_project_progress(project: Project) -> Tuple[int, int, int]:
        if not project or not project.clips:
            return 0, 0, 0
        total = len(project.clips)
        completed = sum(1 for c in project.clips if c.is_completed)
        percentage = int((completed / total) * 100) if total > 0 else 0
        return completed, total, percentage

    @staticmethod
    def calculate_revision_progress(revision: Optional[ProjectRevision]) -> Tuple[int, int, int]:
        if not revision or not revision.tasks:
            return 0, 0, 0
        total = len(revision.tasks)
        completed = sum(1 for t in revision.tasks if t.is_completed)
        percentage = int((completed / total) * 100) if total > 0 else 0
        return completed, total, percentage

    @staticmethod
    def get_active_revision(project: Project) -> Optional[ProjectRevision]:
        if not project or not project.revisions:
            return None
        last_rev = project.revisions[-1]
        if last_rev.status != RevisionStatusEnum.ARCHIVED and last_rev.status != RevisionStatusEnum.COMPLETED:
            return last_rev
        return None

    @staticmethod
    def toggle_clip_status(clip_id: int) -> Optional[bool]:
        project_id = None
        new_status = None
        with db.get_session() as session:
            clip = session.get(ProjectClipItem, clip_id)
            if not clip:
                return None
            
            clip.is_completed = not clip.is_completed
            clip.completed_at = datetime.utcnow() if clip.is_completed else None
            project_id = clip.project_id
            new_status = clip.is_completed
            session.flush()

            project = session.get(Project, project_id)
            total = len(project.clips)
            completed = sum(1 for c in project.clips if c.is_completed)
            active_rev = ProjectController.get_active_revision(project)

            if total > 0 and completed == total:
                if not active_rev:
                    project.workflow_status = WorkflowStatusEnum.READY_FOR_DELIVERY
                if not project.completed_at:
                    project.completed_at = datetime.utcnow()
                ProjectController.log_event(session, project_id, "CLIPS_100", "تکمیل ۱۰۰٪ کلیپ‌های اولیه", f"کلیپ {clip.clip_name} آماده تحویل شد.")
            else:
                if not active_rev:
                    project.workflow_status = WorkflowStatusEnum.EDITING
                project.completed_at = None
                ProjectController.log_event(session, project_id, "CLIP_TOGGLE", f"تغییر وضعیت کلیپ {clip.clip_name}", "تکمیل شد" if clip.is_completed else "به باقی‌مانده بازگشت")

            session.commit()

        global_signals.clip_status_toggled.emit(project_id, clip_id)
        global_signals.project_updated.emit(project_id)
        return new_status

    @staticmethod
    def toggle_revision_task(task_id: int) -> Optional[bool]:
        project_id = None
        revision_id = None
        new_status = None
        with db.get_session() as session:
            task = session.get(ProjectRevisionTask, task_id)
            if not task:
                return None
            
            task.is_completed = not task.is_completed
            task.completed_at = datetime.utcnow() if task.is_completed else None
            new_status = task.is_completed
            session.flush()

            rev = session.get(ProjectRevision, task.revision_id)
            revision_id = rev.id
            project_id = rev.project_id
            
            total_tasks = len(rev.tasks)
            completed_tasks = sum(1 for t in rev.tasks if t.is_completed)
            
            if total_tasks > 0 and completed_tasks == total_tasks:
                rev.status = RevisionStatusEnum.COMPLETED
                rev.completed_at = datetime.utcnow()
                rev.project.workflow_status = WorkflowStatusEnum.REVISION_DONE
                ProjectController.log_event(session, project_id, "REVISION_DONE", f"تکمیل تسک‌های {rev.title}", "تمام موارد اصلاحیه انجام شد.")
            elif completed_tasks > 0:
                rev.status = RevisionStatusEnum.IN_PROGRESS
                rev.completed_at = None
                rev.project.workflow_status = WorkflowStatusEnum.REVISION_IN_PROGRESS
            else:
                rev.status = RevisionStatusEnum.NEW_REQUEST
                rev.completed_at = None
                rev.project.workflow_status = WorkflowStatusEnum.REVISION_IN_PROGRESS

            session.commit()

        global_signals.revision_task_toggled.emit(project_id, revision_id, task_id)
        global_signals.revision_updated.emit(project_id, revision_id)
        global_signals.project_updated.emit(project_id)
        return new_status

    @staticmethod
    def create_revision_round(project_id: int, description: str, tasks: List[str], extra_fee: int = 0) -> bool:
        with db.get_session() as session:
            project = session.get(Project, project_id)
            if not project:
                return False

            active_rev = ProjectController.get_active_revision(project)
            if active_rev:
                return False

            rev_num = len(project.revisions) + 1
            round_title = get_persian_round_title(rev_num)

            rev = ProjectRevision(
                project_id=project.id,
                revision_number=rev_num,
                title=round_title,
                description=description.strip(),
                extra_fee=extra_fee,
                status=RevisionStatusEnum.NEW_REQUEST,
                requested_at=datetime.utcnow()
            )
            session.add(rev)
            session.flush()

            for task_str in tasks:
                t_text = task_str.strip()
                if t_text:
                    session.add(ProjectRevisionTask(
                        revision_id=rev.id,
                        task_text=t_text,
                        is_completed=False
                    ))

            revisions_sum = sum(r.extra_fee for r in project.revisions)
            if (project.base_fee or 0) == 0 and project.total_fee > 0:
                project.base_fee = project.total_fee
            project.total_fee = (project.base_fee or 0) + revisions_sum

            project.is_archived = False
            project.archived_at = None
            project.delivered_at = None
            project.current_stage = StageEnum.CLIENT_REVIEW
            project.workflow_status = WorkflowStatusEnum.REVISION_IN_PROGRESS

            ProjectController.log_event(session, project_id, "REVISION_CREATED", f"ثبت {round_title}", f"{len(tasks)} تغییر درخواستی استودیو ثبت شد.")
            session.commit()

        global_signals.revision_added.emit(project_id)
        global_signals.project_unarchived.emit(project_id)
        global_signals.project_updated.emit(project_id)
        return True

    @staticmethod
    def add_task_to_revision(revision_id: int, task_text: str) -> bool:
        if not task_text.strip():
            return False
        project_id = None
        with db.get_session() as session:
            rev = session.get(ProjectRevision, revision_id)
            if not rev:
                return False
            project_id = rev.project_id
            
            session.add(ProjectRevisionTask(
                revision_id=rev.id,
                task_text=task_text.strip(),
                is_completed=False
            ))
            session.flush()

            rev.status = RevisionStatusEnum.IN_PROGRESS
            rev.completed_at = None

            project = rev.project
            project.is_archived = False
            project.archived_at = None
            project.workflow_status = WorkflowStatusEnum.REVISION_IN_PROGRESS
            session.commit()

        global_signals.revision_updated.emit(project_id, revision_id)
        global_signals.project_updated.emit(project_id)
        return True

    @staticmethod
    def delete_revision_task(task_id: int) -> bool:
        project_id = None
        revision_id = None
        with db.get_session() as session:
            task = session.get(ProjectRevisionTask, task_id)
            if not task:
                return False
            rev = task.revision
            revision_id = rev.id
            project_id = rev.project_id

            session.delete(task)
            session.flush()

            total_tasks = len(rev.tasks)
            completed_tasks = sum(1 for t in rev.tasks if t.is_completed)
            if total_tasks > 0 and completed_tasks == total_tasks:
                rev.status = RevisionStatusEnum.COMPLETED
                rev.completed_at = datetime.utcnow()
                rev.project.workflow_status = WorkflowStatusEnum.REVISION_DONE

            session.commit()

        global_signals.revision_updated.emit(project_id, revision_id)
        global_signals.project_updated.emit(project_id)
        return True

    @staticmethod
    def update_project(project_id: int, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
        with db.get_session() as session:
            project = session.get(Project, project_id)
            if not project:
                return False

            project.title = data.get("title", project.title)
            project.couple_names = data.get("couple_names", project.couple_names)
            project.client_id = data.get("client_id", project.client_id)
            
            if "base_fee" in data:
                project.base_fee = data["base_fee"]
                revisions_sum = sum(r.extra_fee for r in project.revisions)
                project.total_fee = project.base_fee + revisions_sum
            elif "total_fee" in data:
                project.base_fee = data["total_fee"]
                revisions_sum = sum(r.extra_fee for r in project.revisions)
                project.total_fee = project.base_fee + revisions_sum

            project.event_date = data.get("event_date", project.event_date)
            project.deadline = data.get("deadline", project.deadline)
            
            if "nle_project_path" in data:
                project.nle_project_path = data["nle_project_path"]

            if "clips" in data:
                existing_clips_map = {c.clip_name: (c.is_completed, c.completed_at) for c in project.clips}
                for c in list(project.clips):
                    session.delete(c)
                session.flush()
                for clip_name in data["clips"]:
                    prev_status, prev_dt = existing_clips_map.get(clip_name, (False, None))
                    session.add(ProjectClipItem(
                        project_id=project.id,
                        clip_name=clip_name,
                        is_completed=prev_status,
                        completed_at=prev_dt
                    ))

            if "primary_path" in data and data["primary_path"]:
                if project.drive_locations:
                    project.drive_locations[0].absolute_path = data["primary_path"]
                else:
                    session.add(DriveLocation(
                        project_id=project.id,
                        drive_label="هارد اصلی پروژه",
                        absolute_path=data["primary_path"],
                        is_primary=True
                    ))

            session.commit()

        global_signals.project_updated.emit(project_id)
        return True

    @staticmethod
    def delete_project(project_id: int) -> bool:
        with db.get_session() as session:
            project = session.get(Project, project_id)
            if not project:
                return False
            session.delete(project)
            session.commit()

        global_signals.project_deleted.emit(project_id)
        return True

# core/report_exporter.py
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path
from typing import List, Dict, Any

import arabic_reshaper
from bidi.algorithm import get_display
import polars as pl
from reportlab.lib import colors
from reportlab.lib.pagesizes import A4
from reportlab.pdfgen import canvas
from reportlab.platypus import SimpleDocTemplate, Table, TableStyle, Paragraph, Spacer
from reportlab.lib.styles import getSampleStyleSheet, ParagraphStyle

from core.persian_utils import format_toman, gregorian_to_jalali, to_persian_numbers


def shape_persian(text: str) -> str:
    """اصلاح جهت و اتصال حروف فارسی برای موتور ReportLab"""
    if not text:
        return ""
    reshaped_text = arabic_reshaper.reshape(str(text))
    return get_display(reshaped_text)


class ReportExporter:
    @staticmethod
    def export_transactions_to_excel(transactions: List[Dict[str, Any]], output_path: str):
        """خروجی رسمی اکسل از تمام تراکنش‌های مالی با استفاده از Polars"""
        if not transactions:
            return

        df = pl.DataFrame(transactions)
        df.write_excel(output_path)

    @staticmethod
    def generate_invoice_pdf(
        project_title: str,
        client_name: str,
        total_fee: int,
        transactions: List[Dict[str, Any]],
        output_path: str
    ):
        """تولید فاکتور رسمی PDF فارسی برای پروژه تدوین"""
        doc = SimpleDocTemplate(
            output_path,
            pagesize=A4,
            rightMargin=36,
            leftMargin=36,
            topMargin=36,
            bottomMargin=36
        )

        elements = []
        styles = getSampleStyleSheet()

        # استایل‌های متن
        title_style = ParagraphStyle(
            name="InvoiceTitle",
            fontSize=18,
            leading=22,
            alignment=1,  # وسط‌چین
            textColor=colors.HexColor("#1A1A1E")
        )
        
        info_style = ParagraphStyle(
            name="InvoiceInfo",
            fontSize=11,
            leading=16,
            alignment=2,  # راست‌چین
            textColor=colors.HexColor("#2E2E36")
        )

        # ۱. سربرگ فاکتور
        elements.append(Paragraph(shape_persian("صورت‌حساب خدمات تدوین و پس‌تولید ویدیو"), title_style))
        elements.append(Spacer(1, 15))

        # ۲. اطلاعات کلی فاکتور
        today_shamsi = gregorian_to_jalali(datetime.utcnow())
        info_data = [
            [
                shape_persian(f"تاریخ صدور: {today_shamsi}"),
                shape_persian(f"نام کارفرما: {client_name}")
            ],
            [
                shape_persian(f"وضعیت فاکتور: رسمی"),
                shape_persian(f"عنوان پروژه: {project_title}")
            ]
        ]
        info_table = Table(info_data, colWidths=[250, 250])
        info_table.setStyle(TableStyle([
            ('ALIGN', (0,0), (-1,-1), 'RIGHT'),
            ('TEXTCOLOR', (0,0), (-1,-1), colors.HexColor("#24242A")),
            ('FONTSIZE', (0,0), (-1,-1), 10),
            ('BOTTOMPADDING', (0,0), (-1,-1), 6),
        ]))
        elements.append(info_table)
        elements.append(Spacer(1, 20))

        # ۳. جدول ریز تراکنش‌ها و پرداخت‌ها
        paid_sum = sum(t.get("amount", 0) for t in transactions)
        remaining_balance = total_fee - paid_sum

        table_data = [
            [
                shape_persian("مبلغ (تومان)"),
                shape_persian("نوع پرداخت"),
                shape_persian("تاریخ پرداخت"),
                shape_persian("ردیف")
            ]
        ]

        for idx, t in enumerate(transactions, 1):
            table_data.append([
                shape_persian(format_toman(t.get("amount", 0), use_persian_digits=False)),
                shape_persian(t.get("payment_type_label", "واریز")),
                shape_persian(gregorian_to_jalali(t.get("paid_at"))),
                shape_persian(str(idx))
            ])

        # ردیف‌های خلاصه مالی
        table_data.append([
            shape_persian(format_toman(total_fee, use_persian_digits=False)),
            shape_persian("مبلغ کل قرارداد:"),
            "", ""
        ])
        table_data.append([
            shape_persian(format_toman(paid_sum, use_persian_digits=False)),
            shape_persian("مجموع پرداختی:"),
            "", ""
        ])
        table_data.append([
            shape_persian(format_toman(remaining_balance, use_persian_digits=False)),
            shape_persian("مانده حساب:"),
            "", ""
        ])

        trx_table = Table(table_data, colWidths=[140, 140, 140, 50])
        trx_table.setStyle(TableStyle([
            ('BACKGROUND', (0, 0), (-1, 0), colors.HexColor("#6366F1")),
            ('TEXTCOLOR', (0, 0), (-1, 0), colors.whitesmoke),
            ('ALIGN', (0, 0), (-1, -1), 'CENTER'),
            ('GRID', (0, 0), (-1, -1), 0.5, colors.HexColor("#D1D5DB")),
            ('ROWBACKGROUNDS', (0, 1), (-1, -4), [colors.HexColor("#F9FAFB"), colors.white]),
            ('BACKGROUND', (0, -3), (-1, -1), colors.HexColor("#F3F4F6")),
            ('TOPPADDING', (0, 0), (-1, -1), 6),
            ('BOTTOMPADDING', (0, 0), (-1, -1), 6),
        ]))
        elements.append(trx_table)

        doc.build(elements)

import json
import shutil
from pathlib import Path

امیدواریم حالتون عالی باشه.
پروژه تدوین مراسم شما در حال حاضر در وضعیت «{workflow_status}» قرار دارد.

🎬 پیشرفت کلیپ‌ها: {progress_pct}٪

    "studio_logo_path": "",

    # ۵. ایمنی و مسیرها
    "default_drive_path": "",
    "music_vault_path": "",
    "copy_audio_to_vault": True
}


class SettingsManager:
    @staticmethod
    def load_settings() -> dict:
        if not SETTINGS_FILE.exists():
            SettingsManager.save_settings(DEFAULT_SETTINGS)
            return DEFAULT_SETTINGS.copy()
        try:
            with open(SETTINGS_FILE, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                data = json.load(f)
                for k, v in DEFAULT_SETTINGS.items():
                    if k not in data:
                        data[k] = v
                return data
        except Exception:
            return DEFAULT_SETTINGS.copy()

    @staticmethod
    def save_settings(settings_dict: dict) -> bool:
        try:
            with open(SETTINGS_FILE, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                json.dump(settings_dict, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=4)
            return True
        except Exception:
            return False

    @staticmethod
    def backup_database(destination_path: str) -> bool:
        try:
            shutil.copy2(DB_PATH, destination_path)
            return True
        except Exception:
            return False

    @staticmethod
    def restore_database(source_path: str) -> bool:
        try:
            shutil.copy2(source_path, DB_PATH)
            return True
        except Exception:
            return False

    @staticmethod
    def reset_settings_to_default() -> bool:
        return SettingsManager.save_settings(DEFAULT_SETTINGS)

    @staticmethod
    def wipe_entire_database() -> bool:
        try:
            Base.metadata.drop_all(bind=db.engine)
            Base.metadata.create_all(bind=db.engine)
            return True
        except Exception:
            return False

from PySide6.QtCore import QObject, Signal


class SignalBus(QObject):
    # --- Pipeline & Project Signals ---
    project_created = Signal(dict)
    project_updated = Signal(int)       
    stage_changed = Signal(int, str)    
    project_deleted = Signal(int)       

    # --- Clip Status Signals ---
    clip_status_toggled = Signal(int, int)  # (project_id, clip_id)

    # --- Archive & Revision Signals ---
    project_archived = Signal(int)      
    project_unarchived = Signal(int)    
    revision_added = Signal(int)        # (project_id)
    revision_updated = Signal(int, int) # (project_id, revision_id)
    revision_task_toggled = Signal(int, int, int)  # (project_id, revision_id, task_id)
    revision_deleted = Signal(int, int) # (project_id, revision_id)

    # --- Financial & Ledger Signals ---
    transaction_recorded = Signal(int)  
    client_balance_updated = Signal(int)

    # --- Time Tracking & Monitoring Signals ---
    session_started = Signal(int)       # (session_id)
    session_paused = Signal(int)        # (session_id)
    session_resumed = Signal(int)       # (session_id)
    session_ended = Signal(int)         # (session_id)
    timer_tick = Signal(dict)           # (state_snapshot)
    system_activity_updated = Signal(dict)  # (metrics_dict)

    # --- Settings Signals ---
    settings_updated = Signal()

    # --- UI / Navigation Signals ---
    navigate_to_page = Signal(int)      
    navigate_and_focus_project = Signal(int) 
    show_toast = Signal(str, str)       


global_signals = SignalBus()

# FILE: database/db_manager.py
# ==================================================
from contextlib import contextmanager
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Generator
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, text
from sqlalchemy.orm import sessionmaker, Session
from database.models import Base
from config import DB_PATH


class DatabaseManager:
    def __init__(self):
        self.db_file = Path(DB_PATH)
        self.db_file.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self.database_url = f"sqlite:///{self.db_file.absolute()}"
        
        self.engine = create_engine(
            self.database_url,
            connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
            echo=False
        )
        self.SessionLocal = sessionmaker(
            autocommit=False,
            autoflush=False,
            bind=self.engine
        )
        self.init_db()

    def init_db(self):
        try:
            import time_tracking.models  # noqa: F401
        except Exception:
            pass

        Base.metadata.create_all(bind=self.engine)
        self._run_auto_migrations()

    def _run_auto_migrations(self):
        with self.engine.begin() as conn:
            # ---------------- جدول projects ----------------
            try:
                result = conn.execute(text("PRAGMA table_info(projects)")).fetchall()
                columns = [row[1] for row in result]
                
                if "project_number" not in columns:
                    conn.execute(text("ALTER TABLE projects ADD COLUMN project_number INTEGER"))
                
                unassigned = conn.execute(
                    text("SELECT id FROM projects WHERE project_number IS NULL OR project_number = 0 ORDER BY created_at ASC, id ASC")
                ).fetchall()

                if unassigned:
                    max_num_res = conn.execute(text("SELECT MAX(project_number) FROM projects")).scalar()
                    current_seq = max_num_res if (max_num_res and max_num_res > 0) else 0

                    for row in unassigned:
                        current_seq += 1
                        conn.execute(
                            text("UPDATE projects SET project_number = :p_num WHERE id = :p_id"),
                            {"p_num": current_seq, "p_id": row[0]}
                        )

                if "base_fee" not in columns:
                    conn.execute(text("ALTER TABLE projects ADD COLUMN base_fee BIGINT DEFAULT 0"))
                if "total_fee" not in columns:
                    conn.execute(text("ALTER TABLE projects ADD COLUMN total_fee BIGINT DEFAULT 0"))
                if "is_archived" not in columns:
                    conn.execute(text("ALTER TABLE projects ADD COLUMN is_archived BOOLEAN DEFAULT 0"))
                if "workflow_status" not in columns:
                    conn.execute(text("ALTER TABLE projects ADD COLUMN workflow_status VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT 'editing'"))
                if "delivered_at" not in columns:
                    conn.execute(text("ALTER TABLE projects ADD COLUMN delivered_at DATETIME"))
                if "nle_project_path" not in columns:
                    conn.execute(text("ALTER TABLE projects ADD COLUMN nle_project_path VARCHAR(500)"))

                conn.execute(text("UPDATE projects SET base_fee = total_fee WHERE (base_fee IS NULL OR base_fee = 0) AND total_fee > 0"))
                conn.execute(text("UPDATE projects SET workflow_status = 'editing' WHERE workflow_status IS NULL OR workflow_status = ''"))
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"Migration Notice (projects): {e}")

            # ---------------- جدول clients ----------------
            try:
                result = conn.execute(text("PRAGMA table_info(clients)")).fetchall()
                columns = [row[1] for row in result]
                if "instagram" not in columns:
                    conn.execute(text("ALTER TABLE clients ADD COLUMN instagram VARCHAR(100)"))
                if "notes" not in columns:
                    conn.execute(text("ALTER TABLE clients ADD COLUMN notes TEXT"))
                if "company" not in columns:
                    conn.execute(text("ALTER TABLE clients ADD COLUMN company VARCHAR(150)"))
                if "phone" not in columns:
                    conn.execute(text("ALTER TABLE clients ADD COLUMN phone VARCHAR(50)"))
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"Migration Notice (clients): {e}")

            # ---------------- جدول transactions ----------------
            try:
                result = conn.execute(text("PRAGMA table_info(transactions)")).fetchall()
                columns = [row[1] for row in result]
                if "revision_id" not in columns:
                    conn.execute(text("ALTER TABLE transactions ADD COLUMN revision_id INTEGER REFERENCES project_revisions(id)"))
                if "payment_type" not in columns:
                    conn.execute(text("ALTER TABLE transactions ADD COLUMN payment_type VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT 'stage_payment'"))
                if "tracking_code" not in columns:
                    conn.execute(text("ALTER TABLE transactions ADD COLUMN tracking_code VARCHAR(100)"))
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"Migration Notice (transactions): {e}")

            # ---------------- جدول project_revisions ----------------
            try:
                result = conn.execute(text("PRAGMA table_info(project_revisions)")).fetchall()
                columns = [row[1] for row in result]
                if "extra_fee" not in columns:
                    conn.execute(text("ALTER TABLE project_revisions ADD COLUMN extra_fee BIGINT DEFAULT 0"))
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"Migration Notice (project_revisions): {e}")

    @contextmanager
    def get_session(self) -> Generator[Session, None, None]:
        session: Session = self.SessionLocal()
        try:
            yield session
        except Exception:
            session.rollback()
            raise
        finally:
            session.close()

    def get_next_project_number(self, session: Session) -> int:
        max_num = session.execute(text("SELECT MAX(project_number) FROM projects")).scalar()
        if max_num is None or max_num <= 0:
            return 1
        return int(max_num) + 1


db = DatabaseManager()

from datetime import datetime, timezone
from enum import Enum
from sqlalchemy import (
    Column, Integer, String, Boolean, DateTime, 
    ForeignKey, BigInteger, Text
)
from sqlalchemy.types import TypeDecorator
from sqlalchemy.orm import declarative_base, relationship

Base = declarative_base()


def utc_now():
    """تولید زمان UTC سازگار با نسخه‌های جدید پایتون بدون هشدار"""
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc)


class StageEnum(str, Enum):
    INGEST = "ingest"
    ROUGH_CUT = "rough_cut"
    COLOR_AUDIO = "color_audio"
    FINAL_RENDER = "final_render"
    CLIENT_REVIEW = "client_review"
    DELIVERED = "delivered"


class WorkflowStatusEnum(str, Enum):
    EDITING = "editing"
    READY_FOR_DELIVERY = "ready_for_delivery"
    UNDER_REVIEW = "under_review"
    REVISION_IN_PROGRESS = "revision_in_progress"
    REVISION_DONE = "revision_done"
    ARCHIVED = "archived"


class RevisionStatusEnum(str, Enum):
    NEW_REQUEST = "new_request"
    IN_PROGRESS = "in_progress"
    COMPLETED = "completed"
    ARCHIVED = "archived"


class PaymentTypeEnum(str, Enum):
    PREPAYMENT = "prepayment"
    STAGE_PAYMENT = "stage_payment"
    FINAL_SETTLEMENT = "final_settlement"


class SafeEnum(TypeDecorator):
    impl = String(50)
    cache_ok = True

    def __init__(self, enum_type, *args, **kwargs):
        super().__init__(*args, **kwargs)
        self._enum_type = enum_type

    def process_bind_param(self, value, dialect):
        if value is None:
            return None
        if isinstance(value, self._enum_type):
            return value.value
        if isinstance(value, str):
            try:
                return self._enum_type[value].value
            except KeyError:
                return value.lower()
        return str(value)

    def process_result_value(self, value, dialect):
        if value is None:
            return None
        if isinstance(value, self._enum_type):
            return value
        
        val_str = str(value).strip()
        for member in self._enum_type:
            if member.value == val_str or member.value == val_str.lower():
                return member
        for member in self._enum_type:
            if member.name == val_str or member.name == val_str.upper():
                return member
        return list(self._enum_type)[0]


class Client(Base):
    __tablename__ = "clients"

    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, autoincrement=True)
    full_name = Column(String(150), nullable=False)
    company = Column(String(150), nullable=True)
    phone = Column(String(50), nullable=True)
    instagram = Column(String(100), nullable=True)
    notes = Column(Text, nullable=True)
    created_at = Column(DateTime, default=utc_now)

    projects = relationship("Project", back_populates="client")
    transactions = relationship("Transaction", back_populates="client")


class Project(Base):
    __tablename__ = "projects"

    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, autoincrement=True)
    project_number = Column(Integer, unique=True, nullable=True, index=True)
    
    title = Column(String(200), nullable=False)
    couple_names = Column(String(200), nullable=False)
    client_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("clients.id", ondelete="SET NULL"), nullable=True)
    base_fee = Column(BigInteger, default=0)
    total_fee = Column(BigInteger, default=0)
    
    current_stage = Column(SafeEnum(StageEnum), default=StageEnum.ROUGH_CUT)
    workflow_status = Column(SafeEnum(WorkflowStatusEnum), default=WorkflowStatusEnum.EDITING)
    
    event_date = Column(DateTime, nullable=True)
    deadline = Column(DateTime, nullable=True)
    delivered_at = Column(DateTime, nullable=True)
    is_archived = Column(Boolean, default=False)
    archived_at = Column(DateTime, nullable=True)
    completed_at = Column(DateTime, nullable=True)
    created_at = Column(DateTime, default=utc_now)

    nle_project_path = Column(String(500), nullable=True)

    client = relationship("Client", back_populates="projects")
    clips = relationship("ProjectClipItem", back_populates="project", cascade="all, delete-orphan", order_by="ProjectClipItem.id")
    revisions = relationship("ProjectRevision", back_populates="project", cascade="all, delete-orphan", order_by="ProjectRevision.revision_number")
    transactions = relationship("Transaction", back_populates="project", cascade="all, delete-orphan", order_by="Transaction.paid_at.desc()")
    drive_locations = relationship("DriveLocation", back_populates="project", cascade="all, delete-orphan")
    event_logs = relationship("ProjectEventLog", back_populates="project", cascade="all, delete-orphan", order_by="ProjectEventLog.created_at.desc()")


class ProjectEventLog(Base):
    __tablename__ = "project_event_logs"

    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, autoincrement=True)
    project_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("projects.id"), nullable=False)
    event_type = Column(String(100), nullable=False)
    title = Column(String(200), nullable=False)
    description = Column(Text, nullable=True)
    created_at = Column(DateTime, default=utc_now)

    project = relationship("Project", back_populates="event_logs")


class ProjectClipItem(Base):
    __tablename__ = "project_clip_items"

    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, autoincrement=True)
    project_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("projects.id"), nullable=False)
    clip_name = Column(String(150), nullable=False)
    is_completed = Column(Boolean, default=False)
    completed_at = Column(DateTime, nullable=True)

    project = relationship("Project", back_populates="clips")


class ProjectRevision(Base):
    __tablename__ = "project_revisions"

    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, autoincrement=True)
    project_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("projects.id"), nullable=False)
    revision_number = Column(Integer, default=1)
    title = Column(String(150), nullable=False)
    description = Column(Text, nullable=True)
    extra_fee = Column(BigInteger, default=0)
    
    status = Column(SafeEnum(RevisionStatusEnum), default=RevisionStatusEnum.NEW_REQUEST)
    
    requested_at = Column(DateTime, default=utc_now)
    completed_at = Column(DateTime, nullable=True)

    project = relationship("Project", back_populates="revisions")
    tasks = relationship("ProjectRevisionTask", back_populates="revision", cascade="all, delete-orphan", order_by="ProjectRevisionTask.id")
    transactions = relationship("Transaction", back_populates="revision")

    __tablename__ = "project_revision_tasks"

    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, autoincrement=True)
    revision_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("project_revisions.id"), nullable=False)

    is_completed = Column(Boolean, default=False)
    completed_at = Column(DateTime, nullable=True)

class Transaction(Base):
    __tablename__ = "transactions"

    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, autoincrement=True)
    project_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("projects.id"), nullable=True)
    client_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("clients.id", ondelete="SET NULL"), nullable=True)
    revision_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("project_revisions.id", ondelete="SET NULL"), nullable=True)
    amount = Column(BigInteger, nullable=False, default=0)
    tracking_code = Column(String(100), nullable=True)
    
    payment_type = Column(SafeEnum(PaymentTypeEnum), default=PaymentTypeEnum.STAGE_PAYMENT)
    
    notes = Column(String(300), nullable=True)
    paid_at = Column(DateTime, default=utc_now)

    project = relationship("Project", back_populates="transactions")
    client = relationship("Client", back_populates="transactions")
    revision = relationship("ProjectRevision", back_populates="transactions")


class DriveLocation(Base):

    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, autoincrement=True)

import os
import sys

if sys.version_info[:2] >= (3, 0):
    def exec_file_wrapper(fpath, g_vars, l_vars):
        with open(fpath) as f:
            code = compile(f.read(), fpath, 'exec')
            exec(code, g_vars, l_vars)

'''
OpenCV Python binary extension loader
'''
import os
import importlib
import sys

__all__ = []

try:
    import numpy
    import numpy.core.multiarray
except ImportError:
    print('OpenCV bindings requires "numpy" package.')
    print('Install it via command:')
    print('    pip install numpy')
    raise

# TODO
# is_x64 = sys.maxsize > 2**32


def __load_extra_py_code_for_module(base, name, enable_debug_print=False):
    module_name = "{}.{}".format(__name__, name)
    export_module_name = "{}.{}".format(base, name)
    native_module = sys.modules.pop(module_name, None)
    try:
        py_module = importlib.import_module(module_name)
    except (ImportError, AttributeError) as err:
        if enable_debug_print:
            print("Can't load Python code for module:", module_name,
                  ". Reason:", err)
        # Extension doesn't contain extra py code
        return False

    if base in sys.modules and not hasattr(sys.modules[base], name):
        setattr(sys.modules[base], name, py_module)
    sys.modules[export_module_name] = py_module
    # If it is C extension module it is already loaded by cv2 package
    if native_module:
        setattr(py_module, "_native", native_module)
        for k, v in filter(lambda kv: not hasattr(py_module, kv[0]),
                           native_module.__dict__.items()):
            if enable_debug_print: print('    symbol({}): {} = {}'.format(name, k, v))
            setattr(py_module, k, v)
    return True


def __collect_extra_submodules(enable_debug_print=False):
    def modules_filter(module):
        return all((
             # module is not internal
             not module.startswith("_"),
             not module.startswith("python-"),
             # it is not a file
             os.path.isdir(os.path.join(_extra_submodules_init_path, module))
        ))
    if sys.version_info[0] < 3:
        if enable_debug_print:
            print("Extra submodules is loaded only for Python 3")
        return []

    __INIT_FILE_PATH = os.path.abspath(__file__)
    _extra_submodules_init_path = os.path.dirname(__INIT_FILE_PATH)
    return filter(modules_filter, os.listdir(_extra_submodules_init_path))


def bootstrap():
    import sys

    import copy
    save_sys_path = copy.copy(sys.path)

    if hasattr(sys, 'OpenCV_LOADER'):
        print(sys.path)
        raise ImportError('ERROR: recursion is detected during loading of "cv2" binary extensions. Check OpenCV installation.')
    sys.OpenCV_LOADER = True

    DEBUG = False
    if hasattr(sys, 'OpenCV_LOADER_DEBUG'):
        DEBUG = True

    import platform
    if DEBUG: print('OpenCV loader: os.name="{}"  platform.system()="{}"'.format(os.name, str(platform.system())))

    LOADER_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(os.path.realpath(__file__)))

    PYTHON_EXTENSIONS_PATHS = []
    BINARIES_PATHS = []

    g_vars = globals()
    l_vars = locals().copy()

    if sys.version_info[:2] < (3, 0):
        from . load_config_py2 import exec_file_wrapper
    else:
        from . load_config_py3 import exec_file_wrapper

    def load_first_config(fnames, required=True):
        for fname in fnames:
            fpath = os.path.join(LOADER_DIR, fname)
            if not os.path.exists(fpath):
                if DEBUG: print('OpenCV loader: config not found, skip: {}'.format(fpath))
                continue
            if DEBUG: print('OpenCV loader: loading config: {}'.format(fpath))
            exec_file_wrapper(fpath, g_vars, l_vars)
            return True
        if required:
            raise ImportError('OpenCV loader: missing configuration file: {}. Check OpenCV installation.'.format(fnames))

    load_first_config(['config.py'], True)
    load_first_config([
        'config-{}.{}.py'.format(sys.version_info[0], sys.version_info[1]),
        'config-{}.py'.format(sys.version_info[0])
    ], True)

    if DEBUG: print('OpenCV loader: PYTHON_EXTENSIONS_PATHS={}'.format(str(l_vars['PYTHON_EXTENSIONS_PATHS'])))
    if DEBUG: print('OpenCV loader: BINARIES_PATHS={}'.format(str(l_vars['BINARIES_PATHS'])))

    applySysPathWorkaround = False
    if hasattr(sys, 'OpenCV_REPLACE_SYS_PATH_0'):
        applySysPathWorkaround = True
    else:
        try:
            BASE_DIR = os.path.dirname(LOADER_DIR)
            if sys.path[0] == BASE_DIR or os.path.realpath(sys.path[0]) == BASE_DIR:
                applySysPathWorkaround = True
        except:
            if DEBUG: print('OpenCV loader: exception during checking workaround for sys.path[0]')
            pass  # applySysPathWorkaround is False

    for p in reversed(l_vars['PYTHON_EXTENSIONS_PATHS']):
        sys.path.insert(1 if not applySysPathWorkaround else 0, p)

    if os.name == 'nt':
        if sys.version_info[:2] >= (3, 8):  # https://github.com/python/cpython/pull/12302
            for p in l_vars['BINARIES_PATHS']:
                try:
                    os.add_dll_directory(p)
                except Exception as e:
                    if DEBUG: print('Failed os.add_dll_directory(): '+ str(e))
                    pass
        os.environ['PATH'] = ';'.join(l_vars['BINARIES_PATHS']) + ';' + os.environ.get('PATH', '')
        if DEBUG: print('OpenCV loader: PATH={}'.format(str(os.environ['PATH'])))
    else:
        # amending of LD_LIBRARY_PATH works for sub-processes only
        os.environ['LD_LIBRARY_PATH'] = ':'.join(l_vars['BINARIES_PATHS']) + ':' + os.environ.get('LD_LIBRARY_PATH', '')

    if DEBUG: print("Relink everything from native cv2 module to cv2 package")

    py_module = sys.modules.pop("cv2")

    native_module = importlib.import_module("cv2")

    sys.modules["cv2"] = py_module
    setattr(py_module, "_native", native_module)

    for item_name, item in filter(lambda kv: kv[0] not in ("__file__", "__loader__", "__spec__",
                                                           "__name__", "__package__"),
                                  native_module.__dict__.items()):
        if item_name not in g_vars:
            g_vars[item_name] = item

    sys.path = save_sys_path  # multiprocessing should start from bootstrap code (https://github.com/opencv/opencv/issues/18502)

    try:
        del sys.OpenCV_LOADER
    except Exception as e:
        if DEBUG:
            print("Exception during delete OpenCV_LOADER:", e)

    if DEBUG: print('OpenCV loader: binary extension... OK')

    for submodule in __collect_extra_submodules(DEBUG):
        if __load_extra_py_code_for_module("cv2", submodule, DEBUG):
            if DEBUG: print("Extra Python code for", submodule, "is loaded")

    if DEBUG: print('OpenCV loader: DONE')


bootstrap()

# FILE: dist\CutterLogPro\_internal\cv2\mat_wrapper\__init__.py
# ==================================================
__all__ = []

import numpy as np
import cv2 as cv
from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Any

# Same as cv2.typing.NumPyArrayNumeric, but avoids circular dependencies
if TYPE_CHECKING:
    _NumPyArrayNumeric = np.ndarray[Any, np.dtype[np.integer[Any] | np.floating[Any]]]
else:
    _NumPyArrayNumeric = np.ndarray

# NumPy documentation: https://numpy.org/doc/stable/user/basics.subclassing.html


class Mat(_NumPyArrayNumeric):
    '''
    cv.Mat wrapper for numpy array.

    Stores extra metadata information how to interpret and process of numpy array for underlying C++ code.
    '''

    def __new__(cls, arr, **kwargs):
        obj = arr.view(Mat)
        return obj

    def __init__(self, arr, **kwargs):
        self.wrap_channels = kwargs.pop('wrap_channels', getattr(arr, 'wrap_channels', False))
        if len(kwargs) > 0:
            raise TypeError('Unknown parameters: {}'.format(repr(kwargs)))

    def __array_finalize__(self, obj):
        if obj is None:
            return
        self.wrap_channels = getattr(obj, 'wrap_channels', None)


Mat.__module__ = cv.__name__
cv.Mat = Mat
cv._registerMatType(Mat)

import sys
from pathlib import Path

# اضافه کردن پوشه ریشه پروژه به مسیرهای پایتون
ROOT_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine
from sqlalchemy.orm import sessionmaker
from database.models import Base, Client, Project, ProjectClipItem, StageEnum, WorkflowStatusEnum
from database.db_manager import db


@pytest.fixture(scope="function")
def test_db_session():
    """ایجاد دیتابیس موقت در حافظه (in-memory SQLite) برای هر تست"""
    engine = create_engine("sqlite:///:memory:", echo=False)
    Base.metadata.create_all(bind=engine)
    TestingSessionLocal = sessionmaker(autocommit=False, autoflush=False, bind=engine)
    session = TestingSessionLocal()
    
    original_session_factory = db.SessionLocal
    db.SessionLocal = TestingSessionLocal
    
    yield session
    
    session.close()
    db.SessionLocal = original_session_factory
    Base.metadata.drop_all(bind=engine)

# FILE: tests/test_finance.py

from datetime import datetime, timezone
from database.models import Client, Project, Transaction, PaymentTypeEnum, WorkflowStatusEnum
from core.project_controller import ProjectController


def test_project_financial_summary(test_db_session):
    client = Client(full_name="استودیو دیاکو", company="دیاکو")
    test_db_session.add(client)
    test_db_session.commit()

    project = Project(
        project_number=1,
        title="پروژه سارا و علی",
        couple_names="سارا و علی",
        client_id=client.id,
        base_fee=10000000,
        total_fee=10000000,
        delivered_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
        workflow_status=WorkflowStatusEnum.UNDER_REVIEW
    )
    test_db_session.add(project)
    test_db_session.commit()

    # بدون واریزی
    fin = ProjectController.get_project_financial_summary(project)
    assert fin["total_fee"] == 10000000
    assert fin["total_paid"] == 0
    assert fin["remaining"] == 10000000
    assert not fin["is_settled"]

    # ثبت واریزی
    trx = Transaction(
        project_id=project.id,
        client_id=client.id,
        amount=6000000,
        payment_type=PaymentTypeEnum.STAGE_PAYMENT
    )
    test_db_session.add(trx)
    test_db_session.commit()
    test_db_session.refresh(project)

    fin_updated = ProjectController.get_project_financial_summary(project)
    assert fin_updated["total_paid"] == 6000000
    assert fin_updated["remaining"] == 4000000
    assert not fin_updated["is_settled"]

from database.models import Client, Project, ProjectClipItem
from core.project_controller import ProjectController


def test_project_progress_calculation(test_db_session):
    client = Client(full_name="استودیو آوا", company="آوا")
    test_db_session.add(client)
    test_db_session.commit()

    project = Project(
        project_number=2,
        title="پروژه مریم و رضا",
        couple_names="مریم و رضا",
        client_id=client.id,
        base_fee=12000000,
        total_fee=12000000
    )
    test_db_session.add(project)
    test_db_session.flush()

    clip1 = ProjectClipItem(project_id=project.id, clip_name="Long Cut", is_completed=True)
    clip2 = ProjectClipItem(project_id=project.id, clip_name="Teaser", is_completed=False)
    test_db_session.add_all([clip1, clip2])
    test_db_session.commit()
    test_db_session.refresh(project)

    completed, total, pct = ProjectController.calculate_project_progress(project)
    assert completed == 1
    assert total == 2
    assert pct == 50

from core.persian_utils import (
    to_persian_numbers, normalize_to_english_digits,
    format_toman, number_to_persian_words, parse_jalali_text
)


def test_digit_conversions():
    assert to_persian_numbers("1234567890") == "۱۲۳۴۵۶۷۸۹۰"
    assert normalize_to_english_digits("۱۲۳۴۵۶۷۸۹۰") == "1234567890"
    assert normalize_to_english_digits("١٢٣٤٥٦٧٨٩٠") == "1234567890"


def test_format_toman():
    assert format_toman(15000000) == "۱۵,۰۰۰,۰۰۰ تومان"
    assert format_toman(0) == "۰ تومان"


def test_number_to_persian_words():
    assert "پانزده میلیون تومان" in number_to_persian_words(15000000)
    assert "یک میلیون و پانصد هزار تومان" in number_to_persian_words(1500000)


def test_parse_jalali_text():
    j_date = parse_jalali_text("۱۴۰۵/۰۵/۲۵")
    assert j_date is not None
    assert j_date.year == 1405
    assert j_date.month == 5
    assert j_date.day == 25

from datetime import datetime
from enum import Enum
from sqlalchemy import (
    Column, Integer, String, Boolean, DateTime,
    ForeignKey, BigInteger, Text, Float
)
from sqlalchemy.orm import relationship
from database.models import Base, SafeEnum


class SessionStatusEnum(str, Enum):
    ACTIVE = "active"
    PAUSED = "paused"
    COMPLETED = "completed"
    INTERRUPTED = "interrupted"


class ActivityStateEnum(str, Enum):
    EDITING = "editing"
    PROCESSING = "processing"
    IDLE = "idle"
    OUTSIDE_EDITING = "outside_editing"


class WorkSession(Base):
    __tablename__ = "work_sessions"

    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, autoincrement=True)
    project_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("projects.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), nullable=False)
    clip_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("project_clip_items.id", ondelete="SET NULL"), nullable=True)

    start_time = Column(DateTime, nullable=False, default=datetime.utcnow)
    end_time = Column(DateTime, nullable=True)

    active_duration = Column(Integer, default=0)    # به ثانیه
    paused_duration = Column(Integer, default=0)    # به ثانیه
    idle_duration = Column(Integer, default=0)      # به ثانیه
    total_duration = Column(Integer, default=0)     # به ثانیه

    activity_type = Column(String(50), default="editing", nullable=True) # رفع محدودیت NOT NULL
    stage_name = Column(String(50), default="Editing")
    status = Column(SafeEnum(SessionStatusEnum), default=SessionStatusEnum.ACTIVE)
    note = Column(Text, nullable=True)
    is_manual = Column(Boolean, default=False)
    is_clip_completed = Column(Boolean, default=False)

    created_at = Column(DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow)

    # ارتباطات
    project = relationship("Project", backref="work_sessions")
    clip = relationship("ProjectClipItem", backref="work_sessions")
    activity_logs = relationship("ActivityLog", back_populates="session", cascade="all, delete-orphan")


class DailyGoal(Base):
    __tablename__ = "daily_goals"

    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, autoincrement=True)
    date_str = Column(String(20), unique=True, nullable=False)
    target_minutes = Column(Integer, default=480) # پیش‌فرض ۸ ساعت
    updated_at = Column(DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow)


class ActivityLog(Base):
    __tablename__ = "activity_logs"

    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, autoincrement=True)
    session_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("work_sessions.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), nullable=True)

    application_name = Column(String(100), nullable=True)
    window_title = Column(String(250), nullable=True)
    state = Column(SafeEnum(ActivityStateEnum), default=ActivityStateEnum.EDITING)

    cpu_usage = Column(Float, default=0.0)
    gpu_usage = Column(Float, default=0.0)
    ram_usage = Column(Float, default=0.0)

    user_idle_seconds = Column(Integer, default=0)
    timestamp = Column(DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow)

    session = relationship("WorkSession", back_populates="activity_logs")
