import sys
import os
import time
import ctypes
import platform
import subprocess
import psutil
from PySide6.QtCore import QThread, Signal

# نرم‌افزارهای اصلی تدوین
CORE_NLE_PROCESSES = {
    "adobe premiere pro.exe": "Premiere Pro",
    "premiere.exe": "Premiere Pro",
    "davinci resolve.exe": "DaVinci Resolve",
    "resolve.exe": "DaVinci Resolve",
    "afterfx.exe": "After Effects",
    "edius.exe": "EDIUS",
    "final cut pro": "Final Cut Pro"
}

# نرم‌افزارهای کمکی و مجاز
SUPPORTING_PROCESSES = {
    "photoshop.exe": "Photoshop",
    "illustrator.exe": "Illustrator",
    "audition.exe": "Audition",
    "cubase.exe": "Cubase",
    "fl64.exe": "FL Studio"
}


def get_hardware_specifications() -> dict:
    """شناسایی خودکار و Cross-Platform مدل CPU، GPU و حجم کل RAM"""
    specs = {
        "cpu_name": "پردازنده سیستم",
        "gpu_name": "کارت گرافیک",
        "ram_total_str": "حافظه رم"
    }

    # ۱. محاسبه حجم کل رم
    try:
        total_ram_gb = round(psutil.virtual_memory().total / (1024 ** 3))
        specs["ram_total_str"] = f"{total_ram_gb} GB RAM"
    except Exception:
        specs["ram_total_str"] = "RAM"

    # ۲. شناسایی نام و مدل پردازنده (CPU)
    try:
        if sys.platform == "win32":
            import winreg
            key = winreg.OpenKey(winreg.HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE, r"HARDWARE\DESCRIPTION\System\CentralProcessor\0")
            cpu_raw = winreg.QueryValueEx(key, "ProcessorNameString")[0]
            winreg.CloseKey(key)
            specs["cpu_name"] = cpu_raw.strip().replace("Processor", "").replace("(R)", "").replace("(TM)", "").strip()
        elif sys.platform == "darwin":
            cmd = ["sysctl", "-n", "machdep.cpu.brand_string"]
            cpu_raw = subprocess.check_output(cmd, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL).decode().strip()
            specs["cpu_name"] = cpu_raw
        else:
            specs["cpu_name"] = platform.processor() or "CPU"
    except Exception:
        specs["cpu_name"] = platform.processor() or "CPU"

    # تمیزکاری نام CPU برای نمایش فشرده در تایل
    if "Intel" in specs["cpu_name"]:
        specs["cpu_name"] = specs["cpu_name"].replace("Intel Core", "Core").strip()
    elif "AMD" in specs["cpu_name"]:
        specs["cpu_name"] = specs["cpu_name"].replace("AMD Ryzen", "Ryzen").strip()

    # ۳. شناسایی نام و مدل کارت گرافیک (GPU)
    try:
        if sys.platform == "win32":
            # استفاده از دستور سبک و بدون پنجره wmic/powershell
            cmd = "powershell -NoProfile -Command \"Get-CimInstance Win32_VideoController | Select-Object -ExpandProperty Name\""
            startupinfo = subprocess.STARTUPINFO()
            startupinfo.dwFlags |= subprocess.STARTF_USESHOWWINDOW
            output = subprocess.check_output(
                cmd, 
                startupinfo=startupinfo, 
                stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL, 
                shell=False
            ).decode("utf-8", errors="ignore").strip().splitlines()

            # ترجیح کارت‌های مجزا (NVIDIA / AMD) نسبت به گرافیک آنبورد اینتل
            gpus = [g.strip() for g in output if g.strip()]
            if gpus:
                discrete_gpus = [g for g in gpus if "NVIDIA" in g or "Radeon" in g or "RTX" in g or "GTX" in g or "AMD" in g]
                target_gpu = discrete_gpus[0] if discrete_gpus else gpus[0]
                # کوتاه‌سازی نام تجاری برای تناسب باکس
                specs["gpu_name"] = target_gpu.replace("NVIDIA GeForce", "").replace("AMD Radeon", "Radeon").strip()
        elif sys.platform == "darwin":
            cmd = ["system_profiler", "SPDisplaysDataType"]
            output = subprocess.check_output(cmd, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL).decode()
            for line in output.splitlines():
                if "Chipset Model:" in line:
                    specs["gpu_name"] = line.split(":", 1)[1].strip()
                    break
        else:
            specs["gpu_name"] = "GPU"
    except Exception:
        specs["gpu_name"] = "GPU"

    return specs


class LASTINPUTINFO(ctypes.Structure):
    _fields_ = [
        ("cbSize", ctypes.c_uint),
        ("dwTime", ctypes.c_uint)
    ]


def get_windows_idle_seconds() -> float:
    if sys.platform != "win32":
        return 0.0
    try:
        lii = LASTINPUTINFO()
        lii.cbSize = ctypes.sizeof(LASTINPUTINFO)
        if ctypes.windll.user32.GetLastInputInfo(ctypes.byref(lii)):
            millis = ctypes.windll.kernel32.GetTickCount() - lii.dwTime
            return max(0.0, millis / 1000.0)
    except Exception:
        pass
    return 0.0


def get_active_window_process_info() -> tuple[str, str]:
    if sys.platform != "win32":
        return "Unknown", "Unknown"
    try:
        hwnd = ctypes.windll.user32.GetForegroundWindow()
        if not hwnd:
            return "Desktop", "explorer.exe"

        length = ctypes.windll.user32.GetWindowTextLengthW(hwnd)
        buff = ctypes.create_unicode_buffer(length + 1)
        ctypes.windll.user32.GetWindowTextW(hwnd, buff, length + 1)
        win_title = buff.value or "Active Window"

        pid = ctypes.c_ulong()
        ctypes.windll.user32.GetWindowThreadProcessId(hwnd, ctypes.byref(pid))
        
        proc_name = ""
        if pid.value:
            proc = psutil.Process(pid.value)
            proc_name = proc.name().lower()

        return win_title, proc_name
    except Exception:
        return "Unknown", "Unknown"


class SystemActivityMonitorThread(QThread):
    metrics_updated = Signal(dict)

    def __init__(self, check_interval_sec: float = 1.0, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.check_interval_sec = check_interval_sec
        self._is_running = True
        # شناسایی یک‌باره نام قطعات سخت‌افزار در زمان استارت
        self.hardware_specs = get_hardware_specifications()

    def run(self):
        try:
            psutil.cpu_percent(interval=None)
        except Exception:
            pass

        while self._is_running:
            try:
                idle_sec = get_windows_idle_seconds()
                win_title, proc_name = get_active_window_process_info()

                active_app_label = "برنامه متفرقه"
                is_core_nle = False
                is_supporting = False

                for exe_key, friendly_name in CORE_NLE_PROCESSES.items():
                    if exe_key in proc_name:
                        active_app_label = friendly_name
                        is_core_nle = True
                        break

                if not is_core_nle:
                    for exe_key, friendly_name in SUPPORTING_PROCESSES.items():
                        if exe_key in proc_name:
                            active_app_label = friendly_name
                            is_supporting = True
                            break

                cpu_pct = psutil.cpu_percent(interval=None)
                ram_pct = psutil.virtual_memory().percent
                gpu_pct = min(100.0, cpu_pct * 0.85) if cpu_pct > 65 else min(100.0, cpu_pct * 0.4)

                is_rendering = (cpu_pct >= 40.0 or gpu_pct >= 35.0)

                if is_rendering:
                    current_state = "processing"
                elif is_core_nle:
                    current_state = "editing" if idle_sec <= 60 else "idle"
                elif is_supporting:
                    current_state = "supporting" if idle_sec <= 45 else "idle"
                else:
                    current_state = "away" if idle_sec > 45 else "distraction"

                payload = {
                    "active_app": active_app_label,
                    "window_title": win_title,
                    "state": current_state,
                    "idle_seconds": int(idle_sec),
                    "cpu_percent": float(cpu_pct),
                    "gpu_percent": float(gpu_pct),
                    "ram_percent": float(ram_pct),
                    "is_rendering": is_rendering,
                    "is_core_nle": is_core_nle,
                    "is_supporting": is_supporting,
                    "hardware_specs": self.hardware_specs
                }

                self.metrics_updated.emit(payload)

            except Exception:
                pass

            time.sleep(self.check_interval_sec)

    def stop(self):
        self._is_running = False
        self.wait(1000)

# ==================================================
# FILE: time_tracking\timer_engine.py
# ==================================================
# ==================================================
# FILE: time_tracking/timer_engine.py
# ==================================================
from datetime import datetime, date
from typing import Optional, Dict, Any, Tuple
from PySide6.QtCore import QObject, QTimer, Signal
from sqlalchemy import select, func

from database.db_manager import db
from database.models import ProjectClipItem
from time_tracking.models import WorkSession, DailyGoal, ActivityLog, SessionStatusEnum
from time_tracking.system_monitor import SystemActivityMonitorThread
from core.signals import global_signals


class TimeTrackingEngine(QObject):
    _instance = None
    retroactive_prompt_requested = Signal(int, str)

    @classmethod
    def instance(cls):
        if cls._instance is None:
            cls._instance = TimeTrackingEngine()
        return cls._instance

    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.active_session_id: Optional[int] = None
        self.current_project_id: Optional[int] = None
        self.current_clip_id: Optional[int] = None
        
        self.active_seconds = 0
        self.render_seconds = 0
        self.paused_seconds = 0
        self.idle_seconds = 0
        self.away_accumulated_seconds = 0
        
        self.is_paused = False
        self.is_running = False
        self.auto_suspended = False

        self.sessions_memory: Dict[Tuple[int, Optional[int]], Dict[str, Any]] = {}
        self.last_system_metric: Dict[str, Any] = {}

        self.tick_timer = QTimer(self)
        self.tick_timer.setInterval(1000)
        self.tick_timer.timeout.connect(self._on_tick)

        self.auto_save_timer = QTimer(self)
        self.auto_save_timer.setInterval(30000)
        self.auto_save_timer.timeout.connect(self._persist_active_session)

        # ترد پایش فعالیت سیستم
        self.monitor_thread = SystemActivityMonitorThread(check_interval_sec=1.0, parent=self)
        self.monitor_thread.metrics_updated.connect(self._on_system_metrics)
        self.monitor_thread.start()

        self._load_persisted_paused_sessions()

    def _load_persisted_paused_sessions(self):
        try:
            with db.get_session() as session:
                stmt = select(WorkSession).where(
                    WorkSession.status == SessionStatusEnum.PAUSED
                ).order_by(WorkSession.start_time.desc())
                paused_sessions = session.scalars(stmt).all()

                for s in paused_sessions:
                    key = (s.project_id, s.clip_id)
                    self.sessions_memory[key] = {
                        "session_id": s.id,
                        "active_seconds": s.active_duration or 0,
                        "paused_seconds": s.paused_duration or 0,
                        "idle_seconds": s.idle_duration or 0,
                        "is_paused": True,
                        "is_running": True
                    }

                if paused_sessions:
                    latest = paused_sessions[0]
                    self.select_context(latest.project_id, latest.clip_id)
        except Exception:
            pass

    def _on_system_metrics(self, data: dict):
        self.last_system_metric = data
        global_signals.system_activity_updated.emit(data)

        state = data.get("state", "idle")
        is_rendering = data.get("is_rendering", False)
        is_core_nle = data.get("is_core_nle", False)
        is_supporting = data.get("is_supporting", False)

        if not self.is_running or self.is_paused:
            return

        # پایش هوشمند خروج از محیط تدوین
        if state in ["distraction", "away"] and not is_rendering:
            self.away_accumulated_seconds += 1
            if self.away_accumulated_seconds >= 60 and not self.auto_suspended:
                self.auto_suspended = True
                self.pause_session(auto=True)
                global_signals.show_toast.emit("خروج از محیط تدوین؛ جلسه کاری به صورت خودکار متوقف شد.", "info")
        else:
            if self.auto_suspended and (is_core_nle or is_supporting or is_rendering):
                away_sec = self.away_accumulated_seconds
                self.auto_suspended = False
                self.away_accumulated_seconds = 0
                self.resume_session()

                if away_sec >= 90:
                    self.retroactive_prompt_requested.emit(away_sec, data.get("active_app", "سایر برنامه‌ها"))
            else:
                self.away_accumulated_seconds = 0

    def apply_retroactive_time(self, seconds_to_add: int):
        if self.is_running:
            self.active_seconds += seconds_to_add
            self._persist_active_session()
            global_signals.show_toast.emit(f"مدت زمان {seconds_to_add // 60} دقیقه به پروژه افزوده شد.", "success")
            self._emit_snapshot()

    def _on_tick(self):
        if not self.is_running:
            return

        if self.is_paused:
            self.paused_seconds += 1
        else:
            if self.last_system_metric.get("is_rendering"):
                self.render_seconds += 1
            self.active_seconds += 1

        self._emit_snapshot()

    def _emit_snapshot(self):
        total_sec = self.active_seconds + self.paused_seconds
        snapshot = {
            "session_id": self.active_session_id,
            "project_id": self.current_project_id,
            "clip_id": self.current_clip_id,
            "active_seconds": self.active_seconds,
            "render_seconds": self.render_seconds,
            "paused_seconds": self.paused_seconds,
            "idle_seconds": self.idle_seconds,
            "total_seconds": total_sec,
            "is_paused": self.is_paused,
            "is_running": self.is_running
        }
        global_signals.timer_tick.emit(snapshot)

    def select_context(self, project_id: int, clip_id: Optional[int] = None):
        if self.current_project_id is not None:
            old_key = (self.current_project_id, self.current_clip_id)
            if self.is_running:
                self.sessions_memory[old_key] = {
                    "session_id": self.active_session_id,
                    "active_seconds": self.active_seconds,
                    "paused_seconds": self.paused_seconds,
                    "idle_seconds": self.idle_seconds,
                    "is_paused": self.is_paused,
                    "is_running": self.is_running
                }
                self.tick_timer.stop()

        self.current_project_id = project_id
        self.current_clip_id = clip_id
        new_key = (project_id, clip_id)

        if new_key in self.sessions_memory:
            state = self.sessions_memory[new_key]
            self.active_session_id = state["session_id"]
            self.active_seconds = state["active_seconds"]
            self.paused_seconds = state["paused_seconds"]
            self.idle_seconds = state["idle_seconds"]
            self.is_paused = state["is_paused"]
            self.is_running = state["is_running"]
            self.auto_suspended = False

            if self.is_running and not self.is_paused:
                self.tick_timer.start()
        else:
            self.active_session_id = None
            self.active_seconds = 0
            self.paused_seconds = 0
            self.idle_seconds = 0
            self.is_paused = False
            self.is_running = False
            self.auto_suspended = False

        self._emit_snapshot()

    def start_session(self, project_id: int, clip_id: Optional[int] = None, stage_name: str = "Editing") -> int:
        if clip_id:
            with db.get_session() as session:
                clip = session.get(ProjectClipItem, clip_id)
                if clip and clip.is_completed:
                    global_signals.show_toast.emit("این کلیپ تکمیل شده است و امکان ثبت زمان ندارد.", "warning")
                    return 0

        self.current_project_id = project_id
        self.current_clip_id = clip_id
        self.active_seconds = 0
        self.render_seconds = 0
        self.paused_seconds = 0
        self.idle_seconds = 0
        self.away_accumulated_seconds = 0
        self.is_paused = False
        self.auto_suspended = False

        with db.get_session() as session:
            new_sess = WorkSession(
                project_id=project_id,
                clip_id=clip_id,
                start_time=datetime.utcnow(),
                activity_type="editing",
                stage_name=stage_name,
                status=SessionStatusEnum.ACTIVE,
                is_manual=False
            )
            session.add(new_sess)
            session.commit()
            self.active_session_id = new_sess.id

        self.is_running = True
        self.tick_timer.start()
        self.auto_save_timer.start()

        key = (project_id, clip_id)
        self.sessions_memory[key] = {
            "session_id": self.active_session_id,
            "active_seconds": self.active_seconds,
            "paused_seconds": self.paused_seconds,
            "idle_seconds": self.idle_seconds,
            "is_paused": False,
            "is_running": True
        }

        global_signals.session_started.emit(self.active_session_id)
        self._emit_snapshot()
        return self.active_session_id

    def pause_session(self, auto: bool = False):
        if self.is_running and not self.is_paused:
            self.is_paused = True
            self.tick_timer.stop()
            self._persist_active_session()
            
            key = (self.current_project_id, self.current_clip_id)
            if key in self.sessions_memory:
                self.sessions_memory[key]["is_paused"] = True
                self.sessions_memory[key]["active_seconds"] = self.active_seconds

            if self.active_session_id:
                global_signals.session_paused.emit(self.active_session_id)
            self._emit_snapshot()

    def resume_session(self):
        if self.is_running and self.is_paused:
            self.is_paused = False
            self.auto_suspended = False
            self.away_accumulated_seconds = 0
            self.tick_timer.start()
            self._persist_active_session()

            key = (self.current_project_id, self.current_clip_id)
            if key in self.sessions_memory:
                self.sessions_memory[key]["is_paused"] = False

            if self.active_session_id:
                global_signals.session_resumed.emit(self.active_session_id)
            self._emit_snapshot()

    def end_session(self, note: str = "", mark_clip_completed: bool = False) -> bool:
        if not self.is_running or not self.active_session_id:
            return False

        self.tick_timer.stop()
        self.auto_save_timer.stop()

        sess_id = self.active_session_id
        total_sec = self.active_seconds + self.paused_seconds

        with db.get_session() as session:
            sess = session.get(WorkSession, sess_id)
            if sess:
                sess.end_time = datetime.utcnow()
                sess.active_duration = self.active_seconds
                sess.paused_duration = self.paused_seconds
                sess.idle_duration = self.idle_seconds
                sess.total_duration = total_sec
                sess.status = SessionStatusEnum.COMPLETED
                if note:
                    sess.note = note
                sess.is_clip_completed = mark_clip_completed

                if mark_clip_completed and sess.clip_id:
                    clip = session.get(ProjectClipItem, sess.clip_id)
                    if clip:
                        clip.is_completed = True
                        clip.completed_at = datetime.utcnow()
                        session.flush()
                        global_signals.clip_status_toggled.emit(clip.project_id, clip.id)

                session.commit()

        key = (self.current_project_id, self.current_clip_id)
        if key in self.sessions_memory:
            del self.sessions_memory[key]

        self.is_running = False
        self.is_paused = False
        self.auto_suspended = False
        self.active_session_id = None
        self.active_seconds = 0
        self.render_seconds = 0
        self.paused_seconds = 0
        self.idle_seconds = 0

        global_signals.session_ended.emit(sess_id)
        global_signals.project_updated.emit(self.current_project_id or 0)
        self._emit_snapshot()
        return True

    def _persist_active_session(self):
        if not self.active_session_id:
            return
        try:
            with db.get_session() as session:
                sess = session.get(WorkSession, self.active_session_id)
                if sess and sess.status in [SessionStatusEnum.ACTIVE, SessionStatusEnum.PAUSED]:
                    sess.active_duration = self.active_seconds
                    sess.paused_duration = self.paused_seconds
                    sess.idle_duration = self.idle_seconds
                    sess.total_duration = self.active_seconds + self.paused_seconds
                    sess.status = SessionStatusEnum.PAUSED if self.is_paused else SessionStatusEnum.ACTIVE

                    if self.last_system_metric:
                        log = ActivityLog(
                            session_id=sess.id,
                            application_name=self.last_system_metric.get("active_app", ""),
                            window_title=self.last_system_metric.get("window_title", "")[:240],
                            state=self.last_system_metric.get("state", "editing"),
                            cpu_usage=self.last_system_metric.get("cpu_percent", 0.0),
                            gpu_usage=self.last_system_metric.get("gpu_percent", 0.0),
                            ram_usage=self.last_system_metric.get("ram_percent", 0.0),
                            user_idle_seconds=self.last_system_metric.get("idle_seconds", 0)
                        )
                        session.add(log)

                    session.commit()
        except Exception:
            pass

    def get_today_total_active_seconds(self) -> int:
        today_start = datetime.utcnow().replace(hour=0, minute=0, second=0, microsecond=0)
        with db.get_session() as session:
            stmt = select(func.sum(WorkSession.active_duration)).where(
                WorkSession.start_time >= today_start
            )
            res = session.execute(stmt).scalar() or 0
            if self.is_running and not self.is_paused:
                res += self.active_seconds
            return int(res)

    def get_daily_goal_minutes(self) -> int:
        today_str = date.today().isoformat()
        with db.get_session() as session:
            goal = session.scalars(select(DailyGoal).where(DailyGoal.date_str == today_str)).first()
            if goal:
                return goal.target_minutes
            return 480

    def set_daily_goal_minutes(self, minutes: int):
        today_str = date.today().isoformat()
        with db.get_session() as session:
            goal = session.scalars(select(DailyGoal).where(DailyGoal.date_str == today_str)).first()
            if not goal:
                goal = DailyGoal(date_str=today_str, target_minutes=minutes)
                session.add(goal)
            else:
                goal.target_minutes = minutes
                goal.updated_at = datetime.utcnow()
            session.commit()

from PySide6.QtWidgets import (
    QWidget, QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout, QLabel, 
    QPushButton, QFrame, QSizePolicy
)
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt, Signal
from widgets.studio_dialog_base import StudioBaseDialog
from core.persian_utils import to_persian_numbers


class StudioStepperWidget(QFrame):
    """ویجت کنترل عددی مدرن کپسولی با دکمه‌های مجزا و بزرگ + و -"""
    valueChanged = Signal(int)

    def __init__(self, value: int = 8, min_val: int = 1, max_val: int = 24, step: int = 1, unit_label: str = "ساعت", parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.value = value
        self.min_val = min_val
        self.max_val = max_val
        self.step = step
        self.unit_label = unit_label

        self.setFixedHeight(46)
        self.setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy.Policy.Expanding, QSizePolicy.Policy.Fixed)
        self.setStyleSheet("""
            StudioStepperWidget {
                background-color: #161F30;
                border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08);
                border-radius: 8px;
            }
            StudioStepperWidget:hover {
                border-color: rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.4);
            }
        """)
        self.init_ui()

    def init_ui(self):
        layout = QHBoxLayout(self)
        layout.setContentsMargins(6, 4, 6, 4)
        layout.setSpacing(6)

        # دکمه افزایش (+)
        btn_plus = QPushButton("+")
        btn_plus.setFixedSize(34, 34)
        btn_plus.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        btn_plus.setStyleSheet("""
            QPushButton {
                font-family: 'Segoe UI', monospace;
                background-color: #1F2A40;
                color: #818CF8;
                border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.06);
                border-radius: 6px;
                font-size: 16px;
                font-weight: bold;
                padding: 0;
            }
            QPushButton:hover {
                background-color: #6366F1;
                color: #FFFFFF;
                border-color: #818CF8;
            }
        """)
        btn_plus.clicked.connect(self._increase)
        layout.addWidget(btn_plus)

        # نمایشگر عدد و واحد در مرکز
        self.lbl_display = QLabel()
        self.lbl_display.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        self.lbl_display.setStyleSheet("background: transparent; border: none;")
        self._update_label()
        layout.addWidget(self.lbl_display, stretch=1)

        # دکمه کاهش (-)
        btn_minus = QPushButton("-")
        btn_minus.setFixedSize(34, 34)
        btn_minus.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        btn_minus.setStyleSheet("""
            QPushButton {
                font-family: 'Segoe UI', monospace;
                background-color: #1F2A40;
                color: #818CF8;
                border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.06);
                border-radius: 6px;
                font-size: 16px;
                font-weight: bold;
                padding: 0;
            }
            QPushButton:hover {
                background-color: #EF4444;
                color: #FFFFFF;
                border-color: #F87171;
            }
        """)
        btn_minus.clicked.connect(self._decrease)
        layout.addWidget(btn_minus)

    def _update_label(self):
        val_str = f"{self.value:02d}"
        self.lbl_display.setText(f"<b style='font-family:Segoe UI, Vazirmatn; font-size:16px; color:#F8FAFC;'>{to_persian_numbers(val_str)}</b> <span style='font-family:Vazirmatn; font-size:11.5px; color:#94A3B8;'>{self.unit_label}</span>")

    def _increase(self):
        if self.value + self.step <= self.max_val:
            self.value += self.step
            self._update_label()
            self.valueChanged.emit(self.value)

    def _decrease(self):
        if self.value - self.step >= self.min_val:
            self.value -= self.step
            self._update_label()
            self.valueChanged.emit(self.value)

    def set_value(self, new_val: int):
        self.value = max(self.min_val, min(self.max_val, new_val))
        self._update_label()

    def wheelEvent(self, event):
        if event.angleDelta().y() > 0:
            self._increase()
        else:
            self._decrease()
        event.accept()


class DailyGoalSettingDialog(StudioBaseDialog):
    """دیالوگ مدرن تنظیم هدف کاری روزانه با کنترلرهای بازطراحی‌شده"""
    def __init__(self, current_minutes: int = 480, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent=parent)
        self.target_minutes = current_minutes
        self.setFixedWidth(440)
        self.init_ui()

    def init_ui(self):
        self.build_header(
            "تنظیم هدف کاری امروز",
            "مدت‌زمان هدف تدوین روزانه خود را تعیین کنید",
            icon_name="settings",
            icon_color="#6366F1"
        )

        cur_hours = max(1, self.target_minutes // 60)
        cur_mins = self.target_minutes % 60

        # ۱. دکمه‌های انتخاب سریع (Presets)
        lbl_p = QLabel("انتخاب سریع:")
        lbl_p.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 11.5px; color: #8F909D; font-weight: 600;")
        self.container_layout.addWidget(lbl_p)

        preset_row = QHBoxLayout()
        preset_row.setSpacing(6)

        presets = [
            ("۴ ساعت", 240),
            ("۶ ساعت", 360),
            ("۸ ساعت", 480),
            ("۱۰ ساعت", 600)
        ]

        for label, mins in presets:
            btn = QPushButton(label)
            btn.setFixedHeight(32)
            btn.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
            btn.setStyleSheet("""
                QPushButton {
                    font-family: 'Vazirmatn';
                    background-color: #161F30;
                    color: #94A3B8;
                    border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08);
                    border-radius: 6px;
                    font-size: 11.5px;
                    font-weight: 600;
                }
                QPushButton:hover {
                    background-color: #1E283D;
                    color: #F8FAFC;
                    border-color: #6366F1;
                }
            """)
            btn.clicked.connect(lambda _, m=mins: self._set_preset(m))
            preset_row.addWidget(btn)

        self.container_layout.addLayout(preset_row)

        # ۲. کنترلرهای بازطراحی‌شده ساعت و دقیقه در کنار هم
        lbl_adj = QLabel("تنظیم دقیق زمان هدف:")
        lbl_adj.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 11.5px; color: #8F909D; font-weight: 600; margin-top: 4px;")
        self.container_layout.addWidget(lbl_adj)

        steppers_row = QHBoxLayout()
        steppers_row.setSpacing(10)

        # استپر ساعت
        self.hour_stepper = StudioStepperWidget(
            value=cur_hours, 
            min_val=1, 
            max_val=24, 
            step=1, 
            unit_label="ساعت",
            parent=self
        )
        steppers_row.addWidget(self.hour_stepper, stretch=1)

        # استپر دقیقه (گام‌های ۱۵ دقیقه‌ای)
        self.min_stepper = StudioStepperWidget(
            value=cur_mins, 
            min_val=0, 
            max_val=45, 
            step=15, 
            unit_label="دقیقه",
            parent=self
        )
        steppers_row.addWidget(self.min_stepper, stretch=1)

        self.container_layout.addLayout(steppers_row)

        # ۳. دکمه‌های عملیاتی
        btn_box = QHBoxLayout()
        btn_box.setSpacing(8)
        btn_box.setContentsMargins(0, 8, 0, 0)

        btn_save = QPushButton("ذخیره هدف کاری")
        btn_save.setFixedHeight(38)
        btn_save.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        btn_save.setStyleSheet("""
            QPushButton {
                font-family: 'Vazirmatn';
                background-color: #6366F1;
                color: #FFFFFF;
                border: none;
                border-radius: 6px;
                font-size: 12px;
                font-weight: 800;
            }
            QPushButton:hover { background-color: #4F46E5; }
        """)
        btn_save.clicked.connect(self._save)
        btn_box.addWidget(btn_save, stretch=2)

        btn_cancel = QPushButton("انصراف")
        btn_cancel.setFixedHeight(38)
        btn_cancel.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        btn_cancel.setStyleSheet("""
            QPushButton {
                font-family: 'Vazirmatn';
                background-color: #161F30;
                color: #94A3B8;
                border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08);
                border-radius: 6px;
                font-size: 12px;
                font-weight: 600;
            }
            QPushButton:hover { background-color: #1E283D; color: #F8FAFC; }
        """)
        btn_cancel.clicked.connect(self.reject)
        btn_box.addWidget(btn_cancel, stretch=1)

        self.container_layout.addLayout(btn_box)

    def _set_preset(self, minutes: int):
        self.hour_stepper.set_value(minutes // 60)
        self.min_stepper.set_value(minutes % 60)

    def _save(self):
        self.target_minutes = (self.hour_stepper.value * 60) + self.min_stepper.value
        self.accept()

from PySide6.QtWidgets import QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout, QLabel, QTextEdit, QPushButton
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt
from widgets.studio_dialog_base import StudioBaseDialog
from core.persian_utils import to_persian_numbers


class PauseSessionNoteDialog(StudioBaseDialog):
    """پاپ‌آپ دریافت یادداشت جلسه کاری هنگام توقف موقت"""
    def __init__(self, duration_str: str, clip_name: str = "", parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent=parent)
        self.duration_str = duration_str
        self.clip_name = clip_name
        self.session_note = ""

        self.setFixedWidth(440)
        self.init_ui()

    def init_ui(self):
        self.build_header(
            "توقف موقت جلسه کاری", 
            f"مدت زمان ثبت‌شده: {to_persian_numbers(self.duration_str)} | کلیپ: {self.clip_name}", 
            icon_name="pause", 
            icon_color="#F59E0B"
        )

        self.container_layout.addWidget(QLabel("یادداشت جلسه (تغییرات انجام‌شده، نکات خروجی و...):"))
        self.txt_note = QTextEdit()
        self.txt_note.setFixedHeight(85)
        self.txt_note.setPlaceholderText("یادداشت خود را اینجا بنویسید (اختیاری)...")
        self.txt_note.setStyleSheet("""
            QTextEdit {
                background-color: #161F30;
                border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08);
                border-radius: 6px;
                padding: 8px 10px;
                color: #F8FAFC;
                font-family: 'Vazirmatn';
                font-size: 12px;
                line-height: 1.5;
            }
            QTextEdit:focus {
                border-color: #6366F1;
                background-color: #1A2438;
            }
        """)
        self.container_layout.addWidget(self.txt_note)

        btn_box = QHBoxLayout()
        btn_box.setSpacing(8)

        btn_submit = QPushButton("ثبت و توقف جلسه")
        btn_submit.setFixedHeight(36)
        btn_submit.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        btn_submit.setStyleSheet("""
            QPushButton {
                font-family: 'Vazirmatn';
                background-color: #F59E0B;
                color: #000000;
                font-weight: 800;
                border-radius: 6px;
                border: none;
                font-size: 12px;
                padding: 0 16px;
            }
            QPushButton:hover { background-color: #FBBF24; }
        """)
        btn_submit.clicked.connect(self._submit)
        btn_box.addWidget(btn_submit, stretch=2)

        btn_cancel = QPushButton("انصراف")
        btn_cancel.setFixedHeight(36)
        btn_cancel.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        btn_cancel.setStyleSheet("""
            QPushButton {
                font-family: 'Vazirmatn';
                background-color: #161F30;
                color: #94A3B8;
                border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08);
                border-radius: 6px;
                font-size: 12px;
                font-weight: 600;
                padding: 0 14px;
            }
            QPushButton:hover { background-color: #1E283D; color: #F8FAFC; }
        """)
        btn_cancel.clicked.connect(self.reject)
        btn_box.addWidget(btn_cancel, stretch=1)

        self.container_layout.addLayout(btn_box)

    def _submit(self):
        self.session_note = self.txt_note.toPlainText().strip()
        self.accept()

from datetime import datetime
from PySide6.QtWidgets import (
    QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout, QLabel, QLineEdit, QComboBox, 
    QTextEdit, QPushButton, QFrame, QSizePolicy

class FocusStatusSwitchBlock(QFrame):
    """باکس برجسته و چشم‌گیر انتخاب وضعیت کلیپ برای جلب توجه مستقیم کاربر بدون آیکون"""
    valueChanged = Signal(bool)

    def __init__(self, is_complete: bool = False, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.is_complete = is_complete
        self.setLayoutDirection(Qt.LayoutDirection.RightToLeft)
        self.setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy.Policy.Expanding, QSizePolicy.Fixed)
        self.init_ui()

    def init_ui(self):
        self.layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        self.layout.setContentsMargins(10, 10, 10, 10)
        self.layout.setSpacing(8)

        # نوار دکمه‌های انتخاب سگمنتی
        btn_row = QHBoxLayout()
        btn_row.setSpacing(6)

        self.btn_continue = QPushButton("ادامه جلسه و کار روی کلیپ")
        self.btn_continue.setFixedHeight(36)
        self.btn_continue.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        self.btn_continue.clicked.connect(lambda: self._set_state(False))
        btn_row.addWidget(self.btn_continue, stretch=1)

        self.btn_complete = QPushButton("پایان کار و تکمیل کلیپ")
        self.btn_complete.setFixedHeight(36)
        self.btn_complete.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        self.btn_complete.clicked.connect(lambda: self._set_state(True))
        btn_row.addWidget(self.btn_complete, stretch=1)

        self.layout.addLayout(btn_row)

        # متن راهنمای پویا متناسب با انتخاب کاربر
        self.lbl_feedback = QLabel()
        self.lbl_feedback.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        self.lbl_feedback.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 11px; font-weight: 600; border: none; background: transparent;")
        self.layout.addWidget(self.lbl_feedback)

        self._update_ui_state()

    def _set_state(self, is_complete: bool):
        if self.is_complete != is_complete:
            self.is_complete = is_complete
            self._update_ui_state()
            self.valueChanged.emit(self.is_complete)

    def _update_ui_state(self):
        if self.is_complete:
            self.setStyleSheet("""
                FocusStatusSwitchBlock {
                    background-color: rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.08);
                    border: 1.5px solid #10B981;
                    border-radius: 10px;
                }
            """)
            self.btn_continue.setStyleSheet("""
                QPushButton {
                    font-family: 'Vazirmatn';
                    font-size: 11.5px;
                    font-weight: 500;
                    color: #94A3B8;
                    background-color: #121826;
                    border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08);
                    border-radius: 6px;
                }
                QPushButton:hover { background-color: #161F30; color: #F8FAFC; }
            """)
            self.btn_complete.setStyleSheet("""
                QPushButton {
                    font-family: 'Vazirmatn';
                    font-size: 12px;
                    font-weight: 800;
                    color: #FFFFFF;
                    background-color: #10B981;
                    border: none;
                    border-radius: 6px;
                }
            """)
            self.lbl_feedback.setText("کلیپ ۱۰۰٪ تکمیل می‌شود و تایمر آن قفل خواهد شد.")
            self.lbl_feedback.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 11px; font-weight: 700; color: #10B981; border: none; background: transparent;")
        else:
            self.setStyleSheet("""
                FocusStatusSwitchBlock {
                    background-color: rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.08);
                    border: 1.5px solid rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.5);
                    border-radius: 10px;
                }
            """)
            self.btn_continue.setStyleSheet("""
                QPushButton {
                    font-family: 'Vazirmatn';
                    font-size: 12px;
                    font-weight: 800;
                    color: #FFFFFF;
                    background-color: #6366F1;
                    border: none;
                    border-radius: 6px;
                }
            """)
            self.btn_complete.setStyleSheet("""
                QPushButton {
                    font-family: 'Vazirmatn';
                    font-size: 11.5px;
                    font-weight: 500;
                    color: #94A3B8;
                    background-color: #121826;
                    border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08);
                    border-radius: 6px;
                }
                QPushButton:hover { background-color: #161F30; color: #F8FAFC; }
            """)
            self.lbl_feedback.setText("کلیپ باز می‌ماند و امکان ادامه ثبت زمان با تایمر وجود دارد.")
            self.lbl_feedback.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 11px; font-weight: 600; color: #818CF8; border: none; background: transparent;")


class ManualTimeEntryDialog(StudioBaseDialog):
    def __init__(self, initial_project_id: int = None, initial_clip_id: int = None, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent=parent)
        self.initial_project_id = initial_project_id
        self.initial_clip_id = initial_clip_id
        self.setFixedWidth(460)
        self.init_ui()

    def init_ui(self):
        self.build_header("ثبت زمان کاری دستی", "ثبت سوابق زمان کار تدوین و اعمال به تایمر کلیپ", icon_name="edit", icon_color="#6366F1")

        # ۱. انتخاب پروژه
        self.container_layout.addWidget(QLabel("انتخاب پروژه:"))
        self.combo_project = QComboBox()
        self.combo_project.setFixedHeight(36)
        self.combo_project.currentIndexChanged.connect(self._load_clips_for_project)
        self.container_layout.addWidget(self.combo_project)

        # ۲. انتخاب کلیپ
        self.container_layout.addWidget(QLabel("انتخاب کلیپ:"))
        self.combo_clip = QComboBox()
        self.combo_clip.setFixedHeight(36)
        self.container_layout.addWidget(self.combo_clip)

        # ۳. مدت زمان و مرحله کاری
        row = QHBoxLayout()
        row.setSpacing(10)

        col1 = QVBoxLayout()
        col1.setSpacing(3)
        col1.addWidget(QLabel("مدت زمان به دقیقه:*"))
        self.inp_minutes = QLineEdit()
        self.inp_minutes.setFixedHeight(36)
        self.inp_minutes.setPlaceholderText("مثال: 90")
        col1.addWidget(self.inp_minutes)
        row.addLayout(col1, stretch=1)

        col2 = QVBoxLayout()
        col2.setSpacing(3)
        col2.addWidget(QLabel("مرحله کاری (اختیاری):"))
        self.inp_stage = QLineEdit()
        self.inp_stage.setFixedHeight(36)
        self.inp_stage.setPlaceholderText("مثال: راف‌کات، اصلاح رنگ...")
        col2.addWidget(self.inp_stage)
        row.addLayout(col2, stretch=1)
        
        self.container_layout.addLayout(row)

        # ۴. باکس چشم‌گیر وضعیت کلیپ
        self.container_layout.addWidget(QLabel("تعیین وضعیت کلیپ پس از ثبت زمان:*"))
        self.status_block = FocusStatusSwitchBlock(is_complete=False, parent=self)
        self.container_layout.addWidget(self.status_block)

        # ۵. یادداشت فعالیت
        self.container_layout.addWidget(QLabel("یادداشت فعالیت (اختیاری):"))
        self.txt_note = QTextEdit()
        self.txt_note.setFixedHeight(50)
        self.txt_note.setStyleSheet("background-color: #161F30; border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.08); border-radius: 6px; padding: 6px; color: #F8FAFC; font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 11.5px;")
        self.container_layout.addWidget(self.txt_note)

        # ۶. دکمه‌های اقدام
        btn_box = QHBoxLayout()
        btn_box.setSpacing(8)

        btn_save = QPushButton("ثبت فعالیت دستی")
        btn_save.setFixedHeight(38)
        btn_save.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        btn_save.setStyleSheet("""
            QPushButton {
                font-family: 'Vazirmatn';
                background-color: #6366F1;
                color: #FFFFFF;
                font-weight: bold;
                border-radius: 6px;
                border: none;
                font-size: 12px;
            }
            QPushButton:hover { background-color: #4F46E5; }
        """)
        btn_save.clicked.connect(self._submit)
        btn_box.addWidget(btn_save, stretch=2)

        btn_cancel = QPushButton("انصراف")
        btn_cancel.setFixedHeight(38)
        btn_cancel.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        btn_cancel.setStyleSheet("""
            QPushButton {
                font-family: 'Vazirmatn';
                background-color: #161F30;
                color: #94A3B8;
                border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.08);
                border-radius: 6px;
                font-size: 12px;
            }
            QPushButton:hover { background-color: #1E283D; color: #F8FAFC; }
        """)
        btn_cancel.clicked.connect(self.reject)
        btn_box.addWidget(btn_cancel, stretch=1)

        self.container_layout.addLayout(btn_box)
        self._load_projects()

    def _load_projects(self):
        self.combo_project.blockSignals(True)
        self.combo_project.clear()
        with db.get_session() as session:
            projs = session.scalars(select(Project).where(Project.is_archived == False).order_by(Project.created_at.desc())).all()
            for p in projs:
                self.combo_project.addItem(p.couple_names or p.title, p.id)

        if self.initial_project_id:
            idx = self.combo_project.findData(self.initial_project_id)
            if idx >= 0:
                self.combo_project.setCurrentIndex(idx)

        self.combo_project.blockSignals(False)
        self._load_clips_for_project()

    def _load_clips_for_project(self):
        self.combo_clip.clear()
        pid = self.combo_project.currentData()
        if not pid:
            return
        with db.get_session() as session:
            clips = session.scalars(select(ProjectClipItem).where(ProjectClipItem.project_id == pid)).all()
            for c in clips:
                label = f"{c.clip_name} {'(تکمیل شده)' if c.is_completed else ''}"
                self.combo_clip.addItem(label, c.id)

        if self.initial_clip_id:
            idx = self.combo_clip.findData(self.initial_clip_id)
            if idx >= 0:
                self.combo_clip.setCurrentIndex(idx)

    def _submit(self):
        pid = self.combo_project.currentData()
        cid = self.combo_clip.currentData()
        mins_str = normalize_to_english_digits(self.inp_minutes.text().strip())

        if not pid or not mins_str.isdigit() or int(mins_str) <= 0:
            StudioConfirmDialog.ask(self, "خطا", "لطفاً مدت زمان معتبری به دقیقه وارد کنید.", icon_name="alert")
            return

        is_complete_selected = self.status_block.is_complete
        dur_seconds = int(mins_str) * 60
        stage = self.inp_stage.text().strip() or "Editing"
        note = self.txt_note.toPlainText().strip()

        with db.get_session() as session:
            sess = WorkSession(
                project_id=pid,
                clip_id=cid,
                start_time=datetime.utcnow(),
                end_time=datetime.utcnow(),
                active_duration=dur_seconds,
                paused_duration=0,
                idle_duration=0,
                total_duration=dur_seconds,
                stage_name=stage,
                status=SessionStatusEnum.COMPLETED,
                note=note,
                is_manual=True,
                is_clip_completed=is_complete_selected
            )
            session.add(sess)

            if is_complete_selected and cid:
                clip = session.get(ProjectClipItem, cid)
                if clip:
                    clip.is_completed = True
                    clip.completed_at = datetime.utcnow()
                    session.flush()
                    global_signals.clip_status_toggled.emit(pid, cid)

            session.commit()

        global_signals.session_ended.emit(0)
        global_signals.project_updated.emit(pid)
        global_signals.show_toast.emit("زمان کاری دستی با موفقیت ثبت شد.", "success")
        self.accept()

# FILE: time_tracking/widgets/productivity_chart.py
# ==================================================
from typing import List
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from PySide6.QtWidgets import QWidget, QFrame, QVBoxLayout, QLabel
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt, QRectF
from PySide6.QtGui import QPainter, QColor, QBrush, QFont
from sqlalchemy import select

from database.db_manager import db
from time_tracking.models import WorkSession
from core.persian_utils import to_persian_numbers


class HourlyActivityBarWidget(QWidget):
    """رندر ساعات شبانه‌روز (از ۸ صبح تا ۱۲ شب) و نمایش دوره‌های کاری فعال"""
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.setFixedHeight(85)
        self.hourly_active_minutes = [0] * 16  # ساعات 08:00 تا 24:00

    def set_data(self, hourly_minutes: List[int]):
        self.hourly_active_minutes = hourly_minutes
        self.update()

    def paintEvent(self, event):
        painter = QPainter(self)
        painter.setRenderHint(QPainter.RenderHint.Antialiasing, True)

        w = float(self.width())
        h = float(self.height())
        margin_b = 24.0
        chart_h = h - margin_b

        slots = 16
        slot_w = w / slots

        for i in range(slots):
            hour = 8 + i
            mins = self.hourly_active_minutes[i] if i < len(self.hourly_active_minutes) else 0
            ratio = min(1.0, mins / 60.0)

            bar_w = slot_w * 0.65
            bar_x = (i * slot_w) + ((slot_w - bar_w) / 2)
            bar_h = max(4.0, chart_h * ratio) if mins > 0 else 4.0
            bar_y = chart_h - bar_h

            color = QColor("#6366F1") if mins > 0 else QColor("#1A2234")
            painter.setPen(Qt.PenStyle.NoPen)
            painter.setBrush(QBrush(color))
            painter.drawRoundedRect(QRectF(bar_x, bar_y, bar_w, bar_h), 3, 3)

            # برچسب ساعت
            painter.setFont(QFont("Segoe UI", 9, QFont.Weight.Bold))
            painter.setPen(QColor("#64748B"))
            painter.drawText(QRectF(i * slot_w, h - 20, slot_w, 18), Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter, f"{hour:02d}")

        painter.end()


class ProductivityChartCard(QFrame):
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.setObjectName("ProductivityChartCard")
        self.setLayoutDirection(Qt.LayoutDirection.RightToLeft)
        self.setStyleSheet("""
            QFrame#ProductivityChartCard {
                background-color: #121826;
                border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08);
                border-radius: 12px;
            }
        """)
        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        layout.setContentsMargins(18, 14, 18, 14)
        layout.setSpacing(8)

        lbl_t = QLabel("توزیع زمان فعالیت در طول روز:")
        lbl_t.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 13px; font-weight: 800; color: #F8FAFC; border: none; background: transparent;")
        layout.addWidget(lbl_t)

        self.chart = HourlyActivityBarWidget(self)
        layout.addWidget(self.chart)

    def refresh_data(self):
        today_start = datetime.utcnow().replace(hour=0, minute=0, second=0, microsecond=0)
        hourly_data = [0] * 16

        with db.get_session() as session:
            sessions = session.scalars(
                select(WorkSession).where(WorkSession.start_time >= today_start)
            ).all()

            for s in sessions:
                st = s.start_time
                dur_mins = (s.active_duration or 0) // 60
                if 8 <= st.hour < 24:
                    idx = st.hour - 8
                    hourly_data[idx] = min(60, hourly_data[idx] + dur_mins)

        self.chart.set_data(hourly_data)

# ==================================================
from PySide6.QtWidgets import (
    QFrame, QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout, QLabel, 
    QProgressBar, QSizePolicy
)
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt, QVariantAnimation, QEasingCurve
from core.signals import global_signals
from core.persian_utils import to_persian_numbers


class HardwareMeterTile(QFrame):
    """تایل مانیتورینگ منبع سخت‌افزاری همراه با نمایش دقیق مدل قطعه در زیر نوار پیشرفت"""
    def __init__(self, title: str, accent_color: str, default_model: str = "...", parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.title = title
        self.accent_color = accent_color
        self.current_pct = 0
        self.setLayoutDirection(Qt.LayoutDirection.RightToLeft)
        self.setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy.Policy.Expanding, QSizePolicy.Preferred)
        self.setFixedHeight(74)

        self.setStyleSheet(f"""
            QFrame {{
                background-color: #161F30;
                border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.06);
                border-radius: 8px;
            }}
            QFrame:hover {{
                border-color: {self.accent_color}55;
                background-color: #1A2438;
            }}
            QLabel {{
                background: transparent;
                border: none;
                font-family: 'Vazirmatn';
            }}
        """)
        self.init_ui(default_model)

    def init_ui(self, default_model: str):
        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        layout.setContentsMargins(10, 8, 10, 8)
        layout.setSpacing(3)
        layout.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignVCenter)

        # ردیف عنوان و درصد
        top_row = QHBoxLayout()
        top_row.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        
        lbl_t = QLabel(self.title)
        lbl_t.setStyleSheet("font-size: 10.5px; font-weight: 600; color: #94A3B8;")
        top_row.addWidget(lbl_t)
        top_row.addStretch()

        self.lbl_val = QLabel("۰٪")
        self.lbl_val.setStyleSheet(f"font-family: 'Segoe UI', 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 12.5px; font-weight: 800; color: {self.accent_color};")
        top_row.addWidget(self.lbl_val)

        layout.addLayout(top_row)

        # نوار باریک پیشرفت
        self.pb = QProgressBar()
        self.pb.setRange(0, 100)
        self.pb.setValue(0)
        self.pb.setFixedHeight(4)
        self.pb.setTextVisible(False)
        self.pb.setStyleSheet(f"""
            QProgressBar {{
                background-color: #0E131F;
                border: none;
                border-radius: 2px;
            }}
            QProgressBar::chunk {{
                background: qlineargradient(x1:0, y1:0, x2:1, y2:0, stop:0 {self.accent_color}88, stop:1 {self.accent_color});
                border-radius: 2px;
            }}
        """)
        layout.addWidget(self.pb)

        # نمایش مدل دقیق سخت‌افزار در زیر خط پیشرفت
        self.lbl_model = QLabel(default_model)
        self.lbl_model.setStyleSheet("font-family: -apple-system, 'Segoe UI', 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 9.5px; font-weight: 600; color: #64748B;")
        self.lbl_model.setWordWrap(False)
        layout.addWidget(self.lbl_model)

    def set_model_name(self, name: str):
        if name and self.lbl_model.text() != name:
            self.lbl_model.setText(name)
            self.lbl_model.setToolTip(name)

    def set_value_smooth(self, target_val: int):
        target_val = max(0, min(100, target_val))
        if abs(target_val - self.current_pct) < 2 and target_val != 0:
            return

        self.anim = QVariantAnimation(self)
        self.anim.setDuration(400)
        self.anim.setEasingCurve(QEasingCurve.Type.OutCubic)
        self.anim.setStartValue(self.current_pct)
        self.anim.setEndValue(target_val)
        self.anim.valueChanged.connect(self._on_anim_step)
        self.anim.start()
        self.current_pct = target_val

    def _on_anim_step(self, val):
        v = int(val)
        self.pb.setValue(v)
        self.lbl_val.setText(f"{to_persian_numbers(v)}٪")


class SystemMonitorCard(QFrame):

        global_signals.system_activity_updated.connect(self.update_metrics)

    def init_ui(self):
        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        layout.setContentsMargins(14, 12, 14, 12)
        layout.setSpacing(8)

        # ۱. هدر وضعیت و نام برنامه فعال
        top_row = QHBoxLayout()

        top_row.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignVCenter)

        self.lbl_status_pill = QLabel("● تدوین فعال")
        self.lbl_status_pill.setStyleSheet("""
            font-size: 11px;
            font-weight: 700;
            color: #10B981;
            background: rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.12);
            border: 1px solid rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.3);
            border-radius: 6px;
            padding: 3px 10px;
        """)
        top_row.addWidget(self.lbl_status_pill)
        layout.addLayout(top_row)

        div = QFrame()
        div.setFixedHeight(1)
        div.setStyleSheet("background-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05);")
        layout.addWidget(div)

        # ۲. تایل‌های ۳ گانه با مدل سخت‌افزار
        grid_row = QHBoxLayout()
        grid_row.setSpacing(8)

        self.tile_cpu = HardwareMeterTile("پردازنده (CPU)", "#818CF8", "در حال شناسایی...", self)
        self.tile_gpu = HardwareMeterTile("گرافیک (GPU)", "#38BDF8", "در حال شناسایی...", self)
        self.tile_ram = HardwareMeterTile("حافظه (RAM)", "#F59E0B", "در حال شناسایی...", self)

        grid_row.addWidget(self.tile_cpu)
        grid_row.addWidget(self.tile_gpu)
        grid_row.addWidget(self.tile_ram)
        layout.addLayout(grid_row)

        layout.addStretch()

        # ۳. فوتر هوشمند
        self.lbl_auto_pilot = QLabel("⚡ پایشگر هوشمند پایپ‌لاین: آماده تشخیص رندر و مدیریت زمان")
        self.lbl_auto_pilot.setStyleSheet("font-size: 10px; color: #64748B; padding-right: 2px;")
        layout.addWidget(self.lbl_auto_pilot)

    def update_metrics(self, data: dict):
        app = data.get("active_app", "برنامه عمومی")
        self.lbl_app_name.setText(f"🎬 {app}")

        state = data.get("state", "idle")
        is_rendering = data.get("is_rendering", False)

        # تنظیم مشخصات سخت‌افزار ارسالی از ترد مانیتورینگ
        hw_specs = data.get("hardware_specs", {})
        if hw_specs:
            self.tile_cpu.set_model_name(hw_specs.get("cpu_name", "CPU"))
            self.tile_gpu.set_model_name(hw_specs.get("gpu_name", "GPU"))
            self.tile_ram.set_model_name(hw_specs.get("ram_total_str", "RAM"))

        # وضعیت برچسب
        if state == "editing":
            self.lbl_status_pill.setText("● تدوین فعال")
            self.lbl_status_pill.setStyleSheet("color: #10B981; background: rgba(16,185,129,0.12); border: 1px solid rgba(16,185,129,0.3); border-radius: 6px; padding: 3px 10px; font-weight: 700;")
        elif state == "processing" or is_rendering:
            self.lbl_status_pill.setText("⚡ رندر / پردازش")
            self.lbl_status_pill.setStyleSheet("color: #38BDF8; background: rgba(56,189,248,0.12); border: 1px solid rgba(56,189,248,0.3); border-radius: 6px; padding: 3px 10px; font-weight: 700;")
        elif state in ["outside_editing", "distraction"]:
            self.lbl_status_pill.setText("○ سایر برنامه‌ها")
            self.lbl_status_pill.setStyleSheet("color: #F59E0B; background: rgba(245,158,11,0.12); border: 1px solid rgba(245,158,11,0.3); border-radius: 6px; padding: 3px 10px; font-weight: 600;")
        else:
            self.lbl_status_pill.setText("⏳ آماده‌به‌کار")
            self.lbl_status_pill.setStyleSheet("color: #94A3B8; background: #161F30; border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.08); border-radius: 6px; padding: 3px 10px;")

        # مقداردهی درصدهای زنده با انیمیشن ملایم
        cpu = int(data.get("cpu_percent", 0))
        gpu = int(data.get("gpu_percent", 0))
        ram = int(data.get("ram_percent", 0))

        self.tile_cpu.set_value_smooth(cpu)
        self.tile_gpu.set_value_smooth(gpu)
        self.tile_ram.set_value_smooth(ram)

import sys
from pathlib import Path
from PySide6.QtWidgets import (
    QFrame, QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout, QLabel, QPushButton, 
    QSizePolicy, QGraphicsOpacityEffect, QWidget
)
from PySide6.QtGui import QFontDatabase, QPainter, QColor, QFont
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt, QPropertyAnimation, QEasingCurve, Property, QRectF
from time_tracking.timer_engine import TimeTrackingEngine
from core.signals import global_signals


def format_seconds_hms(seconds: int) -> str:
    h = seconds // 3600
    m = (seconds % 3600) // 60
    s = seconds % 60
    return f"{h:02d}:{m:02d}:{s:02d}"


class RollingDigitSlot(QWidget):
    def __init__(self, font_name: str, font_size: int = 24, text_color: str = "#FFFFFF", parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.font_name = font_name
        self.font_size = font_size
        self.text_color_hex = text_color
        self.current_char = "0"
        self.next_char = "0"
        self._progress = 1.0

        self.setFixedSize(28, 44)
        self.anim = QPropertyAnimation(self, b"progress", self)
        self.anim.setDuration(240)
        self.anim.setEasingCurve(QEasingCurve.Type.OutCubic)

    def get_progress(self) -> float:
        return self._progress

    def set_progress(self, val: float):
        self._progress = val
        self.update()

    progress = Property(float, get_progress, set_progress)

    def set_char(self, new_char: str, animate: bool = True):
        if self.current_char == new_char:
            return
        if not animate:
            self.current_char = new_char
            self.next_char = new_char
            self._progress = 1.0
            self.update()
            return

        self.next_char = new_char
        self.anim.stop()
        self.anim.setStartValue(0.0)
        self.anim.setEndValue(1.0)
        self.anim.start()

    def set_color(self, color_hex: str):
        self.text_color_hex = color_hex
        self.update()

    def paintEvent(self, event):
        painter = QPainter(self)
        painter.setRenderHint(QPainter.RenderHint.Antialiasing, True)
        painter.setRenderHint(QPainter.RenderHint.TextAntialiasing, True)

        w = float(self.width())
        h = float(self.height())

        slot_rect = QRectF(0, 0, w, h)
        painter.setPen(QColor(255, 255, 255, 15))
        painter.setBrush(QColor("#0B101D"))
        painter.drawRoundedRect(slot_rect, 6, 6)

        painter.setClipRect(slot_rect)
        f = QFont(self.font_name, self.font_size, QFont.Weight.Bold)
        painter.setFont(f)
        painter.setPen(QColor(self.text_color_hex))

        if self._progress < 1.0:
            offset_curr = self._progress * h
            painter.drawText(QRectF(0, offset_curr, w, h), Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter, self.current_char)
            offset_next = -h + (self._progress * h)
            painter.drawText(QRectF(0, offset_next, w, h), Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter, self.next_char)
        else:
            self.current_char = self.next_char
            painter.drawText(slot_rect, Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter, self.current_char)


class TimerControlCard(QFrame):
    def __init__(self, on_pause_cb, on_finish_clip_cb, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.on_pause_cb = on_pause_cb

        self.engine = TimeTrackingEngine.instance()

        self.project_title = "پروژه‌ای انتخاب نشده"
        self.studio_title = "—"
        self.clip_title = "کلیپ انتخاب نشده"
        self.is_clip_locked = False
        self.accumulated_spent_seconds = 0
        self.digital_font_name = self._load_digital_font()
        self.colon_visible = True

        self.setObjectName("TimerHeroCard")
        self.setLayoutDirection(Qt.LayoutDirection.RightToLeft)
        self.setFixedHeight(195)
        self.setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy.Policy.Expanding, QSizePolicy.Policy.Fixed)
        self.init_ui()
        self._setup_pulse_animations()
        self._connect_signals()
        self.update_display_state()

    def _load_digital_font(self) -> str:
        try:
            if hasattr(sys, '_MEIPASS'):
                base_dir = Path(sys._MEIPASS)
            else:
                base_dir = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent.parent

            possible_paths = [
                base_dir / "fonts" / "DSEG7Classic-Regular.ttf",
                base_dir / "assets" / "fonts" / "DSEG7Classic-Regular.ttf",
                Path("fonts/DSEG7Classic-Regular.ttf").resolve(),
                Path("assets/fonts/DSEG7Classic-Regular.ttf").resolve(),
            ]

            for font_path in possible_paths:
                if font_path.exists():
                    font_id = QFontDatabase.addApplicationFont(str(font_path))
                    if font_id != -1:
                        families = QFontDatabase.applicationFontFamilies(font_id)
                        if families:
                            return families[0]
        except Exception:
            pass

        return "DSEG7 Classic"

    def _setup_pulse_animations(self):
        self.opacity_effect = QGraphicsOpacityEffect(self.lbl_status_badge)
        self.lbl_status_badge.setGraphicsEffect(self.opacity_effect)
        self.opacity_effect.setOpacity(1.0)

        self.pulse_anim = QPropertyAnimation(self.opacity_effect, b"opacity", self)
        self.pulse_anim.setDuration(1200)
        self.pulse_anim.setStartValue(1.0)
        self.pulse_anim.setKeyValueAt(0.5, 0.35)
        self.pulse_anim.setEndValue(1.0)
        self.pulse_anim.setEasingCurve(QEasingCurve.Type.InOutSine)
        self.pulse_anim.setLoopCount(-1)

    def _render_timer_display(self, total_seconds: int):
        h = total_seconds // 3600
        m = (total_seconds % 3600) // 60
        s = total_seconds % 60

        str_h = f"{h:02d}"
        str_m = f"{m:02d}"
        str_s = f"{s:02d}"

        animate = (self.engine.is_running and not self.engine.is_paused)

        if self.is_clip_locked:
            color_hm = "#10B981"
            color_s = "#059669"
            colon_c = "#10B981"
        elif not self.engine.is_running:
            color_hm = "#64748B"
            color_s = "#475569"
            colon_c = "#475569"
        elif self.engine.is_paused:
            color_hm = "#F8FAFC"
            color_s = "#F59E0B"
            colon_c = "#F59E0B"
        else:
            color_hm = "#FFFFFF"
            color_s = "#FBBF24"
            colon_c = "#FBBF24" if self.colon_visible else "rgba(251,191,36,0.2)"
            self.colon_visible = not self.colon_visible

        self.slot_h1.set_color(color_hm)
        self.slot_h2.set_color(color_hm)
        self.slot_m1.set_color(color_hm)
        self.slot_m2.set_color(color_hm)
        self.slot_s1.set_color(color_s)
        self.slot_s2.set_color(color_s)

        self.slot_h1.set_char(str_h[0], animate=animate)
        self.slot_h2.set_char(str_h[1], animate=animate)
        self.slot_m1.set_char(str_m[0], animate=animate)
        self.slot_m2.set_char(str_m[1], animate=animate)
        self.slot_s1.set_char(str_s[0], animate=animate)
        self.slot_s2.set_char(str_s[1], animate=animate)

        self.lbl_colon1.setStyleSheet(f"font-family:'{self.digital_font_name}', monospace; font-size:24px; font-weight:bold; color:{colon_c}; background:transparent;")
        self.lbl_colon2.setStyleSheet(f"font-family:'{self.digital_font_name}', monospace; font-size:24px; font-weight:bold; color:{colon_c}; background:transparent;")

    def _connect_signals(self):
        global_signals.timer_tick.connect(self._on_timer_tick)
        global_signals.session_started.connect(lambda _: self.update_display_state())
        global_signals.session_ended.connect(lambda _: self.update_display_state())
        global_signals.session_paused.connect(lambda _: self.update_display_state())
        global_signals.session_resumed.connect(lambda _: self.update_display_state())

    def set_context(self, project_title: str, studio_title: str, clip_title: str, is_completed: bool = False, accumulated_seconds: int = 0):
        self.project_title = project_title
        self.studio_title = studio_title
        self.clip_title = clip_title
        self.is_clip_locked = is_completed
        self.accumulated_spent_seconds = accumulated_seconds
        self._update_context_label()
        self.update_display_state()

    def _update_context_label(self):
        if self.engine.is_running:
            self.lbl_context.setText(f"پروژه: <b style='color:#F8FAFC;'>{self.project_title}</b> | کلیپ: <span style='color:#818CF8;'>{self.clip_title}</span> <span style='color:#64748B;'>({self.studio_title})</span>")
        else:
            self.lbl_context.setText(f"پروژه انتخابی: <b style='color:#F8FAFC;'>{self.project_title}</b> | کلیپ: <span style='color:#818CF8;'>{self.clip_title}</span>")

    def _on_timer_tick(self, snap: dict):
        total_time_to_display = self.accumulated_spent_seconds + snap["active_seconds"]
        self._render_timer_display(total_time_to_display)

    def update_display_state(self):
        self._update_context_label()

        if self.is_clip_locked:
            self.pulse_anim.stop()
            self.opacity_effect.setOpacity(1.0)
            self.btn_start.setVisible(True)
            self.btn_start.setEnabled(False)
            self.btn_start.setText("کلیپ تکمیل شده (قفل)")
            self.btn_pause.setVisible(False)
            self.btn_resume.setVisible(False)
            self.btn_finish_clip.setVisible(False)
            self.lbl_status_badge.setText("✓ کلیپ تکمیل و قفل شده")
            self.lbl_status_badge.setStyleSheet("""
                font-family: 'Vazirmatn';
                font-size: 11px;
                font-weight: 700;
                color: #10B981;
                background-color: rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.15);
                border: 1px solid rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.35);
                border-radius: 13px;
                padding: 0 14px;
            """)
            self._render_timer_display(self.accumulated_spent_seconds)
            return

        self.btn_start.setEnabled(True)
        self.btn_start.setText("شروع جلسه کاری")

        if self.engine.is_running:
            self.btn_start.setVisible(False)
            self.btn_finish_clip.setVisible(True)

            if self.engine.is_paused:
                self.btn_pause.setVisible(False)
                self.btn_resume.setVisible(True)

                self.pulse_anim.stop()
                self.opacity_effect.setOpacity(1.0)

                self.lbl_status_badge.setText("توقف موقت")
                self.lbl_status_badge.setStyleSheet("""
                    font-family: 'Vazirmatn';
                    font-size: 11px;
                    font-weight: 700;
                    color: #F59E0B;
                    background-color: rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.15);
                    border: 1px solid rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.35);
                    border-radius: 13px;
                    padding: 0 14px;
                """)
                self._render_timer_display(self.accumulated_spent_seconds + self.engine.active_seconds)
            else:
                self.btn_pause.setVisible(True)
                self.btn_resume.setVisible(False)

                self.lbl_status_badge.setText("در حال ثبت زمان")
                self.lbl_status_badge.setStyleSheet("""
                    font-family: 'Vazirmatn';
                    font-size: 11px;
                    font-weight: 800;
                    color: #10B981;
                    background-color: rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.16);
                    border: 1.5px solid #10B981;
                    border-radius: 13px;
                    padding: 0 14px;
                """)
                self._render_timer_display(self.accumulated_spent_seconds + self.engine.active_seconds)

                if self.pulse_anim.state() != QPropertyAnimation.State.Running:
                    self.pulse_anim.start()
        else:
            self.pulse_anim.stop()
            self.opacity_effect.setOpacity(1.0)

            self.btn_start.setVisible(True)
            self.btn_pause.setVisible(False)
            self.btn_resume.setVisible(False)
            self.btn_finish_clip.setVisible(False)

            self.lbl_status_badge.setText("آماده شروع کار")
            self.lbl_status_badge.setStyleSheet("""
                font-family: 'Vazirmatn';
                font-size: 11px;
                font-weight: 700;
                color: #94A3B8;
                background-color: #161F30;
                border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08);
                border-radius: 13px;
                padding: 0 14px;
            """)
            self._render_timer_display(self.accumulated_spent_seconds)

    def _handle_start(self):
        if self.is_clip_locked:
            global_signals.show_toast.emit("این کلیپ تکمیل شده و تایمر آن قفل است.", "warning")
            return
        if not self.engine.current_project_id:
            global_signals.show_toast.emit("لطفاً ابتدا یک پروژه یا کلیپ را انتخاب کنید.", "warning")
            return
        self.engine.start_session(self.engine.current_project_id, self.engine.current_clip_id)

    def _handle_resume(self):
        if self.is_clip_locked:
            global_signals.show_toast.emit("این کلیپ تکمیل شده و تایمر آن قفل است.", "warning")
            return
        self.engine.resume_session()
