        btn_view_proj = QPushButton("پرونده پروژه ←")
        btn_view_proj.setFixedHeight(28)
        btn_view_proj.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        btn_view_proj.setStyleSheet("background: #161F30; color: #818CF8; border: 1px solid rgba(99,102,241,0.3); border-radius: 5px; font-size: 10.5px; padding: 0 10px; font-weight: bold;")
        btn_view_proj.clicked.connect(self._open_project)
        act_row.addWidget(btn_view_proj)

        act_row.addStretch()
        layout.addLayout(act_row)

    def set_project_data(self, p: Optional[Project]):
        if not p:
            self.project_id = None
            self.lbl_proj_title.setText("پروژه فعالی تعریف نشده است")
            self.lbl_studio_name.setText("—")
            self.pb_proj.setValue(0)
            self.lbl_pct.setText("۰٪")
            return

        self.project_id = p.id
        self.lbl_proj_title.setText(p.couple_names or p.title)
        s_name = p.client.company if (p.client and p.client.company) else (p.client.full_name if p.client else "استودیوی عمومی")
        self.lbl_studio_name.setText(f"({s_name})")

        c_cnt, t_cnt, pct = ProjectController.calculate_project_progress(p)
        self.pb_proj.setValue(pct)
        self.lbl_pct.setText(f"{to_persian_numbers(pct)}٪")
        self.lbl_clips_cnt.setText(f"کلیپ‌ها: {to_persian_numbers(c_cnt)} از {to_persian_numbers(t_cnt)}")

        dead_str = gregorian_to_jalali(p.deadline) if p.deadline else "ثبت‌نشده"
        self.lbl_deadline.setText(f"موعد تحویل: {to_persian_numbers(dead_str)}")
        self.lbl_total_fee.setText(f"قرارداد: {format_toman(p.total_fee)}")

        s_info = ProjectController.get_status_info(p)
        self.lbl_status.setText(s_info.get("label", "در حال تدوین"))

        try:
            with db.get_session() as session:
                last_log = session.scalars(
                    select(ProjectEventLog)
                    .where(ProjectEventLog.project_id == p.id)
                    .order_by(ProjectEventLog.created_at.desc())
                    .limit(1)
                ).first()
                if last_log:
                    self.lbl_last_log.setText(f"آخرین رویداد: {last_log.title}")
                else:
                    self.lbl_last_log.setText("آخرین رویداد: ایجاد پرونده")
        except Exception:
            self.lbl_last_log.setText("آخرین رویداد: در حال تدوین")

    def _open_folder(self):
        if self.project_id:
            p = ProjectController.get_project_by_id(self.project_id)
            if p and p.drive_locations and Path(p.drive_locations[0].absolute_path).exists():
                from config import open_path_in_os
                open_path_in_os(p.drive_locations[0].absolute_path)
            else:
                global_signals.show_toast.emit("مسیر پوشه راش‌ها متصل نیست.", "error")

    def _open_project(self):
        if self.project_id:
            global_signals.navigate_and_focus_project.emit(self.project_id)


class TodayInsightsCompactGrid(QFrame):
    """کارت‌های بینش مدیریتی امروز و یادداشت سریع"""
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.setLayoutDirection(Qt.LayoutDirection.RightToLeft)
        self.setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy.Policy.Expanding, QSizePolicy.Preferred)
        self.setStyleSheet("""
            QFrame#InsightsFrame {
                background-color: #121826;
                border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.06);
                border-radius: 8px;
            }
            QLabel { background: transparent; border: none; font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; }
        """)
        self.setObjectName("InsightsFrame")
        self.init_ui()

    def init_ui(self):
        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        layout.setContentsMargins(14, 10, 14, 10)
        layout.setSpacing(6)

        top_row = QHBoxLayout()
        lbl_t = QLabel("💡 بینش‌های مدیریتی امروز")
        lbl_t.setStyleSheet("font-size: 12px; font-weight: 800; color: #10B981;")
        top_row.addWidget(lbl_t)
        top_row.addStretch()

        btn_quick_note = QPushButton("+ یادداشت سریع")
        btn_quick_note.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        btn_quick_note.setStyleSheet("""
            QPushButton {
                font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 10.5px; font-weight: bold; color: #A5B4FC;
                background-color: rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.12); border: 1px solid rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.25);
                border-radius: 5px; padding: 3px 8px;
            }
            QPushButton:hover { background-color: #6366F1; color: #FFFFFF; }
        """)
        btn_quick_note.clicked.connect(self._open_quick_note_modal)
        top_row.addWidget(btn_quick_note)
        layout.addLayout(top_row)

        self.grid = QGridLayout()
        self.grid.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        self.grid.setHorizontalSpacing(8)
        self.grid.setVerticalSpacing(6)

        self.cell_time = self._create_tile("زمان خالص تدوین", "۰:۰۰", "#10B981")
        self.cell_peak = self._create_tile("ساعات اوج سرعت", "۱۶ تا ۱۹", "#818CF8")
        self.cell_debts = self._create_tile("مطالبات وصول‌نشده", "۰ تومان", "#F59E0B")
        self.cell_pace = self._create_tile("پایپ‌لاین هفتگی", "تارگت ۳۶ ساعت", "#38BDF8")

        self.grid.addWidget(self.cell_time["frame"], 0, 0)
        self.grid.addWidget(self.cell_peak["frame"], 0, 1)
        self.grid.addWidget(self.cell_debts["frame"], 1, 0)
        self.grid.addWidget(self.cell_pace["frame"], 1, 1)

        layout.addLayout(self.grid)

    def _create_tile(self, title: str, val: str, col: str) -> dict:
        frame = QFrame()
        frame.setStyleSheet("background-color: #161F30; border-radius: 6px; border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.04);")
        lay = QVBoxLayout(frame)
        lay.setContentsMargins(10, 6, 10, 6)
        lay.setSpacing(2)
        lay.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)

        lbl_k = QLabel(title)
        lbl_k.setStyleSheet("font-size: 10px; color: #94A3B8;")
        lay.addWidget(lbl_k)

        lbl_v = QLabel(val)
        lbl_v.setStyleSheet(f"font-size: 12px; font-weight: 800; color: {col};")
        lay.addWidget(lbl_v)

        return {"frame": frame, "val_lbl": lbl_v, "key_lbl": lbl_k}

    def _open_quick_note_modal(self):
        settings = SettingsManager.load_settings()
        cur_text = settings.get("copilot_scratchpad_text", "")
        text, ok = StudioInputDialog.get_text(
            self.window(), "یادداشت سریع میز کار", "تایم‌کدها یا نکات ضروری تدوین:", initial_text=cur_text, placeholder="تایم‌کدها، نام موزیک و..."
        )
        if ok:
            settings["copilot_scratchpad_text"] = text
            SettingsManager.save_settings(settings)
            global_signals.show_toast.emit("یادداشت با موفقیت ذخیره شد.", "success")

    def update_insights(self, active_hms: str, peak_hours: str, uncollected_toman: str, weekly_summary: str):
        self.cell_time["val_lbl"].setText(to_persian_numbers(active_hms))
        self.cell_peak["val_lbl"].setText(to_persian_numbers(peak_hours))
        self.cell_debts["val_lbl"].setText(uncollected_toman)
        self.cell_pace["val_lbl"].setText(to_persian_numbers(weekly_summary))


class ActivityFeedAndSystemFooter(QFrame):
    """نوار زیرین ترکیبی رویدادها و وضعیت سخت‌افزار"""
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.setLayoutDirection(Qt.LayoutDirection.RightToLeft)
        self.setFixedHeight(48)
        self.setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy.Policy.Expanding, QSizePolicy.Fixed)
        self.setStyleSheet("""
            QFrame#FeedFooter {
                background-color: #0E131F;
                border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05);
                border-radius: 8px;
            }
            QLabel { background: transparent; border: none; font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; }
        """)
        self.setObjectName("FeedFooter")
        self.init_ui()

    def init_ui(self):
        layout = QHBoxLayout(self)
        layout.setContentsMargins(14, 0, 14, 0)
        layout.setSpacing(10)
        layout.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignVCenter)

        lbl_icon = QLabel("🕒")
        lbl_icon.setStyleSheet("font-size: 11px;")
        layout.addWidget(lbl_icon)

        lbl_t = QLabel("آخرین رویدادهای پایپ‌لاین:")
        lbl_t.setStyleSheet("font-size: 11px; font-weight: bold; color: #64748B;")
        layout.addWidget(lbl_t)

        self.lbl_feed_text = QLabel("در حال دریافت تاریخچه فعالیت‌ها...")
        self.lbl_feed_text.setStyleSheet("font-size: 11px; color: #CBD5E1;")
        layout.addWidget(self.lbl_feed_text, stretch=1)

        div = QFrame()
        div.setFrameShape(QFrame.Shape.VLine)
        div.setFixedHeight(20)
        div.setStyleSheet("background-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.06); border: none;")
        layout.addWidget(div)

        self.lbl_sys_status = QLabel("● سیستم پایدار")
        self.lbl_sys_status.setStyleSheet("font-size: 10px; font-weight: bold; color: #10B981;")
        layout.addWidget(self.lbl_sys_status)

        self.lbl_hw_specs = QLabel("CPU: ۰٪  |  GPU: ۰٪  |  RAM: ۰٪")
        self.lbl_hw_specs.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Segoe UI', 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 10px; color: #94A3B8;")
        layout.addWidget(self.lbl_hw_specs)

    def set_feed_items(self, items: List[str]):
        if items:
            self.lbl_feed_text.setText("   •   ".join(items[:2]))
        else:
            self.lbl_feed_text.setText("هنوز رویدادی ثبت نشده است.")

    def update_hardware(self, cpu: int, gpu: int, ram: int, is_rendering: bool):
        if is_rendering:
            self.lbl_sys_status.setText("⚡ رندر فعال")
            self.lbl_sys_status.setStyleSheet("font-size: 10px; font-weight: bold; color: #38BDF8;")
        else:
            self.lbl_sys_status.setText("● سیستم پایدار")
            self.lbl_sys_status.setStyleSheet("font-size: 10px; font-weight: bold; color: #10B981;")

        specs_str = f"CPU: {to_persian_numbers(cpu)}٪  |  GPU: {to_persian_numbers(gpu)}٪  |  RAM: {to_persian_numbers(ram)}٪"
        self.lbl_hw_specs.setText(specs_str)


class CopilotView(QWidget):
    """
    نمای مدرن و بازطراحی‌شده مرکز فرماندهی هوشمند تدوین (Smart Command Center)
    """
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.setLayoutDirection(Qt.LayoutDirection.RightToLeft)
        self.init_ui()
        self._connect_signals()
        self._setup_auto_refresh_timer()
        self.refresh_data()

    def init_ui(self):
        root_layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        root_layout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        root_layout.setSpacing(0)

        scroll = QScrollArea()
        scroll.setWidgetResizable(True)
        scroll.setFrameShape(QFrame.Shape.NoFrame)
        scroll.setStyleSheet("background: transparent; border: none;")
        scroll.setHorizontalScrollBarPolicy(Qt.ScrollBarPolicy.ScrollBarAlwaysOff)

        container = QWidget()
        container.setStyleSheet("background: transparent;")
        self.layout = QVBoxLayout(container)
        self.layout.setContentsMargins(20, 14, 20, 14)
        self.layout.setSpacing(10)
        self.layout.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignTop)

        # ۱. Header
        self.header_bar = CommandHeaderBar(self)
        self.layout.addWidget(self.header_bar)

        # ۲. Today Status & KPIs
        self.overview_bar = TodayOverviewBar(self)
        self.layout.addWidget(self.overview_bar)

        # ۳. Main Copilot Command Center
        self.main_copilot = MainCopilotDecisionCard(self)
        self.layout.addWidget(self.main_copilot)

        # ۴. Priorities / Action Queue
        self.priorities_card = SmartPrioritiesCard(self)
        self.layout.addWidget(self.priorities_card)

        # ۵. Dual Section: Active Project + Today Insights
        mid_grid = QHBoxLayout()
        mid_grid.setSpacing(10)

        self.cockpit_card = ActiveProjectCockpitCard(self)
        mid_grid.addWidget(self.cockpit_card, stretch=3)

        self.insights_grid = TodayInsightsCompactGrid(self)
        mid_grid.addWidget(self.insights_grid, stretch=2)

        self.layout.addLayout(mid_grid)

        # ۶. Bottom Activity Feed & System Mini Status
        self.feed_footer = ActivityFeedAndSystemFooter(self)
        self.layout.addWidget(self.feed_footer)

        scroll.setWidget(container)
        root_layout.addWidget(scroll)

    def _setup_auto_refresh_timer(self):
        self.auto_timer = QTimer(self)
        self.auto_timer.setInterval(20000)
        self.auto_timer.timeout.connect(self.refresh_data)
        self.auto_timer.start()

    def _connect_signals(self):
        trigger = lambda *args: QTimer.singleShot(40, self.refresh_data)
        global_signals.project_created.connect(trigger)
        global_signals.project_updated.connect(trigger)
        global_signals.project_deleted.connect(trigger)
        global_signals.project_archived.connect(trigger)
        global_signals.transaction_recorded.connect(trigger)
        global_signals.session_started.connect(trigger)
        global_signals.session_ended.connect(trigger)
        global_signals.system_activity_updated.connect(self._on_hw_metrics)

    def _on_hw_metrics(self, data: dict):
        cpu = int(data.get("cpu_percent", 0))
        gpu = int(data.get("gpu_percent", 0))
        ram = int(data.get("ram_percent", 0))
        is_rend = data.get("is_rendering", False)
        self.feed_footer.update_hardware(cpu, gpu, ram, is_rend)

    def refresh_data(self):
        settings = SettingsManager.load_settings()
        editor_name = settings.get("editor_name") or "تدوین‌گر"

        prod = CopilotEngine.get_productivity_insights(days_back=14)
        radar = CopilotEngine.get_pipeline_radar()
        rec_proj = CopilotEngine.recommend_next_project()
        fin_data = FinanceController.get_financial_dashboard_data()
        scores = CopilotEngine.get_studio_credit_scores()
        active_projects = ProjectController.get_active_projects()

        now = datetime.now()
        self.header_bar.set_last_sync_time(now.strftime("%H:%M"))

        cards = fin_data.get("summary_cards", {})
        total_contracts = cards.get("total_contracts", 0)
        total_received = cards.get("total_received", 0)
        total_remaining = cards.get("total_remaining", 0)
        fin_pct = int((total_received / total_contracts) * 100) if total_contracts > 0 else 100

        total_clips = sum(len(p.clips) for p in active_projects)
        completed_clips = sum(sum(1 for c in p.clips if c.is_completed) for p in active_projects)
        pipeline_pct = int((completed_clips / total_clips) * 100) if total_clips > 0 else 0

        from time_tracking.timer_engine import TimeTrackingEngine
        today_sec = TimeTrackingEngine.instance().get_today_total_active_seconds()
        t_h = today_sec // 3600
        t_m = (today_sec % 3600) // 60
        time_str = f"{t_h:02d}:{t_m:02d}"

        risks_cnt = radar.get("total_risks_count", 0)
        attention_text = f"امروز {to_persian_numbers(risks_cnt)} موضوع نیازمند توجه است." if risks_cnt > 0 else "پایپ‌لاین در سلامت کامل و بدون ریسک قرار دارد ✓"

        # ۱. بروزرسانی نوار بالایی
        self.overview_bar.update_metrics(
            editor_name=editor_name,
            attention_text=attention_text,
            proj_pct=pipeline_pct,
            fin_pct=fin_pct,
            time_hms=time_str,
            tasks_cnt=risks_cnt
        )

        # ۲. تصمیم‌گیری مغز اصلی Copilot
        if rec_proj:
            insight = f"پروژه «{rec_proj['title']}» آماده شروع تدوین است؛ {rec_proj['recommendation_reason']}."
            why_list = [
                f"استودیو: {rec_proj['studio_name']}",
                f"پیشرفت کلیپ‌ها: {to_persian_numbers(rec_proj['progress_pct'])}٪",
                f"موعد تحویل: {to_persian_numbers(rec_proj['deadline_str'])}",
                "ساعت طلایی بازدهی تدوینگر در حال حاضر فعال است"
            ]
            self.main_copilot.set_content(rec_proj["project_id"], insight, why_list)
        else:
            self.main_copilot.set_content(
                None,
                "تمام پروژه‌های فعال تکمیل شده‌اند یا پروژه‌ای در پایپ‌لاین نیست.",
                ["هیچ اصلاحیه بازی ثبت نشده است", "تمامی راش‌های اولیه تدوین شده‌اند"]
            )

        # ۳. ساخت صف اولویت‌های هوشمند با بج‌های ملایم و دکمه‌های تم استودیو
        p_list = []
        rank_idx = 1

        if radar.get("urgent_deadlines"):
            u = radar["urgent_deadlines"][0]
            p_list.append({
                "rank": f"{rank_idx:02d}", "tag": "فوری", "title": f"ددلاین «{u['title']}»",
                "reason": u["warning"], "action_label": "مشاهده پروژه", "color": "#EF4444",
                "callback": lambda pid=u["id"]: global_signals.navigate_and_focus_project.emit(pid)
            })
            rank_idx += 1

        if rec_proj:
            p_list.append({
                "rank": f"{rank_idx:02d}", "tag": "مهم", "title": f"ادامه تدوین «{rec_proj['title']}»",
                "reason": rec_proj["recommendation_reason"], "action_label": "شروع کات", "color": "#F59E0B",
                "callback": lambda pid=rec_proj["project_id"]: global_signals.navigate_and_focus_project.emit(pid)
            })
            rank_idx += 1

        if total_remaining > 0 and scores:
            bad_client = scores[0]
            p_list.append({
                "rank": f"{rank_idx:02d}", "tag": "پیگیری مالی", "title": f"تسویه مطالبات {bad_client['studio_name']}",
                "reason": f"مانده حساب: {format_toman(bad_client['remaining'])}", "action_label": "دفتر مالی", "color": "#10B981",
                "callback": lambda: global_signals.navigate_to_page.emit(3)
            })
            rank_idx += 1

        self.priorities_card.set_priorities(p_list)

        # ۴. پروژه در دست انجام
        if active_projects:
            active_p = active_projects[0]
            self.cockpit_card.set_project_data(active_p)
        else:
            self.cockpit_card.set_project_data(None)

        # ۵. بینش‌های فشرده مدیریتی
        peak = prod.get("golden_hours_label", "۱۶ تا ۱۹")
        debt_str = format_toman(total_remaining) if total_remaining > 0 else "حساب‌ها تسویه ✓"
        self.insights_grid.update_insights(
            active_hms=f"{t_h} ساعت و {t_m} دقیقه",
            peak_hours=peak,
            uncollected_toman=debt_str,
            weekly_summary="۳۶ ساعت هفتگی"
        )

        # ۶. لاگ‌های اخیر فعالیت
        try:
            with db.get_session() as session:
                logs = session.scalars(select(ProjectEventLog).order_by(ProjectEventLog.created_at.desc()).limit(3)).all()
                feed_items = []
                for l in logs:
                    t_str = l.created_at.strftime("%H:%M") if l.created_at else "--:--"
                    feed_items.append(f"{to_persian_numbers(t_str)} {l.title}")
                self.feed_footer.set_feed_items(feed_items)
        except Exception:
            self.feed_footer.set_feed_items([])

from PySide6.QtWidgets import (
    QWidget, QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout, QLabel, 
    QScrollArea, QFrame, QGridLayout, QPushButton
)
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt, QTimer

from widgets.stat_card import StatCard
from widgets.project_card_mini import ProjectCardMini
from widgets.empty_state import ModernEmptyStateWidget
from widgets.dashboard_welcome_header import DashboardWelcomeHeader
from widgets.media_inspector_widget import MediaInspectorDropZone
from widgets.copilot_briefing_card import CopilotBriefingCard

from core.finance_controller import FinanceController
from core.project_controller import ProjectController
from core.persian_utils import to_persian_numbers
from core.signals import global_signals


class DashboardView(QWidget):
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.setLayoutDirection(Qt.LayoutDirection.RightToLeft)
        self.init_ui()
        self._connect_signals()
        self.refresh_data()

    def init_ui(self):
        main_layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        main_layout.setContentsMargins(28, 16, 28, 16)
        main_layout.setSpacing(12)

        # ۱. سطر ترکیبی اول: خوش‌آمدگویی/ساعت (راست) + دستیار هوشمند خلاصه (چپ)
        top_bar_row = QHBoxLayout()
        top_bar_row.setSpacing(12)

        self.welcome_header = DashboardWelcomeHeader(self)
        top_bar_row.addWidget(self.welcome_header, stretch=1)

        self.copilot_card = CopilotBriefingCard(self)
        self.copilot_card.open_insights_requested.connect(lambda: global_signals.navigate_to_page.emit(7))
        top_bar_row.addWidget(self.copilot_card, stretch=1)

        main_layout.addLayout(top_bar_row)

        # ۲. کارت آنالیزور سریع فوتج (Media Inspector)
        self.media_drop_zone = MediaInspectorDropZone(self)
        main_layout.addWidget(self.media_drop_zone)

        # ۳. کارت‌های شاخص آماری (KPIs)
        self.stats_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        self.stats_layout.setSpacing(10)

        self.card_revenue = StatCard(title="کل درآمد وصول‌شده", initial_value=0, is_currency=True, color_accent="#10B981")
        self.card_pipeline = StatCard(title="ارزش پروژه‌های فعال", initial_value=0, is_currency=True, color_accent="#6366F1")
        self.card_active = StatCard(title="پروژه‌های در حال تدوین", initial_value=0, is_currency=False, color_accent="#38BDF8")
        self.card_reviews = StatCard(title="منتظر تایید / بازبینی", initial_value=0, is_currency=False, color_accent="#F59E0B")

        self.stats_layout.addWidget(self.card_revenue)
        self.stats_layout.addWidget(self.card_pipeline)
        self.stats_layout.addWidget(self.card_active)
        self.stats_layout.addWidget(self.card_reviews)
        main_layout.addLayout(self.stats_layout)

        # ۴. کانتینر اسکرول محتوای پروژه‌ها
        self.scroll_area = QScrollArea()
        self.scroll_area.setWidgetResizable(True)
        self.scroll_area.setFrameShape(QFrame.Shape.NoFrame)
        self.scroll_area.setStyleSheet("QScrollArea { background: transparent; border: none; }")
        self.scroll_area.setHorizontalScrollBarPolicy(Qt.ScrollBarPolicy.ScrollBarAlwaysOff)
        self.scroll_area.setVerticalScrollBarPolicy(Qt.ScrollBarPolicy.ScrollBarAsNeeded)

        self.content_container = QWidget()
        self.content_container.setStyleSheet("background: transparent;")
        self.content_layout = QVBoxLayout(self.content_container)
        self.content_layout.setContentsMargins(0, 4, 0, 10)
        self.content_layout.setSpacing(18)
        self.content_layout.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignTop)

        self.scroll_area.setWidget(self.content_container)
        main_layout.addWidget(self.scroll_area, stretch=1)

    def _connect_signals(self):
        trigger = lambda *args: QTimer.singleShot(0, self.refresh_data)
        global_signals.project_created.connect(trigger)
        global_signals.project_updated.connect(trigger)
        global_signals.stage_changed.connect(trigger)
        global_signals.clip_status_toggled.connect(trigger)
        global_signals.revision_added.connect(trigger)
        global_signals.revision_updated.connect(trigger)
        global_signals.revision_task_toggled.connect(trigger)
        global_signals.project_deleted.connect(trigger)
        global_signals.project_archived.connect(trigger)
        global_signals.project_unarchived.connect(trigger)
        global_signals.transaction_recorded.connect(trigger)
        global_signals.session_started.connect(trigger)
        global_signals.session_ended.connect(trigger)
        global_signals.settings_updated.connect(lambda: self.welcome_header.update_all())

    def refresh_data(self):
        self.welcome_header.update_all()
        if hasattr(self, "copilot_card"):
            self.copilot_card.refresh_briefing()

        metrics = FinanceController.get_dashboard_metrics()
        self.card_revenue.animate_to_value(metrics["total_revenue"])
        self.card_pipeline.animate_to_value(metrics["active_pipeline_value"])
        self.card_active.animate_to_value(metrics["active_projects_count"])
        self.card_reviews.animate_to_value(metrics["review_count"])

        while self.content_layout.count():
            item = self.content_layout.takeAt(0)
            widget = item.widget()
            if widget:
                widget.setParent(None)
                widget.deleteLater()

        projects = ProjectController.get_active_projects()
        if not projects:
            empty_widget = ModernEmptyStateWidget(
                title="هیچ پروژه فعالی در پایپ‌لاین ثبت نشده است",
                description="میز کار شما در حال حاضر خالی است. برای شروع می‌توانید پروژه جدیدی تعریف کنید.",
                button_text="+ تعریف اولین پروژه",
                icon_name="folder"
            )
            empty_widget.action_clicked.connect(lambda: global_signals.navigate_to_page.emit(1))
            self.content_layout.addWidget(empty_widget)
            return

        urgent_projects = []
        review_projects = []
        active_projects = []

        for p in projects:
            active_rev = ProjectController.get_active_revision(p)
            c_cnt, t_cnt, pct = ProjectController.calculate_project_progress(p)
            
            if active_rev:
                urgent_projects.append(p)
            elif t_cnt > 0 and c_cnt == t_cnt:
                review_projects.append(p)
            else:
                active_projects.append(p)

        if urgent_projects:
            self._build_section("⚡ اقدامات فوری و اصلاحیه‌های فعال", urgent_projects, "#EA580C")
        if active_projects:
            self._build_section("🎬 پایپ‌لاین پروژه‌های در حال تدوین", active_projects, "#6366F1")
        if review_projects:
            self._build_section("⏳ منتظر بازبینی استودیو / آماده تحویل نهایی", review_projects, "#F59E0B")

    def _build_section(self, title: str, projects_list: list, accent_color: str):
        sec_widget = QWidget()
        sec_lay = QVBoxLayout(sec_widget)
        sec_lay.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        sec_lay.setSpacing(8)

        sec_header = QHBoxLayout()
        count_str = f"({to_persian_numbers(len(projects_list))} پروژه)"
        lbl_sec = QLabel(f"{title} <span style='font-size: 11px; font-weight: normal; color: #8F909D;'>{count_str}</span>")
        lbl_sec.setStyleSheet(f"font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 13px; font-weight: 800; color: {accent_color}; border: none; background: transparent;")
        sec_header.addWidget(lbl_sec, stretch=1)

        btn_all = QPushButton("مشاهده در پروژه‌ها ←")
        btn_all.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        btn_all.setStyleSheet("""
            QPushButton {
                font-family: 'Vazirmatn';
                background: transparent;
                color: #818CF8;
                border: none;
                font-size: 11px;
                font-weight: 600;
            }
            QPushButton:hover { color: #A5B4FC; }
        """)
        btn_all.clicked.connect(lambda: global_signals.navigate_to_page.emit(1))
        sec_header.addWidget(btn_all)
        sec_lay.addLayout(sec_header)

        grid_widget = QWidget()
        grid = QGridLayout(grid_widget)
        grid.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        grid.setHorizontalSpacing(10)
        grid.setVerticalSpacing(8)
        grid.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignTop)
        grid.setColumnStretch(0, 1)
        grid.setColumnStretch(1, 1)
        grid.setColumnStretch(2, 1)

        for index, p in enumerate(projects_list):
            completed_cnt, total_cnt, pct = ProjectController.calculate_project_progress(p)
            fin = ProjectController.get_project_financial_summary(p)
            active_rev = ProjectController.get_active_revision(p)
            rev_c, rev_t, rev_pct = ProjectController.calculate_revision_progress(active_rev) if active_rev else (0, 0, 0)
            status_info = ProjectController.get_status_info(p)

            clips_data = [
                {
                    "id": c.id,
                    "name": c.clip_name,
                    "is_completed": c.is_completed,
                    "completed_at": c.completed_at
                }
                for c in p.clips
            ]

            p_dict = {
                "id": p.id,
                "project_number": p.project_number or p.id,
                "couple_names": p.couple_names,
                "title": p.title,
                "client_name": p.client.company if (p.client and p.client.company) else (p.client.full_name if p.client else "بدون استودیو"),
                "current_stage": p.current_stage.value if hasattr(p.current_stage, "value") else str(p.current_stage),
                "deadline": p.deadline,
                "event_date": p.event_date,
                "total_fee": fin["total_fee"],
                "remaining_amount": fin["remaining"],
                "is_settled": fin["is_settled"],
                "progress_pct": pct,
                "completed_clips": completed_cnt,
                "total_clips": total_cnt,
                "clips_list": clips_data,
                "active_revision_title": active_rev.title if active_rev else None,
                "rev_progress_pct": rev_pct,
                "rev_completed_tasks": rev_c,
                "rev_total_tasks": rev_t,
                "status_info": status_info
            }
            card = ProjectCardMini(p_dict)
            row = index // 3
            col = index % 3
            grid.addWidget(card, row, col, 1, 1)

        sec_lay.addWidget(grid_widget)
        self.content_layout.addWidget(sec_widget)

class StudioLedgerCard(QFrame):
    """کارت حساب و بدهی کل استودیو"""
    def __init__(self, studio_data: Dict[str, Any], on_settle_callback, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.studio_data = studio_data
        self.on_settle_callback = on_settle_callback
        
        self.setObjectName("FinancialCardItem")
        self.setLayoutDirection(Qt.LayoutDirection.RightToLeft)
        self.setFixedHeight(84)
        self.setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy.Policy.Expanding, QSizePolicy.Policy.Fixed)
        self.init_ui()

    def init_ui(self):
        layout = QHBoxLayout(self)
        layout.setContentsMargins(18, 10, 18, 10)
        layout.setSpacing(16)
        layout.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignVCenter)

        # نام استودیو
        studio_info = QVBoxLayout()
        studio_info.setSpacing(2)
        studio_info.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignVCenter)

        lbl_name = QLabel(self.studio_data.get("studio_name", "استودیو"))
        lbl_name.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 14.5px; font-weight: 800; color: #F8FAFC; border: none; background: transparent;")
        studio_info.addWidget(lbl_name)

        proj_count = self.studio_data.get("projects_count", 0)
        lbl_count = QLabel(f"🏢 {to_persian_numbers(proj_count)} پروژه تحویل‌شده")
        lbl_count.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 11px; color: #94A3B8; border: none; background: transparent;")
        studio_info.addWidget(lbl_count)
        layout.addLayout(studio_info, stretch=2)

        layout.addWidget(self._create_v_divider())

        # قراردادها و دریافتی
        fin_col1 = QVBoxLayout()
        fin_col1.setSpacing(2)
        fin_col1.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignVCenter)
        
        lbl_fee = QLabel(f"مجموع قراردادها: <b style='color:#F8FAFC;'>{format_toman(self.studio_data.get('total_fee', 0))}</b>")
        lbl_fee.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 12px; color: #8F909D; border: none; background: transparent;")
        fin_col1.addWidget(lbl_fee)

        lbl_paid = QLabel(f"کل دریافتی: <b style='color:#10B981;'>{format_toman(self.studio_data.get('total_paid', 0))}</b>")
        lbl_paid.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 12px; color: #8F909D; border: none; background: transparent;")
        fin_col1.addWidget(lbl_paid)
        layout.addLayout(fin_col1, stretch=2)

        layout.addWidget(self._create_v_divider())

        # مانده و درصد وصول
        remaining = self.studio_data.get("remaining", 0)
        total_fee = self.studio_data.get("total_fee", 0)
        total_paid = self.studio_data.get("total_paid", 0)
        pct = int((total_paid / total_fee) * 100) if total_fee > 0 else 100

        fin_col2 = QVBoxLayout()
        fin_col2.setSpacing(2)
        fin_col2.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignVCenter)

        rem_color = "#F59E0B" if remaining > 0 else "#10B981"
        rem_text = f"مانده طلب: <b>{format_toman(remaining)}</b>" if remaining > 0 else "حساب کاملاً تسویه شده ✓"

        lbl_rem = QLabel(rem_text)
        lbl_rem.setStyleSheet(f"font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 12.5px; font-weight: 700; color: {rem_color}; border: none; background: transparent;")
        fin_col2.addWidget(lbl_rem)

        lbl_pct = QLabel(f"نرخ وصول مطالبات: {to_persian_numbers(pct)}٪")
        lbl_pct.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 11px; color: #64748B; border: none; background: transparent;")
        fin_col2.addWidget(lbl_pct)
        layout.addLayout(fin_col2, stretch=2)

        # دکمه‌های اقدام
        btn_box = QHBoxLayout()
        btn_box.setSpacing(6)

        client_id = self.studio_data.get("client_id", 0)

        btn_invoice = QPushButton("فاکتور 📄")
        btn_invoice.setFixedHeight(36)
        btn_invoice.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        btn_invoice.setStyleSheet("""
            QPushButton {
                font-family: 'Vazirmatn';
                background-color: #1E283D;
                color: #F8FAFC;
                font-weight: 600;
                font-size: 11.5px;
                border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08);
                border-radius: 7px;
                padding: 0 12px;
            }
            QPushButton:hover {
                border-color: #6366F1;
                background-color: #242E42;
            }
        """)
        btn_invoice.clicked.connect(lambda: self._open_invoice(client_id))
        btn_box.addWidget(btn_invoice)

        btn_settle = QPushButton("تسویه حساب 💳")
        btn_settle.setFixedHeight(36)
        btn_settle.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        
        if remaining > 0:
            btn_settle.setStyleSheet("""
                QPushButton {
                    font-family: 'Vazirmatn';
                    background-color: #6366F1;
                    color: #FFFFFF;
                    font-weight: 700;
                    font-size: 12px;
                    border: none;
                    border-radius: 7px;
                    padding: 0 14px;
                }
                QPushButton:hover { background-color: #4F46E5; }
            """)
            btn_settle.setEnabled(True)
        else:
            btn_settle.setText("تسویه شده ✓")
            btn_settle.setStyleSheet("""
                QPushButton {
                    font-family: 'Vazirmatn';
                    background-color: rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.12);
                    color: #10B981;
                    font-weight: 700;
                    font-size: 11.5px;
                    border: 1px solid rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.25);
                    border-radius: 7px;
                    padding: 0 12px;
                }
            """)
            btn_settle.setEnabled(False)

        btn_settle.clicked.connect(lambda: self.on_settle_callback(client_id))
        btn_box.addWidget(btn_settle)

        layout.addLayout(btn_box)
        self._apply_style()

    def _open_invoice(self, client_id: int):
        diag = StudioInvoiceDialog(client_id, self.window())
        diag.exec()

    def _create_v_divider(self) -> QFrame:
        div = QFrame()
        div.setFrameShape(QFrame.Shape.VLine)
        div.setFixedHeight(34)
        div.setStyleSheet("background-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05); border: none;")
        return div

    def _apply_style(self):
        self.setStyleSheet("""
            QFrame#FinancialCardItem {
                background-color: #141B2A;
                border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.06);
                border-radius: 10px;
            }
            QFrame#FinancialCardItem:hover {
                background-color: #182236;
                border: 1px solid rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.35);
            }
        """)


class ReceivableProjectCard(QFrame):
    """کارت مدرن پروژه‌های دارای مانده بدهی"""
    def __init__(self, item_data: Dict[str, Any], on_pay_callback, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.item_data = item_data
        self.on_pay_callback = on_pay_callback
        
        self.setObjectName("FinancialCardItem")
        self.setLayoutDirection(Qt.LayoutDirection.RightToLeft)
        self.setFixedHeight(84)
        self.setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy.Policy.Expanding, QSizePolicy.Policy.Fixed)
        self.init_ui()

    def init_ui(self):
        layout = QHBoxLayout(self)
        layout.setContentsMargins(18, 10, 18, 10)
        layout.setSpacing(16)
        layout.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignVCenter)

        p_info = QVBoxLayout()
        p_info.setSpacing(2)
        p_info.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignVCenter)

        p_num = f"{self.item_data.get('project_number', 1):03d}"
        lbl_title = QLabel(f"<span style='color:#818CF8; font-weight:bold;'>{to_persian_numbers(p_num)}</span> — {self.item_data.get('couple_names', 'پروژه')}")
        lbl_title.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 14px; font-weight: 800; color: #F8FAFC; border: none; background: transparent;")
        p_info.addWidget(lbl_title)

        status_text = "آرشیو شده" if self.item_data.get("is_archived") else "تحویل شده به آتلیه"
        lbl_sub = QLabel(f"استودیو: {self.item_data.get('client_name', '-')}    |    وضعیت: {status_text}")
        lbl_sub.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 11px; color: #94A3B8; border: none; background: transparent;")

        fin_col1.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignVCenter)

        lbl_fee.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 12px; color: #8F909D; border: none; background: transparent;")

        lbl_paid = QLabel(f"دریافت‌شده: <b style='color:#10B981;'>{format_toman(self.item_data.get('total_paid', 0))}</b>")

        layout.addLayout(fin_col1, stretch=2)

        layout.addWidget(self._create_v_divider())

        rem_col = QVBoxLayout()
        rem_col.setSpacing(2)
        rem_col.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignVCenter)

        lbl_rem = QLabel(f"مانده بدهی: <b>{format_toman(self.item_data.get('remaining', 0))}</b>")
        lbl_rem.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 13px; font-weight: 800; color: #F59E0B; border: none; background: transparent;")
        rem_col.addWidget(lbl_rem)

        lbl_hint = QLabel("در انتظار واریزی و تسویه")
        lbl_hint.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 11px; color: #64748B; border: none; background: transparent;")
        rem_col.addWidget(lbl_hint)
        layout.addLayout(rem_col, stretch=2)

        btn_pay = QPushButton("ثبت واریزی 💵")
        btn_pay.setFixedHeight(36)
        btn_pay.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        btn_pay.setStyleSheet("""
            QPushButton {
                font-family: 'Vazirmatn';
                background-color: #10B981;
                color: #FFFFFF;
                font-weight: 700;
                border: none;
                border-radius: 7px;
                padding: 0 16px;
                font-size: 12px;
            }
            QPushButton:hover { background-color: #059669; }
        """)
        proj_id = self.item_data.get("project_id", 0)
        btn_pay.clicked.connect(lambda: self.on_pay_callback(proj_id))
        layout.addWidget(btn_pay)

        self._apply_style()

    def _create_v_divider(self) -> QFrame:
        div = QFrame()
        div.setFrameShape(QFrame.Shape.VLine)
        div.setFixedHeight(34)
        div.setStyleSheet("background-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05); border: none;")
        return div

    def _apply_style(self):
        self.setStyleSheet("""
            QFrame#FinancialCardItem {

                background-color: #182236;
                border: 1px solid rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.40);
            }
        """)


class TransactionLedgerCard(QFrame):
    """کارت ثبت تراکنش مالی در دفتر کل همراه با کد رهگیری و دکمه حذف"""
    def __init__(self, tx_data: Dict[str, Any], on_delete_cb, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.tx_data = tx_data
        self.on_delete_cb = on_delete_cb
        
        self.setObjectName("FinancialCardItem")
        self.setLayoutDirection(Qt.LayoutDirection.RightToLeft)
        self.setFixedHeight(76)
        self.setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy.Policy.Expanding, QSizePolicy.Policy.Fixed)
        self.init_ui()

    def init_ui(self):
        layout = QHBoxLayout(self)
        layout.setContentsMargins(18, 10, 18, 10)
        layout.setSpacing(14)
        layout.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignVCenter)

        col_tx = QVBoxLayout()
        col_tx.setSpacing(2)
        col_tx.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignVCenter)

        lbl_code = QLabel(f"TX-{self.tx_data.get('id', 0):04d}")
        lbl_code.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Segoe UI', 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 13px; font-weight: 800; color: #818CF8; border: none; background: transparent;")
        col_tx.addWidget(lbl_code)

        date_str = gregorian_to_jalali(self.tx_data.get("paid_at"))
        lbl_date = QLabel(f"📅 {date_str}")
        lbl_date.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 11px; color: #64748B; border: none; background: transparent;")
        col_tx.addWidget(lbl_date)
        layout.addLayout(col_tx, stretch=1)

        layout.addWidget(self._create_v_divider())

        col_target = QVBoxLayout()
        col_target.setSpacing(2)
        col_target.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignVCenter)

        lbl_proj = QLabel(f"<b>{self.tx_data.get('project_title', 'پروژه')}</b> {self.tx_data.get('target_name', '')}")
        lbl_proj.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 13px; color: #F8FAFC; border: none; background: transparent;")
        col_target.addWidget(lbl_proj)

        lbl_client = QLabel(f"استودیو: {self.tx_data.get('client_name', '-')}")
        lbl_client.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 11px; color: #94A3B8; border: none; background: transparent;")
        col_target.addWidget(lbl_client)
        layout.addLayout(col_target, stretch=2)

        layout.addWidget(self._create_v_divider())

        col_desc = QVBoxLayout()
        col_desc.setSpacing(2)
        col_desc.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignVCenter)

        pt = self.tx_data.get("payment_type", "")
        pt_label = "واریزی مرحله‌ای"
        if pt == "prepayment": pt_label = "پیش‌پرداخت اولیه"
        elif pt == "final_settlement": pt_label = "تسویه نهایی حساب"

        tracking_val = self.tx_data.get("tracking_code")
        tracking_info = f" • پیگیری: {to_persian_numbers(tracking_val)}" if tracking_val else ""

        lbl_pt = QLabel(f"نوع: <span style='color:#A5B4FC; font-weight:600;'>{pt_label}</span>{tracking_info}")
        lbl_pt.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 11px; color: #94A3B8; border: none; background: transparent;")
        col_desc.addWidget(lbl_pt)

        lbl_note = QLabel(f"شرح: {self.tx_data.get('notes', '-')}")
        lbl_note.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 11px; color: #64748B; border: none; background: transparent;")
        col_desc.addWidget(lbl_note)
        layout.addLayout(col_desc, stretch=2)

        layout.addWidget(self._create_v_divider())

        amount = self.tx_data.get("amount", 0)
        lbl_amt = QLabel(f"+ {format_toman(amount)}")
        lbl_amt.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 15px; font-weight: 800; color: #10B981; border: none; background: transparent;")
        layout.addWidget(lbl_amt)

        # دکمه حذف تراکنش اشتباه
        btn_del = QPushButton()
        btn_del.setIcon(IconProvider.get_icon("trash", "#EF4444", size=13))
        btn_del.setFixedSize(28, 28)
        btn_del.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        btn_del.setToolTip("حذف این تراکنش مالی")
        btn_del.setStyleSheet("""
            QPushButton {
                background-color: rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.12);
                border: 1px solid rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.25);
                border-radius: 6px;
            }
            QPushButton:hover {
                background-color: #EF4444;
            }
        """)
        btn_del.clicked.connect(lambda: self.on_delete_cb(self.tx_data["id"], amount))
        layout.addWidget(btn_del)

        self._apply_style()

    def _create_v_divider(self) -> QFrame:
        div = QFrame()
        div.setFrameShape(QFrame.Shape.VLine)
        div.setFixedHeight(30)
        div.setStyleSheet("background-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05); border: none;")
        return div

    def _apply_style(self):
        self.setStyleSheet("""
            QFrame#FinancialCardItem {
                background-color: #141B2A;
                border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.06);
                border-radius: 10px;
            }
            QFrame#FinancialCardItem:hover {
                background-color: #182236;
                border: 1px solid rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.35);
            }
        """)


class MonthlyPerformanceCard(QFrame):
    """کارت ماهانه عملکرد مالی سال جاری"""
    def __init__(self, month_data: Dict[str, Any], parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.month_data = month_data
        
        self.setObjectName("FinancialCardItem")
        self.setLayoutDirection(Qt.LayoutDirection.RightToLeft)
        self.setFixedHeight(72)
        self.setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy.Policy.Expanding, QSizePolicy.Policy.Fixed)
        self.init_ui()

    def init_ui(self):
        layout = QHBoxLayout(self)
        layout.setContentsMargins(18, 10, 18, 10)
        layout.setSpacing(16)
        layout.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignVCenter)

        lbl_month = QLabel(f"📅 <b>{self.month_data.get('month', '')}</b>")
        lbl_month.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 14px; color: #F8FAFC; border: none; background: transparent;")
        layout.addWidget(lbl_month, stretch=1)

        layout.addWidget(self._create_v_divider())

        cnt = self.month_data.get("count", 0)
        cnt_text = f"{to_persian_numbers(cnt)} واریزی ثبت‌شده" if cnt > 0 else "بدون تراکنش"
        lbl_cnt = QLabel(cnt_text)
        lbl_cnt.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 12px; color: #94A3B8; border: none; background: transparent;")
        layout.addWidget(lbl_cnt, stretch=1)

        layout.addWidget(self._create_v_divider())

        amount = self.month_data.get("amount", 0)
        amt_color = "#10B981" if amount > 0 else "#64748B"
        lbl_amt = QLabel(format_toman(amount))
        lbl_amt.setStyleSheet(f"font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 14.5px; font-weight: 800; color: {amt_color}; border: none; background: transparent;")
        layout.addWidget(lbl_amt)

        self._apply_style()

    def _create_v_divider(self) -> QFrame:
        div = QFrame()
        div.setFrameShape(QFrame.Shape.VLine)
        div.setFixedHeight(28)
        div.setStyleSheet("background-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05); border: none;")
        return div

    def _apply_style(self):
        self.setStyleSheet("""
            QFrame#FinancialCardItem {
                background-color: #141B2A;
                border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.06);
                border-radius: 10px;
            }
            QFrame#FinancialCardItem:hover {
                background-color: #182236;
                border: 1px solid rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.35);
            }
        """)


class FinancialsView(QWidget):
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.setLayoutDirection(Qt.LayoutDirection.RightToLeft)
        self.init_ui()
        self._connect_signals()
        self.refresh_all_financial_data()

    def init_ui(self):
        main_layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        main_layout.setContentsMargins(28, 24, 28, 24)

        btn_export.setStyleSheet("""
            QPushButton {
                font-family: 'Vazirmatn';

        header_layout.addWidget(btn_export)

        main_layout.addLayout(header_layout)

        # ۲. نوار فیلترها
        filter_frame = QFrame()
        filter_frame.setObjectName("SurfaceCard")
        filter_frame.setStyleSheet("""
            QFrame#SurfaceCard {
                background-color: #121826;
                border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.06);
                border-radius: 10px;
            }
            QLabel {
                font-family: 'Vazirmatn';
                color: #94A3B8;
                font-size: 12px;
                font-weight: 600;
            }
            QComboBox {
                font-family: 'Vazirmatn';
                background-color: #161F30;
                border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08);
                border-radius: 7px;
                padding: 6px 12px;
                color: #F8FAFC;
                font-size: 12px;
                min-height: 34px;
            }
        """)

        filter_layout.setContentsMargins(16, 10, 16, 10)
        filter_layout.setSpacing(14)

        filter_layout.addWidget(QLabel("استودیو:"))
        self.combo_filter_client = QComboBox()
        self.combo_filter_client.addItem("همه استودیوها", 0)
        self.combo_filter_client.currentIndexChanged.connect(self.refresh_all_financial_data)
        filter_layout.addWidget(self.combo_filter_client, stretch=2)

        filter_layout.addWidget(QLabel("بازه زمانی:"))
        self.combo_filter_date = QComboBox()
        self.combo_filter_date.addItem("همه زمان‌ها", "all")

        self.combo_filter_date.addItem("این هفته", "this_week")
        self.combo_filter_date.addItem("این ماه (شمسی)", "this_month")
        self.combo_filter_date.addItem("ماه قبل", "last_month")
        self.combo_filter_date.addItem("امسال (سال جاری)", "this_year")

        filter_layout.addWidget(self.combo_filter_date, stretch=2)

        filter_layout.addWidget(QLabel("وضعیت مالی:"))

        self.combo_filter_status.addItem("تسویه کامل شده", "settled")
        self.combo_filter_status.currentIndexChanged.connect(self.refresh_all_financial_data)

        btn_reset_filters = QPushButton("بازنشانی")

        self.card_total_remaining = StatCard(title="کل طلب وصول‌نشده", initial_value=0, is_currency=True, color_accent="#F59E0B")

        self.card_projects_count = StatCard(title="پروژه‌های تحویل‌شده", initial_value=0, is_currency=False, color_accent="#38BDF8")

        self.stats_layout.addWidget(self.card_total_contracts)
        self.stats_layout.addWidget(self.card_total_received)
        self.stats_layout.addWidget(self.card_total_remaining)
        self.stats_layout.addWidget(self.card_unsettled_count)
        self.stats_layout.addWidget(self.card_projects_count)
        main_layout.addLayout(self.stats_layout)

        # ۴. تب‌های اختصاصی مالی
        self.tab_widget = QTabWidget()
        self.tab_widget.setStyleSheet("""
            QTabWidget::pane {
                border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.06);
                background-color: #121826;
                border-radius: 10px;
                padding: 14px;
            }
            QTabBar::tab {
                font-family: 'Vazirmatn';
                background-color: #161F30;
                color: #94A3B8;
                border-top-left-radius: 8px;
                border-top-right-radius: 8px;
                padding: 9px 20px;
                margin-left: 6px;
                font-weight: 600;
                font-size: 12.5px;
            }
            QTabBar::tab:selected {
                background-color: #6366F1;
                color: #FFFFFF;
                font-weight: 700;
            }
        """)

        # تب ۱: حساب و بدهی کل استودیوها
        self.tab_studio_overview = self._create_card_tab_container("studios")
        self.tab_widget.addTab(self.tab_studio_overview, "حساب و بدهی کل استودیوها")

        # تب ۲: پروژه‌های دارای مانده
        self.tab_receivables = self._create_card_tab_container("receivables")
        self.tab_widget.addTab(self.tab_receivables, "پروژه‌های دارای مانده")

        # تب ۳: دفتر کل تراکنش‌ها
        self.tab_transactions = self._create_card_tab_container("transactions")
        self.tab_widget.addTab(self.tab_transactions, "دفتر کل تراکنش‌ها")

        # تب ۴: گزارش ماهانه درآمد
        self.tab_monthly = self._build_monthly_income_tab()
        self.tab_widget.addTab(self.tab_monthly, "درآمد ماهانه سال جاری")

        main_layout.addWidget(self.tab_widget, stretch=1)
        self._load_clients_filter_options()

    def _connect_signals(self):
        trigger = lambda *args: QTimer.singleShot(0, self.refresh_all_financial_data)
        global_signals.transaction_recorded.connect(trigger)
        global_signals.project_created.connect(trigger)
        global_signals.project_updated.connect(trigger)
        global_signals.project_deleted.connect(trigger)
        global_signals.project_archived.connect(trigger)
        global_signals.project_unarchived.connect(trigger)
        global_signals.revision_added.connect(trigger)
        global_signals.client_balance_updated.connect(lambda *_: self._load_clients_filter_options())

    def _load_clients_filter_options(self):
        current_id = self.combo_filter_client.currentData()
        self.combo_filter_client.blockSignals(True)
        self.combo_filter_client.clear()
        self.combo_filter_client.addItem("همه استودیوها", 0)
        
        with db.get_session() as session:
            clients = session.scalars(select(Client).order_by(Client.company, Client.full_name)).all()
            for c in clients:
                display = c.company or c.full_name
                self.combo_filter_client.addItem(display, c.id)

        idx = self.combo_filter_client.findData(current_id)
        if idx >= 0:
            self.combo_filter_client.setCurrentIndex(idx)
        self.combo_filter_client.blockSignals(False)

    def _reset_filters(self):
        self.combo_filter_client.setCurrentIndex(0)
        self.combo_filter_date.setCurrentIndex(0)
        self.combo_filter_status.setCurrentIndex(0)

    def _create_card_tab_container(self, category_key: str) -> QWidget:
        widget = QWidget()
        lay = QVBoxLayout(widget)
        lay.setContentsMargins(0, 4, 0, 0)
        lay.setSpacing(10)

        scroll = QScrollArea()
        scroll.setWidgetResizable(True)
        scroll.setFrameShape(QFrame.Shape.NoFrame)
        scroll.setStyleSheet("background: transparent; border: none;")
        scroll.setHorizontalScrollBarPolicy(Qt.ScrollBarPolicy.ScrollBarAlwaysOff)

        container = QWidget()
        container.setStyleSheet("background: transparent;")
        c_lay = QVBoxLayout(container)
        c_lay.setContentsMargins(0, 2, 0, 8)
        c_lay.setSpacing(8)
        c_lay.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignTop)

        scroll.setWidget(container)
        lay.addWidget(scroll)

        if category_key == "studios":
            self.studios_layout = c_lay
        elif category_key == "receivables":
            self.receivables_layout = c_lay
        elif category_key == "transactions":
            self.transactions_layout = c_lay

        return widget

    def _build_monthly_income_tab(self) -> QWidget:
        widget = QWidget()
        lay = QVBoxLayout(widget)
        lay.setContentsMargins(0, 4, 0, 0)
        lay.setSpacing(14)

        self.revenue_chart = RevenueBarChartWidget()
        lay.addWidget(self.revenue_chart)

        scroll = QScrollArea()
        scroll.setWidgetResizable(True)
        scroll.setFrameShape(QFrame.Shape.NoFrame)
        scroll.setStyleSheet("background: transparent; border: none;")
        scroll.setHorizontalScrollBarPolicy(Qt.ScrollBarPolicy.ScrollBarAlwaysOff)

        container = QWidget()
        container.setStyleSheet("background: transparent;")
        self.monthly_cards_layout = QVBoxLayout(container)
        self.monthly_cards_layout.setContentsMargins(0, 2, 0, 8)
        self.monthly_cards_layout.setSpacing(8)
        self.monthly_cards_layout.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignTop)

        scroll.setWidget(container)
        lay.addWidget(scroll, stretch=1)
        return widget

    def refresh_all_financial_data(self):
        client_id = self.combo_filter_client.currentData()
        client_id_val = client_id if (client_id and client_id > 0) else None
        date_key = self.combo_filter_date.currentData() or "all"
        status_filter = self.combo_filter_status.currentData() or "all"

        data = FinanceController.get_financial_dashboard_data(
            client_id=client_id_val,
            date_range_key=date_key,
            status_filter=status_filter
        )

        cards = data["summary_cards"]
        self.card_total_contracts.animate_to_value(cards["total_contracts"])
        self.card_total_received.animate_to_value(cards["total_received"])
        self.card_total_remaining.animate_to_value(cards["total_remaining"])
        self.card_unsettled_count.animate_to_value(cards["unsettled_count"])
        self.card_projects_count.animate_to_value(cards["total_projects"])

        self._render_studios_matrix_cards(data["studio_matrix"])
        self._render_receivables_cards(data["receivables"])
        self._render_transactions_cards(data["transactions"])
        self._render_monthly_cards(data["monthly_income"])

    def _render_studios_matrix_cards(self, studio_matrix: List[dict]):
        while self.studios_layout.count():
            item = self.studios_layout.takeAt(0)
            if item.widget():
                item.widget().deleteLater()

        if not studio_matrix:
            empty = ModernEmptyStateWidget(
                title="هیچ استودیوی واجد شرایط مالی یافت نشد",
                description="تنها استودیوهایی که پروژه‌های تکمیل‌شده و تحویل‌شده به آتلیه دارند در این بخش محاسبه می‌شوند.",
                button_text=""
            )
            self.studios_layout.addWidget(empty)
            return

        for s in studio_matrix:
            card = StudioLedgerCard(s, on_settle_callback=self._open_payment_for_studio)
            self.studios_layout.addWidget(card)

    def _render_receivables_cards(self, receivables: List[dict]):
        while self.receivables_layout.count():
            item = self.receivables_layout.takeAt(0)
            if item.widget():
                item.widget().deleteLater()

        if not receivables:
            empty = ModernEmptyStateWidget(
                title="هیچ پروژه دارای مانده بدهی یافت نشد",
                description="تمامی پروژه‌های تحویل‌شده به آتلیه کاملاً تسویه شده‌اند و طلبی باقی نمانده است.",
                button_text=""
            )
            self.receivables_layout.addWidget(empty)
            return

        for r in receivables:
            card = ReceivableProjectCard(r, on_pay_callback=self._open_payment_for_project)
            self.receivables_layout.addWidget(card)

    def _render_transactions_cards(self, transactions: List[dict]):
        while self.transactions_layout.count():
            item = self.transactions_layout.takeAt(0)
            if item.widget():
                item.widget().deleteLater()

        if not transactions:
            empty = ModernEmptyStateWidget(
                title="هیچ تراکنش مالی ثبت نشده است",
                description="واریزی‌ها و تسویه‌حساب‌های ثبت‌شده در این بخش به عنوان دفتر کل نگهداری می‌شوند.",
                button_text=""
            )
            self.transactions_layout.addWidget(empty)
            return

        for t in transactions:
            card = TransactionLedgerCard(t, on_delete_cb=self._handle_delete_transaction)
            self.transactions_layout.addWidget(card)

    def _render_monthly_cards(self, monthly_data: List[dict]):
        if hasattr(self, "revenue_chart"):
            self.revenue_chart.set_data(monthly_data)

        while self.monthly_cards_layout.count():
            item = self.monthly_cards_layout.takeAt(0)
            if item.widget():
                item.widget().deleteLater()

        for m in monthly_data:
            card = MonthlyPerformanceCard(m)
            self.monthly_cards_layout.addWidget(card)

    def _handle_delete_transaction(self, transaction_id: int, amount: int):
        if StudioConfirmDialog.ask(
            self.window(),
            title="حذف تراکنش مالی",
            message=f"آیا از حذف تراکنش دریافتی به مبلغ {format_toman(amount)} اطمینان دارید؟",
            details="پس از حذف، این مبلغ مجدداً به عنوان مانده بدهی پروژه و استودیو لحاظ خواهد شد.",
            confirm_text="حذف تراکنش",
            is_danger=True,
            icon_name="trash"
        ):
            if FinanceController.delete_transaction(transaction_id):
                global_signals.transaction_recorded.emit(0)
                global_signals.show_toast.emit("تراکنش مالی با موفقیت حذف شد.", "info")
                self.refresh_all_financial_data()

    def _open_new_transaction_dialog(self):
        diag = NewTransactionDialog(None, None, self.window())
        diag.exec()

    def _open_payment_for_studio(self, client_id: int):
        diag = NewTransactionDialog(target_project_id=None, target_client_id=client_id, parent=self.window())
        diag.exec()

    def _open_payment_for_project(self, project_id: int):
        diag = NewTransactionDialog(target_project_id=project_id, target_client_id=None, parent=self.window())
        diag.exec()

    def _export_excel(self):
        file_path, _ = QFileDialog.getSaveFileName(
            self, "ذخیره گزارش مالی اکسل", "CutterLog_Financial_Report.xlsx", "Excel Files (*.xlsx)"
        )
        if file_path:
            client_id = self.combo_filter_client.currentData()
            client_id_val = client_id if (client_id and client_id > 0) else None
            date_key = self.combo_filter_date.currentData() or "all"
            status_filter = self.combo_filter_status.currentData() or "all"

            data = FinanceController.get_financial_dashboard_data(
                client_id=client_id_val,
                date_range_key=date_key,
                status_filter=status_filter
            )

            txs = [{
                "id": t["id"],
                "project": t["project_title"],
                "client": t["client_name"],
                "amount": t["amount"],
                "date": t["paid_at"].strftime("%Y-%m-%d") if t["paid_at"] else ""
            } for t in data["transactions"]]

            if export_transactions_to_excel(txs, file_path):
                StudioConfirmDialog.ask(
                    self, 
                    title="موفقیت", 
                    message="فایل اکسل گزارش مالی با موفقیت صادر شد.", 
                    details=f"مسیر ذخیره:\n{file_path}",
                    confirm_text="متوجه شدم",
                    icon_name="check"
                )
            else:
                StudioConfirmDialog.ask(
                    self, 
                    title="خطا", 
                    message="خطا در تولید فایل اکسل.", 
                    is_danger=True,
                    icon_name="alert"
                )

import sys
from pathlib import Path
from PySide6.QtWidgets import (
    QMainWindow, QWidget, QHBoxLayout, QVBoxLayout, 
    QStackedWidget, QFrame, QLabel
)
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt, QTimer
from PySide6.QtGui import QGuiApplication, QCloseEvent, QIcon

from config import APP_NAME, get_asset_path
from core.signals import global_signals
from widgets.capsule_navbar import CapsuleNavBar

# ویوهای اصلی
from views.dashboard_view import DashboardView
from views.projects_view import ProjectsView

class HeaderActiveSessionIndicator(QFrame):
    """نشانگر وضعیت سشن زنده در نوار بالای برنامه"""
    def __init__(self, on_click_callback, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.on_click_callback = on_click_callback
        self.setLayoutDirection(Qt.LayoutDirection.RightToLeft)
        self.setFixedHeight(30)
        self.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        self.setVisible(False)

        layout = QHBoxLayout(self)
        layout.setContentsMargins(8, 2, 8, 2)
        layout.setSpacing(6)
        layout.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)

        self.dot_rec = QLabel("●")
        self.dot_rec.setStyleSheet("color: #10B981; font-size: 10px; background: transparent; border: none;")
        layout.addWidget(self.dot_rec)

        self.lbl_time = QLabel("00:00:00")
        self.lbl_time.setStyleSheet("""
            font-family: 'Segoe UI', monospace;
            font-size: 11.5px;
            font-weight: 800;
            color: #F8FAFC;
            background: transparent;
            border: none;
        """)
        layout.addWidget(self.lbl_time)

        self.setStyleSheet("""
            QFrame {
                background-color: rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.12);
                border: 1px solid rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.35);
                border-radius: 6px;
            }
            QFrame:hover {
                background-color: rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.22);
                border-color: #10B981;
            }
        """)

        global_signals.timer_tick.connect(self._on_tick)
        global_signals.session_started.connect(lambda _: self._set_active(True))
        global_signals.session_ended.connect(lambda _: self._set_active(False))
        global_signals.session_paused.connect(self._on_pause)
        global_signals.session_resumed.connect(self._on_resume)

    def _set_active(self, active: bool):
        self.setVisible(active)
        if active:
            self.dot_rec.setStyleSheet("color: #10B981; font-size: 10px; background: transparent; border: none;")
            self.setStyleSheet("""
                QFrame {
                    background-color: rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.12);
                    border: 1px solid rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.35);
                    border-radius: 6px;
                }
                QFrame:hover {
                    background-color: rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.22);
                    border-color: #10B981;
                }
            """)

    def _on_pause(self, _):
        self.dot_rec.setStyleSheet("color: #F59E0B; font-size: 10px; background: transparent; border: none;")
        self.setStyleSheet("""
            QFrame {
                background-color: rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.12);
                border: 1px solid rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.35);
                border-radius: 6px;
            }
        """)

    def _on_resume(self, _):
        self._set_active(True)

    def _on_tick(self, snap: dict):
        if not self.isVisible():
            self.setVisible(True)
        sec = snap.get("active_seconds", 0)
        h = sec // 3600
        m = (sec % 3600) // 60
        s = sec % 60
        self.lbl_time.setText(f"{h:02d}:{m:02d}:{s:02d}")

    def mouseReleaseEvent(self, event):
        if event.button() == Qt.MouseButton.LeftButton:
            event.accept()
            self.on_click_callback()
            return
        super().mouseReleaseEvent(event)


class MainWindow(QMainWindow):
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__()
        self.setLayoutDirection(Qt.LayoutDirection.RightToLeft)
        
        # ----------------------------------------------------
        # تنظیم آیکون مستقیماً روی پنجره اصلی (Title Bar & Window)
        # ----------------------------------------------------
        icon_path = get_asset_path("assets/icons/icon.ico")
        if icon_path.exists():
            self.setWindowIcon(QIcon(str(icon_path.resolve())))

        self.update_window_title(is_active=False, is_paused=False)

        screen = QGuiApplication.primaryScreen()
        screen_geo = screen.availableGeometry() if screen else None
        
        if screen_geo:
            target_w = min(1400, int(screen_geo.width() * 0.95))
            target_h = min(900, int(screen_geo.height() * 0.92))
            self.resize(target_w, target_h)
        else:
            self.resize(1280, 800)
            
        self.setMinimumSize(860, 580)
        self.init_ui()
        self._connect_title_signals()

    def update_window_title(self, is_active: bool = False, is_paused: bool = False):
        base = f"{APP_NAME} Pro"
        if is_active and not is_paused:
            self.setWindowTitle(f"{base} - Active")
        elif is_paused:
            self.setWindowTitle(f"{base} - Paused")
        else:
            self.setWindowTitle(f"{base} - Standby")

    def _connect_title_signals(self):
        global_signals.session_started.connect(lambda _: self.update_window_title(is_active=True, is_paused=False))
        global_signals.session_resumed.connect(lambda _: self.update_window_title(is_active=True, is_paused=False))
        global_signals.session_paused.connect(lambda _: self.update_window_title(is_active=True, is_paused=True))
        global_signals.session_ended.connect(lambda _: self.update_window_title(is_active=False, is_paused=False))

    def init_ui(self):
        self.central_widget = QWidget(self)
        self.setCentralWidget(self.central_widget)

        main_layout = QVBoxLayout(self.central_widget)
        main_layout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        main_layout.setSpacing(0)

        # نوار بالای برنامه
        top_header = self._build_top_header()
        main_layout.addWidget(top_header)

        # کانتینر پشته‌ای صفحات
        self.pages_stack = QStackedWidget()
        self.pages_stack.setLayoutDirection(Qt.LayoutDirection.RightToLeft)

        self.dashboard_view = DashboardView()
        self.projects_view = ProjectsView()
        self.archive_view = ArchiveView()
        self.financials_view = FinancialsView()
        self.audio_vault_view = AudioVaultView()
        self.settings_view = SettingsView()
        self.time_tracking_view = TimeTrackingView()
        self.copilot_view = CopilotView()

        self.pages_stack.addWidget(self.dashboard_view)        # 0
        self.pages_stack.addWidget(self.projects_view)         # 1
        self.pages_stack.addWidget(self.archive_view)          # 2
        self.pages_stack.addWidget(self.financials_view)       # 3
        self.pages_stack.addWidget(self.audio_vault_view)      # 4
        self.pages_stack.addWidget(self.settings_view)         # 5
        self.pages_stack.addWidget(self.time_tracking_view)    # 6
        self.pages_stack.addWidget(self.copilot_view)          # 7

        main_layout.addWidget(self.pages_stack, stretch=1)

        # اتصالات ناوبری
        global_signals.navigate_to_page.connect(self._switch_page)
        global_signals.navigate_and_focus_project.connect(self._go_to_projects_tab)

        self.capsule_nav.update_responsive_layout(self.width())

    def _build_top_header(self) -> QFrame:
        header = QFrame()
        header.setObjectName("TopHeaderFrame")
        header.setFixedHeight(50)
        header.setLayoutDirection(Qt.LayoutDirection.RightToLeft)
        header.setStyleSheet("""
            QFrame#TopHeaderFrame {
                background-color: #0B0F17;
                border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.06);
            }
        """)

        layout = QHBoxLayout(header)
        layout.setContentsMargins(14, 0, 14, 0)
        layout.setSpacing(10)

        # نشانگر زمان جلسه فعال
        self.header_timer_badge = HeaderActiveSessionIndicator(
            on_click_callback=lambda: self._switch_page(6), 
            parent=self
        )
        layout.addWidget(self.header_timer_badge)

        layout.addStretch(1)

        # نوار ناوبری کپسولی
        self.capsule_nav = CapsuleNavBar(parent=self)
        self.capsule_nav.page_selected.connect(self._switch_page)
        layout.addWidget(self.capsule_nav)

        layout.addStretch(1)
        return header

    def _switch_page(self, index: int):
        if 0 <= index < self.pages_stack.count():
            if hasattr(self, "audio_vault_view") and self.pages_stack.currentWidget() == self.audio_vault_view and index != 4:
                if hasattr(self.audio_vault_view, "player"):
                    self.audio_vault_view.player.stop()

            self.pages_stack.setCurrentIndex(index)
            self.capsule_nav.select_index(index, emit_signal=False)

            current_widget = self.pages_stack.currentWidget()
            if hasattr(current_widget, "refresh_dashboard"):
                current_widget.refresh_dashboard()
            elif hasattr(current_widget, "refresh_data"):
                current_widget.refresh_data()
            elif hasattr(current_widget, "refresh_projects"):
                current_widget.refresh_projects()
            elif hasattr(current_widget, "refresh_archive"):
                current_widget.refresh_archive()

    def _go_to_projects_tab(self, project_id: int):
        self._switch_page(1)

    def resizeEvent(self, event):
        super().resizeEvent(event)
        if hasattr(self, "capsule_nav"):
            self.capsule_nav.update_responsive_layout(self.width())

    def closeEvent(self, event: QCloseEvent):
        try:
            from time_tracking.timer_engine import TimeTrackingEngine
            engine = TimeTrackingEngine.instance()
            if engine.is_running:
                engine._persist_active_session()
            if hasattr(engine, "monitor_thread") and engine.monitor_thread.isRunning():
                engine.monitor_thread.stop()
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Close cleanup notice: {e}")

        super().closeEvent(event)

from datetime import datetime

class StudioFilterPillButton(QPushButton):
    def __init__(self, title: str, filter_key: str, icon_name: str, color_hex: str = "#818CF8", parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.filter_key = filter_key
        self.icon_name = icon_name
        self.color_hex = color_hex
        self.base_title = title
        self.count = 0

        self.setCheckable(True)
        self.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        self.setFixedHeight(34)
        self.setIconSize(QSize(13, 13))
        self.update_appearance()

    def set_count(self, count: int):
        self.count = count
        cnt_str = f" ({to_persian_numbers(count)})" if count > 0 else ""
        self.setText(f" {self.base_title}{cnt_str}")
        self.setEnabled(count > 0 or self.isChecked())
        self.update_appearance()

    def update_appearance(self):
        if self.isChecked():
            self.setIcon(IconProvider.get_icon(self.icon_name, "#FFFFFF", size=13))
            self.setStyleSheet(f"""
                QPushButton {{
                    font-family: 'Vazirmatn';
                    font-size: 11.5px;
                    font-weight: 700;
                    background-color: {self.color_hex};
                    color: #FFFFFF;
                    border: 1px solid {self.color_hex};
                    border-radius: 7px;
                    padding: 0 12px;
                }}
            """)
        else:
            icon_color = self.color_hex if self.count > 0 else "#64748B"
            text_color = "#F8FAFC" if self.count > 0 else "#64748B"
            self.setIcon(IconProvider.get_icon(self.icon_name, icon_color, size=13))
            self.setStyleSheet(f"""
                QPushButton {{
                    font-family: 'Vazirmatn';
                    font-size: 11.5px;
                    font-weight: 600;
                    background-color: #161F30;
                    color: {text_color};
                    border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08);
                    border-radius: 7px;
                    padding: 0 12px;
                }}
                QPushButton:hover {{
                    background-color: #1E283D;
                    border-color: {self.color_hex}88;
                    color: #FFFFFF;
                }}
            """)

            QFrame#ProjectsFilterPanel {

                border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.06);
                border-radius: 10px;
            }
            QLineEdit {
                font-family: 'Vazirmatn';
                background-color: #161F30;
                border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08);
                border-radius: 7px;
                padding: 0 12px;
                color: #F8FAFC;
                font-size: 12px;
            }
            QLineEdit:focus {
                border-color: #6366F1;

            }

        self.pill_unsettled.clicked.connect(lambda: self._on_quick_pill_clicked("unsettled"))
        panel_layout.addWidget(self.pill_unsettled)
        self.quick_pills["unsettled"] = self.pill_unsettled

        self.btn_reset = QPushButton(" بازنشانی")
        self.btn_reset.setIcon(IconProvider.get_icon("refresh", "#818CF8", size=12))
        self.btn_reset.setIconSize(QSize(12, 12))
        self.btn_reset.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
