        btn_row.addStretch()

        card_backup.add_setting_row(SettingRow("نسخه‌برداری و بازیابی دیتابیس:", "فایل دیتابیس شامل تمام پروژه‌ها، کلیپ‌ها، اصلاحیه‌ها و تراکنش‌ها است.", None))
        card_backup.add_custom_layout(btn_row)

        layout.addWidget(card_backup)
        layout.addStretch()
        return scroll

    def _on_copy_audio_toggled(self, checked: bool):
        self.settings["copy_audio_to_vault"] = checked
        self._persist_settings()
        msg = "حالت کپی فیزیکی فایل‌های صوتی فعال شد." if checked else "حالت فقط خواندن (Reference Mode) فعال شد؛ از این پس هیچ فایل یا پوشه‌ای روی هارد کپی نمی‌شود."
        global_signals.show_toast.emit(msg, "info")

    def _build_reset_danger_tab(self) -> QWidget:
        scroll = self._create_scrollable_container()
        container = scroll.widget()
        layout = container.layout()

        card = SettingsSectionCard("عملیات حساس و پاکسازی داده‌ها (Danger Zone)", "بازنشانی تنظیمات پیکربندی یا پاکسازی کامل اطلاعات ذخیره‌شده")
        card.setStyleSheet("""
            QFrame#SettingsSectionCardFrame {
                background-color: #121826;
                border: 1.5px solid rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.35);
                border-radius: 10px;
            }
        """)

        btn_reset_settings = QPushButton("بازنشانی تنظیمات")
        btn_reset_settings.setFixedHeight(36)
        btn_reset_settings.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        btn_reset_settings.setStyleSheet("color: #F59E0B; background-color: rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.12); border: 1px solid #F59E0B; border-radius: 6px; padding: 0 16px; font-weight: 600; font-size: 12px;")
        btn_reset_settings.clicked.connect(self._reset_settings)
        card.add_setting_row(SettingRow("بازنشانی تنظیمات به مقادیر اولیه (Factory Reset):", "تنها تنظیمات به حالت اولیه بازمی‌گردند؛ پروژه‌ها و حساب‌های مالی حفظ می‌شوند.", btn_reset_settings))

        btn_wipe = QPushButton("پاکسازی دیتابیس")
        btn_wipe.setFixedHeight(36)
        btn_wipe.setIcon(IconProvider.get_icon("trash", "#FFFFFF", size=13))
        btn_wipe.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        btn_wipe.setStyleSheet("background-color: #EF4444; color: #FFFFFF; font-weight: bold; border: none; border-radius: 6px; padding: 0 16px; font-size: 12px;")
        btn_wipe.clicked.connect(self._wipe_database)
        card.add_setting_row(SettingRow("پاکسازی کامل پایگاه داده:", "هشدار: تمامی پروژه‌ها، کلیپ‌ها، لاگ‌ها، استودیوها و تراکنش‌های مالی حذف خواهند شد.", btn_wipe))

        layout.addWidget(card)
        layout.addStretch()
        return scroll

    def _create_scrollable_container(self) -> QScrollArea:
        scroll = QScrollArea()
        scroll.setWidgetResizable(True)
        scroll.setFrameShape(QFrame.Shape.NoFrame)
        scroll.setStyleSheet("background: transparent; border: none;")
        scroll.setHorizontalScrollBarPolicy(Qt.ScrollBarPolicy.ScrollBarAlwaysOff)
        scroll.setLayoutDirection(Qt.LayoutDirection.RightToLeft)

        container = QWidget()
        container.setStyleSheet("background: transparent;")
        container.setLayoutDirection(Qt.LayoutDirection.RightToLeft)
        layout = QVBoxLayout(container)
        layout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        layout.setSpacing(14)
        scroll.setWidget(container)
        return scroll

    def _refresh_packages_grid(self):
        while self.packages_grid.count():
            item = self.packages_grid.takeAt(0)
            if item.widget():
                item.widget().deleteLater()

        packages = self.settings.get("packages", {})
        if not packages:
            empty = QLabel("هنوز پکیج سریعی ثبت نشده است.")
            empty.setStyleSheet("color: #64748B; font-size: 11.5px; border: none;")
            self.packages_grid.addWidget(empty, 0, 0)
            return

        for idx, (pkg_name, clips) in enumerate(packages.items()):
            chip = QFrame()
            chip.setStyleSheet("""
                QFrame {
                    background-color: #161F30;
                    border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08);
                    border-radius: 6px;
                }
                QFrame:hover {
                    border-color: rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.4);
                    background-color: #1A253A;
                }
            """)
            c_lay = QHBoxLayout(chip)
            c_lay.setContentsMargins(10, 6, 8, 6)
            c_lay.setSpacing(6)

            lbl_title = QLabel(f"⚡ <b>{pkg_name}</b>")
            lbl_title.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; color: #F8FAFC; font-size: 11.5px; border: none;")
            c_lay.addWidget(lbl_title, stretch=1)

            lbl_cnt = QLabel(f"{to_persian_numbers(len(clips))} کلیپ")
            lbl_cnt.setStyleSheet("background-color: rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.15); color: #818CF8; border-radius: 4px; padding: 2px 6px; font-size: 10.5px; font-weight: bold; border: none;")
            c_lay.addWidget(lbl_cnt)

            btn_edit = QPushButton("✎")
            btn_edit.setFixedSize(22, 22)
            btn_edit.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
            btn_edit.setStyleSheet("background: transparent; color: #818CF8; border: none; font-size: 12px; font-weight: bold;")
            btn_edit.clicked.connect(lambda _, n=pkg_name, c=clips: self._edit_package(n, c))
            c_lay.addWidget(btn_edit)

            btn_del = QPushButton("×")
            btn_del.setFixedSize(22, 22)
            btn_del.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
            btn_del.setStyleSheet("background: transparent; color: #EF4444; border: none; font-size: 15px; font-weight: bold;")
            btn_del.clicked.connect(lambda _, n=pkg_name: self._delete_package(n))
            c_lay.addWidget(btn_del)

            row = idx // 2
            col = idx % 2
            self.packages_grid.addWidget(chip, row, col)

    def _refresh_clips_grid(self):
        while self.clips_grid.count():
            item = self.clips_grid.takeAt(0)
            if item.widget():
                item.widget().deleteLater()

        clips = self.settings.get("custom_clips", [])
        if not clips:
            empty = QLabel("هیچ کلیپی در بانک عناوین وجود ندارد.")
            empty.setStyleSheet("color: #64748B; font-size: 11.5px; border: none;")
            self.clips_grid.addWidget(empty, 0, 0)
            return

        for idx, clip_name in enumerate(clips):
            chip = QFrame()
            chip.setStyleSheet("""
                QFrame {
                    background-color: #161F30;
                    border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.06);
                    border-radius: 6px;
                }
                QFrame:hover { border-color: rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.35); }
            """)
            c_lay = QHBoxLayout(chip)
            c_lay.setContentsMargins(8, 4, 6, 4)
            c_lay.setSpacing(6)

            lbl_txt = QLabel(clip_name)
            lbl_txt.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; color: #F8FAFC; font-size: 11.5px; border: none;")
            c_lay.addWidget(lbl_txt, stretch=1)

            btn_del = QPushButton("×")
            btn_del.setFixedSize(18, 18)
            btn_del.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
            btn_del.setStyleSheet("background: transparent; color: #EF4444; border: none; font-size: 13px; font-weight: bold;")
            btn_del.clicked.connect(lambda _, n=clip_name: self._delete_clip_by_name(n))
            c_lay.addWidget(btn_del)

            row = idx // 3
            col = idx % 3
            self.clips_grid.addWidget(chip, row, col)

    def _refresh_studios_grid(self):
        while self.studios_grid.count():
            item = self.studios_grid.takeAt(0)
            if item.widget():
                item.widget().deleteLater()

        with db.get_session() as session:
            clients = session.scalars(select(Client).order_by(Client.created_at.desc())).all()

            if not clients:
                empty = QLabel("هنوز استودیویی ثبت نشده است.")
                empty.setStyleSheet("color: #64748B; font-size: 11.5px; border: none;")
                self.studios_grid.addWidget(empty, 0, 0)
                return

            for idx, c in enumerate(clients):
                card = QFrame()
                card.setStyleSheet("""
                    QFrame {
                        background-color: #161F30;
                        border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08);
                        border-radius: 6px;
                    }
                    QFrame:hover { border-color: rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.4); }
                """)
                c_lay = QHBoxLayout(card)
                c_lay.setContentsMargins(10, 6, 8, 6)
                c_lay.setSpacing(6)

                info_col = QVBoxLayout()
                info_col.setSpacing(1)

                display_name = c.company or c.full_name
                lbl_name = QLabel(f"🏢 <b>{display_name}</b>")
                lbl_name.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; color: #F8FAFC; font-size: 11.5px; border: none;")
                info_col.addWidget(lbl_name)

                if c.company and c.full_name and c.company != c.full_name:
                    lbl_sub = QLabel(f"مدیریت: {c.full_name}")
                    lbl_sub.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; color: #94A3B8; font-size: 10px; border: none;")
                    info_col.addWidget(lbl_sub)

                c_lay.addLayout(info_col, stretch=1)

                btn_del = QPushButton("×")
                btn_del.setFixedSize(22, 22)
                btn_del.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
                btn_del.setStyleSheet("background: transparent; color: #EF4444; border: none; font-size: 15px; font-weight: bold;")
                btn_del.clicked.connect(lambda _, cid=c.id: self._delete_studio_by_id(cid))
                c_lay.addWidget(btn_del)

                row = idx // 2
                col = idx % 2
                self.studios_grid.addWidget(card, row, col)

    def _refresh_amounts_grid(self):
        while self.amounts_grid.count():
            item = self.amounts_grid.takeAt(0)
            if item.widget():
                item.widget().deleteLater()

        amounts = self.settings.get("quick_amounts", {})
        if not amounts:
            empty = QLabel("هنوز مبلغ سریعی ثبت نشده است.")
            empty.setStyleSheet("color: #64748B; font-size: 11.5px; border: none;")
            self.amounts_grid.addWidget(empty, 0, 0)
            return

        for idx, (name, val) in enumerate(amounts.items()):
            chip = QFrame()
            chip.setStyleSheet("""
                QFrame {
                    background-color: #161F30;
                    border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08);
                    border-radius: 6px;
                }
                QFrame:hover {
                    border-color: rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.4);
                    background-color: #1A253A;
                }
            """)
            c_lay = QHBoxLayout(chip)
            c_lay.setContentsMargins(10, 6, 8, 6)
            c_lay.setSpacing(6)

            lbl_title = QLabel(f"💳 <b>{name}</b>: <span style='color:#10B981;'>{format_toman(val)}</span>")
            lbl_title.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; color: #F8FAFC; font-size: 11.5px; border: none;")
            c_lay.addWidget(lbl_title, stretch=1)

            btn_edit = QPushButton("✎")
            btn_edit.setFixedSize(22, 22)
            btn_edit.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
            btn_edit.setStyleSheet("background: transparent; color: #10B981; border: none; font-size: 12px; font-weight: bold;")
            btn_edit.clicked.connect(lambda _, n=name, v=val: self._edit_amount(n, v))
            c_lay.addWidget(btn_edit)

            btn_del = QPushButton("×")
            btn_del.setFixedSize(22, 22)
            btn_del.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
            btn_del.setStyleSheet("background: transparent; color: #EF4444; border: none; font-size: 15px; font-weight: bold;")
            btn_del.clicked.connect(lambda _, n=name: self._delete_amount(n))
            c_lay.addWidget(btn_del)

            row = idx // 2
            col = idx % 2
            self.amounts_grid.addWidget(chip, row, col)

    def _create_new_amount(self):
        diag = QuickAmountDialog("", 0, self.window())
        if diag.exec():
            if "quick_amounts" not in self.settings:
                self.settings["quick_amounts"] = {}
            self.settings["quick_amounts"][diag.amount_name] = diag.amount_val
            self._persist_settings()
            self._refresh_amounts_grid()
            global_signals.show_toast.emit(f"مبلغ سریع «{diag.amount_name}» با موفقیت اضافه شد.", "success")

    def _edit_amount(self, old_name: str, old_val: int):
        diag = QuickAmountDialog(old_name, old_val, self.window())
        if diag.exec():
            if diag.amount_name != old_name and old_name in self.settings.get("quick_amounts", {}):
                del self.settings["quick_amounts"][old_name]
            self.settings["quick_amounts"][diag.amount_name] = diag.amount_val
            self._persist_settings()
            self._refresh_amounts_grid()
            global_signals.show_toast.emit("مبلغ سریع با موفقیت ویرایش شد.", "success")

    def _delete_amount(self, amount_name: str):
        if StudioConfirmDialog.ask(
            self.window(),
            title="تأیید حذف مبلغ",
            message=f"آیا از حذف مبلغ سریع «{amount_name}» اطمینان دارید؟",
            confirm_text="حذف مبلغ",
            is_danger=True,
            icon_name="trash"
        ):
            if "quick_amounts" in self.settings and amount_name in self.settings["quick_amounts"]:
                del self.settings["quick_amounts"][amount_name]
                self._persist_settings()
                self._refresh_amounts_grid()
                global_signals.show_toast.emit(f"مبلغ «{amount_name}» حذف گردید.", "info")

    def _create_new_package(self):
        avail_clips = self.settings.get("custom_clips", [])
        diag = QuickPackageDialog("", [], avail_clips, self.window())
        if diag.exec():
            if "packages" not in self.settings:
                self.settings["packages"] = {}
            self.settings["packages"][diag.package_name] = diag.selected_clips
            self._persist_settings()
            self._refresh_packages_grid()
            global_signals.show_toast.emit(f"پکیج «{diag.package_name}» با موفقیت ایجاد شد.", "success")

    def _edit_package(self, pkg_name: str, current_clips: list):
        avail_clips = self.settings.get("custom_clips", [])
        diag = QuickPackageDialog(pkg_name, current_clips, avail_clips, self.window())
        if diag.exec():
            if diag.package_name != pkg_name and pkg_name in self.settings.get("packages", {}):
                del self.settings["packages"][pkg_name]
            self.settings["packages"][diag.package_name] = diag.selected_clips
            self._persist_settings()
            self._refresh_packages_grid()
            global_signals.show_toast.emit("پکیج با موفقیت به‌روزرسانی شد.", "success")

    def _delete_package(self, pkg_name: str):
        if StudioConfirmDialog.ask(
            self.window(),
            title="تأیید حذف پکیج",
            message=f"آیا از حذف پکیج سریع «{pkg_name}» اطمینان دارید؟",
            confirm_text="حذف پکیج",
            is_danger=True,
            icon_name="trash"
        ):
            if "packages" in self.settings and pkg_name in self.settings["packages"]:
                del self.settings["packages"][pkg_name]
                self._persist_settings()
                self._refresh_packages_grid()
                global_signals.show_toast.emit(f"پکیج «{pkg_name}» حذف شد.", "info")

    def _add_clip(self):
        txt = self.input_new_clip.text().strip()
        if txt and txt not in self.settings.get("custom_clips", []):
            if "custom_clips" not in self.settings:
                self.settings["custom_clips"] = []
            self.settings["custom_clips"].append(txt)
            self.input_new_clip.clear()
            self._persist_settings()
            self._refresh_clips_grid()
            global_signals.show_toast.emit(f"کلیپ «{txt}» به بانک عناوین اضافه شد.", "success")

    def _delete_clip_by_name(self, clip_name: str):
        if "custom_clips" in self.settings and clip_name in self.settings["custom_clips"]:
            self.settings["custom_clips"].remove(clip_name)
            self._persist_settings()
            self._refresh_clips_grid()
            global_signals.show_toast.emit(f"کلیپ «{clip_name}» حذف گردید.", "info")

    def _add_studio_to_db(self):
        comp = self.input_studio_company.text().strip()
        manager = self.input_studio_manager.text().strip()
        if not comp and not manager:
            StudioConfirmDialog.ask(self.window(), "خطا در ثبت", "لطفاً نام آتلیه یا نام مدیریت را وارد کنید.", icon_name="alert")
            return

        with db.get_session() as session:
            client = Client(full_name=manager or comp, company=comp or manager)
            session.add(client)
            session.commit()
            client_id = client.id

        self.input_studio_company.clear()
        self.input_studio_manager.clear()
        self._refresh_studios_grid()
        global_signals.client_balance_updated.emit(client_id)
        global_signals.settings_updated.emit()
        global_signals.show_toast.emit(f"استودیوی «{comp or manager}» با موفقیت ثبت شد.", "success")

    def _delete_studio_by_id(self, client_id: int):
        with db.get_session() as session:
            client = session.get(Client, client_id)
            if not client:
                return

            client_name = client.company or client.full_name
            projects_count = len(client.projects)

            details_text = f"توجه: تعداد {to_persian_numbers(projects_count)} پروژه به این استودیو متصل هستند و به استودیوی «بدون استودیو / متفرقه» منتقل می‌شوند." if projects_count > 0 else ""

            if not StudioConfirmDialog.ask(
                self.window(),
                title="تأیید حذف استودیو",
                message=f"آیا از حذف استودیو «{client_name}» اطمینان دارید؟",
                details=details_text,
                confirm_text="حذف استودیو",
                is_danger=True,
                icon_name="trash"
            ):
                return

            fallback_client = session.scalars(
                select(Client).where((Client.company == "بدون استودیو / متفرقه") | (Client.full_name == "استودیوی عمومی"))
            ).first()

            if not fallback_client:
                fallback_client = Client(
                    full_name="استودیوی عمومی",
                    company="بدون استودیو / متفرقه",
                    notes="استودیوی سیستمی جهت نگهداری پروژه‌های بدون صاحب"
                )
                session.add(fallback_client)
                session.flush()

            fallback_id = fallback_client.id

            if client.id == fallback_id:
                StudioConfirmDialog.ask(self.window(), "عملیات غیرمجاز", "امکان حذف استودیوی پیش‌فرض سیستمی وجود ندارد.", icon_name="alert")
                return

            session.execute(
                text("UPDATE projects SET client_id = :new_id WHERE client_id = :old_id"),
                {"new_id": fallback_id, "old_id": client_id}
            )
            session.execute(
                text("UPDATE transactions SET client_id = :new_id WHERE client_id = :old_id"),
                {"new_id": fallback_id, "old_id": client_id}
            )

            client.projects.clear()
            client.transactions.clear()

            session.delete(client)
            session.commit()

        self._refresh_studios_grid()
        global_signals.client_balance_updated.emit(fallback_id)
        global_signals.project_updated.emit(0)
        global_signals.settings_updated.emit()
        global_signals.show_toast.emit(f"استودیوی «{client_name}» حذف شد.", "info")

    def _browse_default_drive(self):
        folder = QFileDialog.getExistingDirectory(self, "انتخاب پوشه پیش‌فرض ذخیره‌سازی")
        if folder:
            self.input_default_drive.setText(folder)
            self.settings["default_drive_path"] = folder
            self._persist_settings()
            global_signals.show_toast.emit("مسیر پیش‌فرض هارد راش‌ها تنظیم شد.", "success")

    def _browse_music_vault_drive(self):
        current = self.input_music_drive.text()
        chosen = QFileDialog.getExistingDirectory(self, "انتخاب پوشه مقصد برای آرشیو موزیک", current)
        if chosen and chosen != current:
            if AudioVaultManager.set_custom_vault_path(chosen):
                self.input_music_drive.setText(str(Path(chosen).resolve()))
                self.settings["music_vault_path"] = str(Path(chosen).resolve())
                self._persist_settings()
                global_signals.show_toast.emit("مسیر آرشیو موزیک با موفقیت به‌روزرسانی شد.", "success")
            else:
                StudioConfirmDialog.ask(self.window(), "خطا", "خطا در تنظیم مسیر پوشه انتخابی.", icon_name="alert")

    def _backup_db(self):
        path, _ = QFileDialog.getSaveFileName(self, "ذخیره فایل پشتیبان دیتابیس", "CutterLog_Backup.db", "SQLite Database (*.db)")
        if path:
            if SettingsManager.backup_database(path):
                StudioConfirmDialog.ask(
                    self.window(),
                    title="پشتیبان‌گیری موفق",
                    message="نسخه پشتیبان پایگاه داده با موفقیت ذخیره شد.",
                    details=f"مسیر ذخیره:\n{path}",
                    confirm_text="متوجه شدم",
                    icon_name="check"
                )

    def _restore_db(self):
        path, _ = QFileDialog.getOpenFileName(self, "انتخاب فایل پشتیبان", "", "SQLite Database (*.db)")
        if path:
            if SettingsManager.restore_database(path):
                self._refresh_studios_grid()
                global_signals.settings_updated.emit()
                StudioConfirmDialog.ask(
                    self.window(),
                    title="بازیابی موفق",
                    message="اطلاعات پایگاه داده با موفقیت بازیابی شد.",
                    details="پیشنهاد می‌شود برای اعمال کامل تغییرات، برنامه را یک‌بار مجدداً راه‌اندازی کنید.",
                    confirm_text="متوجه شدم",
                    icon_name="check"
                )

    def _reset_settings(self):
        if StudioConfirmDialog.ask(
            self.window(),
            title="تأیید بازنشانی تنظیمات",
            message="آیا مطمئن هستید که می‌خواهید تمام تنظیمات به مقادیر اولیه بازگردند؟",
            details="پروژه‌ها، اطلاعات مالی و دایرکتوری استودیوها بدون تغییر باقی خواهند ماند.",
            confirm_text="بازنشانی تنظیمات",
            is_danger=True,
            icon_name="alert"
        ):
            SettingsManager.reset_settings_to_default()
            self.settings = SettingsManager.load_settings()
            self._persist_settings()
            self._refresh_packages_grid()
            self._refresh_clips_grid()
            self._refresh_studios_grid()
            self._refresh_amounts_grid()
            self._refresh_branding_previews()
            self.input_music_drive.setText(str(get_vault_root_dir().resolve()))
            self.txt_client_template.setPlainText(self.settings.get("client_message_template", DEFAULT_CLIENT_MESSAGE_TEMPLATE))
            global_signals.show_toast.emit("تمام تنظیمات به حالت اولیه کارخانه بازنشانی شدند.", "success")

    def _wipe_database(self):
        if StudioConfirmDialog.ask(
            self.window(),
            title="هشدار فوق‌العاده حساس: پاکسازی دیتابیس",
            message="آیا از پاکسازی کامل پایگاه داده اطمینان دارید؟",
            details="هشدار: تمام پروژه‌ها، کلیپ‌ها، دوره‌های اصلاحیه، لاگ‌ها، استودیوها و تراکنش‌های مالی برای همیشه حذف خواهند شد!",
            confirm_text="پاکسازی دائمی کل داده‌ها",
            is_danger=True,
            icon_name="trash"
        ):
            SettingsManager.wipe_entire_database()
            global_signals.project_deleted.emit(0)
            self._refresh_studios_grid()
            global_signals.settings_updated.emit()
            global_signals.show_toast.emit("پایگاه داده به‌صورت کامل پاکسازی و صفر شد.", "info")

from pathlib import Path
from PySide6.QtWidgets import (
    QFrame, QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout, QLabel, 
    QPushButton, QMenu, QSizePolicy
)

class ArchiveProjectCard(BaseProjectCard):
    """
    کارت فشرده و متراکم آرشیو با نمایش کامل عناوین فارسی
    """
    def __init__(self, project_data: dict, parent=None):
        project_id = project_data.get("id", 0)
        project_number = project_data.get("project_number", 1)
        
        super().__init__(
            project_id=project_id,
            project_number=project_number,
            parent=parent,
            accent_color="#10B981",
            card_type="archive"
        )
        self.project = project_data
        
        # افزایش ارتفاع به ۱۸۸ پیکسل جهت جلوگیری از برش خطوط
        self.setFixedHeight(188)
        self.setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy.Policy.Expanding, QSizePolicy.Policy.Fixed)
        self.card_layout.setContentsMargins(12, 10, 12, 10)
        self.card_layout.setSpacing(6)
        
        self.init_archive_content()

    def init_archive_content(self):
        # ۱. هدر: بج‌ها + منوی گزینه‌ها
        self.status_badge = StatusPillBadge(

                font-weight: 600;

            self.header_tags_layout.addWidget(rev_badge)

        rem_amount = self.project.get("remaining_amount", 0)
        is_settled = self.project.get("is_settled", False)

        self.header_tags_layout.addStretch()

        btn_more = QPushButton("⋮")
        btn_more.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)

            QPushButton {

            }

            }

        btn_more.clicked.connect(lambda: self._show_context_menu(btn_more))

            btn_pay = QPushButton("واریزی")
            btn_pay.setIcon(IconProvider.get_icon("dollar", "#10B981", size=11))
            btn_pay.setIconSize(QSize(11, 11))
            btn_pay.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
            btn_pay.setStyleSheet("""
                QPushButton {
                    font-family: 'Vazirmatn';
                    background-color: #161F30;
                    color: #10B981;
                    border: 1px solid rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.25);
                    border-radius: 5px;
                    padding: 3px 6px;
                    font-size: 10px;
                    font-weight: 600;
                }
                QPushButton:hover {
                    background-color: #10B981;
                    color: #FFFFFF;
                }
            """)
            btn_pay.clicked.connect(self._open_payment)
            foot_row.addWidget(btn_pay)

        foot_row.addStretch()

        btn_view = QPushButton("پرونده ←")
        btn_view.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        btn_view.setStyleSheet("""
            QPushButton {
                font-family: 'Vazirmatn';
                background-color: transparent;
                color: #6366F1;
                border: none;
                font-size: 10.5px;
                font-weight: 700;
                padding: 2px 2px;
            }
            QPushButton:hover { color: #818CF8; }
        """)
        btn_view.clicked.connect(self._open_details)
        foot_row.addWidget(btn_view)

        self.card_layout.addLayout(foot_row)
        self.clicked.connect(lambda _: self._open_details())

    def _show_context_menu(self, parent_btn: QPushButton):
        menu = QMenu(self)
        menu.setLayoutDirection(Qt.LayoutDirection.RightToLeft)

        act_unarchive = menu.addAction("بازگردانی به پروژه‌های فعال 🔄")
        act_delete = menu.addAction("حذف کامل پروژه 🗑️")

        action = menu.exec(parent_btn.mapToGlobal(parent_btn.rect().bottomLeft()))
        if action == act_unarchive:
            self._unarchive_project()
        elif action == act_delete:
            self._delete_project()

    def _unarchive_project(self):
        couple = self.project.get('couple_names') or 'پروژه'
        if StudioConfirmDialog.ask(
            self.window(),
            title="تأیید خروج از آرشیو",
            message=f"آیا می‌خواهید پرونده «{couple}» مجدداً به بخش پروژه‌های فعال بازگردد؟",
            details="پروژه به صفحه اصلی و لیست در حال انجام اضافه خواهد شد.",
            confirm_text="بازگردانی به فعال",
            is_danger=False,
            icon_name="revision"
        ):
            ProjectController.unarchive_project(self.project_id)

    def _delete_project(self):
        couple = self.project.get('couple_names') or 'پروژه'
        if StudioConfirmDialog.ask(
            self.window(),
            title="حذف کامل پروژه",
            message=f"آیا از حذف کامل پرونده «{couple}» اطمینان دارید؟",
            details="هشدار: تمام کلیپ‌ها، دوره‌های اصلاحیه، لاگ‌ها و تراکنش‌های مالی متصل به این پروژه برای همیشه پاک خواهند شد.",
            confirm_text="حذف دائمی",
            is_danger=True,
            icon_name="trash"
        ):
            ProjectController.delete_project(self.project_id)

    def _open_new_revision(self):
        diag = NewRevisionDialog(self.project_id, self.window())
        diag.exec()

    def _open_payment(self):
        proj_id = self.project_id
        win = self.window()
        QTimer.singleShot(0, lambda: NewTransactionDialog(proj_id, win).exec())

    def _open_passport(self):
        proj_id = self.project_id
        win = self.window()
        QTimer.singleShot(0, lambda: ProjectPassportDialog(proj_id, win).exec())

    def _open_details(self):
        proj_id = self.project_id
        win = self.window()
        QTimer.singleShot(0, lambda: ProjectDetailsDialog(proj_id, win).exec())

    def _open_folder(self):
        path_str = self.project.get("primary_path")
        if not path_str or not Path(path_str).exists():
            global_signals.show_toast.emit("مسیر پوشه مشخص نشده یا هارد متصل نیست", "error")
            return
        
        couple_name = self.project.get("couple_names") or self.project.get("title", "پروژه")
        win = self.window()
        diag = ProjectMediaExplorerDialog(folder_path=path_str, project_title=couple_name, parent=win)
        diag.exec()

from PySide6.QtWidgets import (
    QFrame, QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout, QLabel, QStyleOption, QStyle
)
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt, Signal, QRectF, QSize
from PySide6.QtGui import QPainter, QColor, QFont, QFontMetrics, QMouseEvent


class BaseProjectCard(QFrame):
    clicked = Signal(int)

    def __init__(
        self,
        project_id: int,
        project_number: int,
        parent=None,
        accent_color: str = "#6366F1",
        card_type: str = "project"
    ):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.project_id = project_id
        self.project_number = project_number
        self.accent_color = accent_color
        self.card_type = card_type
        
        self.formatted_number = f"{self.project_number:03d}" if self.project_number < 1000 else str(self.project_number)
        self.is_hovered = False
        
        if self.card_type == "dashboard":
            self.base_wm_opacity = 0.040
            self.hover_wm_opacity = 0.080
            self.card_fixed_height = 145
        elif self.card_type == "archive":
            self.base_wm_opacity = 0.035
            self.hover_wm_opacity = 0.070
            self.card_fixed_height = 188
        else:
            self.base_wm_opacity = 0.040
            self.hover_wm_opacity = 0.080
            self.card_fixed_height = 230

        self.current_wm_opacity = self.base_wm_opacity

        self.setObjectName("BaseCardFrame")
        self.setLayoutDirection(Qt.LayoutDirection.RightToLeft)
        self.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        self.setAttribute(Qt.WidgetAttribute.WA_Hover, True)
        self.setFixedHeight(self.card_fixed_height)

        self.card_layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        self.card_layout.setContentsMargins(18, 14, 18, 14)
        self.card_layout.setSpacing(8)

        self._setup_base_ui()
        self._apply_base_styles()

    def sizeHint(self) -> QSize:
        return QSize(360, self.card_fixed_height)

    def minimumSizeHint(self) -> QSize:
        return QSize(280, self.card_fixed_height)

    def _setup_base_ui(self):
        self.header_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        self.header_layout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        self.header_layout.setSpacing(8)

        self.lbl_number_badge = QLabel(self.formatted_number)
        self.lbl_number_badge.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        self.lbl_number_badge.setFixedHeight(24)
        self.lbl_number_badge.setMinimumWidth(38)
        self.lbl_number_badge.setStyleSheet("""
            QLabel {
                font-family: 'Segoe UI', 'Vazirmatn', sans-serif;
                font-size: 11px;
                font-weight: 800;
                color: #A5B4FC;
                background-color: rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.12);
                border: 1px solid rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.25);
                border-radius: 6px;
                padding: 0 6px;
                letter-spacing: 0.5px;
            }
        """)
        self.header_layout.addWidget(self.lbl_number_badge)

        self.header_tags_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        self.header_tags_layout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        self.header_tags_layout.setSpacing(6)
        self.header_layout.addLayout(self.header_tags_layout, stretch=1)

        self.card_layout.addLayout(self.header_layout)

    def _apply_base_styles(self):
        self.setStyleSheet("""
            QFrame#BaseCardFrame {
                background-color: #121826;
                border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.06);
                border-radius: 12px;
            }
            QFrame#BaseCardFrame:hover {
                background-color: #161F30;
                border: 1px solid rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.40);
            }
        """)

    def paintEvent(self, event):
        opt = QStyleOption()
        opt.initFrom(self)
        painter = QPainter(self)
        self.style().drawPrimitive(QStyle.PrimitiveElement.PE_Widget, opt, painter, self)

        painter.setRenderHint(QPainter.RenderHint.Antialiasing, True)
        painter.setRenderHint(QPainter.RenderHint.TextAntialiasing, True)

        wm_color = QColor(self.accent_color)
        wm_color.setAlphaF(self.current_wm_opacity)
        painter.setPen(wm_color)

        target_height = float(self.height())
        font = QFont("Segoe UI", 10, QFont.Weight.Black)
        font.setStyleStrategy(QFont.StyleStrategy.PreferAntialias)

        fm = QFontMetrics(font)
        if fm.capHeight() > 0:
            scale_factor = (target_height * 0.96) / fm.capHeight()
            font.setPointSizeF(10.0 * scale_factor)

        painter.setFont(font)

        full_fit_rect = QRectF(2.0, 0.0, float(self.width() * 0.70), target_height)
        painter.drawText(
            full_fit_rect,
            Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignLeft | Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignVCenter,
            self.formatted_number
        )
        painter.end()

    def enterEvent(self, event):
        self.is_hovered = True
        self.current_wm_opacity = self.hover_wm_opacity
        self.update()
        super().enterEvent(event)

    def leaveEvent(self, event):
        self.is_hovered = False
        self.current_wm_opacity = self.base_wm_opacity
        self.update()
        super().leaveEvent(event)

    def mousePressEvent(self, event: QMouseEvent):
        if event.button() == Qt.MouseButton.LeftButton:
            event.accept()
            return
        super().mousePressEvent(event)

    def mouseReleaseEvent(self, event: QMouseEvent):
        if event.button() == Qt.MouseButton.LeftButton:
            # کلیک فقط در صورتی اعمال می‌شود که روی المان‌های تعاملی داخلی نباشد
            child = self.childAt(event.pos())
            if child in (self, self.lbl_number_badge) or isinstance(child, QLabel):
                event.accept()
                self.clicked.emit(self.project_id)
                return
        super().mouseReleaseEvent(event)

from typing import List, Optional
from dataclasses import dataclass
from PySide6.QtWidgets import QWidget, QHBoxLayout, QPushButton, QFrame, QSizePolicy
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt, Signal, QPropertyAnimation, QEasingCurve, QRect, QTimer, QSize

from core.icon_provider import IconProvider


@dataclass
class DeckNavItem:
    title: str
    icon_name: str
    page_index: int
    group: int = 1


class MatteDeckTabButton(QPushButton):
    """تب کنسولی واکنش‌گرا با پشتیبانی از ۳ مود: فول، فشرده و مینی‌مال (فقط آیکون)"""
    def __init__(self, item: DeckNavItem, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.item = item
        self.is_active = False
        self.mode = "full"  # "full", "compact", "icon_only"

        self.setCheckable(True)
        self.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        self.setFocusPolicy(Qt.FocusPolicy.NoFocus)
        self.setFixedHeight(34)
        self.setToolTip(item.title)
        self.setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy.Policy.Preferred, QSizePolicy.Policy.Fixed)
        self._update_display()

    def set_active_state(self, active: bool):
        self.is_active = active
        self.setChecked(active)
        self._update_style()

    def set_responsive_mode(self, mode: str):
        if self.mode != mode:
            self.mode = mode
            self._update_display()

    def _update_display(self):
        if self.mode == "icon_only":
            self.setText("")
            self.setFixedWidth(36)
        elif self.mode == "compact":
            self.setText(f" {self.item.title}")
            self.setMinimumWidth(0)
            self.setMaximumWidth(16777215)
        else:  # full
            self.setText(f"  {self.item.title}")
            self.setMinimumWidth(0)
            self.setMaximumWidth(16777215)

        self._update_style()

    def _update_style(self):
        icon_color = "#818CF8" if self.is_active else "#64748B"
        self.setIcon(IconProvider.get_icon(self.item.icon_name, icon_color, size=15))
        self.setIconSize(QSize(15, 15))

        pad_x = 0 if self.mode == "icon_only" else (7 if self.mode == "compact" else 12)
        font_sz = 11.5 if self.mode == "compact" else 12

        if self.is_active:
            self.setStyleSheet(f"""
                QPushButton {{
                    font-family: 'Vazirmatn';
                    font-size: {font_sz}px;
                    font-weight: 800;
                    color: #F8FAFC;
                    background-color: #161F30;
                    border: none;
                    border-radius: 6px;
                    padding: 0 {pad_x}px;
                }}
            """)
        else:
            self.setStyleSheet(f"""
                QPushButton {{
                    font-family: 'Vazirmatn';
                    font-size: {font_sz}px;
                    font-weight: 500;
                    color: #8F909D;
                    background-color: transparent;
                    border: none;
                    border-radius: 6px;
                    padding: 0 {pad_x}px;
                }}
                QPushButton:hover {{
                    color: #F8FAFC;
                    background-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.04);
                }}
            """)


class CapsuleNavBar(QFrame):
    page_selected = Signal(int)

    # فهرست کامل تب‌های نوار ناوبری بالا
    ITEMS = [
        DeckNavItem("پیشخوان", "dashboard", 0, group=1),
        DeckNavItem("پروژه‌ها", "projects", 1, group=1),
        DeckNavItem("آرشیو", "archive", 2, group=1),
        DeckNavItem("موزیک", "music", 4, group=1),
        DeckNavItem("دستیار هوشمند", "sparkles", 7, group=2),
        DeckNavItem("زمان و راندمان", "play", 6, group=2),
        DeckNavItem("مالی", "dollar", 3, group=2),
        DeckNavItem("تنظیمات", "settings", 5, group=2),
    ]

    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.current_index = 0
        self.buttons: List[MatteDeckTabButton] = []
        self.dividers: List[QFrame] = []

        self.setObjectName("MatteDeckContainer")
        self.setLayoutDirection(Qt.LayoutDirection.RightToLeft)
        self.setFixedHeight(42)
        self.setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy.Policy.Preferred, QSizePolicy.Policy.Fixed)
        self.setStyleSheet("""
            QFrame#MatteDeckContainer {
                background-color: #0B0F17;
                border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.06);
                border-radius: 8px;
            }
        """)

        # خط شاخص متحرک نوری
        self.indicator_line = QFrame(self)
        self.indicator_line.setFixedHeight(2)
        self.indicator_line.setStyleSheet("background-color: #6366F1; border-radius: 1px;")
        self.indicator_line.setAttribute(Qt.WidgetAttribute.WA_TransparentForMouseEvents)

        self.anim = QPropertyAnimation(self.indicator_line, b"geometry")
        self.anim.setDuration(160)
        self.anim.setEasingCurve(QEasingCurve.Type.OutCubic)

        self.init_ui()

    def init_ui(self):
        self.main_layout = QHBoxLayout(self)
        self.main_layout.setContentsMargins(4, 3, 4, 3)
        self.main_layout.setSpacing(3)
        self.main_layout.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)

        current_grp = 1
        for item in self.ITEMS:
            if item.group != current_grp:
                current_grp = item.group
                div = QFrame()
                div.setFrameShape(QFrame.Shape.VLine)
                div.setFixedHeight(18)
                div.setStyleSheet("background-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.06); border: none;")
                self.main_layout.addWidget(div)
                self.dividers.append(div)

            btn = MatteDeckTabButton(item, self)
            btn.clicked.connect(lambda _, idx=item.page_index: self.select_index(idx, emit_signal=True))
            self.main_layout.addWidget(btn)
            self.buttons.append(btn)

        self.select_index(0, emit_signal=False, animate=False)

    def select_index(self, index: int, emit_signal: bool = True, animate: bool = True):
        self.current_index = index
        target_btn: Optional[MatteDeckTabButton] = None

        for btn in self.buttons:
            is_active = (btn.item.page_index == index)
            btn.set_active_state(is_active)
            if is_active:
                target_btn = btn

        if target_btn:
            self._reposition_indicator(target_btn, animate=animate)

        if emit_signal:
            self.page_selected.emit(index)

    def _reposition_indicator(self, btn: QPushButton, animate: bool = True):
        geo = btn.geometry()
        if geo.width() <= 0:
            return

        w = max(16, int(geo.width() * 0.65))
        x = geo.x() + int((geo.width() - w) / 2)
        y = self.height() - 3
        target_rect = QRect(x, y, w, 2)

        if animate:
            self.anim.stop()
            self.anim.setStartValue(self.indicator_line.geometry())
            self.anim.setEndValue(target_rect)
            self.anim.start()
        else:
            self.indicator_line.setGeometry(target_rect)

    def update_responsive_layout(self, window_width: int):
        if window_width < 1020:
            mode = "icon_only"
            spacing = 2
            margins = (3, 3, 3, 3)
        elif window_width < 1280:
            mode = "compact"
            spacing = 3
            margins = (4, 3, 4, 3)
        else:
            mode = "full"
            spacing = 4
            margins = (6, 3, 6, 3)

        for btn in self.buttons:
            btn.set_responsive_mode(mode)

        self.main_layout.setSpacing(spacing)
        self.main_layout.setContentsMargins(*margins)

        self.adjustSize()
        self.updateGeometry()

        QTimer.singleShot(20, lambda: self.select_index(self.current_index, emit_signal=False, animate=False))

    def showEvent(self, event):
        super().showEvent(event)
        QTimer.singleShot(40, lambda: self.select_index(self.current_index, emit_signal=False, animate=False))

    def resizeEvent(self, event):
        super().resizeEvent(event)
        QTimer.singleShot(20, lambda: self.select_index(self.current_index, emit_signal=False, animate=False))

from pathlib import Path
from PySide6.QtWidgets import (
    QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout, QLabel, 
    QPushButton, QFrame
)
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt, QUrl, QSize, QRectF
from PySide6.QtGui import QDesktopServices, QPixmap, QPainter, QPainterPath, QColor, QFont, QPen

from config import get_asset_path, get_bundle_dir
from core.icon_provider import IconProvider
from widgets.studio_dialog_base import StudioBaseDialog


def get_circular_avatar_pixmap(size: int = 68) -> QPixmap:
    """برش پرتره با فوکوس دقیق بر چهره و پشتیبانی از پکیج‌های چندسکویی"""
    candidate_paths = [
        get_asset_path("developer.jpg"),
        get_asset_path("developer.png"),
        get_asset_path("assets/developer.jpg"),
        get_asset_path("assets/developer.png"),
        get_asset_path("assets/images/developer.jpg"),
        get_bundle_dir() / "developer.jpg",
        get_bundle_dir() / "assets" / "developer.jpg",
        Path("developer.jpg"),
        Path("assets/developer.jpg"),
        Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "assets" / "developer.jpg",
    ]
    
    img_path = None
    for p in candidate_paths:
        try:
            if p.exists() and p.is_file():
                img_path = p
                break
        except Exception:
            continue

    target_pixmap = QPixmap(size, size)
    target_pixmap.fill(Qt.GlobalColor.transparent)

    painter = QPainter(target_pixmap)
    painter.setRenderHint(QPainter.RenderHint.Antialiasing, True)
    painter.setRenderHint(QPainter.RenderHint.SmoothPixmapTransform, True)

    inner_margin = 1.0
    draw_rect = QRectF(inner_margin, inner_margin, size - (inner_margin * 2), size - (inner_margin * 2))

    clip_path = QPainterPath()
    clip_path.addEllipse(draw_rect)
    painter.setClipPath(clip_path)

    painter.setPen(Qt.PenStyle.NoPen)
    painter.setBrush(QColor("#161F30"))
    painter.drawEllipse(draw_rect)

    if img_path:
        src = QPixmap(str(img_path.resolve()))
        if not src.isNull():
            sw, sh = src.width(), src.height()
            crop_size = int(min(sw, sh) * 0.90)
            center_x = int(sw * 0.48)
            center_y = int(sh * 0.36)

            crop_x = max(0, min(sw - crop_size, center_x - crop_size // 2))
            crop_y = max(0, min(sh - crop_size, center_y - crop_size // 2))

            cropped = src.copy(crop_x, crop_y, crop_size, crop_size)
            scaled = cropped.scaled(
                int(draw_rect.width()), int(draw_rect.height()),
                Qt.AspectRatioMode.KeepAspectRatioByExpanding,
                Qt.TransformationMode.SmoothTransformation
            )
            painter.drawPixmap(int(draw_rect.x()), int(draw_rect.y()), scaled)

            painter.setClipping(False)
            painter.setPen(QPen(QColor(99, 102, 241, 80), 1.2))
            painter.setBrush(Qt.BrushStyle.NoBrush)
            painter.drawEllipse(draw_rect)
            painter.end()
            return target_pixmap

    painter.setPen(QColor("#818CF8"))
    font = QFont("Segoe UI", int(size * 0.32), QFont.Weight.Bold)
    painter.setFont(font)
    painter.drawText(draw_rect, Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter, "MB")
    painter.end()
    return target_pixmap


class ContactActionRow(QFrame):
    def __init__(self, title: str, handle: str, icon_name: str, color_hex: str, url_target: str, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.url_target = url_target
        self.setLayoutDirection(Qt.LayoutDirection.RightToLeft)
        self.setFixedHeight(50)
        self.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        self.setObjectName("ContactActionRowFrame")

        layout = QHBoxLayout(self)
        layout.setContentsMargins(14, 6, 14, 6)
        layout.setSpacing(12)
        layout.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignVCenter)

        icon_lbl = QLabel()
        icon_lbl.setPixmap(IconProvider.get_pixmap(icon_name, color_hex, size=18))
        layout.addWidget(icon_lbl)

        info_col = QVBoxLayout()
        info_col.setSpacing(1)
        lbl_title = QLabel(title)
        lbl_title.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 11px; color: #8F909D; border: none; background: transparent;")
        lbl_handle = QLabel(handle)
        lbl_handle.setStyleSheet("font-family: -apple-system, 'Segoe UI', 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 12.5px; font-weight: 700; color: #F8FAFC; border: none; background: transparent;")
        info_col.addWidget(lbl_title)
        info_col.addWidget(lbl_handle)
        layout.addLayout(info_col, stretch=1)

        btn_open = QLabel("↗")
        btn_open.setStyleSheet(f"font-family: -apple-system, 'Segoe UI'; font-size: 13px; font-weight: bold; color: {color_hex}; border: none; background: transparent;")
        layout.addWidget(btn_open)

        self.setStyleSheet(f"""
            QFrame#ContactActionRowFrame {{
                background-color: #161F30;
                border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.06);
                border-radius: 8px;
            }}
            QFrame#ContactActionRowFrame:hover {{
                background-color: #1A253A;
                border-color: {color_hex}55;
            }}
        """)

    def mouseReleaseEvent(self, event):
        if event.button() == Qt.MouseButton.LeftButton:
            event.accept()
            QDesktopServices.openUrl(QUrl(self.url_target))
            return
        super().mouseReleaseEvent(event)

from PySide6.QtWidgets import (
    QFrame, QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout, QLabel, QPushButton, QSizePolicy
)
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt, Signal
from core.copilot_engine import CopilotEngine
from core.icon_provider import IconProvider
from core.signals import global_signals


class CopilotBriefingCard(QFrame):
    """
    کادر فشرده و خلاصه دستیار هوشمند استودیو برای قرارگیری در کنار ساعت
    """
    open_insights_requested = Signal()

    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.setObjectName("CopilotBriefingCompactFrame")
        self.setLayoutDirection(Qt.LayoutDirection.RightToLeft)
        self.setFixedHeight(120)
        self.setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy.Policy.Expanding, QSizePolicy.Policy.Fixed)
        self.setStyleSheet("""
            QFrame#CopilotBriefingCompactFrame {
                background-color: #111624;
                border: 1px solid rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.22);
                border-radius: 12px;
            }
            QLabel {
                background: transparent;
                border: none;
                font-family: 'Vazirmatn';
            }
        """)
        self.init_ui()
        self.refresh_briefing()

    def init_ui(self):
        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        layout.setContentsMargins(14, 10, 14, 10)
        layout.setSpacing(6)

        # نوار بالای کادر دستیار
        top_row = QHBoxLayout()
        top_row.setSpacing(6)
        top_row.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignVCenter)

        lbl_icon = QLabel()
        lbl_icon.setPixmap(IconProvider.get_pixmap("sparkles", "#818CF8", size=15))
        top_row.addWidget(lbl_icon)

        lbl_title = QLabel("دستیار هوشمند (Copilot)")
        lbl_title.setStyleSheet("font-size: 12.5px; font-weight: 800; color: #F8FAFC;")
        top_row.addWidget(lbl_title)

        live_dot = QLabel("● زنده")
        live_dot.setStyleSheet("""
            font-size: 9px;
            font-weight: 700;
            color: #38BDF8;
            background-color: rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.12);
            border: 1px solid rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.25);
            border-radius: 4px;
            padding: 1px 5px;
        """)
        top_row.addWidget(live_dot)
        top_row.addStretch()

        btn_more = QPushButton("تحلیل جامع ←")
        btn_more.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        btn_more.setStyleSheet("""
            QPushButton {
                font-family: 'Vazirmatn';
                background-color: rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.12);
                color: #818CF8;
                border: 1px solid rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.3);
                border-radius: 5px;
                padding: 2px 8px;
                font-size: 10.5px;
                font-weight: 700;
            }
            QPushButton:hover {
                background-color: #6366F1;
                color: #FFFFFF;
            }
        """)
        btn_more.clicked.connect(self.open_insights_requested.emit)
        top_row.addWidget(btn_more)

        layout.addLayout(top_row)

        # ردیف‌های متنی بسیار خلاصه (حداکثر ۲ تا ۳ پیام کلیدی)
        self.points_container = QVBoxLayout()
        self.points_container.setSpacing(4)
        layout.addLayout(self.points_container)

    def refresh_briefing(self):
        while self.points_container.count():
            item = self.points_container.takeAt(0)
            w = item.widget()
            if w:
                w.deleteLater()

        briefing = CopilotEngine.generate_daily_briefing()
        bullets = briefing.get("bullet_points", [])

        # نمایش ۲ الی ۳ مورد از مهم‌ترین پیام‌ها برای حفظ تناسب ارتفاع
        for b in bullets[:2]:
            row = QHBoxLayout()
            row.setContentsMargins(6, 2, 6, 2)
            row.setSpacing(6)
            row.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignVCenter)

            icon_lbl = QLabel(b.get("icon", "•"))
            icon_lbl.setStyleSheet("font-size: 10.5px;")
            row.addWidget(icon_lbl)

            txt_lbl = QLabel(b.get("text", ""))
            txt_lbl.setWordWrap(False)
            txt_lbl.setStyleSheet("font-size: 11px; color: #CBD5E1; font-weight: 500;")
            row.addWidget(txt_lbl, stretch=1)

            pill_frame = QFrame()
            pill_frame.setStyleSheet("background-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.025); border-radius: 5px;")
            pill_frame.setLayout(row)
            self.points_container.addWidget(pill_frame)

from PySide6.QtWidgets import (
    QWidget, QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout, QLabel, QFrame, QScrollArea, QGridLayout, QPushButton

from PySide6.QtCore import Qt

class CopilotInsightsDialog(StudioBaseDialog):
    """دیالوگ تحلیلی جامع دستیار: وضعیت راندمان، خوش‌حسابی استودیوها و رادار ریسک"""
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent=parent)
        self.setMinimumSize(780, 620)
        self.resize(840, 680)
        self.init_ui()

    def init_ui(self):
        self.build_header(
            "مرکز هوش تحلیلی و دستیار استودیو (Copilot Insights)",
            "تحلیل عادات کاری، ارزیابی اعتبار مالی استودیوها و هشدارهای ریسک پایپ‌لاین",
            icon_name="sparkles",
            icon_color="#818CF8"
        )

        scroll = QScrollArea()
        scroll.setWidgetResizable(True)
        scroll.setFrameShape(QFrame.Shape.NoFrame)
        scroll.setStyleSheet("background: transparent; border: none;")

        container = QWidget()
        container.setStyleSheet("background: transparent;")
        c_lay = QVBoxLayout(container)
        c_lay.setContentsMargins(4, 4, 4, 4)
        c_lay.setSpacing(16)

        prod = CopilotEngine.get_productivity_insights()
        scores = CopilotEngine.get_studio_credit_scores()
        radar = CopilotEngine.get_pipeline_radar()

        # ۱. بخش راندمان و زمان طلایی
        lbl_s1 = QLabel("۱. تحلیل راندمان و ساعات طلایی تمرکز")
        lbl_s1.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 13px; font-weight: 800; color: #6366F1;")
        c_lay.addWidget(lbl_s1)

        card_prod = QFrame()
        card_prod.setStyleSheet("background-color: #121826; border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.06); border-radius: 8px; padding: 12px;")
        cp_lay = QVBoxLayout(card_prod)
        cp_lay.setSpacing(6)

        lbl_gold = QLabel(f"⏱️ بازه طلایی اوج بازدهی: <b style='color:#10B981;'>{prod.get('golden_hours_label')}</b>")
        lbl_gold.setStyleSheet("font-size: 12px; color: #F8FAFC;")
        cp_lay.addWidget(lbl_gold)

        lbl_foc = QLabel(f"🎯 شاخص تمرکز مفید روی تایم‌لاین: <b style='color:#38BDF8;'>{to_persian_numbers(prod.get('focus_ratio_pct', 0))}٪</b>")
        lbl_foc.setStyleSheet("font-size: 12px; color: #F8FAFC;")
        cp_lay.addWidget(lbl_foc)

        lbl_adv = QLabel(prod.get("efficiency_advice", ""))
        lbl_adv.setWordWrap(True)
        lbl_adv.setStyleSheet("font-size: 11px; color: #94A3B8; margin-top: 4px;")
        cp_lay.addWidget(lbl_adv)

        c_lay.addWidget(card_prod)

        # ۲. ارزیابی خوش‌حسابی استودیوها
        lbl_s2 = QLabel("۲. رتبه‌بندی و اعتبار مالی استودیوها (Studio Credit Score)")
        lbl_s2.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 13px; font-weight: 800; color: #6366F1;")
        c_lay.addWidget(lbl_s2)

        if not scores:
            empty_lbl = QLabel("استودیویی با پروژه‌های تحویل‌شده برای ارزیابی یافت نشد.")
            empty_lbl.setStyleSheet("color: #64748B; font-size: 11px;")
            c_lay.addWidget(empty_lbl)
        else:
            for s in scores:
                s_card = QFrame()
                s_card.setStyleSheet("background-color: #121826; border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.06); border-radius: 8px; padding: 10px;")
                s_lay = QVBoxLayout(s_card)
                s_lay.setSpacing(4)

                top_s = QHBoxLayout()
                lbl_name = QLabel(f"🏢 <b>{s['studio_name']}</b>")
                lbl_name.setStyleSheet("font-size: 12.5px; color: #F8FAFC;")
                top_s.addWidget(lbl_name, stretch=1)

                badge_score = QLabel(f"نمره {to_persian_numbers(s['credit_score'])}/۱۰۰ ({s['grade']}) - {s['tag']}")
                badge_score.setStyleSheet(f"background-color: {s['tag_color']}18; color: {s['tag_color']}; font-weight: bold; border-radius: 4px; padding: 2px 8px; font-size: 10.5px;")
                top_s.addWidget(badge_score)
                s_lay.addLayout(top_s)

                lbl_tip = QLabel(f"💡 توصیه دستیار: {s['action_tip']}")
                lbl_tip.setStyleSheet("font-size: 11px; color: #94A3B8;")
                s_lay.addWidget(lbl_tip)

                c_lay.addWidget(s_card)

        # ۳. رادار ریسک و ددلاین‌ها
        lbl_s3 = QLabel("۳. رادار ریسک و پروژه‌های نیازمند توجه فوری")
        lbl_s3.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 13px; font-weight: 800; color: #6366F1;")
        c_lay.addWidget(lbl_s3)

        if radar["total_risks_count"] == 0:
            no_risk = QLabel("هیچ ریسک، تأخیر یا بحرانی در پایپ‌لاین فعال وجود ندارد ✓")
            no_risk.setStyleSheet("color: #10B981; font-weight: bold; font-size: 11.5px;")
            c_lay.addWidget(no_risk)
        else:
            for r in radar["urgent_deadlines"]:
                r_box = QLabel(f"⚠️ {r['title']}: {r['warning']}")
                r_box.setStyleSheet("background-color: rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.12); color: #EF4444; border-radius: 6px; padding: 8px; font-size: 11.5px;")
                c_lay.addWidget(r_box)

            for a in radar["pending_review_alerts"]:
                a_box = QLabel(f"⏳ {a['title']}: {a['notice']}")
                a_box.setStyleSheet("background-color: rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.12); color: #F59E0B; border-radius: 6px; padding: 8px; font-size: 11.5px;")
                c_lay.addWidget(a_box)

        scroll.setWidget(container)
        self.container_layout.addWidget(scroll, stretch=1)

        # دکمه بستن
        btn_close = QPushButton("بستن")
        btn_close.setFixedHeight(36)
        btn_close.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        btn_close.setStyleSheet("background-color: #161F30; color: #94A3B8; border-radius: 6px; font-size: 12px;")
        btn_close.clicked.connect(self.accept)
        self.container_layout.addWidget(btn_close)

from PySide6.QtWidgets import QWidget, QHBoxLayout, QLabel, QPushButton
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt, Signal
from core.persian_utils import to_persian_numbers


class ModernNumberStepper(QWidget):
    """کنترلر عددی مدرن با دکمه‌های تفکیک‌شده مثبت و منفی و نمایش واضح عدد"""
    valueChanged = Signal(int)

    def __init__(self, min_val: int = 0, max_val: int = 100, initial_val: int = 0, unit: str = "روز", zero_text: str = None, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.min_val = min_val
        self.max_val = max_val
        self.current_val = initial_val
        self.unit = unit
        self.zero_text = zero_text
        self.setLayoutDirection(Qt.LayoutDirection.RightToLeft)
        self.init_ui()

    def init_ui(self):
        layout = QHBoxLayout(self)
        layout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        layout.setSpacing(6)

        # دکمه افزایش (+)
        self.btn_plus = QPushButton("+")
        self.btn_plus.setFixedSize(36, 36)
        self.btn_plus.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        self.btn_plus.setStyleSheet("""
            QPushButton {
                font-family: 'Vazirmatn';
                background-color: #242E42;
                color: #F8FAFC;
                border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08);
                border-radius: 8px;
                font-size: 16px;
                font-weight: 700;
            }
            QPushButton:hover {
                background-color: #6366F1;
                border-color: #6366F1;
                color: #FFFFFF;
            }
            QPushButton:pressed {
                background-color: #4F46E5;
            }
        """)
        self.btn_plus.clicked.connect(self._increment)
        layout.addWidget(self.btn_plus)

        # کادر نمایش عدد
        self.lbl_display = QLabel()
        self.lbl_display.setFixedHeight(36)
        self.lbl_display.setMinimumWidth(110)
        self.lbl_display.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        self.lbl_display.setStyleSheet("""
            QLabel {
                font-family: 'Vazirmatn';
                background-color: #161F30;
                color: #F8FAFC;
                border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08);
                border-radius: 8px;
                font-size: 13px;
                font-weight: 700;
                padding: 0 10px;
            }
        """)
        layout.addWidget(self.lbl_display)

        # دکمه کاهش (−)
        self.btn_minus = QPushButton("−")
        self.btn_minus.setFixedSize(36, 36)
        self.btn_minus.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        self.btn_minus.setStyleSheet("""
            QPushButton {
                font-family: 'Vazirmatn';
                background-color: #242E42;
                color: #F8FAFC;
                border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08);
                border-radius: 8px;
                font-size: 16px;
                font-weight: 700;
            }
            QPushButton:hover {
                background-color: #242E42;
                border-color: #EF4444;
                color: #EF4444;
            }
            QPushButton:pressed {
                background-color: #1A2234;
            }
        """)
        self.btn_minus.clicked.connect(self._decrement)
        layout.addWidget(self.btn_minus)

        self._update_display()

    def _update_display(self):
        if self.current_val == 0 and self.zero_text:
            self.lbl_display.setText(self.zero_text)
            self.lbl_display.setStyleSheet("""
                QLabel {
                    font-family: 'Vazirmatn';
                    background-color: #161F30;
                    color: #94A3B8;
                    border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08);
                    border-radius: 8px;
                    font-size: 12px;
                    font-weight: 600;
                    padding: 0 10px;
                }
            """)
        else:
            self.lbl_display.setText(f"{to_persian_numbers(self.current_val)} {self.unit}")
            self.lbl_display.setStyleSheet("""
                QLabel {
                    font-family: 'Vazirmatn';
                    background-color: #161F30;
                    color: #F8FAFC;
                    border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08);
                    border-radius: 8px;
                    font-size: 13px;
                    font-weight: 700;
                    padding: 0 10px;
                }
            """)

    def _increment(self):
        if self.current_val < self.max_val:
            self.current_val += 1
            self._update_display()
            self.valueChanged.emit(self.current_val)

    def _decrement(self):
        if self.current_val > self.min_val:
            self.current_val -= 1
            self._update_display()
            self.valueChanged.emit(self.current_val)

    def value(self) -> int:
        return self.current_val

    def setValue(self, val: int):
        self.current_val = max(self.min_val, min(self.max_val, val))
        self._update_display()

class AnimatedDigitCell(QWidget):
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.setFixedSize(12, 22)
        self._current_char = "۰"
        self._next_char = "۰"
        self._offset = 0.0

        self.anim = QPropertyAnimation(self, b"offset")
        self.anim.setDuration(240)
        self.anim.setEasingCurve(QEasingCurve.Type.OutCubic)

    def get_offset(self) -> float:
        return self._offset

    def set_offset(self, val: float):
        self._offset = val
        self.update()

    offset = Property(float, get_offset, set_offset)

    def set_char(self, new_char: str):
        if self._current_char == new_char:
            return
        self._next_char = new_char
        self.anim.stop()
        self.anim.setStartValue(0.0)
        self.anim.setEndValue(1.0)
        self.anim.finished.connect(self._on_anim_finished)
        self.anim.start()

    def set_char_instant(self, char: str):
        self._current_char = char
        self._next_char = char
        self._offset = 0.0
        self.update()

    def _on_anim_finished(self):
        try:
            self.anim.finished.disconnect(self._on_anim_finished)
        except Exception:
            pass
        self._current_char = self._next_char
        self._offset = 0.0
        self.update()

    def paintEvent(self, event):
        painter = QPainter(self)
        painter.setRenderHint(QPainter.RenderHint.Antialiasing, True)
        painter.setRenderHint(QPainter.RenderHint.TextAntialiasing, True)

        font = QFont("Vazirmatn", 11, QFont.Weight.Black)
        font.setStyleStrategy(QFont.StyleStrategy.PreferAntialias)
        painter.setFont(font)

        w, h = float(self.width()), float(self.height())

        if self._offset == 0.0:
            painter.setPen(QColor("#A5B4FC"))
            painter.drawText(QRectF(0, 0, w, h), Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter, self._current_char)
        else:
            old_y = -self._offset * h
            alpha_old = max(0, int(255 * (1.0 - self._offset)))
            painter.setPen(QColor(165, 180, 252, alpha_old))
            painter.drawText(QRectF(0, old_y, w, h), Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter, self._current_char)

            new_y = (1.0 - self._offset) * h
            alpha_new = min(255, int(255 * self._offset))
            painter.setPen(QColor(165, 180, 252, alpha_new))
            painter.drawText(QRectF(0, new_y, w, h), Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter, self._next_char)

        painter.end()


class TimeSegmentBlock(QFrame):
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.setFixedSize(32, 26)
        self.setStyleSheet("""
            QFrame {
                background-color: rgba(14, 20, 34, 0.90);
                border: 1px solid rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.22);
                border-radius: 5px;
            }
        """)
        layout = QHBoxLayout(self)
        layout.setContentsMargins(1, 0, 1, 0)
        layout.setSpacing(0)
        layout.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)

        self.digit1 = AnimatedDigitCell(self)
        self.digit2 = AnimatedDigitCell(self)
        layout.addWidget(self.digit1)
        layout.addWidget(self.digit2)


class StudioTimecodeWidget(QWidget):
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.setLayoutDirection(Qt.LayoutDirection.LeftToRight)
        self.setFixedHeight(28)
        self.setFixedWidth(132)
        self._colon_visible = True
        self.init_ui()

    def init_ui(self):
        layout = QHBoxLayout(self)
        layout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        layout.setSpacing(3)
        layout.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)

        self.block_hour = TimeSegmentBlock(self)
        layout.addWidget(self.block_hour)

        self.lbl_colon1 = QLabel(":")
        self.lbl_colon1.setFixedWidth(4)
        self.lbl_colon1.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        self.lbl_colon1.setStyleSheet("color: #6366F1; font-family: 'Segoe UI'; font-size: 13px; font-weight: 900; background: transparent; border: none;")
        layout.addWidget(self.lbl_colon1)

        self.block_min = TimeSegmentBlock(self)
        layout.addWidget(self.block_min)

        self.lbl_colon2 = QLabel(":")
        self.lbl_colon2.setFixedWidth(4)
        self.lbl_colon2.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        self.lbl_colon2.setStyleSheet("color: #6366F1; font-family: 'Segoe UI'; font-size: 13px; font-weight: 900; background: transparent; border: none;")
        layout.addWidget(self.lbl_colon2)

        self.block_sec = TimeSegmentBlock(self)
        layout.addWidget(self.block_sec)

    def set_time_string(self, time_str: str, animated: bool = True):
        digits = [c for c in time_str if c.isdigit()]
        if len(digits) < 6:
            return

        cells = [
            self.block_hour.digit1, self.block_hour.digit2,
            self.block_min.digit1, self.block_min.digit2,
            self.block_sec.digit1, self.block_sec.digit2
        ]

        for i, d in enumerate(digits):
            fa_char = to_persian_numbers(d)
            if animated:
                cells[i].set_char(fa_char)
            else:
                cells[i].set_char_instant(fa_char)

        self._colon_visible = not self._colon_visible
        colon_color = "#818CF8" if self._colon_visible else "rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.40)"
        self.lbl_colon1.setStyleSheet(f"color: {colon_color}; font-family: 'Segoe UI'; font-size: 13px; font-weight: 900; background: transparent; border: none;")
        self.lbl_colon2.setStyleSheet(f"color: {colon_color}; font-family: 'Segoe UI'; font-size: 13px; font-weight: 900; background: transparent; border: none;")


class DashboardWelcomeHeader(QFrame):
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)

        self.setLayoutDirection(Qt.LayoutDirection.RightToLeft)

    def init_ui(self):

        self.lbl_quote.setGraphicsEffect(self.opacity_effect)
        quote_row.addWidget(self.lbl_quote, stretch=1)

        text_col.addWidget(quote_card)
        layout.addLayout(text_col, stretch=1)

        # ۲. پاد ساعت استودیویی
        time_pod = QFrame()
        time_pod.setFixedWidth(175)
        time_pod.setStyleSheet("""

            }

        time_col.setContentsMargins(8, 6, 8, 6)
        time_col.setSpacing(2)
        time_col.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)

        top_meta = QHBoxLayout()
        top_meta.setContentsMargins(2, 0, 2, 0)
        top_meta.setSpacing(4)
        top_meta.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)

        dot_rec = QLabel("●")
        dot_rec.setStyleSheet("color: #EF4444; font-size: 7.5px; background: transparent; border: none;")
        top_meta.addWidget(dot_rec)

        lbl_tc = QLabel("TIMECODE • 25 FPS")
        lbl_tc.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Segoe UI', sans-serif; font-size: 8px; font-weight: 800; color: #818CF8; letter-spacing: 0.5px; background: transparent; border: none;")
        top_meta.addWidget(lbl_tc)
        time_col.addLayout(top_meta)

        self.timecode_widget = StudioTimecodeWidget(time_pod)
        time_col.addWidget(self.timecode_widget, alignment=Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)

        self.lbl_date = QLabel()
        self.lbl_date.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        self.lbl_date.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 9.5px; color: #94A3B8; font-weight: 600; border: none; background: transparent;")
        time_col.addWidget(self.lbl_date)

        layout.addWidget(time_pod)

    def paintEvent(self, event):
        painter = QPainter(self)
        painter.setRenderHint(QPainter.RenderHint.Antialiasing, True)

        rect = QRectF(0, 0, self.width(), self.height())
        path = QPainterPath()
        path.addRoundedRect(rect, 12, 12)
        painter.setClipPath(path)

        painter.fillRect(rect, QColor("#111624"))

        glow = QRadialGradient(QPointF(self.width() * 0.90, 0), self.width() * 0.55)
        glow.setColorAt(0.0, self._glow_color)
        glow.setColorAt(1.0, QColor(17, 22, 36, 0))
        painter.fillRect(rect, glow)

        painter.setClipping(False)
        painter.setPen(QPen(QColor(255, 255, 255, 14), 1.0))
        painter.setBrush(Qt.BrushStyle.NoBrush)
        painter.drawRoundedRect(QRectF(0.5, 0.5, self.width() - 1.0, self.height() - 1.0), 12, 12)
        painter.end()

    def _setup_timer(self):
        self.timer = QTimer(self)
        self.timer.timeout.connect(self._tick)
        self.timer.start(1000)

    def _tick(self):
        now = datetime.now()
        now_time = QTime.currentTime().toString("HH:mm:ss")
        self.timecode_widget.set_time_string(now_time, animated=True)

        greeting_text, icon_symbol, glow_col = self._get_time_greeting(now.hour)
        current_greeting_key = f"{greeting_text}_{icon_symbol}"
        if current_greeting_key != self._last_greeting_key:
            self._last_greeting_key = current_greeting_key
            self._glow_color = glow_col
            self._refresh_greeting_text()
            self.update()

        today_key = (now.year, now.month, now.day)
        if today_key != self._last_day_key:
            self._last_day_key = today_key
            today_j = jdatetime.date.today()
            self.lbl_date.setText(get_persian_date_readable(today_j))

    def _get_time_greeting(self, hour: int = None) -> tuple[str, str, QColor]:
        if hour is None:
            hour = datetime.now().hour
        if 5 <= hour < 11:
            return "صبح بخیر", "☀️", QColor(245, 158, 11, 40)
        elif 11 <= hour < 17:
            return "ظهر بخیر", "🌤️", QColor(56, 189, 248, 38)
        elif 17 <= hour < 21:
            return "عصر بخیر", "🌆", QColor(251, 146, 60, 42)
        else:
            return "شب بخیر", "🌙", QColor(99, 102, 241, 45)

    def _refresh_greeting_text(self):
        settings = SettingsManager.load_settings()
        editor_name = settings.get("editor_name") or "تدوین‌گر گرامی"
        greeting_text, icon_symbol, _ = self._get_time_greeting()
        self.lbl_greeting.setText(f"{greeting_text}، {editor_name} {icon_symbol}")

    def update_all(self):
        now = datetime.now()
        today_j = jdatetime.date.today()
        self._last_day_key = (now.year, now.month, now.day)
        
        greeting_text, icon_symbol, glow_col = self._get_time_greeting(now.hour)
        self._last_greeting_key = f"{greeting_text}_{icon_symbol}"
        self._glow_color = glow_col
        
        self._refresh_greeting_text()

        self.current_quote_idx = (today_j.day - 1) % len(EDITING_QUOTES)
        self.lbl_quote.setText(f"« {EDITING_QUOTES[self.current_quote_idx]} »")
        self.opacity_effect.setOpacity(1.0)

        self.lbl_date.setText(get_persian_date_readable(today_j))
        
        now_time = QTime.currentTime().toString("HH:mm:ss")
        self.timecode_widget.set_time_string(now_time, animated=False)
        self.update()
