# ==================================================
# FILE: widgets/dynamic_clip_bar.py
# ==================================================
from typing import List, Dict, Any
from PySide6.QtWidgets import QWidget, QToolTip
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt, QRectF, Signal
from PySide6.QtGui import QPainter, QColor, QBrush, QLinearGradient, QMouseEvent

from core.persian_utils import to_persian_numbers, gregorian_to_jalali
from core.project_controller import ProjectController


class DynamicClipOverviewBar(QWidget):
    """
    نوار پیشرفت سگمنتی تعاملی به سبک تایم‌لاین NLE (DaVinci / Premiere)
    امکان تغییر وضعیت مستقیم کلیپ‌ها با کلیک روی هر سگمنت
    """
    clip_toggled = Signal(int, bool)  # (clip_id, new_status)

    def __init__(self, clips_data: List[Dict[str, Any]] = None, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.clips = clips_data or []
        self.setFixedHeight(10)  # ارتفاع مناسب برای کلیک راحت
        self.setMouseTracking(True)
        self.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        self.hovered_idx = -1

    def set_clips(self, clips_data: List[Dict[str, Any]]):
        self.clips = clips_data or []
        self.update()

    def _get_segment_geometry(self, index: int) -> tuple[float, float, float, float]:
        w = float(self.width())
        h = float(self.height())
        total_clips = len(self.clips)
        if total_clips == 0:
            return 0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0
        
        spacing = 3.0 if total_clips <= 8 else 2.0
        total_spacing = spacing * (total_clips - 1)
        seg_w = max(4.0, (w - total_spacing) / total_clips)
        x = index * (seg_w + spacing)
        return x, 1.0, seg_w, h - 2.0

    def paintEvent(self, event):
        painter = QPainter(self)
        painter.setRenderHint(QPainter.RenderHint.Antialiasing, True)

        w = float(self.width())
        h = float(self.height())

        if not self.clips:
            painter.setPen(Qt.PenStyle.NoPen)
            painter.setBrush(QBrush(QColor("#161F30")))
            painter.drawRoundedRect(QRectF(0, 1.0, w, h - 2.0), 3, 3)
            painter.end()
            return

        total_clips = len(self.clips)
        for i, clip in enumerate(self.clips):
            x, y, seg_w, seg_h = self._get_segment_geometry(i)
            rect = QRectF(x, y, seg_w, seg_h)
            is_completed = clip.get("is_completed", False)

            grad = QLinearGradient(x, y, x, y + seg_h)
            if is_completed:
                if i == self.hovered_idx:
                    grad.setColorAt(0.0, QColor("#34D399"))
                    grad.setColorAt(1.0, QColor("#10B981"))
                else:
                    grad.setColorAt(0.0, QColor("#10B981"))
                    grad.setColorAt(1.0, QColor("#059669"))
            else:
                if i == self.hovered_idx:
                    grad.setColorAt(0.0, QColor("#3A4866"))
                    grad.setColorAt(1.0, QColor("#242E42"))
                else:
                    grad.setColorAt(0.0, QColor("#1E283D"))
                    grad.setColorAt(1.0, QColor("#121826"))

            painter.setPen(Qt.PenStyle.NoPen)
            painter.setBrush(QBrush(grad))
            painter.drawRoundedRect(rect, 2.5, 2.5)

        painter.end()

    def mouseMoveEvent(self, event: QMouseEvent):
        if not self.clips:
            return

        pos_x = event.position().x() if hasattr(event, "position") else event.pos().x()
        global_pos = event.globalPosition().toPoint() if hasattr(event, "globalPosition") else event.globalPos()

        total_clips = len(self.clips)
        hovered_idx = -1

        for i in range(total_clips):
            x, _, seg_w, _ = self._get_segment_geometry(i)
            if x <= pos_x <= (x + seg_w + 2.0):
                hovered_idx = i
                break

        if 0 <= hovered_idx < total_clips:
            if self.hovered_idx != hovered_idx:
                self.hovered_idx = hovered_idx
                self.update()

            clip = self.clips[hovered_idx]
            name = clip.get("name") or clip.get("clip_name", f"کلیپ {hovered_idx + 1}")
            is_completed = clip.get("is_completed", False)
            comp_dt = clip.get("completed_at")

            status_txt = "✓ تکمیل شده (کلیک برای بازگردانی)" if is_completed else "○ باقی‌مانده (کلیک برای ثبت تکمیل)"
            dt_txt = f"\nزمان تحویل: {gregorian_to_jalali(comp_dt)}" if (is_completed and comp_dt) else ""

            tip = (
                f"🎬 {name}\n"
                f"وضعیت: {status_txt}{dt_txt}"
            )
            QToolTip.showText(global_pos, tip, self)
        else:
            if self.hovered_idx != -1:
                self.hovered_idx = -1
                self.update()
                QToolTip.hideText()

        super().mouseMoveEvent(event)

    def leaveEvent(self, event):
        if self.hovered_idx != -1:
            self.hovered_idx = -1
            self.update()
            QToolTip.hideText()
        super().leaveEvent(event)

    def mousePressEvent(self, event: QMouseEvent):
        # مهار کلیک برای جلوگیری از نفوذ به کارت پس‌زمینه
        if event.button() == Qt.MouseButton.LeftButton:
            event.accept()
            return
        super().mousePressEvent(event)

    def mouseReleaseEvent(self, event: QMouseEvent):
        # تغییر وضعیت مستقیم کلیپ با مهار کامل رویداد
        if event.button() == Qt.MouseButton.LeftButton:
            event.accept()
            pos_x = event.position().x() if hasattr(event, "position") else event.pos().x()
            for i, clip in enumerate(self.clips):
                x, _, seg_w, _ = self._get_segment_geometry(i)
                if x <= pos_x <= (x + seg_w + 3.0):
                    clip_id = clip.get("id")
                    if clip_id:
                        new_res = ProjectController.toggle_clip_status(clip_id)
                        if new_res is not None:
                            clip["is_completed"] = new_res
                            self.clip_toggled.emit(clip_id, new_res)
                            self.update()
                    break
            return
        super().mouseReleaseEvent(event)

    def _load_clients(self):
        self.combo_client.clear()
        with db.get_session() as session:
            clients = session.scalars(select(Client).order_by(Client.created_at.desc())).all()
            for c in clients:
                display_name = c.company if c.company else c.full_name
                if c.company and c.full_name and c.company != c.full_name:
                    display_name += f" (مدیریت: {c.full_name})"
                self.combo_client.addItem(display_name, c.id)

    def _load_project_data(self):
        project = ProjectController.get_project_by_id(self.project_id)
        if not project:
            QMessageBox.critical(self, "خطا", "پروژه یافت نشد.")
            self.reject()
            return

        self.input_couple.setText(project.couple_names or "")
        current_fee = project.base_fee if (project.base_fee and project.base_fee > 0) else project.total_fee
        self.input_fee.setText(str(current_fee or 0))

        idx = self.combo_client.findData(project.client_id)
        if idx >= 0:
            self.combo_client.setCurrentIndex(idx)

        if project.event_date:
            j_date = jdatetime.datetime.fromgregorian(datetime=project.event_date).strftime("%Y/%m/%d")
            self.input_event_date.setText(to_persian_numbers(j_date))

        if project.deadline:
            j_dl = jdatetime.datetime.fromgregorian(datetime=project.deadline).strftime("%Y/%m/%d")
            self.input_deadline.setText(to_persian_numbers(j_dl))

        if project.drive_locations:
            self.input_path.setText(project.drive_locations[0].absolute_path)

        if project.nle_project_path:
            self.input_nle_path.setText(project.nle_project_path)

        active_clips = [c.clip_name for c in project.clips]
        for cb, name in self.clip_checkboxes:
            if name in active_clips:
                cb.setChecked(True)

    def _on_fee_changed(self, text_val: str):
        clean = normalize_to_english_digits(text_val)
        if clean.isdigit() and int(clean) > 0:
            self.lbl_fee_words.setText(number_to_persian_words(int(clean)))
        else:
            self.lbl_fee_words.setText("")

    def _on_event_date_changed(self, text_val: str):
        j_date = parse_jalali_text(text_val)
        if j_date:
            self.lbl_event_date_readable.setText(get_persian_date_readable(j_date))
        else:
            self.lbl_event_date_readable.setText("")

    def _on_deadline_changed(self, text_val: str):
        j_date = parse_jalali_text(text_val)
        if j_date:
            self.lbl_deadline_relative.setText(get_deadline_relative_text(j_date))
        else:
            self.lbl_deadline_relative.setText("")

    def _browse_folder(self):
        settings = SettingsManager.load_settings()
        default_dir = settings.get("default_drive_path", "")
        folder = QFileDialog.getExistingDirectory(self, "انتخاب پوشه راش‌ها", default_dir)
        if folder:
            self.input_path.setText(folder)

    def _browse_nle_file(self):
        file_path, _ = QFileDialog.getOpenFileName(
            self, "انتخاب فایل پروژه Premiere یا DaVinci", "",
            "NLE Projects (*.prproj *.drp);;Premiere Pro (*.prproj);;DaVinci Resolve (*.drp);;All Files (*.*)"
        )
        if file_path:
            self.input_nle_path.setText(file_path)

    def _submit(self):
        couple = self.input_couple.text().strip()
        if not couple:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "خطا", "نام عروس و داماد نمی‌تواند خالی باشد.")
            return

        client_id = self.combo_client.currentData()
        if not client_id:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "خطا", "لطفاً یک استودیو انتخاب کنید.")
            return

        fee_str = normalize_to_english_digits(self.input_fee.text().strip())
        fee = int(fee_str) if fee_str.isdigit() else 0

        event_j = parse_jalali_text(self.input_event_date.text())
        event_dt = datetime.combine(event_j.togregorian(), datetime.min.time()) if event_j else None

        deadline_j = parse_jalali_text(self.input_deadline.text())
        deadline_dt = datetime.combine(deadline_j.togregorian(), datetime.min.time()) if deadline_j else None

        path = self.input_path.text().strip()
        nle_path = self.input_nle_path.text().strip() or None

        selected_clips = [name for cb, name in self.clip_checkboxes if cb.isChecked()]
        if not selected_clips:
            selected_clips = ["فیلم کامل مراسم (Long Cut)"]

        update_data = {
            "title": f"پروژه {couple}",
            "couple_names": couple,
            "client_id": client_id,
            "base_fee": fee,
            "event_date": event_dt,
            "deadline": deadline_dt,
            "clips": selected_clips,
            "primary_path": path,
            "nle_project_path": nle_path
        }

        try:
            ProjectController.update_project(self.project_id, update_data)
            self.accept()
        except Exception as e:
            QMessageBox.critical(self, "خطا", f"خطا در ویرایش پروژه: {e}")

from PySide6.QtWidgets import QWidget, QVBoxLayout, QLabel, QPushButton
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt, Signal
from core.icon_provider import IconProvider


class ModernEmptyStateWidget(QWidget):
    """
    نمایش وضعیت خالی مینیمال، مدرن و متناسب با تم تاریک سیستم
    """
    action_clicked = Signal()

    def __init__(
        self, 
        title: str = "هیچ پروژه‌ای یافت نشد", 
        description: str = "برای شروع تدوین و ایجاد پایپ‌لاین، اولین پروژه را ثبت کنید.",
        button_text: str = "+ تعریف پروژه جدید",
        icon_name: str = "folder",
        parent=None
    ):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.setLayoutDirection(Qt.LayoutDirection.RightToLeft)
        
        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        layout.setContentsMargins(30, 40, 30, 40)
        layout.setSpacing(12)
        layout.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)

        # آیکون
        lbl_icon = QLabel()
        lbl_icon.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        lbl_icon.setPixmap(IconProvider.get_icon(icon_name, "#475569", size=42).pixmap(42, 42))
        layout.addWidget(lbl_icon)

        # عنوان
        lbl_title = QLabel(title)
        lbl_title.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        lbl_title.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 15px; font-weight: 800; color: #F8FAFC;")
        layout.addWidget(lbl_title)

        # توضیحات
        lbl_desc = QLabel(description)
        lbl_desc.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        lbl_desc.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 12px; color: #64748B; max-width: 380px;")
        lbl_desc.setWordWrap(True)
        layout.addWidget(lbl_desc)

        if button_text:
            layout.addSpacing(6)
            btn_action = QPushButton(button_text)
            btn_action.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
            btn_action.setFixedHeight(38)
            btn_action.setStyleSheet("""
                QPushButton {
                    font-family: 'Vazirmatn';
                    background-color: #6366F1;
                    color: #FFFFFF;
                    border: none;
                    border-radius: 8px;
                    padding: 0 20px;
                    font-size: 12.5px;
                    font-weight: 700;
                }
                QPushButton:hover {
                    background-color: #4F46E5;
                }
            """)
            btn_action.clicked.connect(self.action_clicked.emit)
            layout.addWidget(btn_action, alignment=Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)

from PySide6.QtWidgets import QFrame, QHBoxLayout, QLabel, QVBoxLayout
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt
from core.persian_utils import to_persian_numbers


class HeaderSummaryPill(QFrame):
    """
    کامپوننت استاندارد و مینیمال برای نمایش خلاصه وضعیت تعداد در هدر صفحات (پروژه‌ها و آرشیو)
    """
    def __init__(self, title: str, count: int = 0, sub_text: str = "", accent_color: str = "#6366F1", parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.setObjectName("HeaderSummaryPillFrame")
        self.setLayoutDirection(Qt.LayoutDirection.RightToLeft)
        self.accent_color = accent_color
        self.title_text = title
        self.sub_text = sub_text

        self.setFixedHeight(40)
        self.init_ui()
        self.set_count(count)

    def init_ui(self):
        layout = QHBoxLayout(self)
        layout.setContentsMargins(14, 0, 14, 0)
        layout.setSpacing(10)
        layout.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignVCenter)

        # نشانگر وضعیت بسیار ظریف (Status Dot Indicator)
        self.dot_indicator = QLabel()
        self.dot_indicator.setFixedSize(7, 7)
        self.dot_indicator.setStyleSheet(f"""
            background-color: {self.accent_color};
            border-radius: 3.5px;
            border: none;
        """)
        layout.addWidget(self.dot_indicator)

        # بخش عنوان و توضیح
        text_col = QVBoxLayout()
        text_col.setSpacing(0)
        text_col.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        text_col.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignVCenter)

        self.lbl_title = QLabel(self.title_text)
        self.lbl_title.setStyleSheet("""
            font-family: 'Vazirmatn';
            font-size: 11.5px;
            font-weight: 500;
            color: #8F909D;
            background: transparent;
            border: none;
        """)
        text_col.addWidget(self.lbl_title)

        if self.sub_text:
            self.lbl_sub = QLabel(self.sub_text)
            self.lbl_sub.setStyleSheet("""
                font-family: 'Vazirmatn';
                font-size: 9.5px;
                font-weight: 400;
                color: #64748B;
                background: transparent;
                border: none;
            """)
            text_col.addWidget(self.lbl_sub)

        layout.addLayout(text_col)

        # خط جداکننده داخلی ظریف
        divider = QFrame()
        divider.setFrameShape(QFrame.Shape.VLine)
        divider.setFixedHeight(18)
        divider.setStyleSheet("background-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08); border: none;")
        layout.addWidget(divider)

        # عدد اصلی و برجسته
        self.lbl_count = QLabel("۰")
        self.lbl_count.setStyleSheet(f"""
            font-family: 'Vazirmatn';
            font-size: 15px;
            font-weight: 800;
            color: {self.accent_color};
            background: transparent;
            border: none;
            padding-right: 2px;
        """)
        layout.addWidget(self.lbl_count)

        self.setStyleSheet("""
            QFrame#HeaderSummaryPillFrame {
                background-color: #121826;
                border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08);
                border-radius: 8px;
            }
            QFrame#HeaderSummaryPillFrame:hover {
                background-color: #161F30;
                border-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.12);
            }
        """)

    def set_count(self, count: int):
        self.lbl_count.setText(to_persian_numbers(count))

    import cv2
    HAS_CV2 = True
except ImportError:
    HAS_CV2 = False

def format_file_size_bytes(size_bytes: int) -> str:
    if size_bytes < 1024 * 1024:
        return f"{size_bytes / 1024:.1f} KB"
    elif size_bytes < 1024 * 1024 * 1024:
        return f"{size_bytes / (1024 * 1024):.1f} MB"
    else:
        return f"{size_bytes / (1024 * 1024 * 1024):.2f} GB"


def _gcd(a: int, b: int) -> int:
    while b:
        a, b = b, a % b
    return a if a > 0 else 1


def analyze_video_file(file_path: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
    path = Path(file_path)
    if not path.exists() or not path.is_file():
        return None

    size_bytes = path.stat().st_size
    ext = path.suffix.replace(".", "").upper()

    info = {
        "file_name": path.name,
        "file_path": str(path.resolve()),
        "ext": ext,
        "size_str": format_file_size_bytes(size_bytes),
        "resolution": "نامشخص",
        "width": 1920,
        "height": 1080,
        "fps": 25.0,
        "fps_str": "25 fps",
        "duration_sec": 0.0,
        "duration_str": "--:--",
        "total_frames": 0,
        "aspect_ratio": "16:9",
        "bitrate_mbps": 0.0,
        "timeline_match_warning": None,
        "slow_mo_info": None,
        "thumbnail_pixmap": None,
    }

    if not HAS_CV2:
        return info

    cap = None
    try:
        cap = cv2.VideoCapture(str(path.resolve()))
        if cap.isOpened():
            w = int(cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_WIDTH))
            h = int(cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_HEIGHT))
            fps = cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FPS)
            total_frames = int(cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_COUNT))

            info["width"] = w if w > 0 else 1920
            info["height"] = h if h > 0 else 1080
            info["total_frames"] = total_frames

            if w > 0 and h > 0:
                if w >= 3840 and h >= 2160:
                    info["resolution"] = f"4K UHD ({w} × {h})"
                elif w >= 1920 and h >= 1080:
                    info["resolution"] = f"Full HD ({w} × {h})"
                elif w >= 1280 and h >= 720:
                    info["resolution"] = f"HD ({w} × {h})"
                else:
                    info["resolution"] = f"{w} × {h}"

                gcd_val = _gcd(w, h)
                ar_w, ar_h = w // gcd_val, h // gcd_val
                if (ar_w, ar_h) in [(16, 9), (9, 16), (4, 3), (1, 1), (21, 9)]:
                    info["aspect_ratio"] = f"{ar_w}:{ar_h}"
                else:
                    info["aspect_ratio"] = f"{w/h:.2f}:1"

            if fps and fps > 0:
                info["fps"] = fps
                info["fps_str"] = f"{fps:.3f}".rstrip('0').rstrip('.') + " fps"

                rounded_fps = round(fps, 2)
                if rounded_fps in [29.97, 30.0, 59.94, 60.0]:
                    info["timeline_match_warning"] = "⚠️ فریم‌ریت NTSC (احتمال عدم تطابق با تایم‌لاین ۲۵/۵۰ فریم)"
                elif rounded_fps in [23.976, 24.0]:
                    info["timeline_match_warning"] = "🎬 فریم‌ریت سینمایی ۲۴ فریم"

                if rounded_fps >= 100:
                    speed_pct = int((25.0 / rounded_fps) * 100)
                    info["slow_mo_info"] = f"⚡ پتانسیل اسلوموشن {int(rounded_fps // 25)}X (سرعت {to_persian_numbers(speed_pct)}٪ در تایم‌لاین ۲۵ فریم)"
                elif rounded_fps >= 50:
                    speed_pct = int((25.0 / rounded_fps) * 100)
                    info["slow_mo_info"] = f"⚡ پتانسیل اسلوموشن ۲X (سرعت {to_persian_numbers(speed_pct)}٪ در تایم‌لاین ۲۵ فریم)"

                if total_frames > 0:
                    dur_sec = total_frames / fps
                    info["duration_sec"] = dur_sec
                    m = int(dur_sec // 60)
                    s = int(dur_sec % 60)
                    info["duration_str"] = f"{m:02d}:{s:02d}"

                    if dur_sec > 0:
                        mbps = (size_bytes * 8) / (dur_sec * 1_000_000)
                        info["bitrate_mbps"] = round(mbps, 1)

            ret, frame = cap.read()
            if ret and frame is not None:
                small = cv2.resize(frame, (180, 100), interpolation=cv2.INTER_AREA)
                rgb = cv2.cvtColor(small, cv2.COLOR_BGR2RGB)
                fh, fw, ch = rgb.shape
                qimg = QImage(rgb.data, fw, fh, ch * fw, QImage.Format.Format_RGB888).copy()
                info["thumbnail_pixmap"] = QPixmap.fromImage(qimg)

    except Exception:
        pass
    finally:
        if cap:
            cap.release()

    return info


class MediaInspectorDialog(StudioBaseDialog):
    """دیالوگ نمایش مشخصات فنی و تحلیل هوشمند فوتج با ساختار تایل‌های شیشه‌ای مدرن"""
    def __init__(self, data: Dict[str, Any], parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent=parent)
        self.data = data
        self.setFixedWidth(540)
        self.init_ui()

    def _make_clean_label(self, text: str, style: str = "") -> QLabel:
        lbl = QLabel(text)
        lbl.setStyleSheet(f"background: transparent !important; border: none !important; outline: none !important; {style}")
        return lbl

    def init_ui(self):
        self.build_header(
            "تحلیل فنی فوتج (Media Inspector)",
            "بررسی مشخصات رزولوشن، فریم‌ریت و بیت‌ریت ویدیو",
            icon_name="nle_launch",
            icon_color="#38BDF8"
        )

        # ۱. هدر نام فایل و مسیر
        top_card = QFrame()
        top_card.setStyleSheet("background-color: #121826; border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08); border-radius: 8px;")
        t_lay = QHBoxLayout(top_card)
        t_lay.setContentsMargins(12, 10, 12, 10)
        t_lay.setSpacing(12)

        if self.data.get("thumbnail_pixmap"):
            thumb_lbl = QLabel()
            thumb_lbl.setFixedSize(92, 54)
            thumb_lbl.setScaledContents(True)
            thumb_lbl.setPixmap(self.data["thumbnail_pixmap"])
            thumb_lbl.setStyleSheet("border-radius: 6px; border: 1px solid rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.3); background: transparent;")
            t_lay.addWidget(thumb_lbl)

        name_col = QVBoxLayout()
        name_col.setSpacing(3)
        lbl_name = self._make_clean_label(self.data.get("file_name", "فایل ویدیو"), "font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 13.5px; font-weight: 800; color: #F8FAFC;")
        name_col.addWidget(lbl_name)

        lbl_path = self._make_clean_label(self.data.get("file_path", ""), "font-family: 'Segoe UI'; font-size: 10px; color: #64748B;")
        lbl_path.setWordWrap(True)
        name_col.addWidget(lbl_path)
        t_lay.addLayout(name_col, stretch=1)

        self.container_layout.addWidget(top_card)

        # ۲. هشدارهای هوشمند فریم‌ریت و اسلوموشن
        if self.data.get("timeline_match_warning"):
            warn_box = QFrame()
            warn_box.setStyleSheet("background-color: rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.12); border: 1px solid rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.25); border-radius: 8px;")
            w_lay = QHBoxLayout(warn_box)
            w_lay.setContentsMargins(12, 8, 12, 8)
            lbl_w = self._make_clean_label(self.data["timeline_match_warning"], "font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 11.5px; font-weight: 600; color: #FBBF24;")
            w_lay.addWidget(lbl_w)
            self.container_layout.addWidget(warn_box)

        if self.data.get("slow_mo_info"):
            slow_box = QFrame()
            slow_box.setStyleSheet("background-color: rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.12); border: 1px solid rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.25); border-radius: 8px;")
            s_lay = QHBoxLayout(slow_box)
            s_lay.setContentsMargins(12, 8, 12, 8)
            lbl_s = self._make_clean_label(self.data["slow_mo_info"], "font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 11.5px; font-weight: 700; color: #34D399;")
            s_lay.addWidget(lbl_s)
            self.container_layout.addWidget(slow_box)

        # ۳. کارت مشخصات فنی
        specs_card = QFrame()
        specs_card.setStyleSheet("background-color: #121826; border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.06); border-radius: 8px;")
        s_grid = QVBoxLayout(specs_card)
        s_grid.setContentsMargins(16, 14, 16, 14)
        s_grid.setSpacing(9)

        items = [
            ("کیفیت و رزولوشن:", self.data.get("resolution", "—"), "#38BDF8"),
            ("فریم‌ریت دقیق (FPS):", to_persian_numbers(self.data.get("fps_str", "—")), "#818CF8"),
            ("نسبت تصویر (Aspect Ratio):", self.data.get("aspect_ratio", "—"), "#F8FAFC"),
            ("مدت زمان ویدیو:", to_persian_numbers(self.data.get("duration_str", "--:--")), "#FBBF24"),
            ("تعداد کل فریم‌ها:", to_persian_numbers(f"{self.data.get('total_frames', 0):,} فریم"), "#94A3B8"),
            ("بیت‌ریت ویدیو:", to_persian_numbers(f"{self.data.get('bitrate_mbps', 0)} Mbps") if self.data.get("bitrate_mbps") else "—", "#10B981"),
            ("فرمت و کانتینر:", self.data.get("ext", "RAW"), "#C084FC"),
            ("حجم فایل روی دیسک:", to_persian_numbers(self.data.get("size_str", "—")), "#F8FAFC"),
        ]

        for k, v, col in items:
            row = QHBoxLayout()
            lbl_k = self._make_clean_label(k, "font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 12px; color: #8F909D;")
            lbl_v = self._make_clean_label(str(v), f"font-family: 'Segoe UI', 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 12px; font-weight: 700; color: {col};")
            row.addWidget(lbl_k)
            row.addStretch()
            row.addWidget(lbl_v)
            s_grid.addLayout(row)

        self.container_layout.addWidget(specs_card)

        # ۴. دکمه‌ها
        btn_box = QHBoxLayout()
        btn_box.setSpacing(8)

        btn_copy = QPushButton("کپی مشخصات فوتج")
        btn_copy.setFixedHeight(38)
        btn_copy.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        btn_copy.setStyleSheet("""
            QPushButton {
                font-family: 'Vazirmatn';
                background-color: #6366F1;
                color: #FFFFFF;
                border: none;
                border-radius: 6px;
                font-weight: 700;
                font-size: 12px;
            }
            QPushButton:hover {
                background-color: #4F46E5;
            }
        """)
        btn_copy.clicked.connect(self._copy_specs)
        btn_box.addWidget(btn_copy, stretch=2)

        btn_close = QPushButton("بستن")
        btn_close.setFixedHeight(38)
        btn_close.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        btn_close.setStyleSheet("""
            QPushButton {
                font-family: 'Vazirmatn';
                background-color: #161F30;
                color: #94A3B8;
                border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08);
                border-radius: 6px;
                font-size: 12px;
                font-weight: 600;
            }
            QPushButton:hover {
                color: #F8FAFC;
                border-color: #6366F1;
            }
        """)
        btn_close.clicked.connect(self.accept)
        btn_box.addWidget(btn_close, stretch=1)

        self.container_layout.addLayout(btn_box)

    def _copy_specs(self):
        text = (
            f"🎬 فایل: {self.data.get('file_name')}\n"
            f"📐 رزولوشن: {self.data.get('resolution')}\n"
            f"⏱️ فریم‌ریت: {self.data.get('fps_str')}\n"
            f"⏳ زمان: {self.data.get('duration_str')}\n"
            f"💾 حجم: {self.data.get('size_str')}\n"
            f"📊 بیت‌ریت: {self.data.get('bitrate_mbps')} Mbps"
        )
        QApplication.clipboard().setText(text)
        global_signals.show_toast.emit("مشخصات فنی فوتج در کلیپ‌بورد کپی شد.", "success")


class MediaInspectorDropZone(QFrame):
    """
    کارت مدرن، شیشه‌ای و چشم‌نواز آنالیزور فوتج در پیشخوان (Drag & Drop Studio Inspector)
    """
    file_inspected = Signal(dict)

    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.setObjectName("MediaDropZoneFrame")
        self.setAcceptDrops(True)
        self.setLayoutDirection(Qt.LayoutDirection.RightToLeft)
        self.setFixedHeight(68)
        self.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        self.setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy.Policy.Expanding, QSizePolicy.Policy.Fixed)
        self._is_drag_active = False
        self.init_ui()

    def init_ui(self):
        layout = QHBoxLayout(self)
        layout.setContentsMargins(16, 10, 16, 10)
        layout.setSpacing(14)
        layout.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignVCenter)

        # ۱. آیکون کپسولی مانیتورینگ
        icon_frame = QFrame()
        icon_frame.setFixedSize(44, 44)
        icon_frame.setStyleSheet("""
            QFrame {
                background-color: rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.12);
                border: 1px solid rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.28);
                border-radius: 10px;
            }
        """)
        icon_layout = QVBoxLayout(icon_frame)
        icon_layout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        icon_layout.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)

        icon_lbl = QLabel()
        icon_lbl.setPixmap(IconProvider.get_pixmap("nle_launch", "#818CF8", size=20))
        icon_lbl.setStyleSheet("border: none; background: transparent;")
        icon_layout.addWidget(icon_lbl)
        layout.addWidget(icon_frame)

        # ۲. متن‌های عنوان و زیرعنوان همراه با تگ‌های پسوند
        info_col = QVBoxLayout()
        info_col.setSpacing(3)
        info_col.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignVCenter)

        title_row = QHBoxLayout()
        title_row.setSpacing(8)
        title_row.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignRight | Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignVCenter)

        lbl_title = QLabel("آنالیزور هوشمند فوتج و راش‌ها (Media Inspector)")
        lbl_title.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 13.5px; font-weight: 800; color: #F8FAFC; border: none; background: transparent;")
        title_row.addWidget(lbl_title)

        live_tag = QLabel("⚡ تشخیص آنی")
        live_tag.setStyleSheet("""
            QLabel {
                font-family: 'Vazirmatn';
                font-size: 9.5px;
                font-weight: 700;
                color: #38BDF8;
                background-color: rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.12);
                border: 1px solid rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.25);
                border-radius: 4px;
                padding: 1px 5px;
            }
        """)
        title_row.addWidget(live_tag)
        title_row.addStretch()
        info_col.addLayout(title_row)

        sub_row = QHBoxLayout()
        sub_row.setSpacing(6)
        sub_row.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignRight | Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignVCenter)

        lbl_sub = QLabel("ویدیو را به این کادر بکشید (Drag & Drop) یا برای تحلیل مشخصات کلیک کنید")
        lbl_sub.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 11px; color: #94A3B8; border: none; background: transparent;")
        sub_row.addWidget(lbl_sub)

        # تگ‌های فرمت پشتیبانی‌شده
        formats = ["MP4", "MOV", "ProRes", "BRAW", "MXF", "4K"]
        for fmt in formats:
            f_lbl = QLabel(fmt)
            f_lbl.setStyleSheet("""
                font-family: -apple-system, 'Segoe UI', sans-serif;
                font-size: 9px;
                font-weight: 700;
                color: #64748B;
                background-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.04);
                border-radius: 3px;
                padding: 1px 4px;
                border: none;
            """)
            sub_row.addWidget(f_lbl)

        sub_row.addStretch()
        info_col.addLayout(sub_row)
        layout.addLayout(info_col, stretch=1)

        # ۳. دکمه انتخاب فایل
        btn_select = QPushButton("انتخاب فوتج...")
        btn_select.setFixedHeight(34)
        btn_select.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        btn_select.setIcon(IconProvider.get_icon("search", "#FFFFFF", size=12))
        btn_select.setIconSize(QSize(12, 12))
        btn_select.setStyleSheet("""
            QPushButton {
                font-family: 'Vazirmatn';
                background-color: #6366F1;
                color: #FFFFFF;
                border: none;
                border-radius: 7px;
                padding: 0 16px;
                font-size: 11.5px;
                font-weight: 700;
            }
            QPushButton:hover {
                background-color: #4F46E5;
            }
        """)
        btn_select.clicked.connect(self._select_file_dialog)
        layout.addWidget(btn_select)

    def paintEvent(self, event):
        painter = QPainter(self)
        painter.setRenderHint(QPainter.RenderHint.Antialiasing, True)

        rect = QRectF(0, 0, self.width(), self.height())
        path = QPainterPath()
        path.addRoundedRect(rect, 10, 10)
        painter.setClipPath(path)

        if self._is_drag_active:
            # حالت درگ اکتیو با پس‌زمینه زمردی و حاشیه روشن
            painter.fillRect(rect, QColor("#14232C"))
            pen = QPen(QColor("#10B981"), 1.8, Qt.PenStyle.DashLine)
        else:
            # حالت عادی استودیویی
            grad = QLinearGradient(0, 0, self.width(), self.height())
            grad.setColorAt(0.0, QColor("#121826"))
            grad.setColorAt(1.0, QColor("#0E131F"))
            painter.fillRect(rect, grad)
            pen = QPen(QColor(255, 255, 255, 14), 1.0)

        painter.setClipping(False)
        painter.setPen(pen)
        painter.setBrush(Qt.BrushStyle.NoBrush)
        painter.drawRoundedRect(QRectF(0.5, 0.5, self.width() - 1.0, self.height() - 1.0), 10, 10)
        painter.end()

    def dragEnterEvent(self, event: QDragEnterEvent):
        if event.mimeData().hasUrls():
            event.acceptProposedAction()
            self._is_drag_active = True
            self.update()

    def dragLeaveEvent(self, event):
        self._is_drag_active = False
        self.update()

    def dropEvent(self, event: QDropEvent):
        self._is_drag_active = False
        self.update()
        urls = event.mimeData().urls()
        if urls:
            file_path = urls[0].toLocalFile()
            self._process_file(file_path)

    def mousePressEvent(self, event):
        if event.button() == Qt.MouseButton.LeftButton:
            self._select_file_dialog()
        super().mousePressEvent(event)

    def _select_file_dialog(self):
        file_path, _ = QFileDialog.getOpenFileName(
            self, "انتخاب فایل ویدیو جهت آنالیز فنی", "",
            "Video Files (*.mp4 *.mov *.mkv *.avi *.mxf *.braw *.r3d *.prores);;All Files (*.*)"
        )
        if file_path:
            self._process_file(file_path)

    def _process_file(self, file_path: str):
        data = analyze_video_file(file_path)
        if data:
            self.file_inspected.emit(data)
            diag = MediaInspectorDialog(data, self.window())
            diag.exec()

        btn_layout.addWidget(btn_submit, stretch=2)

        btn_cancel = QPushButton("انصراف")
        btn_cancel.setFixedHeight(38)
        btn_cancel.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        btn_cancel.setStyleSheet("""
            QPushButton {
                font-family: 'Vazirmatn';
                background-color: #161F30;
                color: #94A3B8;
                border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08);
                border-radius: 6px;
                font-size: 12px;
                font-weight: 500;
                padding: 0 14px;
            }
            QPushButton:hover {
                background-color: #1E283D;
                color: #F8FAFC;
            }
        """)
        btn_cancel.clicked.connect(self.reject)
        btn_layout.addWidget(btn_cancel, stretch=1)

        self.container_layout.addLayout(btn_layout)

    def _submit(self):
        name = self.input_name.text().strip()
        if not name:
            StudioConfirmDialog.ask(self, "خطا در ثبت", "لطفاً نام کارفرما را وارد کنید.", icon_name="alert")
            return

        company = self.input_company.text().strip() or None
        phone = self.input_phone.text().strip() or None
        notes = self.input_notes.toPlainText().strip() or None

        with db.get_session() as session:
            client = Client(
                full_name=name,
                company=company,
                phone=phone,
                notes=notes
            )
            session.add(client)
            session.commit()
            client_id = client.id

        global_signals.client_balance_updated.emit(client_id)
        self.accept()

        clips_section_header.addWidget(lbl_clips_title)

        packages = settings.get("packages", {})
        if packages:
            capsule_row = QHBoxLayout()
            capsule_row.setSpacing(6)

            for pkg_name, pkg_clips in packages.items():

        self._on_event_date_changed(self.input_event_date.text())

    def _apply_amount(self, amount_val: int):
        self.input_fee.setText(str(amount_val))

    def _on_fee_changed(self, text_val: str):
        clean = normalize_to_english_digits(text_val)
        if clean.isdigit() and int(clean) > 0:
            self.lbl_fee_words.setText(number_to_persian_words(int(clean)))
        else:
            self.lbl_fee_words.setText("")

    def _on_event_date_changed(self, text_val: str):
        j_date = parse_jalali_text(text_val)
        if j_date:
            self.lbl_event_date_readable.setText(get_persian_date_readable(j_date))
        else:
            self.lbl_event_date_readable.setText("")

    def _on_deadline_changed(self, text_val: str):
        j_date = parse_jalali_text(text_val)
        if j_date:
            self.lbl_deadline_relative.setText(get_deadline_relative_text(j_date))
        else:
            self.lbl_deadline_relative.setText("")

    def _apply_package(self, package_clips: list):
        for cb, name in self.clip_checkboxes:
            cb.setChecked(name in package_clips)

    def _load_clients(self):
        self.combo_client.clear()
        with db.get_session() as session:
            clients = session.scalars(select(Client).order_by(Client.created_at.desc())).all()
            for c in clients:
                display_name = c.company if c.company else c.full_name
                if c.company and c.full_name and c.company != c.full_name:
                    display_name += f" (مدیریت: {c.full_name})"
                self.combo_client.addItem(display_name, c.id)

    def _browse_folder(self):
        settings = SettingsManager.load_settings()
        default_dir = settings.get("default_drive_path", "")
        folder = QFileDialog.getExistingDirectory(self, "انتخاب پوشه راش‌ها", default_dir)
        if folder:
            self.input_path.setText(folder)

    def _browse_nle_file(self):
        file_path, _ = QFileDialog.getOpenFileName(
            self, "انتخاب فایل پروژه Premiere یا DaVinci", "",
            "NLE Projects (*.prproj *.drp);;Premiere Pro (*.prproj);;DaVinci Resolve (*.drp);;All Files (*.*)"
        )
        if file_path:
            self.input_nle_path.setText(file_path)

    def _submit(self):
        couple = self.input_couple.text().strip()
        if not couple:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "خطا", "نام عروس و داماد نمی‌تواند خالی باشد.")
            return

        client_id = self.combo_client.currentData()
        if not client_id:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "خطا", "لطفاً یک استودیو انتخاب کنید.")
            return

        fee_str = normalize_to_english_digits(self.input_fee.text().strip())
        fee = int(fee_str) if fee_str.isdigit() else 0

        event_j = parse_jalali_text(self.input_event_date.text())
        event_dt = datetime.combine(event_j.togregorian(), datetime.min.time()) if event_j else None

        deadline_j = parse_jalali_text(self.input_deadline.text())
        deadline_dt = datetime.combine(deadline_j.togregorian(), datetime.min.time()) if deadline_j else None

        path = self.input_path.text().strip()
        nle_path = self.input_nle_path.text().strip() or None

        selected_clips = [name for cb, name in self.clip_checkboxes if cb.isChecked()]
        if not selected_clips:
            selected_clips = ["فیلم کامل مراسم (Long Cut)"]

        with db.get_session() as session:
            next_pnum = db.get_next_project_number(session)

            proj = Project(
                project_number=next_pnum,
                title=f"پروژه {couple}",
                couple_names=couple,
                client_id=client_id,
                base_fee=fee,
                total_fee=fee,
                event_date=event_dt,
                deadline=deadline_dt,
                current_stage=StageEnum.ROUGH_CUT,
                workflow_status=WorkflowStatusEnum.EDITING,
                is_archived=False,
                nle_project_path=nle_path
            )
            session.add(proj)
            session.flush()

            for c_name in selected_clips:
                session.add(ProjectClipItem(project_id=proj.id, clip_name=c_name))

            if path:
                session.add(DriveLocation(
                    project_id=proj.id,
                    drive_label="هارد اصلی پروژه",
                    absolute_path=path,
                    is_primary=True
                ))

            session.commit()
            proj_id = proj.id
            proj_num = proj.project_number

        global_signals.project_created.emit({
            "id": proj_id,
            "project_number": proj_num,
            "title": f"پروژه {couple}"
        })
        self.accept()

# ==================================================
# FILE: widgets\new_transaction_dialog.py
# ==================================================
# ==================================================
# FILE: widgets/new_transaction_dialog.py
# ==================================================
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Optional
import jdatetime
from PySide6.QtWidgets import (
    QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout, QLabel, 
    QLineEdit, QPushButton, QComboBox, 
    QFrame, QRadioButton, QButtonGroup
)
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt, QSize
from sqlalchemy import select

from database.db_manager import db
from database.models import Project, Transaction, Client, PaymentTypeEnum
from core.project_controller import ProjectController
from core.finance_controller import FinanceController
from core.persian_utils import normalize_to_english_digits, format_toman, to_persian_numbers
from core.icon_provider import IconProvider
from core.signals import global_signals
from widgets.studio_dialog_base import StudioBaseDialog, StudioConfirmDialog


class NewTransactionDialog(StudioBaseDialog):
    """دیالوگ مدرن استودیو برای ثبت واریزی و تسویه مالی همراه با کد رهگیری"""
    def __init__(self, target_project_id: Optional[int] = None, target_client_id: Optional[int] = None, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent=parent)
        self.target_project_id = target_project_id
        self.target_client_id = target_client_id
        self.suggested_amount = 0

        self.setFixedWidth(560)
        self.setStyleSheet("""

            }
            QLineEdit, QComboBox {
                font-family: 'Vazirmatn';

            }

                border: 1px solid #6366F1;
                background-color: #1A2438;
            }
            QRadioButton {
                font-family: 'Vazirmatn';
                color: #F8FAFC;
                font-size: 12px;
                font-weight: 600;
            }
        """)
        self.init_ui()

            QPushButton {
                font-family: 'Vazirmatn';
                background-color: #161F30;
                color: #94A3B8;
                border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08);
                border-radius: 6px;

        btn_layout.addWidget(self.btn_cancel, stretch=1)

        self.container_layout.addLayout(btn_layout)

        self._load_clients()
        self._load_projects()
        self._on_mode_changed()

    def _on_mode_changed(self):
        is_studio_mode = self.rb_studio.isChecked()
        self.lbl_select_client.setVisible(is_studio_mode)
        self.combo_client.setVisible(is_studio_mode)
        
        self.lbl_select_project.setVisible(not is_studio_mode)
        self.combo_project.setVisible(not is_studio_mode)
        
        self._update_balance_info()

    def _load_clients(self):
        self.combo_client.clear()
        with db.get_session() as session:
            clients = session.scalars(select(Client).order_by(Client.company, Client.full_name)).all()
            for idx, c in enumerate(clients):
                display = c.company or c.full_name
                self.combo_client.addItem(display, c.id)
                if self.target_client_id and c.id == self.target_client_id:
                    self.combo_client.setCurrentIndex(idx)

    def _load_projects(self):
        self.combo_project.clear()
        all_projects = ProjectController.get_all_projects() + ProjectController.get_archived_projects()
        seen = set()
        
        for p in all_projects:
            if p.id in seen:
                continue
            
            if FinanceController.is_project_eligible_for_finance(p):
                fin = ProjectController.get_project_financial_summary(p)
                if fin["remaining"] > 0 or (self.target_project_id and p.id == self.target_project_id):
                    seen.add(p.id)
                    client_name = p.client.company if (p.client and p.client.company) else (p.client.full_name if p.client else "بدون استودیو")
                    p_num = f"{p.project_number:03d}" if p.project_number else f"{p.id:03d}"
                    rem_str = f" [مانده: {format_toman(fin['remaining'])}]" if fin['remaining'] > 0 else " [تسویه کامل]"
                    
                    self.combo_project.addItem(f"{to_persian_numbers(p_num)} — {p.couple_names} ({client_name}){rem_str}", (p.id, p.client_id))
                    
                    if self.target_project_id and p.id == self.target_project_id:
                        self.combo_project.setCurrentIndex(self.combo_project.count() - 1)

    def _update_balance_info(self):
        if self.rb_studio.isChecked():
            client_id = self.combo_client.currentData()
            if not client_id:
                return
            summary = FinanceController.get_studio_debt_summary(client_id)
            self.suggested_amount = summary["total_remaining"]

            if self.suggested_amount == 0:
                self.lbl_balance_info.setText("وضعیت حساب استودیو: <b style='color:#10B981;'>تسویه کامل (مانده کل: ۰ تومان) ✓</b>")
                self.btn_use_suggested.setEnabled(False)
            else:
                unsettled_cnt = len(summary["unsettled_projects"])
                self.lbl_balance_info.setText(
                    f"کل طلب استودیو: <b style='color:#F59E0B;'>{format_toman(self.suggested_amount)}</b> "
                    f"({to_persian_numbers(unsettled_cnt)} پروژه بدهکار)"
                )
                self.btn_use_suggested.setEnabled(True)
        else:
            proj_data = self.combo_project.currentData()
            if not proj_data:
                self.lbl_balance_info.setText("هیچ پروژه بدهکاری برای تسویه یافت نشد.")
                self.btn_use_suggested.setEnabled(False)
                return
            project_id, _ = proj_data
            project = ProjectController.get_project_by_id(project_id)
            if project:
                fin = ProjectController.get_project_financial_summary(project)
                self.suggested_amount = fin["remaining"]
                if self.suggested_amount == 0:
                    self.lbl_balance_info.setText("وضعیت این پروژه: <b style='color:#10B981;'>تسویه کامل ✓</b>")
                    self.btn_use_suggested.setEnabled(False)
                else:
                    self.lbl_balance_info.setText(
                        f"مانده بدهی این پروژه: <b style='color:#F59E0B;'>{format_toman(self.suggested_amount)}</b>"
                    )
                    self.btn_use_suggested.setEnabled(True)

    def _fill_suggested_amount(self):
        if self.suggested_amount > 0:
            self.input_amount.setText(str(self.suggested_amount))

    def _on_amount_changed(self, text: str):
        clean_digits = normalize_to_english_digits(text)
        if clean_digits.isdigit() and int(clean_digits) > 0:
            val = int(clean_digits)
            self.lbl_amount_preview.setText(f"معادل: {format_toman(val)}")
        else:
            self.lbl_amount_preview.setText("")

    def _submit(self):
        amount_clean = normalize_to_english_digits(self.input_amount.text())
        if not amount_clean.isdigit() or int(amount_clean) <= 0:
            StudioConfirmDialog.ask(self, "خطا در ثبت واریزی", "لطفاً مبلغ واریزی معتبری وارد کنید.", icon_name="alert")
            return

        amount = int(amount_clean)
        pay_type = self.combo_type.currentData()
        tracking = self.input_tracking.text().strip()
        note = self.input_note.text().strip()

        if self.rb_studio.isChecked():
            client_id = self.combo_client.currentData()
            if not client_id:
                StudioConfirmDialog.ask(self, "خطا", "استودیویی انتخاب نشده است.", icon_name="alert")
                return

            FinanceController.allocate_client_payment(
                client_id=client_id,
                total_amount=amount,
                payment_type=pay_type,
                notes=note,
                tracking_code=tracking
            )
            global_signals.transaction_recorded.emit(0)
            global_signals.client_balance_updated.emit(client_id)
            self.accept()
        else:
            proj_data = self.combo_project.currentData()
            if not proj_data:
                StudioConfirmDialog.ask(self, "خطا", "پروژه‌ای انتخاب نشده است.", icon_name="alert")
                return
            project_id, client_id = proj_data

            with db.get_session() as session:
                trx = Transaction(
                    project_id=project_id,
                    client_id=client_id,
                    amount=amount,
                    payment_type=pay_type,
                    notes=note,
                    tracking_code=tracking or None,
                    paid_at=datetime.utcnow()
                )
                session.add(trx)
                ProjectController.log_event(
                    session, project_id, "TRANSACTION_ADDED", 
                    f"دریافت مبلغ {format_toman(amount)}", 
                    f"شماره پیگیری: {tracking or '-'} | {note or 'واریز ثبت شد.'}"
                )
                session.commit()
                trx_id = trx.id

            global_signals.transaction_recorded.emit(trx_id)
            global_signals.project_updated.emit(project_id)
            if client_id:
                global_signals.client_balance_updated.emit(client_id)
            self.accept()

def progress_pct_to_words(pct: int, completed_clips: int, total_clips: int) -> str:
    if total_clips == 0:
        return "بدون کلیپ"
    if completed_clips == 0:
        return "شروع نشده"
    if pct >= 100 or completed_clips == total_clips:
        return "تکمیل شده"

    ones = ["", "یک", "دو", "سه", "چهار", "پنج", "شش", "هفت", "هشت", "نه"]
    teens = ["ده", "یازده", "دوازده", "سیزده", "چهارده", "پانزده", "شانزده", "هفده", "هجده", "نوزده"]
    tens = ["", "", "بیست", "سی", "چهل", "پنجاه", "شصت", "هفتاد", "هشتاد", "نود"]

    def _num_to_text(n: int) -> str:
        if n <= 0:
            return "صفر"
        if n < 10:
            return ones[n]
        if n < 20:
            return teens[n - 10]
        if n < 100:
            t = n // 10
            o = n % 10
            if o > 0:
                return f"{tens[t]} و {ones[o]}"
            return tens[t]
        return "صد"

    return f"{_num_to_text(pct)} درصد انجام شده"


class ArchiveConfirmDialog(StudioBaseDialog):
    def __init__(self, project_id: int, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.project_id = project_id
        self.setFixedWidth(450)
        self.init_ui()

        fin = ProjectController.get_project_financial_summary(project)

        desc_card = QFrame()
        desc_card.setStyleSheet("background-color: #121826; border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.06); border-radius: 8px;")

        card_lay.setContentsMargins(14, 12, 14, 12)
        card_lay.setSpacing(6)

        lbl_proj = QLabel(f"پروژه: <b style='color:#F8FAFC;'>{project.couple_names}</b>")
        lbl_proj.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 12px; color: #94A3B8;")
        card_lay.addWidget(lbl_proj)

        lbl_status = QLabel("وضعیت تدوین: <b style='color:#10B981;'>کلیپ‌ها تکمیل و تحویل داده شده ✓</b>")

        card_lay.addWidget(lbl_status)

        lbl_fin = QLabel(f"وضعیت حساب: <b style='color:{fin['status_color']};'>{fin['status_label']}</b>")
        lbl_fin.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 12px; color: #94A3B8;")
        card_lay.addWidget(lbl_fin)
        self.container_layout.addWidget(desc_card)

        note = QLabel("توجه: پرونده به بخش آرشیو منتقل می‌شود اما پیگیری مطالبات مالی و ثبت اصلاحیه همچنان فعال خواهد بود.")

        btn_box = QHBoxLayout()

        btn_confirm = QPushButton("✓ تأیید و انتقال به آرشیو")
        btn_confirm.setFixedHeight(36)
        btn_confirm.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        btn_confirm.setStyleSheet("""
            QPushButton {
                font-family: 'Vazirmatn';
                background-color: #10B981;
                color: #FFFFFF;
                border: none;
                border-radius: 6px;
                font-weight: 700;
                font-size: 12px;
            }
            QPushButton:hover { background-color: #059669; }
        """)
        btn_confirm.clicked.connect(self._confirm)
        btn_box.addWidget(btn_confirm, stretch=2)

        btn_cancel = QPushButton("انصراف")
        btn_cancel.setFixedHeight(36)
        btn_cancel.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        btn_cancel.setStyleSheet("""
            QPushButton {
                font-family: 'Vazirmatn';
                background-color: #161F30;
                color: #94A3B8;

        """)
        btn_cancel.clicked.connect(self.reject)
        btn_box.addWidget(btn_cancel, stretch=1)

        self.container_layout.addLayout(btn_box)

    def _confirm(self):
        ProjectController.archive_project(self.project_id)
        self.accept()


class ProjectCard(BaseProjectCard):
    def __init__(self, project_data: dict, parent=None):
        project_id = project_data.get("id", 0)
        project_number = project_data.get("project_number", 1)
        
        super().__init__(
            project_id=project_id,
            project_number=project_number,
            parent=parent,
            accent_color="#818CF8",
            card_type="project"
        )
        self.project = project_data
        self.setFixedHeight(230)
        self.setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy.Policy.Expanding, QSizePolicy.Policy.Fixed)
        self.init_card_content()

    def init_card_content(self):
        active_rev = self.project.get("active_revision_title")
        if active_rev:
            prog_val = self.project.get("rev_progress_pct", 0)
            c_cnt = self.project.get("rev_completed_tasks", 0)
            t_cnt = self.project.get("rev_total_tasks", 0)
            status_text = f"اصلاحیه: {progress_pct_to_words(prog_val, c_cnt, t_cnt)}"
            status_type = StatusType.WARNING
            status_symbol = "●"
        else:
            prog_val = self.project.get("progress_pct", 0)
            c_cnt = self.project.get("completed_clips", 0)
            t_cnt = self.project.get("total_clips", 0)
            
            status_info = self.project.get("status_info", {})
            status_key = status_info.get("key", "")
            
            if status_key == "under_review":

                    status_symbol = "●"

            label=status_text,

            custom_symbol=status_symbol
        )
        self.header_tags_layout.addWidget(self.status_badge)

    def _show_context_menu(self, parent_btn: QPushButton):
        menu = QMenu(self)
        menu.setLayoutDirection(Qt.LayoutDirection.RightToLeft)

        act_nle = menu.addAction("اجرای فایل پروژه 🎬")
        act_edit = menu.addAction("ویرایش مشخصات پروژه ✏️")
        act_share = menu.addAction("ارسال فاکتور و وضعیت به استودیو 💬")
        act_archive = menu.addAction("انتقال به آرشیو 📁")
        act_delete = menu.addAction("حذف کامل پروژه 🗑️")

        action = menu.exec(parent_btn.mapToGlobal(parent_btn.rect().bottomLeft()))
        if action == act_nle:
            self._launch_nle_project()
        elif action == act_edit:
            self._open_edit_dialog()
        elif action == act_share:
            self._open_share_dialog()
        elif action == act_archive:
            self._open_archive_confirm()
        elif action == act_delete:
            self._delete_project()

    def _mark_delivered(self):
        ProjectController.mark_as_delivered(self.project_id)

    def _launch_nle_project(self):
        nle_path = self.project.get("nle_project_path", "")
        success, message = NleLauncherHelper.launch_project(nle_path)
        if success:
            global_signals.show_toast.emit(message, "success")
        else:
            global_signals.show_toast.emit(message, "error")

    def _open_fast_messenger_popup(self, anchor_btn: QWidget):
        popup = QuickMessengerMiniPopup(self.project, self.window())
        popup.show_beside_widget(anchor_btn)

    def _open_edit_dialog(self):
        diag = EditProjectDialog(self.project_id, self.window())
        diag.exec()

    def _open_share_dialog(self):
        diag = StudioShareDialog(self.project_id, self.window())
        diag.exec()

    def _delete_project(self):
        couple = self.project.get('couple_names') or 'پروژه'
        if StudioConfirmDialog.ask(
            self.window(),
            title="حذف کامل پروژه",
            message=f"آیا از حذف کامل پرونده «{couple}» اطمینان دارید؟",
            details="هشدار: تمام کلیپ‌ها، دوره‌های اصلاحیه، لاگ‌ها و تراکنش‌های مالی متصل به این پروژه برای همیشه پاک خواهند شد.",
            confirm_text="حذف دائمی",
            is_danger=True,
            icon_name="trash"
        ):
            ProjectController.delete_project(self.project_id)

    def _open_new_revision(self):
        diag = NewRevisionDialog(self.project_id, self.window())
        diag.exec()

    def _open_archive_confirm(self):
        diag = ArchiveConfirmDialog(self.project_id, self.window())
        diag.exec()

    def _open_details(self):
        proj_id = self.project_id
        win = self.window()
        diag = ProjectDetailsDialog(proj_id, win)
        diag.exec()

    def _open_payment(self):
        proj_id = self.project_id
        win = self.window()
        diag = NewTransactionDialog(proj_id, parent=win)
        diag.exec()

    def _open_folder(self):
        path_str = self.project.get("primary_path")
        if not path_str or not Path(path_str).exists():
            global_signals.show_toast.emit("مسیر پوشه مشخص نشده یا هارد متصل نیست", "error")
            return
        
        couple_name = self.project.get("couple_names") or self.project.get("title", "پروژه")
        win = self.window()
        diag = ProjectMediaExplorerDialog(folder_path=path_str, project_title=couple_name, parent=win)
        diag.exec()

from PySide6.QtWidgets import (
    QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout, QLabel, QProgressBar, 
    QPushButton, QSizePolicy
)

import jdatetime

from core.persian_utils import gregorian_to_jalali, to_persian_numbers

from widgets.project_details_dialog import ProjectDetailsDialog


def progress_pct_to_words(pct: int, completed_clips: int, total_clips: int) -> str:
    if total_clips == 0:
        return "بدون کلیپ"
    if completed_clips == 0:
        return "شروع نشده"
    if pct >= 100 or completed_clips == total_clips:
        return "تکمیل شده"

    ones = ["", "یک", "دو", "سه", "چهار", "پنج", "شش", "هفت", "هشت", "نه"]
    teens = ["ده", "یازده", "دوازده", "سیزده", "چهارده", "پانزده", "شانزده", "هفده", "هجده", "نوزده"]
    tens = ["", "", "بیست", "سی", "چهل", "پنجاه", "شصت", "هفتاد", "هشتاد", "نود"]

    def _num_to_text(n: int) -> str:
        if n <= 0:
            return "صفر"
        if n < 10:
            return ones[n]
        if n < 20:
            return teens[n - 10]
        if n < 100:
            t = n // 10
            o = n % 10
            if o > 0:
                return f"{tens[t]} و {ones[o]}"
            return tens[t]
        return "صد"

    return f"{_num_to_text(pct)} درصد انجام شده"


class ProjectCardMini(BaseProjectCard):
    """
    کارت فشرده پیشخوان با دکمه پرش مستقیم به تب پروژه‌ها و فوکوس روی کارت
    """
    def __init__(self, project_data: dict, parent=None):
        project_id = project_data.get("id", 0)
        project_number = project_data.get("project_number", project_id)
        
        super().__init__(
            project_id=project_id,
            project_number=project_number,
            parent=parent,
            accent_color="#6366F1",
            card_type="dashboard"
        )
        self.project_data = project_data
        # ارتفاع افزایش‌یافته برای نمایش کامل نام پروژه بدون تداخل با بج‌ها و فوتر
        self.setFixedHeight(145)
        self.setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy.Policy.Expanding, QSizePolicy.Policy.Fixed)
        self.init_mini_content()

    def init_mini_content(self):
        # ۱. ردیف بالا: وضعیت + دکمه پرش به پروژه
        active_rev_title = self.project_data.get("active_revision_title")
        if active_rev_title:
            progress_val = self.project_data.get("rev_progress_pct", 0)
            completed_cnt = self.project_data.get("rev_completed_tasks", 0)
            total_cnt = self.project_data.get("rev_total_tasks", 0)
            status_text = f"اصلاحیه: {progress_pct_to_words(progress_val, completed_cnt, total_cnt)}"
            status_type = StatusType.WARNING
            status_symbol = "●"
            bar_color = "#EA580C"
        else:
            progress_val = self.project_data.get("progress_pct", 0)
            completed_cnt = self.project_data.get("completed_clips", 0)
            total_cnt = self.project_data.get("total_clips", 0)
            status_text = progress_pct_to_words(progress_val, completed_cnt, total_cnt)
            bar_color = "#10B981" if progress_val >= 100 else "#6366F1"
            
            if progress_val >= 100 or (total_cnt > 0 and completed_cnt == total_cnt):
                status_type = StatusType.COMPLETED
                status_symbol = "✓"
            elif completed_cnt == 0:
                status_type = StatusType.NEUTRAL
                status_symbol = "○"
            else:
                status_type = StatusType.ACTIVE
                status_symbol = "●"

        self.status_badge = StatusPillBadge(
            label=status_text,
            status_type=status_type,
            custom_symbol=status_symbol
        )
        self.header_tags_layout.addWidget(self.status_badge)
        self.header_tags_layout.addStretch()

        self.btn_focus_project = QPushButton("↗")
        self.btn_focus_project.setFixedSize(22, 22)
        self.btn_focus_project.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        self.btn_focus_project.setToolTip("مشاهده و فوکوس روی این پروژه در تب پروژه‌ها")
        self.btn_focus_project.setStyleSheet("""
            QPushButton {
                font-family: 'Segoe UI', 'Vazirmatn';
                background-color: #1A2234;
                color: #818CF8;
                border: 1px solid rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.3);
                border-radius: 5px;
                font-size: 13px;
                font-weight: bold;
                padding-bottom: 2px;
            }
            QPushButton:hover {
                background-color: #6366F1;
                color: #FFFFFF;
                border-color: #6366F1;
            }
        """)
        self.btn_focus_project.clicked.connect(self._go_to_project_tab)
        self.header_layout.addWidget(self.btn_focus_project)

        # ۲. عنوان و استودیو با چیدمان آزاد
        title_box = QVBoxLayout()
        title_box.setSpacing(2)
        title_box.setContentsMargins(0, 4, 0, 4)

        couple_name = self.project_data.get("couple_names") or self.project_data.get("title", "پروژه بدون نام")
        self.lbl_title = QLabel(couple_name)
        self.lbl_title.setWordWrap(True)
        self.lbl_title.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 14px; font-weight: 800; color: #F8FAFC; background: transparent; border: none; padding: 1px 0;")
        title_box.addWidget(self.lbl_title)

        client_name = self.project_data.get("client_name") or "بدون استودیو"
        self.lbl_studio = QLabel(f"استودیو: {client_name}")
        self.lbl_studio.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 11px; color: #94A3B8; font-weight: 500; background: transparent; border: none;")
        title_box.addWidget(self.lbl_studio)

        self.card_layout.addLayout(title_box)

        # ۳. پروگرس‌بار باریک
        pb = QProgressBar()
        pb.setRange(0, 100)
        pb.setValue(progress_val)
        pb.setTextVisible(False)
        pb.setFixedHeight(3)
        pb.setStyleSheet(f"""
            QProgressBar {{
                background-color: #242E42;
                border: none;
                border-radius: 1.5px;
            }}
            QProgressBar::chunk {{
                background-color: {bar_color};
                border-radius: 1.5px;
            }}
        """)
        self.card_layout.addWidget(pb)

        # ۴. فوتر
        footer_row = QHBoxLayout()
        footer_row.setContentsMargins(0, 2, 0, 0)
        footer_row.setSpacing(6)

        deadline_dt = self.project_data.get("deadline")
        if deadline_dt:
            dead_str = gregorian_to_jalali(deadline_dt)
            today = jdatetime.date.today()
            try:
                dead_j = jdatetime.date.fromgregorian(date=deadline_dt.date())
                days_left = (dead_j - today).days
                if days_left < 0:
                    dead_color = "#EF4444"
                elif days_left <= 2:
                    dead_color = "#F59E0B"
                else:
                    dead_color = "#64748B"
            except Exception:
                dead_color = "#64748B"

            lbl_dead = QLabel(f"تحویل: {dead_str}")
            lbl_dead.setStyleSheet(f"font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 10.5px; color: {dead_color}; background: transparent; border: none;")
            footer_row.addWidget(lbl_dead)

        footer_row.addStretch()

        btn_view = QLabel("پرونده ←")
        btn_view.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; color: #6366F1; font-size: 11px; font-weight: 700; background: transparent; border: none;")
        footer_row.addWidget(btn_view)

        self.card_layout.addLayout(footer_row)
        self.clicked.connect(lambda _: self._open_details())

    def _go_to_project_tab(self):
        global_signals.navigate_and_focus_project.emit(self.project_id)

    def _open_details(self):
        proj_id = self.project_id
        win = self.window()
        QTimer.singleShot(0, lambda: ProjectDetailsDialog(proj_id, win).exec())

from datetime import datetime
from PySide6.QtWidgets import (
    QWidget, QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout, QLabel, 
    QProgressBar, QPushButton, QFrame
)
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt, Signal
import jdatetime
from core.persian_utils import to_persian_numbers
from core.project_controller import ProjectController


class ClipChecklistItem(QFrame):
    toggled = Signal(int)

    def __init__(self, clip_id: int, clip_name: str, is_completed: bool, completed_at: datetime = None, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.clip_id = clip_id
        self.clip_name = clip_name
        self.is_completed = is_completed
        self.completed_at = completed_at
        self.setObjectName("ClipItemFrame")
        self.setLayoutDirection(Qt.LayoutDirection.RightToLeft)
        self.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        self.init_ui()

    def init_ui(self):
        self.layout = QHBoxLayout(self)
        self.layout.setContentsMargins(14, 10, 14, 10)
        self.layout.setSpacing(12)

        # دکمه چک‌باکس دایره‌ای
        self.btn_check = QPushButton()
        self.btn_check.setFixedSize(24, 24)
        self.btn_check.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        self.btn_check.clicked.connect(self._on_clicked)
        self.layout.addWidget(self.btn_check)

        # نام کلیپ
        self.lbl_name = QLabel(self.clip_name)
        self.layout.addWidget(self.lbl_name, stretch=1)

        # وضعیت و تاریخ
        self.lbl_status = QLabel()
        self.layout.addWidget(self.lbl_status)

        self._update_appearance()

    def _format_datetime(self, dt: datetime) -> str:
        if not dt:
            return ""
        try:
            j_dt = jdatetime.datetime.fromgregorian(datetime=dt)
            date_str = j_dt.strftime("%Y/%m/%d")
            time_str = j_dt.strftime("%H:%M")
            return f"({to_persian_numbers(date_str)} - {to_persian_numbers(time_str)})"
        except Exception:
            return ""

    def _update_appearance(self):
        if self.is_completed:
            self.setStyleSheet("""
                QFrame#ClipItemFrame {
                    background-color: rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.08);
                    border: 1px solid rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.3);
                    border-radius: 8px;
                }
                QLabel {
                    background: transparent;
                    border: none;
                }
            """)
            self.btn_check.setText("✓")
            self.btn_check.setStyleSheet("""
                QPushButton {
                    background-color: #10B981;
                    color: #FFFFFF;
                    border: none;
                    border-radius: 12px;
                    font-size: 13px;
                    font-weight: bold;
                }
            """)
            self.lbl_name.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 13px; font-weight: 700; color: #F8FAFC; border: none; background: transparent;")
            
            dt_str = self._format_datetime(self.completed_at)
            status_txt = f"تکمیل شده {dt_str}" if dt_str else "تکمیل شده"
            self.lbl_status.setText(status_txt)
            self.lbl_status.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 11px; color: #10B981; font-weight: 600; border: none; background: transparent;")
        else:
            self.setStyleSheet("""
                QFrame#ClipItemFrame {
                    background-color: #161F30;
                    border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05);
                    border-radius: 8px;
                }
                QFrame#ClipItemFrame:hover {
                    background-color: #1A253A;
                    border-color: rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.4);
                }
                QLabel {
                    background: transparent;
                    border: none;
                }
            """)
            self.btn_check.setText("")
            self.btn_check.setStyleSheet("""
                QPushButton {
                    background-color: transparent;
                    border: 2px solid #475569;
                    border-radius: 12px;
                }
                QPushButton:hover {
                    border-color: #6366F1;
                }
            """)
            self.lbl_name.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 13px; font-weight: 500; color: #94A3B8; border: none; background: transparent;")
            self.lbl_status.setText("باقی‌مانده")
            self.lbl_status.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 11px; color: #64748B; border: none; background: transparent;")

    def _on_clicked(self):
        self.toggled.emit(self.clip_id)

    def mousePressEvent(self, event):
        if event.button() == Qt.MouseButton.LeftButton:
            event.accept()
            return
        super().mousePressEvent(event)

    def mouseReleaseEvent(self, event):
        if event.button() == Qt.MouseButton.LeftButton:
            event.accept()
            self._on_clicked()
            return
        super().mouseReleaseEvent(event)


class ProjectChecklistWidget(QWidget):
    progress_changed = Signal(int, int, int)

    def __init__(self, project_id: int, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.project_id = project_id
        self.setLayoutDirection(Qt.LayoutDirection.RightToLeft)
        self.clip_items: dict[int, ClipChecklistItem] = {}
        self.init_ui()

    def init_ui(self):
        self.main_layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        self.main_layout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        self.main_layout.setSpacing(10)

        header_row = QHBoxLayout()
        self.lbl_header = QLabel("وضعیت پیشرفت کلیپ‌ها")
        self.lbl_header.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 13px; font-weight: 600; color: #94A3B8; border: none; background: transparent;")
        header_row.addWidget(self.lbl_header, stretch=1)

        self.lbl_fraction = QLabel("۰ / ۰")
        self.lbl_fraction.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 13.5px; font-weight: 800; color: #6366F1; border: none; background: transparent;")
        header_row.addWidget(self.lbl_fraction)
        self.main_layout.addLayout(header_row)

        self.progress_bar = QProgressBar()
        self.progress_bar.setRange(0, 100)
        self.progress_bar.setValue(0)
        self.progress_bar.setTextVisible(False)
        self.progress_bar.setFixedHeight(5)
        self.main_layout.addWidget(self.progress_bar)

        self.items_container = QVBoxLayout()
        self.items_container.setSpacing(8)
        self.main_layout.addLayout(self.items_container)

        self.refresh_checklist()

    def refresh_checklist(self):
        project = ProjectController.get_project_by_id(self.project_id)
        if not project or not project.clips:
            self.lbl_fraction.setText("۰ / ۰")
            self.progress_bar.setValue(0)
            return

        completed_cnt, total_cnt, pct = ProjectController.calculate_project_progress(project)
        self.lbl_fraction.setText(f"{to_persian_numbers(completed_cnt)} از {to_persian_numbers(total_cnt)} کلیپ ({to_persian_numbers(pct)}٪)")
        self.progress_bar.setValue(pct)
        
        if pct == 100:
            self.progress_bar.setStyleSheet("QProgressBar { background-color: #242E42; border: none; border-radius: 2.5px; } QProgressBar::chunk { background-color: #10B981; border-radius: 2.5px; }")
            self.lbl_fraction.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 13.5px; font-weight: 800; color: #10B981; border: none; background: transparent;")
        else:
            self.progress_bar.setStyleSheet("QProgressBar { background-color: #242E42; border: none; border-radius: 2.5px; } QProgressBar::chunk { background-color: #6366F1; border-radius: 2.5px; }")
            self.lbl_fraction.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 13.5px; font-weight: 800; color: #6366F1; border: none; background: transparent;")

        if not self.clip_items:
            for clip in project.clips:
                item_widget = ClipChecklistItem(clip.id, clip.clip_name, clip.is_completed, clip.completed_at)
                item_widget.toggled.connect(self._handle_clip_toggle)
                self.items_container.addWidget(item_widget)
                self.clip_items[clip.id] = item_widget
        else:
            for clip in project.clips:
                if clip.id in self.clip_items:
                    widget = self.clip_items[clip.id]
                    widget.is_completed = clip.is_completed
                    widget.completed_at = clip.completed_at
                    widget._update_appearance()

        self.progress_changed.emit(completed_cnt, total_cnt, pct)

    def _handle_clip_toggle(self, clip_id: int):
        ProjectController.toggle_clip_status(clip_id)
        self.refresh_checklist()
