from datetime import datetime
from typing import List, Dict, Any
from PySide6.QtWidgets import (
    QWidget, QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout, QLabel, 
    QPushButton, QFrame, QLineEdit, 
    QCheckBox, QMessageBox, QScrollArea, QListWidget, QListWidgetItem, QProgressBar,
    QSizePolicy
)
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt, QTimer
import jdatetime

from database.models import ProjectRevision, ProjectRevisionTask, RevisionStatusEnum
from core.project_controller import ProjectController, get_persian_round_title
from core.persian_utils import format_toman, gregorian_to_jalali, to_persian_numbers, normalize_to_english_digits
from core.icon_provider import IconProvider
from core.signals import global_signals
from widgets.studio_dialog_base import StudioBaseDialog


class RevisionTaskItemWidget(QFrame):
    """ردیف هر تسک با استایل کامپکت و پشتیبانی از متن چندخطی"""
    def __init__(self, task: ProjectRevisionTask, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.task_id = task.id
        self.is_completed = task.is_completed
        self.task = task
        self.setObjectName("RevisionTaskItem")
        self.setLayoutDirection(Qt.LayoutDirection.RightToLeft)
        self.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        self.setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy.Policy.Expanding, QSizePolicy.Policy.Minimum)
        self.init_ui()

    def init_ui(self):
        layout = QHBoxLayout(self)
        layout.setContentsMargins(12, 8, 12, 8)
        layout.setSpacing(10)

        # دکمه چک‌باکس
        self.btn_check = QPushButton()
        self.btn_check.setFixedSize(22, 22)
        self.btn_check.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        self.btn_check.clicked.connect(self._toggle)
        layout.addWidget(self.btn_check, alignment=Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignTop)

        # متن تسک
        self.lbl_text = QLabel(self.task.task_text)
        self.lbl_text.setWordWrap(True)
        self.lbl_text.setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy.Policy.Expanding, QSizePolicy.Policy.Minimum)
        self.lbl_text.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 12px; font-weight: 500; border: none; background: transparent; padding: 2px 0;")
        layout.addWidget(self.lbl_text, stretch=1)

        # زمان و حذف
        left_box = QHBoxLayout()
        left_box.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        left_box.setSpacing(6)
        left_box.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignTop | Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignLeft)

        self.lbl_dt = QLabel()
        self.lbl_dt.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 11px; color: #10B981; border: none; background: transparent; padding-top: 2px;")
        if self.task.is_completed and self.task.completed_at:
            j_dt = jdatetime.datetime.fromgregorian(datetime=self.task.completed_at).strftime("%H:%M")
            self.lbl_dt.setText(f"✓ ({to_persian_numbers(j_dt)})")
        left_box.addWidget(self.lbl_dt)

        btn_del = QPushButton("×")
        btn_del.setFixedSize(20, 20)
        btn_del.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        btn_del.setStyleSheet("background: transparent; color: #EF4444; font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold; border: none;")
        btn_del.clicked.connect(self._delete_self)
        left_box.addWidget(btn_del)

        layout.addLayout(left_box)
        self._update_appearance()

    def _update_appearance(self):
        if self.is_completed:
            self.setStyleSheet("""
                QFrame#RevisionTaskItem {
                    background-color: rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.08);
                    border: none;
                    border-radius: 6px;
                }
            """)
            self.btn_check.setText("✓")
            self.btn_check.setStyleSheet("background-color: #10B981; color: #FFFFFF; border: none; border-radius: 11px; font-size: 11px; font-weight: bold;")
            self.lbl_text.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 12px; color: #F8FAFC; border: none; background: transparent; padding: 2px 0;")
        else:
            self.setStyleSheet("""
                QFrame#RevisionTaskItem {
                    background-color: #161F30;
                    border: none;
                    border-radius: 6px;
                }
                QFrame#RevisionTaskItem:hover {
                    background-color: #1E283D;
                }
            """)
            self.btn_check.setText("")
            self.btn_check.setStyleSheet("background-color: #242E42; border: none; border-radius: 11px;")
            self.lbl_text.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 12px; color: #94A3B8; border: none; background: transparent; padding: 2px 0;")

    def _toggle(self):
        res = ProjectController.toggle_revision_task(self.task_id)
        if res is not None:
            self.is_completed = res
            self._update_appearance()

    def _delete_self(self):
        ProjectController.delete_revision_task(self.task_id)

    def mouseReleaseEvent(self, event):
        if event.button() == Qt.MouseButton.LeftButton:
            event.accept()
            self._toggle()
            return
        super().mouseReleaseEvent(event)


class RevisionRoundFolderDialog(StudioBaseDialog):
    """دیالوگ مشاهده و مدیریت تسک‌های دور اصلاحیه با هدر استودیویی"""
    def __init__(self, revision_id: int, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.revision_id = revision_id
        self.setFixedWidth(540)
        self.setMinimumHeight(440)
        
        self.init_ui()
        self._connect_signals()
        self.refresh_tasks_only()

    def init_ui(self):
        self.build_header("مشاهده و مدیریت دور اصلاحیه", "چک‌لیست تغییرات و تسک‌های اعمال‌شده", icon_name="folder", icon_color="#6366F1")

        # اطلاعات وضعیت
        meta_row = QHBoxLayout()
        meta_row.setSpacing(6)
        
        self.status_tag = QLabel()
        meta_row.addWidget(self.status_tag)
        
        self.date_lbl = QLabel()
        self.date_lbl.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 11.5px; color: #94A3B8; border: none;")
        meta_row.addWidget(self.date_lbl, stretch=1)
        
        self.prog_lbl = QLabel()
        self.prog_lbl.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-weight: bold; font-size: 12px; border: none;")
        meta_row.addWidget(self.prog_lbl)
        
        self.container_layout.addLayout(meta_row)

        self.pb = QProgressBar()
        self.pb.setRange(0, 100)
        self.pb.setTextVisible(False)
        self.pb.setFixedHeight(4)
        self.container_layout.addWidget(self.pb)

        # ورودی تسک جدید
        add_box = QHBoxLayout()
        add_box.setSpacing(8)
        self.inp_new_task = QLineEdit()
        self.inp_new_task.setFixedHeight(36)
        self.inp_new_task.setPlaceholderText("+ افزودن تغییر جدید به این دور...")
        self.inp_new_task.setStyleSheet("background-color: #161F30; border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.08); border-radius: 6px; padding: 0 12px; font-size: 12px; color: #F8FAFC;")
        self.inp_new_task.returnPressed.connect(self._add_task)
        add_box.addWidget(self.inp_new_task, stretch=1)

        btn_add = QPushButton("افزودن")
        btn_add.setFixedHeight(36)
        btn_add.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        btn_add.setStyleSheet("background-color: #6366F1; color: #FFFFFF; font-weight: bold; border: none; border-radius: 6px; padding: 0 16px; font-size: 12px;")
        btn_add.clicked.connect(self._add_task)
        add_box.addWidget(btn_add)
        self.container_layout.addLayout(add_box)

        # اسکرول تسک‌ها
        scroll = QScrollArea()
        scroll.setWidgetResizable(True)
        scroll.setFrameShape(QFrame.Shape.NoFrame)
        scroll.setHorizontalScrollBarPolicy(Qt.ScrollBarPolicy.ScrollBarAlwaysOff)
        scroll.setStyleSheet("background: transparent; border: none;")
        
        self.tasks_container = QWidget()
        self.tasks_container.setStyleSheet("background: transparent;")
        self.t_lay = QVBoxLayout(self.tasks_container)
        self.t_lay.setContentsMargins(0, 4, 0, 4)
        self.t_lay.setSpacing(6)

        scroll.setWidget(self.tasks_container)
        self.container_layout.addWidget(scroll, stretch=1)

        # دکمه بستن
        foot_row = QHBoxLayout()
        foot_row.addStretch()
        btn_close = QPushButton("بستن جزئیات")
        btn_close.setFixedHeight(36)
        btn_close.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        btn_close.setStyleSheet("background-color: #161F30; color: #94A3B8; font-weight: 600; border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.08); border-radius: 6px; padding: 0 20px; font-size: 12px;")
        btn_close.clicked.connect(self.accept)
        foot_row.addWidget(btn_close)
        self.container_layout.addLayout(foot_row)

    def refresh_tasks_only(self):
        rev = None
        for p in ProjectController.get_all_projects() + ProjectController.get_archived_projects():
            for r in p.revisions:
                if r.id == self.revision_id:
                    rev = r
                    break
            if rev: 
                break

        if not rev:
            self.reject()
            return

        completed_cnt, total_cnt, pct = ProjectController.calculate_revision_progress(rev)
        is_completed = (pct == 100) and (total_cnt > 0)

        tag_bg = "rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.15)" if is_completed else "rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.15)"
        tag_color = "#10B981" if is_completed else "#F59E0B"
        self.status_tag.setText("✓ تکمیل شده" if is_completed else "● در حال انجام")
        self.status_tag.setStyleSheet(f"background-color: {tag_bg}; color: {tag_color}; border: none; padding: 3px 8px; border-radius: 5px; font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-weight: bold; font-size: 11px;")

        self.date_lbl.setText(f"ثبت: {gregorian_to_jalali(rev.requested_at)} | کل تغییرات: {to_persian_numbers(total_cnt)}")

        self.prog_lbl.setText(f"{to_persian_numbers(completed_cnt)} از {to_persian_numbers(total_cnt)} ({to_persian_numbers(pct)}٪)")
        self.prog_lbl.setStyleSheet(f"font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-weight: bold; font-size: 12px; color: {tag_color}; border: none;")
        self.pb.setValue(pct)
        self.pb.setStyleSheet(f"QProgressBar {{ background-color: #1A2234; border: none; border-radius: 2px; }} QProgressBar::chunk {{ background-color: {tag_color}; border-radius: 2px; }}")

        while self.t_lay.count():
            item = self.t_lay.takeAt(0)
            w = item.widget()
            if w:
                w.deleteLater()

        if rev.tasks:
            for t in rev.tasks:
                self.t_lay.addWidget(RevisionTaskItemWidget(t))
        else:
            empty_lbl = QLabel("هنوز تغییری در این دوره ثبت نشده است.")
            empty_lbl.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; color: #64748B; font-size: 12px; padding: 24px 0; border: none;")
            empty_lbl.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
            self.t_lay.addWidget(empty_lbl)

        self.t_lay.addStretch()

    def _add_task(self):
        txt = self.inp_new_task.text().strip()
        if txt:
            ProjectController.add_task_to_revision(self.revision_id, txt)
            self.inp_new_task.clear()

    def _connect_signals(self):
        global_signals.revision_task_toggled.connect(lambda _p, rid, _t: self._on_sync(rid))
        global_signals.revision_updated.connect(lambda _p, rid: self._on_sync(rid))

    def _on_sync(self, rid: int):
        if rid == self.revision_id:
            QTimer.singleShot(0, self.refresh_tasks_only)


class RevisionFolderSummaryCard(QFrame):
    """کارت خلاصه هر دوره اصلاحیه در پرونده پروژه"""
    def __init__(self, revision: ProjectRevision, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.setObjectName("SurfaceCard")
        self.revision = revision
        self.revision_id = revision.id
        self.setLayoutDirection(Qt.LayoutDirection.RightToLeft)
        self.init_ui()

    def init_ui(self):
        completed_cnt, total_cnt, pct = ProjectController.calculate_revision_progress(self.revision)
        is_completed = (pct == 100) and (total_cnt > 0)
        is_active = not is_completed

        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        layout.setContentsMargins(14, 12, 14, 12)
        layout.setSpacing(8)

        top_row = QHBoxLayout()
        top_row.setSpacing(6)

        lbl_title = QLabel(self.revision.title)
        lbl_title.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 13.5px; font-weight: 800; color: #F8FAFC; border: none;")
        top_row.addWidget(lbl_title, stretch=1)

        status_lbl = QLabel()
        if is_completed:
            status_lbl.setText("✓ تکمیل شده")
            status_lbl.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 10.5px; font-weight: 600; color: #10B981; background-color: rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.12); border-radius: 4px; padding: 2px 6px;")
        elif completed_cnt > 0:
            status_lbl.setText("● در حال انجام")
            status_lbl.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 10.5px; font-weight: 600; color: #F59E0B; background-color: rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.12); border-radius: 4px; padding: 2px 6px;")
        else:
            status_lbl.setText("○ در انتظار شروع")
            status_lbl.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 10.5px; font-weight: 500; color: #94A3B8; background-color: #242E42; border-radius: 4px; padding: 2px 6px;")
        top_row.addWidget(status_lbl)
        layout.addLayout(top_row)

        mid_row = QHBoxLayout()
        mid_row.setSpacing(6)
        fraction_text = f"{to_persian_numbers(completed_cnt)} از {to_persian_numbers(total_cnt)} تغییر انجام شده"
        lbl_fraction = QLabel(fraction_text)
        lbl_fraction.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 11.5px; font-weight: 500; color: #94A3B8; border: none;")
        mid_row.addWidget(lbl_fraction, stretch=1)

        pct_color = "#10B981" if is_completed else ("#F59E0B" if is_active else "#6366F1")
        lbl_pct = QLabel(f"{to_persian_numbers(pct)}٪")
        lbl_pct.setStyleSheet(f"font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 12.5px; font-weight: 800; color: {pct_color}; border: none;")
        mid_row.addWidget(lbl_pct)
        layout.addLayout(mid_row)

        pb = QProgressBar()
        pb.setRange(0, 100)
        pb.setValue(pct)
        pb.setTextVisible(False)
        pb.setFixedHeight(3)
        pb.setStyleSheet(f"QProgressBar {{ background-color: #242E42; border: none; border-radius: 1.5px; }} QProgressBar::chunk {{ background-color: {pct_color}; border-radius: 1.5px; }}")
        layout.addWidget(pb)

        date_row = QHBoxLayout()
        date_row.setSpacing(8)
        reg_date = gregorian_to_jalali(self.revision.requested_at)
        dates_text = f"ثبت: {reg_date}"
        if self.revision.completed_at:
            dates_text += f" | تکمیل: {gregorian_to_jalali(self.revision.completed_at)}"
        elif is_active:
            rem_cnt = total_cnt - completed_cnt
            if rem_cnt > 0:
                dates_text += f" | {to_persian_numbers(rem_cnt)} تغییر مانده"

        lbl_dates = QLabel(dates_text)
        lbl_dates.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 10.5px; color: #64748B; border: none;")
        date_row.addWidget(lbl_dates, stretch=1)
        layout.addLayout(date_row)

        divider = QFrame()
        divider.setFrameShape(QFrame.Shape.HLine)
        divider.setFixedHeight(1)
        divider.setStyleSheet("background-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05); border: none;")
        layout.addWidget(divider)

        foot_row = QHBoxLayout()
        foot_row.setSpacing(6)
        if self.revision.extra_fee > 0:
            lbl_fee = QLabel(f"هزینه دوره: {format_toman(self.revision.extra_fee)}")
            lbl_fee.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 11px; font-weight: 600; color: #FBBF24; border: none;")
            foot_row.addWidget(lbl_fee)

        foot_row.addStretch()
        btn_action = QPushButton("ادامه اصلاحیه ←" if is_active else "مشاهده جزئیات ←")
        btn_action.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        if is_active:
            btn_action.setStyleSheet("QPushButton { font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 11.5px; font-weight: 700; background-color: rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.15); color: #F59E0B; border: none; border-radius: 5px; padding: 4px 10px; } QPushButton:hover { background-color: #F59E0B; color: #000000; }")
        else:
            btn_action.setStyleSheet("QPushButton { font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 11.5px; font-weight: 600; background-color: transparent; color: #6366F1; border: none; padding: 4px 6px; } QPushButton:hover { color: #7C80F8; }")
        btn_action.clicked.connect(self._open_folder)
        foot_row.addWidget(btn_action)
        layout.addLayout(foot_row)

        self.setStyleSheet("QFrame#SurfaceCard { background-color: #161F30; border: none; border-radius: 8px; } QFrame#SurfaceCard:hover { background-color: #1A253A; }")

    def _open_folder(self):
        diag = RevisionRoundFolderDialog(self.revision_id, self.window())
        diag.exec()

    def mouseReleaseEvent(self, event):
        if event.button() == Qt.MouseButton.LeftButton:
            event.accept()
            QTimer.singleShot(0, self._open_folder)
            return
        super().mouseReleaseEvent(event)


class ProjectTimelineWidget(QWidget):
    """بخش تاریخچه دوره‌های اصلاحیه در پاپ‌آپ پرونده"""
    def __init__(self, project_id: int, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.project_id = project_id
        self.setLayoutDirection(Qt.LayoutDirection.RightToLeft)
        self.init_ui()
        self.refresh_timeline()

    def init_ui(self):
        self.main_layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        self.main_layout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        self.main_layout.setSpacing(8)

    def refresh_timeline(self):
        while self.main_layout.count():
            item = self.main_layout.takeAt(0)
            w = item.widget()
            if w:
                w.deleteLater()

        project = ProjectController.get_project_by_id(self.project_id)
        if not project:
            return

        if not project.revisions:
            empty_frame = QFrame()
            empty_frame.setStyleSheet("background-color: #121826; border: none; border-radius: 8px; padding: 18px;")
            empty_lay = QVBoxLayout(empty_frame)
            empty_lay.setSpacing(4)
            empty_lay.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)

            lbl_empty_title = QLabel("هنوز دوره‌ای از اصلاحیه برای این پروژه ثبت نشده است.")
            lbl_empty_title.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 13px; font-weight: 700; color: #94A3B8; border: none;")
            lbl_empty_title.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
            empty_lay.addWidget(lbl_empty_title)

            lbl_empty_desc = QLabel("دوره‌های اصلاحیه پس از تحویل اولیه و با ارسال موارد تغییرات از طرف استودیو ایجاد می‌شوند.")
            lbl_empty_desc.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 11px; color: #64748B; border: none;")
            lbl_empty_desc.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
            empty_lay.addWidget(lbl_empty_desc)

            self.main_layout.addWidget(empty_frame)
            return

        for rev in project.revisions:
            self.main_layout.addWidget(RevisionFolderSummaryCard(rev))


class NewRevisionDialog(StudioBaseDialog):
    """دیالوگ ثبت دور جدید اصلاحیه با سیستم دیالوگ استودیو"""
    def __init__(self, project_id: int, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.project_id = project_id
        
        project = ProjectController.get_project_by_id(self.project_id)
        self.round_number = (len(project.revisions) + 1) if project else 1
        self.round_title = get_persian_round_title(self.round_number)

        self.setFixedWidth(480)
        self.init_ui()

    def init_ui(self):
        self.build_header(f"ثبت {self.round_title}", "تعریف موارد تغییرات درخواستی استودیو", icon_name="revision", icon_color="#EA580C")

        self.container_layout.addWidget(QLabel("تغییرات مدنظر را وارد کرده و دکمه افزودن را بزنید:"))

        add_task_row = QHBoxLayout()
        add_task_row.setSpacing(8)
        self.input_task = QLineEdit()
        self.input_task.setFixedHeight(36)
        self.input_task.setPlaceholderText("مثال: اصلاح رنگ سکانس رقص، تعویض موزیک...")
        self.input_task.setStyleSheet("background-color: #161F30; border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.08); border-radius: 6px; padding: 0 10px; font-size: 12px; color: #F8FAFC;")
        self.input_task.returnPressed.connect(self._add_task)
        add_task_row.addWidget(self.input_task, stretch=1)

        btn_add = QPushButton("+ افزودن")
        btn_add.setFixedHeight(36)
        btn_add.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        btn_add.setStyleSheet("background-color: #6366F1; color: #FFFFFF; font-weight: bold; border-radius: 6px; padding: 0 14px; font-size: 12px; border: none;")
        btn_add.clicked.connect(self._add_task)
        add_task_row.addWidget(btn_add)
        self.container_layout.addLayout(add_task_row)

        self.tasks_list = QListWidget()
        self.tasks_list.setFixedHeight(120)
        self.tasks_list.setStyleSheet("""
            QListWidget {
                background-color: #121826;
                border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.06);
                border-radius: 6px;
                color: #F8FAFC;
                padding: 4px;
                font-family: 'Vazirmatn';
                font-size: 11.5px;
            }
            QListWidget::item {
                padding: 6px 10px;
                border-radius: 4px;
                background-color: #161F30;
                margin-bottom: 3px;
            }
        """)
        self.tasks_list.itemDoubleClicked.connect(self._remove_selected_task)
        self.container_layout.addWidget(self.tasks_list)

        lbl_hint = QLabel("<span style='color:#64748B; font-size:10.5px;'>* برای حذف هر مورد، روی آن دوبار کلیک کنید.</span>")
        self.container_layout.addWidget(lbl_hint)

        self.chk_fee = QCheckBox("این دوره از اصلاحیه‌ها مشمول هزینه مازاد است")
        self.chk_fee.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 11px; color: #94A3B8;")
        self.chk_fee.toggled.connect(lambda v: self.input_fee.setEnabled(v))
        self.container_layout.addWidget(self.chk_fee)

        self.input_fee = QLineEdit()
        self.input_fee.setFixedHeight(34)
        self.input_fee.setPlaceholderText("مبلغ توافق‌شده به تومان (مثال: 500000)...")
        self.input_fee.setStyleSheet("background-color: #161F30; border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.08); border-radius: 6px; padding: 0 10px; font-size: 12px; color: #F8FAFC;")
        self.input_fee.setEnabled(False)
        self.container_layout.addWidget(self.input_fee)

        btn_box = QHBoxLayout()
        btn_box.setSpacing(8)

        self.btn_submit = QPushButton(f"ایجاد {self.round_title}")
        self.btn_submit.setFixedHeight(38)
        self.btn_submit.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        self.btn_submit.setStyleSheet("background-color: #EA580C; color: #FFFFFF; font-weight: bold; border-radius: 6px; border: none; font-size: 12.5px;")
        self.btn_submit.clicked.connect(self._submit)
        btn_box.addWidget(self.btn_submit, stretch=2)

        btn_cancel = QPushButton("انصراف")
        btn_cancel.setFixedHeight(38)
        btn_cancel.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        btn_cancel.setStyleSheet("background-color: #161F30; color: #94A3B8; border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.08); border-radius: 6px; font-size: 12px;")
        btn_cancel.clicked.connect(self.reject)
        btn_box.addWidget(btn_cancel, stretch=1)

        self.container_layout.addLayout(btn_box)

    def _add_task(self):
        t = self.input_task.text().strip()
        if t:
            self.tasks_list.addItem(t)
            self.input_task.clear()

    def _remove_selected_task(self, item: QListWidgetItem):
        row = self.tasks_list.row(item)
        self.tasks_list.takeItem(row)

    def _submit(self):
        tasks = [self.tasks_list.item(i).text().strip() for i in range(self.tasks_list.count())]
        current_input = self.input_task.text().strip()
        if current_input and current_input not in tasks:
            tasks.append(current_input)

        if not tasks:
            return

        fee = 0
        if self.chk_fee.isChecked():
            fee_str = normalize_to_english_digits(self.input_fee.text().strip())
            fee = int(fee_str) if fee_str.isdigit() else 0

        self.btn_submit.setEnabled(False)
        if ProjectController.create_revision_round(self.project_id, "", tasks, fee):
            self.accept()
        else:
            self.btn_submit.setEnabled(True)

from PySide6.QtWidgets import QFrame, QHBoxLayout, QPushButton, QLabel, QWidget
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt, QPoint, QSize, QTimer
from core.icon_provider import IconProvider
from core.nle_messenger_helper import ClientMessengerHelper
from core.signals import global_signals


class QuickMessengerMiniPopup(QFrame):
    def __init__(self, project_data: dict, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent, Qt.WindowType.Popup | Qt.WindowType.FramelessWindowHint)
        self.project_data = project_data
        self.setAttribute(Qt.WidgetAttribute.WA_TranslucentBackground, True)
        self.setLayoutDirection(Qt.LayoutDirection.RightToLeft)
        self.init_ui()

    def init_ui(self):
        self.setObjectName("QuickMessengerContainer")
        self.setStyleSheet("""
            QFrame#QuickMessengerContainer {
                background-color: #121826;
                border: 1px solid rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.45);
                border-radius: 20px;
            }
        """)

        layout = QHBoxLayout(self)
        layout.setContentsMargins(6, 4, 6, 4)
        layout.setSpacing(4)

        # دکمه تلگرام
        btn_tg = QPushButton("تلگرام")
        btn_tg.setIcon(IconProvider.get_icon("telegram", "#38BDF8", size=13))
        btn_tg.setIconSize(QSize(13, 13))
        btn_tg.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        btn_tg.setFixedHeight(28)
        btn_tg.setStyleSheet("""
            QPushButton {
                font-family: 'Vazirmatn';
                font-size: 11px;
                font-weight: 700;
                color: #F8FAFC;
                background-color: rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.12);
                border: 1px solid rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.25);
                border-radius: 14px;
                padding: 0 10px;
            }
            QPushButton:hover {
                background-color: #0284C7;
                color: #FFFFFF;
            }
        """)
        btn_tg.clicked.connect(lambda: self._handle_send("telegram"))
        layout.addWidget(btn_tg)

        # دکمه واتساپ
        btn_wa = QPushButton("واتساپ")
        btn_wa.setIcon(IconProvider.get_icon("whatsapp", "#10B981", size=13))
        btn_wa.setIconSize(QSize(13, 13))
        btn_wa.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        btn_wa.setFixedHeight(28)
        btn_wa.setStyleSheet("""
            QPushButton {
                font-family: 'Vazirmatn';
                font-size: 11px;
                font-weight: 700;
                color: #F8FAFC;
                background-color: rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.12);
                border: 1px solid rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.25);
                border-radius: 14px;
                padding: 0 10px;
            }
            QPushButton:hover {
                background-color: #059669;
                color: #FFFFFF;
            }
        """)
        btn_wa.clicked.connect(lambda: self._handle_send("whatsapp"))
        layout.addWidget(btn_wa)

    def _handle_send(self, app_type: str):
        msg = ClientMessengerHelper.generate_client_message(self.project_data)
        ClientMessengerHelper.copy_message_to_clipboard(msg)
        ClientMessengerHelper.launch_messenger_app(app_type)
        global_signals.show_toast.emit(f"متن پیام کپی شد و {app_type.capitalize()} اجرا گردید.", "success")
        self.close()

    def show_beside_widget(self, target_widget: QWidget):
        self.adjustSize()
        glob_pos = target_widget.mapToGlobal(QPoint(0, 0))
        # قرارگیری نرم دقیقاً بالای دکمه یا کنار آن
        target_x = glob_pos.x() - (self.width() // 2) + (target_widget.width() // 2)
        target_y = glob_pos.y() - self.height() - 6
        if target_y < 50:
            target_y = glob_pos.y() + target_widget.height() + 6
        self.move(target_x, target_y)
        self.show()

# FILE: widgets/revenue_bar_chart.py
# ==================================================
from typing import List, Dict, Any
from PySide6.QtWidgets import QWidget, QToolTip
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt, QRectF
from PySide6.QtGui import QPainter, QColor, QBrush, QLinearGradient, QFont, QPen

from core.persian_utils import format_toman, to_persian_numbers


class RevenueBarChartWidget(QWidget):
    """
    نمودار میله‌ای اختصاصی و کاملاً ریسپانسیو برای نمایش درآمد ۱۲ ماه سال
    دارای محاسبه خودکار ضخامت میله‌ها و مقیاس فونت متناسب با عرض متغیر پنجره
    """
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.setLayoutDirection(Qt.LayoutDirection.RightToLeft)
        self.setMouseTracking(True)
        self.setMinimumHeight(220)
        self.monthly_data: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        self.hovered_idx = -1

    def set_data(self, monthly_data: List[Dict[str, Any]]):
        self.monthly_data = monthly_data
        self.update()

    def paintEvent(self, event):
        painter = QPainter(self)
        painter.setRenderHint(QPainter.RenderHint.Antialiasing, True)
        painter.setRenderHint(QPainter.RenderHint.TextAntialiasing, True)

        w = float(self.width())
        h = float(self.height())

        margin_top = 28.0
        margin_bottom = 36.0
        margin_x = 16.0

        chart_w = max(10.0, w - (margin_x * 2.0))
        chart_h = max(10.0, h - margin_top - margin_bottom)

        if not self.monthly_data:
            painter.setPen(QColor("#64748B"))
            painter.setFont(QFont("Vazirmatn", 11))
            painter.drawText(self.rect(), Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter, "داده‌ای برای نمایش درآمد ماهانه ثبت نشده است.")
            painter.end()
            return

        max_amount = max([m.get("amount", 0) for m in self.monthly_data] + [1])
        count = len(self.monthly_data)
        col_slot_w = chart_w / max(1, count)
        
        # محاسبه ریسپانسیو ضخامت میله‌ها متناسب با پهنای صفحه
        bar_w = max(12.0, min(36.0, col_slot_w * 0.52))

        # خطوط راهنمای افقی (Grid Lines)
        painter.setPen(QPen(QColor(255, 255, 255, 12), 1, Qt.PenStyle.DashLine))
        for step in range(1, 4):
            y_grid = margin_top + (chart_h * (step / 3.0))
            painter.drawLine(int(margin_x), int(y_grid), int(w - margin_x), int(y_grid))

        for i, item in enumerate(self.monthly_data):
            amount = item.get("amount", 0)
            month_name = item.get("month", "")

            # محاسبه مختصات از راست به چپ
            x_center = margin_x + (i + 0.5) * col_slot_w
            bar_x = x_center - (bar_w / 2.0)

            ratio = amount / max_amount if max_amount > 0 else 0
            bar_h = max(4.0, chart_h * ratio) if amount > 0 else 4.0
            bar_y = margin_top + chart_h - bar_h

            rect = QRectF(bar_x, bar_y, bar_w, bar_h)

            # گرادینت ستون
            grad = QLinearGradient(bar_x, bar_y, bar_x, bar_y + bar_h)
            if i == self.hovered_idx:
                grad.setColorAt(0.0, QColor("#34D399"))
                grad.setColorAt(1.0, QColor("#059669"))
            elif amount > 0:
                grad.setColorAt(0.0, QColor("#818CF8"))
                grad.setColorAt(1.0, QColor("#4F46E5"))
            else:
                grad.setColorAt(0.0, QColor("#1E283D"))
                grad.setColorAt(1.0, QColor("#161F30"))

            painter.setPen(Qt.PenStyle.NoPen)
            painter.setBrush(QBrush(grad))
            painter.drawRoundedRect(rect, 4, 4)

            # نام ماه
            font_size = 8 if col_slot_w < 45 else 9
            painter.setFont(QFont("Vazirmatn", font_size, QFont.Weight.Bold if i == self.hovered_idx else QFont.Weight.Normal))
            painter.setPen(QColor("#F8FAFC" if i == self.hovered_idx else "#94A3B8"))
            lbl_rect = QRectF(x_center - (col_slot_w / 2.0), h - margin_bottom + 8, col_slot_w, 20)
            painter.drawText(lbl_rect, Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter, month_name)

            # مبلغ بالای ستون
            if amount > 0 and (i == self.hovered_idx or (ratio > 0.35 and col_slot_w >= 45)):
                painter.setFont(QFont("Vazirmatn", 8, QFont.Weight.Bold))
                painter.setPen(QColor("#10B981" if i == self.hovered_idx else "#818CF8"))
                val_str = to_persian_numbers(f"{amount // 1_000_000:,} م") if amount >= 1_000_000 else to_persian_numbers(f"{amount // 1_000:,} هـ")
                val_rect = QRectF(x_center - 30, bar_y - 18, 60, 16)
                painter.drawText(val_rect, Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter, val_str)

        painter.end()

    def mouseMoveEvent(self, event):
        if not self.monthly_data:
            return

        w = float(self.width())
        margin_x = 16.0
        chart_w = max(10.0, w - (margin_x * 2.0))
        col_slot_w = chart_w / len(self.monthly_data)

        pos_x = event.position().x() if hasattr(event, "position") else event.pos().x()
        global_pos = event.globalPosition().toPoint() if hasattr(event, "globalPosition") else event.globalPos()

        if margin_x <= pos_x <= (w - margin_x):
            idx = int((pos_x - margin_x) // col_slot_w)
            if 0 <= idx < len(self.monthly_data):
                if self.hovered_idx != idx:
                    self.hovered_idx = idx
                    self.update()

                item = self.monthly_data[idx]
                month = item.get("month", "")
                amount_str = format_toman(item.get("amount", 0))
                count_str = to_persian_numbers(item.get("count", 0))
                tip = f"{month}\nدرآمد وصول‌شده: {amount_str}\nتعداد واریزی: {count_str} تراکنش"
                QToolTip.showText(global_pos, tip, self)
                return

        if self.hovered_idx != -1:
            self.hovered_idx = -1
            self.update()
            QToolTip.hideText()

        super().mouseMoveEvent(event)

    def leaveEvent(self, event):
        if self.hovered_idx != -1:
            self.hovered_idx = -1
            self.update()
            QToolTip.hideText()
        super().leaveEvent(event)

# ==================================================
# FILE: widgets\segmented_timeline.py
# ==================================================
# widgets/segmented_timeline.py
from PySide6.QtWidgets import QWidget, QToolTip
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt, QRectF, QVariantAnimation, QEasingCurve
from PySide6.QtGui import QPainter, QColor, QBrush
from config import PIPELINE_STAGES


class SegmentedTimelineWidget(QWidget):
    """
    ویجت تایم‌لاین سگمنتی به سبک نرم‌افزارهای تدوین NLE
    نمایش مراحل پروژه به صورت بلاک‌های گرافیکی تفکیک‌شده
    """
    def __init__(self, current_stage="rough_cut", parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.current_stage = self._normalize_stage(current_stage)
        self.setFixedHeight(10)
        self.setMouseTracking(True)
        
        self.stages_keys = list(PIPELINE_STAGES.keys())
        self.anim_progress = float(self._get_target_progress())

        self.anim = QVariantAnimation(self)
        self.anim.setDuration(250)
        self.anim.setEasingCurve(QEasingCurve.Type.OutCubic)
        self.anim.valueChanged.connect(self._on_anim_value_changed)

    def _normalize_stage(self, stage_val) -> str:
        if hasattr(stage_val, "value"):
            stage_str = str(stage_val.value).lower()
        else:
            stage_str = str(stage_val).lower() if stage_val else "rough_cut"
        
        stage_mapping = {
            "ingest": "rough_cut",
            "color_audio": "color_grading",
            "audio_vfx": "audio_vfx",
        }
        return stage_mapping.get(stage_str, stage_str)

    def _get_target_progress(self) -> float:
        if self.current_stage not in self.stages_keys:
            return 0.2
        idx = self.stages_keys.index(self.current_stage)
        return (idx + 1) / max(1, len(self.stages_keys))

    def _on_anim_value_changed(self, value):
        self.anim_progress = float(value)
        self.update()

    def set_stage(self, stage_key):
        normalized = self._normalize_stage(stage_key)
        if self.current_stage == normalized:
            return
        self.current_stage = normalized
        target = float(self._get_target_progress())
        self.anim.stop()
        self.anim.setStartValue(float(self.anim_progress))
        self.anim.setEndValue(float(target))
        self.anim.start()

    def paintEvent(self, event):
        painter = QPainter(self)
        painter.setRenderHint(QPainter.RenderHint.Antialiasing, True)

        w = float(self.width())
        h = float(self.height())
        total_stages = len(self.stages_keys)
        spacing = 4.0
        total_spacing = spacing * (total_stages - 1)
        seg_w = max(4.0, (w - total_spacing) / total_stages)

        current_idx = self.stages_keys.index(self.current_stage) if self.current_stage in self.stages_keys else 0

        for i, key in enumerate(self.stages_keys):
            x = i * (seg_w + spacing)
            rect = QRectF(x, 1.0, seg_w, h - 2.0)
            stage_color = QColor(PIPELINE_STAGES[key].get("color", "#6366F1"))

            painter.setPen(Qt.PenStyle.NoPen)
            if i <= current_idx:
                painter.setBrush(QBrush(stage_color))
            else:
                painter.setBrush(QBrush(QColor("#242E42")))

            painter.drawRoundedRect(rect, 3, 3)

        painter.end()

    def mouseMoveEvent(self, event):
        w = float(self.width())
        total_stages = len(self.stages_keys)
        spacing = 4.0
        seg_w = max(4.0, (w - (spacing * (total_stages - 1))) / total_stages)

        if hasattr(event, "pos"):
            pos_x = event.pos().x()
            global_pos = event.globalPos()
        else:
            pos_x = event.position().x()
            global_pos = event.globalPosition().toPoint()

        hovered_idx = int(pos_x // (seg_w + spacing))

        if 0 <= hovered_idx < total_stages:
            key = self.stages_keys[hovered_idx]
            label = PIPELINE_STAGES[key].get("label", key)
            curr_idx = self.stages_keys.index(self.current_stage) if self.current_stage in self.stages_keys else 0
            
            if hovered_idx < curr_idx:
                status = "✓ تکمیل شده"
            elif hovered_idx == curr_idx:
                status = "● در حال انجام"
            else:
                status = "○ در نوبت"

            QToolTip.showText(global_pos, f"{label} ({status})", self)
        else:
            QToolTip.hideText()
            
        super().mouseMoveEvent(event)

# ==================================================
# FILE: widgets\stage_stepper.py
# ==================================================
# widgets/stage_stepper.py
from PySide6.QtWidgets import QWidget, QHBoxLayout, QPushButton, QLabel, QFrame
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt, Signal
from config import PIPELINE_STAGES


class StageStepper(QWidget):
    stage_changed = Signal(str)

    def __init__(self, current_stage: str = "rough_cut", parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.setLayoutDirection(Qt.LayoutDirection.RightToLeft)
        self.current_stage = current_stage
        self.buttons = {}
        self.init_ui()

    def init_ui(self):
        layout = QHBoxLayout(self)
        layout.setContentsMargins(0, 6, 0, 6)
        layout.setSpacing(4)

        stages_list = list(PIPELINE_STAGES.items())
        current_idx = self._get_stage_index(self.current_stage)

        for idx, (key, info) in enumerate(stages_list):
            btn = QPushButton(info["label"])
            btn.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
            btn.setFixedHeight(28)
            
            # استایل‌دهی بر اساس وضعیت مرحله
            if idx < current_idx:
                # مرحله پشت سر گذاشته شده (تکمیل)
                btn.setStyleSheet("""
                    QPushButton {
                        background-color: rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.15);
                        color: #10B981;
                        border: 1px solid rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.3);
                        border-radius: 6px;
                        font-size: 11px;
                        font-weight: bold;
                        padding: 0 8px;
                    }
                    QPushButton:hover {
                        background-color: rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.25);
                    }
                """)
            elif idx == current_idx:
                # مرحله جاری و فعال
                btn.setStyleSheet("""
                    QPushButton {
                        background-color: #6366F1;
                        color: #FFFFFF;
                        border: 1px solid #7C80F8;
                        border-radius: 6px;
                        font-size: 11px;
                        font-weight: bold;
                        padding: 0 8px;
                    }
                    QPushButton:hover {
                        background-color: #7C80F8;
                    }
                """)
            else:
                # مراحل آینده
                btn.setStyleSheet("""
                    QPushButton {
                        background-color: #242E42;
                        color: #94A3B8;
                        border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08);
                        border-radius: 6px;
                        font-size: 11px;
                        padding: 0 8px;
                    }
                    QPushButton:hover {
                        background-color: #2D3952;
                        color: #F8FAFC;
                        border-color: rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.5);
                    }
                """)

            btn.clicked.connect(lambda _, k=key: self._on_step_clicked(k))
            layout.addWidget(btn, stretch=1)
            self.buttons[key] = btn

            # خط اتصال بین مراحل
            if idx < len(stages_list) - 1:
                line = QFrame()
                line.setFrameShape(QFrame.Shape.HLine)
                line.setFixedHeight(2)
                line_color = "#10B981" if idx < current_idx else ("rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.5)" if idx == current_idx else "rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08)")
                line.setStyleSheet(f"background-color: {line_color}; border: none;")
                layout.addWidget(line)

    def _get_stage_index(self, stage_key: str) -> int:
        stages_keys = list(PIPELINE_STAGES.keys())
        return stages_keys.index(stage_key) if stage_key in stages_keys else 0

    def _on_step_clicked(self, stage_key: str):
        self.current_stage = stage_key
        self.stage_changed.emit(stage_key)
        self._refresh_styles()

    def set_stage(self, stage_key: str):
        self.current_stage = stage_key
        self._refresh_styles()

    def _refresh_styles(self):
        # پاکسازی و چینش دوباره
        while self.layout().count():
            item = self.layout().takeAt(0)
            w = item.widget()
            if w:
                w.deleteLater()
        self.init_ui()

from enum import Enum
from typing import Optional
from PySide6.QtWidgets import QFrame, QHBoxLayout, QLabel
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt


class StatusType(str, Enum):
    ACTIVE = "active"          # سبز نیترو/ملایم (در حال تدوین، فعال)
    COMPLETED = "completed"    # زمردی (تکمیل شده، تحویل موفق)
    PENDING = "pending"        # زرد/کهربایی ملایم (منتظر بازبینی، در انتظار)
    WARNING = "warning"        # نارنجی ملایم (نزدیک به ددلاین، اصلاحیه)
    DANGER = "danger"          # قرمز ملایم (تأخیر، ددلاین گذشته)
    NEUTRAL = "neutral"        # خاکستری ملایم/خنثی (آرشیو شده، بدون فعالیت)


STATUS_THEMES = {
    StatusType.ACTIVE: {
        "bg": "rgba(52, 211, 153, 0.10)",
        "border": "rgba(52, 211, 153, 0.25)",
        "text": "#34D399",
        "dot": "#10B981",
        "symbol": "●"
    },
    StatusType.COMPLETED: {
        "bg": "rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.12)",
        "border": "rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.28)",
        "text": "#10B981",
        "dot": "#10B981",
        "symbol": "✓"
    },
    StatusType.PENDING: {
        "bg": "rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.10)",
        "border": "rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.25)",
        "text": "#FBBF24",
        "dot": "#F59E0B",
        "symbol": "●"
    },
    StatusType.WARNING: {
        "bg": "rgba(249, 115, 22, 0.10)",
        "border": "rgba(249, 115, 22, 0.25)",
        "text": "#FB923C",
        "dot": "#EA580C",
        "symbol": "!"
    },
    StatusType.DANGER: {
        "bg": "rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.10)",
        "border": "rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.28)",
        "text": "#F87171",
        "dot": "#EF4444",
        "symbol": "!"
    },
    StatusType.NEUTRAL: {
        "bg": "rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.08)",
        "border": "rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.18)",
        "text": "#94A3B8",
        "dot": "#64748B",
        "symbol": "●"
    },
}


class StatusPillBadge(QFrame):
    """بج کپسولی استاندارد، بدون وابستگی مخرب و دارای ابعاد پایدار"""
    def __init__(
        self,
        label: str,
        status_type: StatusType = StatusType.ACTIVE,
        custom_symbol: Optional[str] = None,
        parent=None
    ):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.label_text = label
        self.status_type = status_type
        self.custom_symbol = custom_symbol

        self.setObjectName("StatusPillBadgeFrame")
        self.setLayoutDirection(Qt.LayoutDirection.RightToLeft)
        self.setFixedHeight(24)
        self.setAttribute(Qt.WidgetAttribute.WA_TransparentForMouseEvents, True)
        self.init_ui()

    def init_ui(self):
        theme = STATUS_THEMES.get(self.status_type, STATUS_THEMES[StatusType.NEUTRAL])
        symbol = self.custom_symbol or theme["symbol"]

        layout = QHBoxLayout(self)
        layout.setContentsMargins(8, 0, 8, 0)
        layout.setSpacing(4)
        layout.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)

        self.lbl_indicator = QLabel(symbol)
        self.lbl_indicator.setStyleSheet(f"""
            font-family: 'Vazirmatn', sans-serif;
            font-size: 9.5px;
            font-weight: 900;
            color: {theme['dot']};
            background: transparent;
            border: none;
        """)
        layout.addWidget(self.lbl_indicator)

        self.lbl_text = QLabel(self.label_text)
        self.lbl_text.setStyleSheet(f"""
            font-family: 'Vazirmatn';
            font-size: 11px;
            font-weight: 600;
            color: {theme['text']};
            background: transparent;
            border: none;
        """)
        layout.addWidget(self.lbl_text)

        self.setStyleSheet(f"""
            QFrame#StatusPillBadgeFrame {{
                background-color: {theme['bg']};
                border: 1px solid {theme['border']};
                border-radius: 12px;
            }}
        """)

    def update_status(self, label: str, status_type: StatusType, custom_symbol: Optional[str] = None):
        self.label_text = label
        self.status_type = status_type
        self.custom_symbol = custom_symbol

        theme = STATUS_THEMES.get(self.status_type, STATUS_THEMES[StatusType.NEUTRAL])
        symbol = self.custom_symbol or theme["symbol"]

        self.lbl_indicator.setText(symbol)
        self.lbl_indicator.setStyleSheet(f"color: {theme['dot']}; background: transparent; border: none; font-size: 9.5px; font-weight: 900;")
        self.lbl_text.setText(self.label_text)
        self.lbl_text.setStyleSheet(f"color: {theme['text']}; background: transparent; border: none; font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 11px; font-weight: 600;")
        
        self.setStyleSheet(f"""
            QFrame#StatusPillBadgeFrame {{
                background-color: {theme['bg']};
                border: 1px solid {theme['border']};
                border-radius: 12px;
            }}
        """)

from PySide6.QtWidgets import QFrame, QVBoxLayout, QLabel, QSizePolicy
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt, QVariantAnimation, QEasingCurve
from core.persian_utils import format_toman, to_persian_numbers


class StatCard(QFrame):
    """
    کارت آماری مینیمال، مات و هماهنگ با دیزاین سیستم نرم‌افزار
    """
    def __init__(
        self, 
        title: str, 
        initial_value: int = 0, 
        is_currency: bool = False, 
        color_accent: str = "#818CF8", 
        parent=None
    ):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.setObjectName("SurfaceStatCard")
        self.setLayoutDirection(Qt.LayoutDirection.RightToLeft)
        self.title = title
        self.current_value = initial_value
        self.is_currency = is_currency
        self.color_accent = color_accent

        self.setFixedHeight(88)
        self.setMinimumWidth(160)
        self.setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy.Policy.Expanding, QSizePolicy.Fixed)

        self.init_ui()
        self._apply_style()

    def init_ui(self):
        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        layout.setContentsMargins(16, 12, 16, 12)
        layout.setSpacing(4)
        layout.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignVCenter)

        # عنوان کارت
        self.lbl_title = QLabel(self.title)
        self.lbl_title.setStyleSheet("""
            QLabel {
                font-family: 'Vazirmatn';
                font-size: 11.5px;
                font-weight: 500;
                color: #8F909D;
                background: transparent;
                border: none;
            }
        """)
        layout.addWidget(self.lbl_title)

        # مقدار اصلی
        self.lbl_value = QLabel()
        self.lbl_value.setStyleSheet(f"""
            QLabel {{
                font-family: 'Vazirmatn';
                font-size: 18px;
                font-weight: 800;
                color: {self.color_accent};
                background: transparent;
                border: none;
            }}
        """)
        self._render_value(self.current_value)
        layout.addWidget(self.lbl_value)

    def _apply_style(self):
        self.setStyleSheet("""
            QFrame#SurfaceStatCard {
                background-color: #121826;
                border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.06);
                border-radius: 8px;
            }
            QFrame#SurfaceStatCard:hover {
                background-color: #161F30;
                border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.12);
            }
        """)

    def _render_value(self, val: int):
        if self.is_currency:
            self.lbl_value.setText(format_toman(val))
        else:
            self.lbl_value.setText(to_persian_numbers(val))

    def animate_to_value(self, target_value: int):
        self.anim = QVariantAnimation(self)
        self.anim.setDuration(350)
        self.anim.setEasingCurve(QEasingCurve.Type.OutCubic)
        self.anim.setStartValue(self.current_value)
        self.anim.setEndValue(target_value)
        self.anim.valueChanged.connect(lambda v: self._render_value(int(v)))
        self.anim.start()
        self.current_value = target_value

from typing import Optional, Tuple
from PySide6.QtWidgets import (
    QDialog, QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout, QLabel, 
    QPushButton, QFrame, QGraphicsOpacityEffect, QLineEdit
)
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt, QPropertyAnimation, QEasingCurve, QSize
from PySide6.QtGui import QGuiApplication
from core.icon_provider import IconProvider


class StudioBaseDialog(QDialog):
    """
    کلاس پایه تمامی پاپ‌آپ‌ها و دیالوگ‌های نرم‌افزار با قابلیت:
    - ریسپانسیو بودن کامل و مهار ابعاد بر اساس ابعاد مانیتور فعال
    - فریم‌لس بودن و انیمیشن Fade-In روان
    - گوشه‌های گرد ۱۲px با بردر ظریف و تم تاریک هماهنگ
    """
    def __init__(self, parent=None, is_danger: bool = False):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.is_danger = is_danger
        self.setWindowFlags(Qt.WindowType.FramelessWindowHint | Qt.WindowType.Dialog)
        self.setAttribute(Qt.WidgetAttribute.WA_TranslucentBackground, True)
        self.setLayoutDirection(Qt.LayoutDirection.RightToLeft)

        # ساختار ریشه
        self._root_layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        self._root_layout.setContentsMargins(10, 10, 10, 10)

        # کانتینر اصلی
        self.modal_container = QFrame(self)
        self.modal_container.setObjectName("StudioModalContainer")
        
        border_color = "rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.40)" if is_danger else "rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08)"
        self.modal_container.setStyleSheet(f"""
            QFrame#StudioModalContainer {{
                background-color: #0E131F;
                border: 1px solid {border_color};
                border-radius: 12px;
            }}
            QLabel {{
                background: transparent;
                border: none;
            }}
        """)
        self._root_layout.addWidget(self.modal_container)

        self.container_layout = QVBoxLayout(self.modal_container)
        self.container_layout.setContentsMargins(20, 18, 20, 18)
        self.container_layout.setSpacing(14)

        # انیمیشن ورود نرم (Fade-In)
        self._opacity_effect = QGraphicsOpacityEffect(self.modal_container)
        self.modal_container.setGraphicsEffect(self._opacity_effect)
        self._anim = QPropertyAnimation(self._opacity_effect, b"opacity")
        self._anim.setDuration(140)
        self._anim.setStartValue(0.0)
        self._anim.setEndValue(1.0)
        self._anim.setEasingCurve(QEasingCurve.Type.OutQuad)

    def showEvent(self, event):
        super().showEvent(event)
        # مهار و ریسپانسیو کردن ابعاد دیالوگ متناسب با مانیتور جاری
        screen = self.screen() or QGuiApplication.primaryScreen()
        if screen:
            avail = screen.availableGeometry()
            max_w = int(avail.width() * 0.94)
            max_h = int(avail.height() * 0.92)
            self.setMaximumSize(max_w, max_h)
            if self.width() > max_w or self.height() > max_h:
                self.resize(min(self.width(), max_w), min(self.height(), max_h))
        self._anim.start()

    def build_header(self, title: str, subtitle: str = "", icon_name: str = "info", icon_color: str = "#818CF8"):
        header_row = QHBoxLayout()
        header_row.setSpacing(10)
        header_row.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignVCenter)

        icon_lbl = QLabel()
        icon_lbl.setPixmap(IconProvider.get_pixmap(icon_name, icon_color, size=20))
        header_row.addWidget(icon_lbl)

        title_col = QVBoxLayout()
        title_col.setSpacing(2)
        
        lbl_t = QLabel(title)
        lbl_t.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 14px; font-weight: 800; color: #F8FAFC;")
        title_col.addWidget(lbl_t)

        if subtitle:
            lbl_s = QLabel(subtitle)
            lbl_s.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 11px; color: #64748B;")
            title_col.addWidget(lbl_s)

        header_row.addLayout(title_col, stretch=1)

        btn_close = QPushButton("×")
        btn_close.setFixedSize(24, 24)
        btn_close.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        btn_close.setStyleSheet("""
            QPushButton {
                background: transparent;
                color: #64748B;
                font-size: 18px;
                font-weight: bold;
                border: none;
                border-radius: 4px;
            }
            QPushButton:hover {
                color: #F8FAFC;
                background-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.06);
            }
        """)
        btn_close.clicked.connect(self.reject)
        header_row.addWidget(btn_close)

        self.container_layout.addLayout(header_row)

        divider = QFrame()
        divider.setFrameShape(QFrame.Shape.HLine)
        divider.setFixedHeight(1)
        divider.setStyleSheet("background-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05); border: none;")
        self.container_layout.addWidget(divider)


class StudioConfirmDialog(StudioBaseDialog):
    """جایگزین مدرن و یکپارچه QMessageBox با استایل بومی نرم‌افزار"""
    def __init__(self, title: str, message: str, details: str = "", confirm_text: str = "تأیید", 
                 is_danger: bool = False, icon_name: str = "alert", parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent=parent, is_danger=is_danger)
        self.setFixedWidth(440)

        icon_color = "#EF4444" if is_danger else "#F59E0B"
        self.build_header(title, "", icon_name=icon_name, icon_color=icon_color)

        # متن پیام
        body_card = QFrame()
        body_card.setStyleSheet("background-color: #121826; border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.04); border-radius: 8px;")
        b_lay = QVBoxLayout(body_card)
        b_lay.setContentsMargins(14, 12, 14, 12)
        b_lay.setSpacing(6)

        lbl_msg = QLabel(message)
        lbl_msg.setWordWrap(True)
        lbl_msg.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 12px; font-weight: 600; color: #F8FAFC; line-height: 1.5;")
        b_lay.addWidget(lbl_msg)

        if details:
            lbl_det = QLabel(details)
            lbl_det.setWordWrap(True)
            lbl_det.setStyleSheet("font-family: 'Vazirmatn'; font-size: 11px; color: #94A3B8; line-height: 1.5;")
            b_lay.addWidget(lbl_det)

        self.container_layout.addWidget(body_card)

        # دکمه‌های اقدام
        btn_box = QHBoxLayout()
        btn_box.setSpacing(8)

        btn_confirm = QPushButton(confirm_text)
        btn_confirm.setFixedHeight(36)
        btn_confirm.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        if is_danger:
            btn_confirm.setStyleSheet("""
                QPushButton {
                    font-family: 'Vazirmatn';
                    background-color: #EF4444;
                    color: #FFFFFF;
                    border: none;
                    border-radius: 6px;
                    font-size: 12px;
                    font-weight: 700;
                    padding: 0 16px;
                }
                QPushButton:hover { background-color: #DC2626; }
            """)
        else:
            btn_confirm.setStyleSheet("""
                QPushButton {
                    font-family: 'Vazirmatn';
                    background-color: #6366F1;
                    color: #FFFFFF;
                    border: none;
                    border-radius: 6px;
                    font-size: 12px;
                    font-weight: 700;
                    padding: 0 16px;
                }
                QPushButton:hover { background-color: #4F46E5; }
            """)
        btn_confirm.clicked.connect(self.accept)
        btn_box.addWidget(btn_confirm, stretch=2)

        btn_cancel = QPushButton("انصراف")
        btn_cancel.setFixedHeight(36)
        btn_cancel.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        btn_cancel.setStyleSheet("""
            QPushButton {
                font-family: 'Vazirmatn';
                background-color: #161F30;
                color: #94A3B8;
                border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08);
                border-radius: 6px;
                font-size: 12px;
                font-weight: 500;
                padding: 0 14px;
            }
            QPushButton:hover { color: #F8FAFC; background-color: #1E283D; }
        """)
        btn_cancel.clicked.connect(self.reject)
        btn_box.addWidget(btn_cancel, stretch=1)

        self.container_layout.addLayout(btn_box)

    @classmethod
    def ask(cls, parent, title: str, message: str, details: str = "", confirm_text: str = "تأیید", 
            is_danger: bool = False, icon_name: str = "alert") -> bool:
        diag = cls(title, message, details, confirm_text, is_danger, icon_name, parent)
        return diag.exec() == QDialog.DialogCode.Accepted


class StudioInputDialog(StudioBaseDialog):
    """جایگزین مدرن و یکپارچه QInputDialog برای دریافت ورودی‌های متنی"""
    def __init__(self, title: str, label_text: str, initial_text: str = "", placeholder: str = "", parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent=parent)
        self.setFixedWidth(420)
        self.build_header(title, "", icon_name="edit", icon_color="#6366F1")

        self.container_layout.addWidget(QLabel(label_text))
        
        self.input_field = QLineEdit(initial_text)
        self.input_field.setFixedHeight(36)
        self.input_field.setPlaceholderText(placeholder)
        self.input_field.setStyleSheet("""
            QLineEdit {
                background-color: #161F30;
                border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08);
                border-radius: 6px;
                padding: 0 10px;
                font-family: 'Vazirmatn';
                font-size: 12px;
                color: #F8FAFC;
            }
            QLineEdit:focus {
                border-color: #6366F1;
                background-color: #1A2438;
            }
        """)
        self.input_field.returnPressed.connect(self.accept)
        self.container_layout.addWidget(self.input_field)

        btn_box = QHBoxLayout()
        btn_box.setSpacing(8)

        btn_ok = QPushButton("تأیید و ثبت")
        btn_ok.setFixedHeight(36)
        btn_ok.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        btn_ok.setStyleSheet("""
            QPushButton {
                font-family: 'Vazirmatn';
                background-color: #6366F1;
                color: #FFFFFF;
                border: none;
                border-radius: 6px;
                font-size: 12px;
                font-weight: 700;
            }
            QPushButton:hover { background-color: #4F46E5; }
        """)
        btn_ok.clicked.connect(self.accept)
        btn_box.addWidget(btn_ok, stretch=2)

        btn_cancel = QPushButton("انصراف")
        btn_cancel.setFixedHeight(36)
        btn_cancel.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        btn_cancel.setStyleSheet("""
            QPushButton {
                font-family: 'Vazirmatn';
                background-color: #161F30;
                color: #94A3B8;
                border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08);
                border-radius: 6px;
                font-size: 12px;
            }
            QPushButton:hover { color: #F8FAFC; background-color: #1E283D; }
        """)
        btn_cancel.clicked.connect(self.reject)
        btn_box.addWidget(btn_cancel, stretch=1)

        self.container_layout.addLayout(btn_box)

    @classmethod
    def get_text(cls, parent, title: str, label_text: str, initial_text: str = "", placeholder: str = "") -> Tuple[str, bool]:
        diag = cls(title, label_text, initial_text, placeholder, parent)
        ok = (diag.exec() == QDialog.DialogCode.Accepted)
        return diag.input_field.text().strip(), ok
